prathamāhnikam|

praṇamya gaurīśam acintyaśaktiṃ sarvajñam ajñānatamaḥpradīpam|

kāṇādasūtrasya mayā nibandho vidhīyate śaṅkarakiṅkareṇa||

mahādevopadiṣṭasya sūtrasyaitannibandhanam|

sevyatāṃ sakalairarthayathārthajñānahetave||

athāto dharmaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ|| 1-1-1||

tatrāyamathaśabda ānantarye, taccānantaryaṃ na
yataḥ kutaścidapi śabdena samarpaṇīyam,
vaiyarthyāt| na hi yatkiṃcidānantaryaṃ
dharmavyākhyānasya kvacidupayujyate|
tasmādānantaryamātravācako 'apyayam athaśabda īśvarapraṇāmādeva granthaparisamāptisiddheriti yuktam|
iṣṭadevatānamaskārānantarameva granthādyārambhaḥ kartavya iti śiṣyajñāpanārthatvāt|
anyathā hi iṣṭadevatāpraṇāmamantareṇa granthamārabhamāṇaḥ śiṣyā granthaparisamāptipracayahetunamaskārajanyadharmākhyasahakārivirahāviphalārambhā bhaveyuḥ|
na cābhīṣṭadevatānamaskārajanyadharmasya granthasamāptipracayahetutve mānābhāvaḥ|
avigītaśiṣṭācārānumitavedādestatra pramāṇatvāt|
kathaṃ tarhīṣṭadevatānamaskārapūrvakatve .api kādambaryāderaparisamāptiriti cet---na,
namaskārajanyadharmasattve [.api]
granthasamāptihetukartṛdehādivaikalyāt|
na hyasmākaṃ namaskārajanyadharmamātraṃ granthasamāptihetuḥ, api tu kartṛdehavighnanāśādirapi kartṛdehādivaikalyasya ca bhāvenādṛṣṭādivināśāt|
na ca ta[rhi] kartṛ[dṛṣṭa]hetusākalye granthasamāpteravyatirekadarśanānna namaskārajanyadharmasya granthasamāptihetutvamiti yuktam| vyatirekābhāvena namaskārajanyadharmasya granthasamāptikāraṇatvābhāvānumāne śiṣṭācārānu[mitadevāpramāṇyāpatteḥ] granthasamāptijanakātmādau vyabhicārācca|
yatra na granthasamāptistatra kathaṃ namaskārajanyadharmavināśaḥ
phalavināśyatvāddharmasyeti
cet---nimittakāraṇadehavināśāt,
mocakātmasākṣātkārādvā|
na ca mīmāṃsābhāṣyādernamaskārābhāve .api
samāptidarśanānna namaskāra[ja]nyadharmasya
granthasamāptihetutvamiti
yuktam| tatrāpi
śiṣṭācārānumitavedapramāṇakamānasābhinamaskārābhāvasya
yogyānupalambhavirahe niścetumaśakyatvāt|
tasmādīśvarapraṇāmānantaryamathaśabdārthaḥ|

yadvā
parameśvaraprasādāsāditadharmādisākṣātkāraparameśvaradharmopadeśaniyogayorānantaryamathaśabdārthaḥ|
yadi
parameśvaraprasādāsāditadharmajñānaniyogayoranantaraṃ
dharmaṃ nopadiśettataḥ
paramagurorumāpaterājñāmatikrāmanpratyaveyāt| na
ca teṣāṃ tejoviśeṣeṇa pratyavāyo na vidyata iti
vācyam| parameśvarājñātikramasya
sakalasaṃsāritejobhibhāvakatvāt| na ca
parameśvaraprasādāsāditadharmādisākṣātkāravānparameśvaraniyuktaśca
bhagavānkaṇādo munirityatra mānābhāvaḥ,
sāmpradāyikācārapramāṇatvāt| upadiśanti hi
[sāmpradāyikāḥ] kaṇādo
munirulūkaveṣadhāriṇaḥ
parameśvarāddharmādisākṣātkāradharmopadeśaniyogamāsādya
vaiśeṣikasūtramasūtrayaditi|

nanvastu
dharmasākṣātkāraparameśvaraniyogānantaryaṃ
dharmavyākhyānasya
vaiśeṣikaśāstraprāmāṇyajñāpanārtham|
a[na]dhigate hi tattvasākṣātkārapūrvakatve
vaiśeṣikaśāstrasya tatprāmāṇyaṃ
śiṣyairaśakyamadhigantum|
pramitipūrvakavākyatvasya prāmāṇyāvyabhicārāt|

[ata iti hetau| yataḥ]
adhītasāṅgavedāvagatasvargāpavargārthinaḥ
tatsādhanajijñāsavo grahaṇadhāraṇādisamarthāḥ
vedācca tatsādhanamanadhigacchantaḥ p.cchanti ata
ityarthaḥ|

[tridivābhilāṣī yajatāmityādetridivasādhanaṃ] dharmo
.avagamyata iti cet---na, dhātvarthasya yāgādeḥ pratyayārthasya
ca bhāvanāyāḥ ciravinaṣṭatvena
kālāntarabhāvisvargasādhanatvasya vedasahasreṇāpi
bodhayitumaśakyatvāt|
yogyatāvirahādavyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartivyāpārakṣaṇavartitvaṃ sādhyatve yogyatā|
nanu phalāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartivyāpāratve phalotpādanaśaktimattvaṃ vā sādhanatve yogyatā|
na tu phalāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartitvaṃ sādhanatve yogyatā|
sādhanavyāpārakṣaṇavartitvaṃ sādhanaśaktiviṣayatvaṃ svasādhyatvaṃ vā yogyatā|
na tu sādhanāvyavahitottarakṣaṇavartitvaṃ sādhyatve yogyateti cet---na, svargayāgādisādhyasādhanabhāvāvagamātpūrvamasyā
yogyatāyā avagantumaśakyatvāt| na hyanavagate pūrvavyāpāre
svargāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartivyāpāravattvaṃ śakyamadhigantum,
viśeṣaṇajñānapūrvakatvādviśeṣyajñānasya|
nāpyanavagatāyāṃ śaktau tadutpādakatvaṃ śaktimattvamavagantuṃ
tata eva| na cānavagataiva yogyatānvayapratīti[janiketi] samucitam|
anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ yogyatāvagateranvayapratītijanakatvasya
nirṇayāt| na ca sādhyasādhanabhāvāvagamanānantarabhāvinī
yogyatāpratītiḥ, anvayāvagamakā[rya]kṣaṇavartitvavirahāt,
asambhavācca| svargayāgayoḥ sādhyasādhanabhāvānavagame
tadanyathānupapattijanyāpūrvapratīteranudayāt| atha na
yogyatāvadhāraṇamanvayapratītikāraṇam, kiṃ tu
yogyatābhāvāvadhāraṇamanvayapratītipratibandhakaṃ
[tadabhāvaścānvayapratītihetuḥ|
sva]rgāvyavahitapurrvakṣaṇavartyapūrvarūpayāgavyāpārābhāvasya
pratyakṣādinā
vākyārthāvagamātpūrvaṃ
nirṇetumaśakyatvāditi manyase| tadapi na, vikalpāsahatvāt| tathā
hi---kiṃ sādhyāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartivyāpāravattvaṃ
sādhanatvena yogyatā,
uta svargāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartivyāpāravattvaṃ yāgasya
svargasādhanatve yogyatā| nādyaḥ, odanakāmaḥ pacet, tṛptikāmo
bhuñjītetyādāvanvayapratītivirahāpatteḥ,
pākabhojanayorodanatṛptikṣaṇavartivyāpāravattvasābhāvasyānirṇayāt,
pākabhojanayorodanatṛptisādhanatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
svargāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartivyāpāravattvādirūpayogyatāvirahāvagamasyāvidyamānatvena
svargayāgasādhyasādhanabhāvāvagamapratibandhakatvānupapatteḥ|
vidyamānatve vā svargayāgasādhyasādhanabhāvāvagamānupapatteḥ
[yāge]ṣu svarāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavṛttivyāpāravattvasya
svargasādhanatvayogyatārūpatve yāgādeḥ
svargasādhanatvamanyadvaktavyam| na hi sādhanatvameva sādhane
yogyatānvayapratītikāraṇam, tadvirahe .apyagninā
siñcedityādāvanvayapratīterutpādāt kevalaṃ
nādhigatatvānnāsau pramā|
svargayāgādisādhyasādhanabhāvāvagamastu
bādhābhāvātpramaiveti manyase| na,
svargāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartitvasya svargasādhanatve bādhāyā
durvāratvāt| svargāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartivyāpāratvasya ca
sādhanatve sādhanasādhanatve bodhakayajerapūrvabodhakatvāpatteḥ|
evamastviti cet---na, pūrvamapūrvānavagatau yajestatra
vācakatvānavagamāt, yajito .apūrvamavagamya punastatra
yajervācakatvāvagama iti cet---na, parasparāśrayaprasaṅgāt|
yajito .apūrve .avagate yajerapūrvavācakatvajñānam,
yajerapūrvavācakatvajñāne ca yajerapūrvāvagama iti| etena
yāgamātraṃ yajerarthaḥ| sādhanatvaṃ
punariṣṭasādhanatvavācino liṅpratyayasyetyapi pratyuktam
svargāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartivyāpāravattvarūpasādhanatvavācakatve
liṅpratyayasyāpūrvavācakatvaprasaṅga
ityāderapasiddhāntaprasaṅgācca,
sādhyasādhanabhāvāvagamānantarakalpyatvādapūrvasya|
yadāhuḥ---

phalāya vihitaṃ karma kṣaṇikaṃ parabhāvine|

tatsiddhirnānyathetyevam apūrvam api kalpyate||

iti|

athāstu kāmaṃ svargāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartitvaṃ sādhanatvaṃ yāgasya|
na ca tacciravinaṣṭasya svargaṃ prati bādhitam|
yāgānantarameva svargo .apīti
svīkārādityabhidhatse [cet] tadapi na,
yāgāvyavahitottarakṣaṇe svargasya
yogyānupalambhanirastatvāt|tadānīṃ
sūkṣmatvādayogyo .asāviti cet---na, kālāntare
.apyayogyatvāpatteḥ| kālāntare sthūlatāmāpanno
yogya iti cet---na,
sūkṣmasthūlatvayorviruddhadharmatvena
dharmibhedakatvāt| upalambhayogyasya ca
yāgāvyavahitottarakṣaṇavartitvābhāvena
yāgajanyatadanutpatteḥ| atha
nāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartitvādikaṃ sādhanatvaṃ
kiṃ tūtpādakatvam| tacca na yāgasya svargaṃ
pratibādhitam, indriyādinā yāgasya
svargotpādakābhāvaniścayāt|
tadabādhitavedāvagatasvargādiśreyaḥsādhanābhāvo
yāgādireva dharmatā iti kathaṃ na
vedāddharmāvagatiriti manyase---tadapi na,
avyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartitvādivyatiriktotpādakasattve
mānābhāvāt| syāvyavahita tpādakamiti
vyavahita
vyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavṛttitvādevopapatteḥ|
astu
vāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartitvādavyatiriktotpādakatvam,
yāgasya tu svargotpādakatve mānābhāvaḥ|

svargakāmo yajeta iti vedaḥ pramāṇamiti cet---na,
yajeryāgamātrārthatvāt, [sva]rgakāmapadasya
ca svargakāmanāviśiṣṭapuruṣapratipādakatvāt|
athākhyātapratyayeneṣṭasādhanatvarūpo
vidhirabhidhīyate| kiṃ tadiṣṭamityapekṣāyāṃ
nirupādhikāmanāviṣayaḥ svargaḥ sambadhyate|
iṣṭasādhanatvasya vācāśrayākāṅkṣāyāṃ
prakṛtyartho yāgaḥ sambadhyate, tena
svargasādhanaṃ yāga iti vākyārthaḥ sampadyata iti
cet---na, iṣṭasya svargapadopāttatvena
tadviśiṣṭasādhanatvasya liṅarthe vaiyarthyāt|

astu sādhanatvameva liṅarthaḥ| iṣṭāvacchedastu
tasya svargasamabhivyāhāreṇa lakṣyata iti cet---na,
sādhanatvasya liṅarthatve pramāṇābhāvāt| astu
veṣṭasādhanatvaṃ liṅarthaḥ| svarga eva ceṣṭaḥ
sa[ḥ|] tathāpi sādhanatvāśrayākāṅkṣāyāṃ
prakṛtyarthādyāgātsannihitatvena pratyayārtho
bhāvanaivānvīyate| tena bhāvanā
svargasādhanatvamiti vākyārthasampattau kathaṃ
yāgasya svargasādhanatvasiddhiriti|

api ca bhaṭṭamate svargakāmo yajetetyatra
preraṇāgocarībhūtā bhāvanā pratīyate| sā ca
prathamaṃ vibhāvyamapekṣate
bhāvyaniṣṭhabhāvakavyāpāratvāt| tasyāḥ (|)
tena bhāvyākāṅkṣāyāṃ dvitīyāsamanārthatvena
kimiti nopāttaḥ svargaḥ sādhyatayā sambadhyate|
tataḥ keneti sādhanākāṅkṣāyāṃ prakṛtyartho
yāgādiḥ sādhanatvenānvīyate| tena
bhāvyasvargaviśiṣṭā sādhanayāgaviśiṣṭā ca
bhāvanaiva vākyārtha iti kathaṃ yāgasya
svargasādhamatvasiddhiḥ| atha bhāvanā bhāvyamiva
bhāvyasādhanamapekṣate iti bhāvyasādhanatayā
yāgasyānvaya iti matam---tanna, yāgasya
svargasādhanatvānupapatteruktatvāditi|

prābhākarapakṣe .api svargakāmo yajetetyatra
liṅpratyayena sthāyyapūrvaṃ kāryamabhidhīyate| na
tu yāgādi| tasya ciravinaṣṭasya
kāmyamānasvargasādhanasya svargakāmena kāryatayā
boddhumaśakyatvāt| kāmī hi kāmyamānaṃ sādhanaṃ
kāryatayā budhyata iti nyāyāt| kāryaṃ ca
kṛtinirūpyam, kṛtyuddeśakṛtisādhyatvāttasya|
kṛtiśca prayatnaḥ, sa ca
kṣā[sthā]nādivadviṣayanirūpya iti
viṣayākāṅkṣāyāṃ yāgaḥ
prayatnaviṣayatvenānvīyate| tena yajeteti
yāgaviṣayakṛtisādhyaṃ kṛtyuddeśyaṃ
vāpūrvamityarthaḥ sampadyate| kṛtiśca yathā
viṣayamapekṣate tathāśrayamapi| sa eva
kṛterāśrayaḥ yo mamedaṃ kāryamiti kāryaṃ
budhyate tena kāryaboddhrapekṣāyāṃ
svargakāmapadena kāryaboddhā niyojyaḥ samarpyate|
kāryaṃ ca svabhāvataḥ sādhyamapi kāmyamānaṃ
svargasādhanatayāvatiṣṭhate| kāmī
hikāmyamānasādhanaṃ kāryatayā budhyata iti
nyāyāt| seyaṃ [sarvaṃ]
sādhyavṛtti[vivṛtti]rityucyate sādhyasyāpūrvasya
svargasya sādhyāntarasvīkārāt|

atha yāgasya svargasādhanatvābhāve
svargakāmakṛtiviṣayatvaṃ na syādityucyate|
[na, svasādhanāpūrvasādhanatvena] tena
svargaśarīrabhūtamapūrvaṃ niyogāparaparyāyaḥ
svargakāmaboddhṛviśiṣṭaṃ
yāgaviṣayakṛtijanyam| kṛtyuddeśyaṃ ca vākyārtha
iti kathaṃ yāgasya svargasādhanatvāvagatiḥ|
tatpratikṣepeṇaiva
liṅastātparyāpūrvavācakatvasiddheḥ vidhā

asmanmate .abādhitānubhūtitvasya
pramāṇalakṣaṇatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ, abhidhāne vā
tata eva kṛtyupapattau kāryavācitvāsiddheḥ| atha
neṣṭasādhanatvamātrāt tanna|,
kṛtijanyeṣṭasādhanatvāvagamādeva
pravṛttyupapattau
kṛtyuddeśyatvāderabhidheyatvaprasaṅgāt| na
dvitīyaḥ, vikalpānupapatteḥ| tathā
hi---kimagṛhītasaṅgataya eva liṅgādayo
niyogamabhidadhati, gṛhītasaṅgatayo vā| nādyaḥ,
bālānāmapi tadavagamaprasaṅgāt| dvitīye .api,
kimanavagate saṅgatisaṃgrahe .a[graho]vagate vā|
nādyaḥ, vācyāvagamasya
tadvācakāvagamakāraṇatvāt| dvitīye .api
tadavagatiḥ pramāṇāttarkādvā|

nādyaḥ, niyogasya bhavadbhiḥ
pramāṇāntaravedyatvānaṅgīkārāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
vyāpyāropeṇa vyāpakāpādanasya tarkatvāt| na ca
niyogajñānātpūrvaṃ niyogavyāpyatayā
kiṃcitpramitam, yadāropeṇa niyogāpādanaṃ syāt|
atha niyojyaparaḥ
svargakāmapadasamabhivyāhāraparyālocanayā
sthāyyapūrvaṃ liṅā pratipādanīyamiti
tarkajñānamajñāyata iti cet---na, sthāyyapūrvaṃ
vinā liṅā pratipādanīyamiti liṅāḥ
sthāyyapūrvavācakatvajñānam| na cāpūrvaṃ
jñānamantareṇa sambhavati| apūrvajñānaṃ ca na
pramāṇāt, tasya mānāntarāvedyatvāt| nāpi
tarkāt, tasyāpi
pūrvavattarkāntarāpekṣāyāmanavasthāpaka[tva]at|

kiṃ ca
svargakāmādipadasamabhivyāhāraparyālocanayetyatra
kimanavagatasaṃgatisvargakāmapadayajatisambhūyoccāritaliṅpratyayaparyālocanayā
iti vivakṣitam, kiṃ vā
avagatasaṃgatisvargakāmapadayajatisamabhivyāhāraparyālocanayeti|
nādyaḥ, bālādīnāmapi tathāpratyayaprasaṅgāt|
dvitīye .api kimanavagatasaṃgateḥ liṅpratyayasya
samabhivyāhāraparyālocanayeti vivakṣitam,
utāvagatasaṃgateḥ| nādyaḥ, pūrvoktadoṣāt|
dvitīye .api
kimapūrvakāryāvagatasaṃgaterliṅpratyayasya
paryālocanayā, kiṃ vā
kriyākāryāvagaterliṅpratyayasya paryālocanayā|
nādyaḥ,
saṃgatigrahaṇārthamapūrvopasthāpakatarkātpūrvaṃ
liṅo .avagatasaṃgatitvānupapatteḥ| dvitīye .api
pakṣe kiṃ kriyākāryaṃ
svargakāmaniyojyānvayayogyaṃ na vā| prathame
svargakāmaniyojyānvitakriyākāryāvagamaḥ syāt|
evaṃvidhasamabhivyāhāraparyālocanasyaivaṃvidhapratyayajanakatvāt|
na tu sthāyyapūrvapratyayaḥ
svargakāmaniyojyapadayajisamabhivyāhārakriyākāryāvagatasaṃgatiliṅpratyayaparyālocanasya pratyakṣādivyatiriktajñānajanakatve mānābhāvāt|
atha yo yatkāmaniyojyavācakapadasamabhivyāhṛto
liṅpratyayaḥ sa
tadavyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartikāryavācaka iti
vyāptiniścayāt
liṅādivākyātsthāyyapūrvapratyaya iti na
sāmānyato vyāpyāvagame .apyayaṃ liṅpratyayaḥ
sthāyyapūrvavācaka iti
niṣedhaniṣṭhajñānānudayātsvargakāmādiniyojyavācakapadasamabhivyāhṛtaśca
liṅpratyaya iti viśeṣaniṣṭhavyāpyajñānasahito
vyāptiniścayo liṅaḥ
sthāyyapūrvavācakatvapratyayaheturiti
cet---anumānaṃ tarhīdam, na tu tarkajñānam|
evamastviti cet---na, apūrvasya
mānāntarāvedyatvāt|
na cānumānamapi sambhavati| ayaṃ liṅpratyayaḥ
svargāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartiyāgakṛtijanyayāgakṛtyuddeśyavācakaḥ,
svargakāmapadādisamabhivyāhṛtaliṅpratyayatvāt| yo
yatkāmādipadasamabhivyāhṛtaliṅpratyayaḥ sa
tadavyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartikāryavācakaḥ| yathā
tṛptikāmo bhuñjītetyevamādiliṅpratyaya
ityāderaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt,
svargotpādakayāgakṛtijanyakṛtyuddeśyatadvācakatvayorasmānpratyasiddhatvāt|
saṃgatigrahaṇātpūrvamevaṃvidhakāryavācakatvasya
prābhākarāṇāmapyasiddhatvācca| yo
yatkāmādipadasamabhivyāhṛtaliṅpratyayaḥ sa
tatkāmyamānotpādakārthavācaka iti
sāmānyenānvaya iti cet---na, yattacchabdayoḥ
prakṛtasvargakāmādisamabhivyāhṛtaliṅpratyayatvasvargotpādakakāryavācakaparatve
pūrvoktānvayavirahasya tādavasthyāt,
tṛptakāmapadasamabhivyāhṛtaliṅpratyayatvatṛptisādhanakāryavācakatvaparatve
tayorevānvayasiddhau
svargakāmādipadasamabhivyāhṛtaliṅpratyayatvasvargotpādakakāryavācakatvayoranvayābhāvasya
tadavasthatvāt| na ca yattacchabdayoranugataṃ
pratipādyamasti mānābhāvāditi
evaṃvidhatarkajñānasya niṣpramāṇakatvācca|
padānāṃ tātparyāvadhārako nyāyastarkaḥ,
tadupasthite pūrve saṃgati iti cet
pratyastamānayoreva nyāyapadāntara kiṃ ca ca
tarko .anubhūtiḥ smṛtirvā| ādye pramaiva
[a]bādhitānubhūtitvasya pramāṇalakṣaṇatvāt| na
dvitīyaḥ, saṃskārājanyatvāt atha
samānaviṣaya te, tanna, anubhavasāpekṣatvaṃ
yadyanubhavajanyatvam, tatprakṛte na sambhavati,
bhāvinaḥ pūrvakṣaṇavartitvābhāvenājanakatvāt|
janyatvāvyatiriktasya cānubhavasāpekṣatvasya
niṣpramāṇatvāt| na ca
smṛtyanubhavavyatiriktastarka iti,
smṛtyāhitajñānasyānubhavatvāt
anubhavatvarahitasya ca jñānasya smṛtitvāt|
tasmālloke liṅādīnāṃ kriyākārye vyutpattiḥ|
vede tu mānāntarāpūrve sthāyikārya iti
durghaṭameveti|

anye tu vṛddhavyavahāraparyālocanayā
kāryaparatvāvagamo liṅādīnāṃ tadanantaraṃ vede
svargakāmaniyojyasamabhivyāhārānupapattyā
sthāyyapūrvakārye .avyutpattiḥ
vācakatvabodhāparaparyāyaliṅādīnām|
tadanantaramapūrvakāryavācināṃ liṅādīnāṃ loke
yatkāryabodhakatvaṃ tallakṣaṇayeti nirṇīyate|
apūrvakāryavācināṃ kāryamātre lakṣaṇayāpi
prayogopapattau vācakatvakalpakābhāvāt|
kriyākārye vācakatvamapūrve tu lakṣaṇe
.atidurghaṭameva|
apūrvasya pramāṇāntaragocaratvena tatra liṅādīnāṃ lakṣaṇānupapatteḥ|
mukhyārthabādhe tatsambandhini smṛtirūpatvāt tasyāḥ|
asmṛtitve 'api vācyasambandhāvagamasya lakṣaṇāṅgatvasvīkārāt|
tasmādapūrva eva dharmarūpe vyutpattiḥ,
prāmāṇyaṃ ca liṅādīnāmiti|
tadapi na, apūrve vyutpattivirahasya vyutpāditatvāt|
loke ca liṅādīnāṃ vṛddhavyavahārabalenānyatraiva vācakatvavyutpādanāditi|

anye tu vṛddhavyavahāraparyālocanayā kārye vyutpattirliṅādīnām|
na tu dhātvarthāparaparyāyakriyātvaviśeṣite 'apyavacchedyābhāvena viśeṣaṇavaiyarthyāt,
anyathā prameyatvābhidheyatvādiviśiṣṭakriyākārye liṅādīnāṃ vyutpattiprasaṅgāt|
tathā ca yogavyutpattibalenākāṅkṣitasannihitayogyasvargakāmaniyojyānvitakāryaṃ

vede liṅādibhirabhidhīyate| tacca na
yāgādidhātvapūrvaṃ tasya ciravinaṣṭasya
kālāntarabhāvikāmyamānasvargasādhanatvānupapattau
yogyatāviraheṇa svargakāmaniyojyānvayānupapatteḥ|
kāmī hi kāmyamānaṃ sādhanaṃ kāryatayā budhyata
iti nyāyāt| tacca [na?] yāgātiriktaṃ kāryaṃ
śabdātiriktamānāgocaratvādapūrvamityucyate|
svātmānaṃ niyuṅkte prerayatīti niyoga
ityākhyāyate| tena lokāvagatakāryavyutpattibalena
sthāyikārye vedasya prāmāṇyam| yatkāryaṃ
tatsamavāyikāraṇajanyam| lokāvagatavyāptibalena
cānumānasya paramāṇvādāviti---tadapi na yuktam,
loke .apyānayanādeḥ kāryasya dhātvarthatvena
pratyayārthatvānupapatteḥ|
dhātvarthātiriktakāryasya ca loke
prābhākarairanabhyupagamāt, ānayanādayaḥ
kāryaviśeṣā vā dhātvarthāḥ| anugataṃ tu
kāryaṃ pratyayārtha iti cet---na,
ānayanādiviśeṣaniṣṭhakāryatvātiriktānugatakārye
.anumānābhāvāt| kāryaṃ kāryamityanugataḥ
pratyayo mānamiti cet---na, kāryatvādeva
tadupapatteḥ| anyathā
śābaleyādiviśeṣaniṣṭhagotvātiriktānugatagopadārthānupapatteḥ|

atha loke kāryatve vyutpattirliṅādīnām, tadā
kāryatvamākāṅkṣitasannihitayogyānayanādidhātvarthānvitaṃ
loke .abhidhīyate| vede tu na
yāgādidhātvarthānvitaṃ kāryatvamabhidhātuṃ
śakyate, tasya ciravinaṣṭasya
kālāntaragāmisvargasādhanatvānupapattau
svargakāminā kāryatvena boddhumaśakyatvāt| tena
vede dhātvarthātiriktasthāyikāryatvaṃ
siddheraparyavasānāditi| tadapi na,
vacakatvātiriktāparyavasānānirukteḥ|
na tāvadiyaṃ lakṣaṇā, vācyasambandhitayā pramāṇāntarāvagate lakṣaṇāsvīkārādapūrvakāryasya|
sa ca mānāntarāgocaratvāt vidhau ca lakṣaṇāṃ vadatā
na vidhau paraḥ śabdārtha iti sūtrasākṣikaṃ
prābhākaramanuvistṛtam|

nāpi gauṇī, vācyakāryaguṇavattāyā
apūrvakāryasyānavagamāt| nāpi
vācakatvalakṣaṇā, gauṇīvyatiriktapadasya
vṛttyantaramasti pramāṇābhāvāt| atha kāryasya
svabhāvaḥ kāryatvam| tacca
kāryaghaṭitamamūrtitvāt| kāryatvamanabhidhāya
liṅādibhiḥ nābhidhātuṃ śakyate
ityaparyavasānalabhyamiti cet---na, yadi
kāryatvamanabhidhāya dhātvarthamanabhidhātuṃ
śakyate kāryatvamanabhidheyameva| na
tvaparyavasānalabhyam| evaṃ loke .api
kāryamabhidheyameveti gataṃ liṅādīnāṃ
kāryatvavācakatvakalpanayā| tasmānnaṣṭaṃ
prābhākaramate .api dharme vedasya prāmāṇyamiti|

nanu yadi na dharme vedasya prāmāṇyam, kva tarhi
svargakāmo yajetetyasya prāmāṇyam| dharmasādhye
svarge tatsādhakena ca yāge| tathā ca
praśastapādāḥ---tasya tu sādhanāni
śrutismṛtivihitānītyādi| kaḥ punaḥ svargakāmo
yajetetyasyārthaḥ svargakāmo yāgaṃ kuryāditi|

nanu yadi na yāgaḥ svargasādhanaṃ kimarthaṃ tarhi
svargakāmena kriyate|
svargotpādakadharmārthamityavaihi|
svargotpādako yāgajanyo dharmaḥ
punarvaiśeṣikaśāstravyutpāditānumānasamadhigamya
eveti|

tatsiddhaṃ yato na vedāddharmāvagatiḥ, ato dharmaṃ
vyākhyāsyāmaḥ| viśabdo viśeṣe| viśeṣaśca
prādhānyam| tena viśeṣataḥ prādhānyena dharmaṃ
vyākhyāsyāmaḥ| dravyādiṣaṭpadārthāstu
tadaṅgatayeti sidhyati| yadvā viśabdo vividhayogena|
vividhaṃ pravartakanivartakādibhedenānekaprakāraṃ
dharmaṃ vyākhyāsyāma ityarthaḥ|
āṅabhividhau na maryādāyām, parivarjanīyābhāvātsarvasyāpi dharmasya pratipādanamiti cet---na, dharmapadasya yatkiṃciddharmajātīyapratipādanena caritārthasya sakaladharmapratipādakatvābhāvāt|
anyathā gāmabhyājetyādau sarvagavāmānayanapravṛtteḥ gopadena sarvagavāmabhidhānāt|
vyākhyāsyāma iti khyā prakathana ityasya [dhāto rūpam|
prakṛṣṭa[tvaṃ
nirduṣṭadharmapramāṇatvapratipādakatvam|] tacca na śāstrāntare
.asti, tatpratipāditapramāṇānāṃ yathāsthānaṃ
doṣābhidhānāt| yadā tu na śāstrāntarāṇi
dharmapratipādakāni kiṃ tu
vaiśeṣikaśāstrametaddharmapratipādakamiti nirbharaḥ, tadā na
śāstrāntarasya dharmakathanatvābhāvāt nedaṃ
prakṛṣṭadharmakathanamiti khyā prakathana ityasyedaṃ rūpam, api
tu cakṣiṅaḥ khyāñityādeśasya| ātmanepadaprasaṅga iti
cet---na, vyākhyānakartṛgāmiphalābhāvāt| svaritañitaḥ
kartrabhiprāye kriyāphale iti pāṇinismaraṇāt|
parameśvaraprasādāsāditasakalapuruṣārthasya bhagavataḥ
sūtrakārasyātmagāmiphalābhāvāt| kimarthaṃ tarhi
dharmavyākhyānam| parārthamityavaihi| parairavagato dharmo
.anuṣṭhīyate ca pareṣāṃ svargāpavargasiddheḥ| paragatau
svargāpavargau na phalamiti cet---na, pareṣāmeva phalatvāt|
paragato .aparasya na phalamiti cet---na, nityamuktasya bhagavataḥ
parameśvarasya dharmādharmasādhanopadeśavedapraṇetuḥ
paragatasvargāpavargaprayojanatvena dṛṣṭāntavirahāt,
vaiśeṣikasūtrapraṇetuḥ paragatau svargāpavargau na prayojanamiti
cet---na, īśvarasyāpi prācīnavaiśeṣikamahāsūtrapraṇetṛtvābhyupagamāt|

etadvaiśeṣikasūtrapraṇetuḥ paragatau svargāpavargau na prayojanamiti cet---na, īśvarasyāpyetatsūtrapraṇetṛtvābhyupagamāt|
anīśvaropadeśānāṃ dvikartṛkatvāddṛṣṭāntābhāvācceti|

atrākārapraśleṣaṇādadharmavyākhyānapratijñāpi draṣṭavyā|
tathā ca yato vedārthavigatanarakādijihāsavaḥ
tatsādhanajijñāsavaḥ pṛcchanti, ato .adharmaṃ vyākhyāsyāma
ityarthaḥ| anekārthaparatve vākyabheda iti cet---na,
akārapraśleṣāpraśleṣābhyāṃ vākyabhedasyeṣṭatvāt,
akārapraśleṣamantareṇāpi sūtrāṇāmanekārthadarśanena
vākyabhedasyādūṣaṇatvācceti| viśvatomukhamityanena
sūtrāṇāmanekārthapratipādakatvasya vivakṣitatvāt| tadevaṃ
prathamasūtreṇa dharmādharmau śāstrapratipādyau darśitau|| 1||

nanu yadi na dharme vedaḥ pramāṇam, kiṃ tarhi
pramāṇamityāśaṅkāparihārārthaṃ sūtram---

yato .abhyudayaniḥśreyasasiddhiḥ sa dharmaḥ|| 1-1-2||

iti abhyudayaḥ svargādisukhaṃ niḥśreyasaṃ muktiḥ| tayoḥ
siddhirutpattiḥ sā yasmāt
sa dharmaḥ| tayoḥ siddhiriti siddheḥ pakṣadharmatvaṃ darśayati|
nanu utpattiprāgabhāvapratiyogitvāditi rūpāyā ajanyatvāt|
kathaṃ yato .abhyudayaniḥśreyasasiddhiriti hetau
pañcamīsmaraṇāttaseśca pañcamyarthe viva[kṣitatvāt
]dutpattiṃ prati ca dharmasyāhetutvāditi na utpattimatā
kāraṇasambandhādyata utpattirityuktam| yathā tantubhyaḥ paṭo
jāyata ityādi yato .abhyudayaniḥśreyase sa dharma
ityetāvataivābhyudayaniḥśreyasayoḥ kāryayoḥ dharme
pramāṇatvenābhidhānādvyarthaṃ siddhigrahaṇamiti cet---na,
svargāpavargayordharmatvāt, utpatterliṅgatvāt, liṅgasya
pramāṇatvāditi|

tathā ca
svargāpavargāvasmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitaguṇajanyau
utpatteḥ| yatra utpattiḥ
tadasmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitaguṇajanyam,
yathā patanam| tathā cedam, tasmāttatheti| utpattimattvaṃ hetuḥ,
na tūtpattiriti cet---na, utpattervyabhicārābhāvena
matvarthavaiyarthyāt| evaṃ tarhi dhūmādityeva vaktavyam, na tu
dhūmavattvāditi cet---evamastu
vicārayiṣyāmaścedamanumānāvasare| patanaṃ
cāsmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitagurutvaguṇajanyatvātsapakṣa
cātra patanatvamupādhiḥ, avayavagurutvajanye avayavigurutve
sādhyavyāpakatvabhaṅgāt| uktasādhyasattve .api tatra
patanatvābhāvāt| evaṃ viśeṣaṇagurutvajanye
gurutvaviśiṣṭajñāne gurutvajanye abhighātādau ca
sādhyāvyāpakatvabhaṅgo draṣṭavyaḥ|

nāpi gurutvajanyatvamatropādhiḥ, gurutvaviśeṣaṇasya
pakṣatulyamātravyāpakatvāt, ajanyānāṃ ca janyatvenaiva
vyāvṛttatvāt| pakṣatulyamātravyāvartakaviśeṣaṇaśca pakṣe
gamakatvenānupādhitvāt, sakartṛkatve śarīrajanyatvāt|
svargāpavargau
nāsmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitaguṇajanyau
gurutvājanyatvāt, ākāśavaditi cet---na, ajanyatve .api
sopādhitvāt| nāpi bhāvatvamatropādhiḥ gurutvadhvaṃse
sādhyavyāpatvabhaṅgāt|
tasyāsmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitagurutvaguṇajanyatvāt,

abhāvatvācca| gurutvadhvaṃso nāgurutvajanyaḥ| ayaṃ
gurutvadhvaṃso naitadgurutvajanyo vā dhvaṃsatvāt, abhāvatvācca
ghaṭadhvaṃsavaditi cet---gurutvajanyatvādeḥ
kvacidapramitāva[tva?]prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt|
pratiyogipramitipūrvakatvādabhāvapramiteranvayavyatirekasahitapratyakṣatvāt,
kvacitpramitau prakṛtenaiva
bādhātsādhyasiddhyarthamupajīvyatvāttasyetyācāryāḥ|

vayaṃ tu brūmaḥ| gurutvaṃ gurutvajanyabhāvatvarahitajanakam|
etadgurutvametajjanyabhāvatvarahitajanakam, vācakatvāt,
ghaṭavaditi siddhe gurutvajanye .abhāve
dhvaṃsatvādervyabhicāraḥ, mūlānumānopādheḥ bhāvatvasya
sākṣādvyāpakatvabhaṅgaśceti| tasyāpi gurutvājanyatvasādhane
dharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhāt gurutvajanyatvenaiva tatsiddheḥ| tena
muktirnāsmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitaguṇajanyā
abhāvatvāt, anyonyābhāvavaditi pratiṣedho nirastaḥ|
gurutvahanyatvenāvasite .abhāve vyabhicārāt, ajanyatvena
sopādhitvācca| ato na muktirnādṛṣṭajanyā
bhogasādhanetaratvādityudayano .api

nirastaḥ, ajanyatvena sopādhitvādadṛṣṭanivṛttau
vyabhicārācca| anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ pratiyoginaḥ
pradhvaṃsajanakatvasiddheḥ| udayano .api
pākajotpattau prāgabhāvasya pratiyogikāraṇatvavyutpādanāt,
adṛṣṭadhvaṃse cādṛṣṭasyaiva prāgabhāvatvasvīkārāt,
bhogatatsādhanetaratvaṃ ca sukhaduḥkhatatsādhanetaratvaṃ vā,
sukhaduḥkhānubhavatatsādhanetaratvaṃ vā,
sukhaduḥkhanivṛttitatsādhanetaratvaṃ vā| prathamadvitīyayoḥ
yogajadharmātmajanyātmasākṣātkāre vyabhicārāt| so .api
sukhaduḥkhaheturit cet---tanna, mānābhāvāt| tṛtīye tvasiddheḥ,
mukterduḥkhanivṛttitvena taditaratvāsiddheḥ| duḥkhādau
gatatvādutpattiranaikāntikīti cet---na,
dharmajanyatvasyāsādhyatvāt|
asmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvaguṇarahitaguṇajanyatvasādhyābhāvasya
duḥkhābhāvaniścayāt|
asmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitādharmaguṇajanyatvasambhavāt|
sandigdhasādhye duḥkhādau gatatvādutpattiḥ
sandigdhānaikāntikīti cet---na, sandigdhasādhyaniṣṭhatvasya
dūṣaṇatve sarvānumānavilayāt, sarvahetūnāṃ
sandigdhasādhyapakṣaniṣṭhatvāt| anyathā
pakṣadharmatvasiddherdurvāratvāt|
pakṣatvarahitasandigdhasādhyaniṣṭhatvaṃ dūṣaṇaṃiti cet---na,
pakṣatvaṃ kiṃ sandigdhasādhyatvaṃ dharmitvenopāttaṃ vā|
prathame duḥkhāderapi pakṣatvāt|
pakṣatvarahitasandigdhasādhyaniṣṭhatvenotpatterduṣṭatvānupapatteḥ|
dvitīye dhūmāderapi duṣṭatvam,
dharmitvopāttaparvatavyatiriktasandigdhasādhyaparvatāntaraniṣṭhatvena
sandigdhānaikāntikatvāt|

atha dharmitvopāttavyatiriktaḥ sandigdhasādhyaḥ pakṣatulya
ucyate, pakṣavatsandigdhasādhyatvāt| sandigdhasādhyaniṣṭhatvaṃ
ca pakṣatvarahitaviśeṣitamaviśeṣitaṃ vā na,
dūṣaṇamanumānamātravilayādityucyate| tattarhi prakṛte .api
samānamiti santoṣṭavyam|

na cātra gurutvena siddhasādhanam, karmādergurutvajanyatve
mānābhāvāt| mokṣādirgurutvajanyaḥ, kāryatvāt, patanavaditi
cet---na, adravyatvena sopādhitvāt. nityadravyāṇāṃ
vipakṣāṇāṃ vyāvartyatvātpakṣāntaratvānupapatteḥ|

atha mā bhūdgurutvena siddhasādhanam, arthāntaraṃ tu
bhaviṣyatīti cet---na,
kimidamanumānamasmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitaguṇasya
gurutvajātiṃ gṛhṇātītyarthāntaratā, gurutvajātivirahaṃ na
gṛhṇātīti vā| nādyaḥ,
gurutvajāterguṇaviśeṣaṇatvenānupāttatvāt| anupāttāpi
gurutvajātirvyāptibalena sidhyatīti cet---na,
asmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitagurutvaguṇajanyatve
dravyatvasyevopādhervidyamānatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
gurutvajātiviraha
[gu]rutvajātivirahastadgrahaṇātkathamasmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitamokṣajanakaguṇasya
gurutvaviraha iti cet---śṛṇu,
ayaṃ gurutvajātirahitaḥ,
mokṣajanakatvāt, tattvasākṣātkāravadityādeḥ| na
cātrāsmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitaguṇatvābhāva
upādhiḥ,
asmatpratyakṣaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitaviśeṣaṇasya
vipakṣarūpavyāvartyābhāvādvipakṣabhūtasya gurutvasya
guṇatvāntarbhāvenaiva vyāvṛttatvāt|
vyāvartyavipakṣarahitaviśeṣaṇavataśca pakṣetaratvāt, tathāpi
parvato vahnimānityatrāparvatamavahnimattvasya
pakṣetaratvam| aparvataviśeṣaṇasya vyāvartyavipakṣavirahāt,
vahnirahitānāṃ ca vahnirahitatvena vyāvṛttatvāditi|

athāyaṃ gurutvajātimān,
asmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitaguṇatvāt,
gurutvāditi
satpratipakṣatvam| na cātra mokṣajanakatvena sopādhitvam,
gurutvajātirahitajanakānāmajanakatvenaiva vyāvṛttatvāt,
ajanakasya cāntyadhvaṃsādermokṣaviśeṣaṇenāpyavyāvṛtteḥ|
ajanake mokṣajanakasya
vidyamānatvādvipakṣarūpavyāvartyarahitaviśeṣaṇavataśca
pakṣetaratvāditi manyase, tanna,
etatpatanametajjanakagurutvajātimattvarahitāsmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitaguṇajanyam,
kāryatvāt, anyapatanavaditi siddhe gurutvajātirahite
.asmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitaguṇatvādhāre
vyabhicārāt|

na cānenaiva dharmasiddheḥ prathamānumānavaiyarthyam, asya
dharmādharmasādhāraṇatvena dharmasādhakatvāt|
prathamānumānasya ca svargāpavargajanakadharmāsādhakatvāt| atha
mā bhūdgurutvenārthāntaratā, adharmeṇa tu bhaviṣyatīti
cet---kiṃ
muktirasmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitaguṇajanyetyanumānaṃ
muktijanakaguṇasyādharmatvaṃ gṛhṇātītyarthāntaratā, uta
dharmatvena gṛhṇātīti| nādyaḥ, adharmatvasya vyāpakāṃśe
.anutpādanāt, sādhanavato
gurutvādervyāvṛttitvenāvyāpakatvācca| na dvitīyaḥ,
dharmatvāgrahaṇe .api yatra dharmatvaṃ tadgrahaṇāt|

kathamatra dharmatvamiti cet---śṛṇu, ayaṃ dharmatvādhāro
duḥkhājanakagurutvarahitatāsmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitaṃ
guṇatvāt, vakṣyamāṇasukhajanakavatsukhajanakatvamupādhiḥ,
mokṣahetoryogajadharmasyāṣṭamahāsiddhihetutvena
sukhajanakatvāṅgīkārāt|

na ca dharmatvāsiddherasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā,
asmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitasukhajanako
guṇaḥ dharmalakṣaṇavataḥ
vyāvṛttatvānadhikaraṇadharmalakṣaṇavadvṛttidharmalakṣaṇarahitavṛttitvānadhikaraṇajātimān,
jātimattvāt, govaditi dharmatvasiddheḥ| dharmalakṣaṇaṃ ca
duḥkhājanakagurutvarahitāsmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitaguṇatvam|
asmadyogidaśāyāṃ gurutvadharmatvāderasmatpratyakṣatvena
tanniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajāterapyasmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhatvādguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvādgurutvadharmāderapyasmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvena
tadabhihitaguṇatvānupapatteḥ aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ bādhaśca
prathamānumāna iti cet---na,
yogājanyajanyasākṣātkāragocaratvamasmatpratyakṣatvam, tacca
gurutvadharmādau nāstīti nāpi bādha iti|

nanu yogajaśabdena yogajo dharmo vivakṣitaḥ, tadanyatvaṃ vā|
prathame .aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam, dharmāsiddhau yogajadharmasya
sutarāmaprasiddheḥ| dvitīye tu aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvabādhau
tadavasthau gurutvadharmādigocarayogisākṣātkārasyāpi
yogajayogadhvaṃsādyajanyatvena
gurutvadharmāderapyasmatpratyakṣatvāditi cet---na,
anupāttadharmatadanyatvaviśeṣaṇe
yogajanyasya vivakṣitatvāt| viśeṣaṇavivakṣāyāṃ
sarvānumānavilayāditi| etaccharīrajanyasākṣātkāragocaratvaṃ
vāsmatpratyakṣatvam| na caitaccharīrajanyasākṣātkāragocaratvaṃ
dharmāderapi sambhāvyamiti vācyam|

yena rūpeṇa śarīrādisākṣātkārajanakena
dharmādigocarasākṣātkāro janyate, tasyaiva tacchabdena
vivakṣitatvāditi| yadi vā devadatto
devadattagatāsmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitasukhajanakaguṇavān,
ātmatvāt, yajñadattavaditi dharmasiddhiḥ| na
cātrāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam,
yajñadattādāvasmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitaguṇatvāprasiddhāvapi
yajñadattāgatatve
satyasmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitasukhajanakaguṇavattvasya
prasiddhatvāt|

na caitadātmamanaḥsaṃyogānadhikaraṇatvamatropādhiḥ,
ātmamanp.api[na iti] viśeṣaṇasya
vyāvartyavipakṣābhāvāddravyāṇāṃ vipakṣāṇāṃ
saṃyogādhikaraṇatvenaiva vyāvṛtteḥ| adravyāṇāṃ
cātmamanaḥsaṃyogānadhikaraṇatvenāpyavyāvṛtteḥ|
nāpyetadātmamanaḥsaṃyogānadhikaraṇatvenā[satpratipakṣatā]smatpratyakṣatā,
anātmatvena sopādhitvāt, na cārthāntaratāśaṅkā
adharmaguṇatvavyatiriktarūpādiguṇānāmasmatpratyakṣarūpādiniṣṭhagurutvāvāntararūpādijātimattvāt|
bhāvanāyā
apyasmatpratyakṣaveganiṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarasaṃskāratvajātimattvāditi,
adharmagurutvayogyasukhājanakatvāt, gurutvasyātmani bādhācca|

na ca rūpādivyatiriktaḥ pañcaviṃśatitamo guṇaḥ setsyatīti| na
tu dharma evetyarthāntaratā,
asmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitasukhajanakaguṇatvasyaiva
dharmalakṣaṇatvāt| caturviṃśatitamadharmasiddhau
pañcaviṃśati[ta]menārthāntarasyodbhāvayitumaśakyatvācceti|
yadvā devadatto
devadattabuddhīcchāprayatnatvarahitasukhajanakaguṇavān,
ātmatvāt, yajñadattavaditi dharmasiddhiḥ|
arthāntaratvādinirāsaḥ pūrvavat| ātmatvasya ca dharme
pramāṇatvaṃ yato .abhyudayasiddhirityanena sukhajanakaṃ sādhyaṃ
mūrchayatāsūcitam|
buddhyādivyāvarthakamasmatpratyakṣaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitatvādi
viśeṣaṇaṃ ca|

yato .abhyudayasiddhiḥ sa dharma iti vadatā yato
.anabhyudayasiddhiḥ so .adharma iti sūcitam, tena prathamānumānena
duḥkhapakṣīkaraṇena dvitīyatṛtīyayośca sukhajanakapadasthāne
duḥkhajanakapadaprakṣepeṇādharmasiddhirdraṣṭavyā|
sarvārthānāṃ sākṣādabhidhāne sūtratvavyākopāt, yadāhuḥ ---

alpākṣaram asaṃdigdhaṃ sāravadviśvatomukham|

astobham anavadyaṃ ca sūtraṃ sūtravido viduḥ||

iti|| 2||

svargāpavargasādhakamānābhāvādāśrayāsiddhiḥ prathamānumāna
iti śaṅkāparihārārthaṃ sūtram---

tadvacanādāmnāyaprāmāṇyam|| 1-1-3||

iti|

taditi prakṛtau svargāpavargau parāmṛśati| tena tayoḥ
svargāpavargayoḥ pratipādanātpramitijananāt, āmnāyasya
śrutismṛtītihāsapurāṇādeḥ prāmāṇyaṃ pramitikaraṇatvam|
āmnāyate dharmakāraṇatvena paṭhyata ityāmnāyaḥ| tathā hi
yadyavadhṛtaprāmāṇyo .apyāmnāyaḥ svargādau na pramāṇam,
tarhi pratipādyāntarābhāvādapramāṇameva syāt, na ca dharme
vedaḥ pramāṇamityuktam| [na?] sa ca svargādāvapramāṇasya
tatsādhane sādhanasādhane vā prāmāṇyaṃ sambhavati| bhavatu vā
yāgādipadānāṃ yāgādau prāmāṇyam, anuṣṭhāpakatvaṃ
durghaṭameva, prayojanamantareṇa prekṣāvatāmapravṛtteḥ|
tenānuṣṭhānāṅgaṃ
pramitijanakatvavirahādananuṣṭhāpakatvalakṣaṇamaprāmāṇyaṃ
vedasya durvāramiti|

atra ca tacchabdena narakasya parāmarśatvātsvargavannarake .api
vedaḥ pramāṇamiti draṣṭavyam|

tadevaṃ prathamasūtreṇa śāstrapratipādyau dharmādharmau
darśitau| dvitīyasūtreṇa tu svargāpavarganarakapakṣīkaraṇe
dharmādharmayoḥ pramāṇaṃ darśitam| tṛtīyasūtreṇa tu
svargāpavarganarakeṣvāmnāyaḥ pramāṇamabhihitamiti trisūtrī
vyākhyātā|

yadvā prathamasūtreṇa śāstrābhidheyaṃ dharmamabhidhāya yato
.abhyudayaniḥśreyasasiddhiḥ sa dharma iti dvitīyasūtre
prayojanaṃ pratipādyate| na hyanavagate śāstre pratipādyasya
dharmasya svargāpavargasādhanatve śrotṝṇāṃ pravṛttiḥ
sambhavati, iṣṭasādhanabodhapūrvakatvāddharmasya
svargāpavargasādhanatvābhāve
śāstraśravaṇajanyadharmabodhasyeṣṭatvānupapatteḥ
svargāpavargajanyadharmasādhanānuṣṭhānānaṅgatvāt| tasmāt
śrotṝṇāṃ śāstreṇa pravṛttimutpādayatāṃ dharmasya
svargāpavargasādhanatvaṃ pratipādanīyamiti|

nanu niṣpramāṇakaḥ svargāpavargayoḥ kathaṃ
prayojanatvamityāśaṅkya tadvacanādāmnāyaprāmāṇyamiti
tṛtīyasūtreṇa prayojanabhūtayoḥ svargāpavargayoḥ vedaḥ
pramāṇamiti pratipādyate|

nanu śāstratātparyagocaradharmapratipādanaparatve sūtraṃ
vyartham, śāstrādeva tadavagamāt| anavagate tasya
śāstragocaratvānupapattau

prathamasūtrasya śāstratātparyagocaradharmapratipādakatvasya
vyākopāditaḥ
śrotṛpravṛttijanakaśrotṛgataśāstratātparyaviṣayadharmasvargāpavargasādhanatve
bodhajanakatayā dharmabodhasya śrotrapravṛtteḥ
pūrvamevāpekṣitatvāt| na hyanavagate dharme tasya
svargāpavargasādhanatvaṃ śakyamadhigantum,
viśeṣyajñānajanyatvādviśiṣṭajñānasya, tena śrotṝṇāṃ
śāstraśravaṇāṅgadharmasvargāpavargasādhanatvabodhāya
śāstrādyutpādyadharmabodhaḥ kartavya iti saphalaṃ sūtram|

na hya[na?]bhidheyapravṛttimantareṇa śrotṝṇāṃ na śāstre
pravṛttiḥ| na hya[na?]bhidheyādipratipādakasūtre
vi[pi?]nābhidheyādipratipattimantareṇa śrotṝṇāṃ pravṛttiriti
tatrāpi abhidheyādipratipādakaṃ sūtrāntaramārambhaṇīyam|
tatrāpyevaṃ tatrāpyevamityanavastheti cet---na,

śāstrābhidheyadharmajijñāsāyāmavataratā prathamasūtre .atra
śāstrapratipādyavatsvapratipādyasyāpi pratipāditatvāt|
śrotṛtvāddharmasyaivobhayapratipādyatvāt| pratipanne ca
śāstrapratipādyapratipāditasūtrārthe
tatpratipādyajijñāsānudaye
tajjñāpakasūtrāntarārambhaṇānavastheti|

evaṃ śāstraprayojanapratipādakenaiva svaprayojanatvasyāpi
pratipāditatvāt na tatpratipādanārthaṃ sūtrāntarārambhaṃ[ḥ?]
śāstraprayojanasyaiva
śāstraprayojanapratipādya[da]kaprayojanatvāditi|

yadvā,
dravyādipadārthapratipādakaśāstraprayojanadharmapratipādakamathāto
dharmaṃ vyākhyāsyāma iti sūtraṃ na
śāstrābhidheyābhidhāyakam, vākyānāmanabhidhāyakatvāt,
abhidhāyāḥ padadharmatvāt| nāpi
śāstrapratipādyapratipādakaśāstrapratipādyadravyānyapratipādakatvāt|
dharmasya ca trisūtrīvyatiriktaśāstrapratipādyatvāt
guṇavibhāgasūtreṇa dharmādharmayoḥ pratipādyatvāt| vākārasya
ca sūtrāntarapratipāditadharmādharmasamuccayaparatve
mānāntarābhāvāt|

agnerūrdhvajvalanaṃ vāyostiryakpavanamaṇumanasoścādyaṃ
karmetyadṛṣṭakāritāni [5-2-14]
ityādeśca dṛṣṭānuvādena kāraṇatvapratipādakatvāt|
icchādveṣapūrvikā dharmādharmayoḥ pravṛttiḥ [6-2-17]
ityādeśca dharmādharmānuvādena
rāgadveṣajanyatvapratipādakatvāt|
kārakatvakāryatvaviśiṣṭadharmādharmasvarūpapratipādakāni
bhavantīti cet---na, prakaraṇādiparyālocanayā kāraṇatvādereva
jijñāsitatvāt| agnerūrdhvajvalanādeḥ kāraṇatvaṃ jijñāsitam,
na tu kāraṇatvamātramiti cet---na, dharmādharmayoḥ
prathamasūtrādeva jñātatvāt| tasya pratipādyāntarābhāve
dharmādharmasvarūpagocaratvāt|

atrāpi śāstrapratipādyatvaṃ dharmādisvarūpātiriktaṃ
pratipādyamasti| pratipādyatve vā viśeṣaṇībhūtasūtrasya
śāstrapratipādyatvāditi cet---na, śāstrapratipādyatvasya
śāstrāpratipādyatāt| pratipādyatve vā
viśeṣaṇībhūtaśāstrasyāpi śāstrapratipādyatvāpatteḥ|
gurutvadravatvapratipādakaṃ tu sūtrāntaramastīti
gurutvātpatanamastīti| nātra dravatvaṃ syandanakāraṇatvam [dra@
5-2-5] ityādisūtrāṇāmeva patanasyandanakāraṇatvam|
viśiṣṭagurutvadravatvādiparatvamaṅgīkṛtam|

na ca patanādikāraṇatvapratipādakasūtreṇa
gurutvādisiddhivadūrdhvajvalanādikāraṇatvādipratipādakasūtraireva
dharmādisiddhirastu, kiṃ prathamsūtreṇeti vācyam,
sūtrāntarārthasyāpi
vyākhyātṛvacanādavagatisambhavātsūtrāntarānārambhāpatteḥ|
dravyādirpatipādakaṃ śāstratātparyavṛttyā dharmaṃ
pratipādayatīti cet---na, tasya dravyatvādāveva
tātparyātsvarūpeṇa dravyādīnāmapuruṣārthatvāt|
dharmasādhanatayā dravyatvādayaḥ pratipādanīyā iti
dravyādidharmasādhanatve śāstrasya tātparyaṃ vaktavyam|

tathā ca nāgṛhītaviśeṣaṇanyāyena sādhanaviśeṣaṇadharme
.api śāstratātparyasiddhiriti cet---na, prathamasūtre satyasati ca
śāstrasya dharmagocaratātparyāvadhāraṇānupapatteḥ|

sati prathamasūtre tata eva dharmasiddheḥ śāstrasya tatra
tātparyābhāvāt| asati ca prathamasūtre dharmāsiddhau śāstrasya
tadgocaratātparyāvadhāraṇasya durghaṭatvāt|

atha ca kiṃ samastaśāstrapratipādakamidaṃ sūtram, kiṃ vā
śāstraikadeśārthapratipādakam| nādyaḥ,
śāstravaiyarthyāpatteḥ| na dvitīyaḥ,
śeṣaśāstrārthāpratipādanavadasyāpratipādane .api
śrotṛpravṛttighaṭanāt|

asya vistarārthaṃ śāstramiti cet---na, pratipādyānatireke
vistaravaiyarthyāt| etatsūtravyākhyānārthaṃ śāstramiti
cet---na, kiṃ vyākhyānamantareṇāsya sūtrasyārtho [na?]
jñāyate na vā| prathame vyākhyānasya vaiyarthyam, dvitīye
sūtravaiyarthyam, śāstrādau pratipādyabodhajanakatvāt|
śāstrasya nirapekṣasya bodhajanakatvāt|
tasmātsātiśayānekavidhapravartakanivartakadharmasyāśrayopakaraṇakāryakāraṇopabhogavināśakaraṇasahitasya
śāstrapratipādyasya
pratipādakamityātreyācāryamatamupekṣaṇīyamiti
prayojanapratipādanaparamevedaṃ sūtramiti|

śāstrapratipādyānavagame tasyedaṃ prayojanamiti
śiṣyairavagantumaśakyamiti cet---na, etacchāstrajanyasya
jñānasya dharmaḥ prayojanamityavagantuṃ śakyatvāt,
athaitacchāstrapratipādyānavagame tadviṣayametacchāstrajanyaṃ
jñānamavagantumśakyamiti cet---na, etacchāstrajanyaṃ jñānamiti
śabdena viṣayānavacchinasyāpi bidhāt anyathā jñānaśabdenāpi
jñānānavagamāpatteḥ| astu vā sāmānyataḥ kiṃcidasya
śāstrapratipādyāvagamaḥ na caivaṃ sati tadarthamevedaṃ
sūtramastīti vācyam, vākyatvādeva kiṃcitpratipādyāvagamāt|
sūtreṇa ca pratipādyadharmaviśeṣapratipādanāditi|

nanu na dharmaḥ sukhaṃ nāpi suḥkhanivṛttiḥ| kathaṃ
prayojanamityāśaṅkāparihārārthaṃ sūtram---yato
.abhyudayaniḥśreyasasiddhiḥ sa dharmaḥ| [1-1-2] iti|
sūkhādivattatsādhanamapi puruṣasyeṣṭamiti bhavati dharmaḥ
prayojanamannodakādivaditi bhāvaḥ| tadevaṃ sūtradvayena
prayojanaṃ samarthitam| kiṃ punaradharme
pramāṇamityāśaṅkāyāmanumānapratipādakaṃ dvitīyaṃ sūtram|
āgamapratipādakaṃ tṛtīyaṃ
sūtram---tadvacanādāmnāyaprāmāṇyam [1-1-3] iti| taditi
prakṛtaṃ svargāpavargasādhanaṃ dharmaṃ parāmṛśati| tena
svargāpavargasādhanadharmavacanādāmnāyasya prāmāṇyamiti|
dharmapramāṇatvenāmnāyate paṭhyata ityāmnāyaḥ
caturdaśavidyāsthānamiti| ācāraśca| yadāha yājñavalkyaḥ---

purāṇanyāyamīmāṃsādharmaśāstrāṅgamiśritāḥ|

vedāḥ sthānāni vidyānāṃ dharmasya ca caturdaśa||

iti|

ācāraścaiva sādhūnām|
iti ca|

kathaṃ dharmabodhakatvaṃ vedasyeti
cet---liṅādīnāmiṣṭasādhanabodhakatvāditi brūmaḥ| tathā
hyodanakāmaḥ
pacedityādyuttamavṛddhaprayuktavākyaśravaṇānantaraṃ
madhyamavṛddhaṃ pravartamānamavalokya
bālastāvadevamākalayati---ayaṃ kāyaparispando
janyeṣṭasādhanabodhakajanyecchājanyayatnajanyaḥ, pravṛttitvāt,
matpravṛttivaditi| tadanantaramayamiṣṭasādhanatābodho
nendriyajanyaḥ[janyajanyaḥ?]
svādhyāpakāderindriyāsannikṛṣṭatvāt, atītavartamānayoḥ
prāgabhāvarūpakāraṇarahitayorasādhyatvāt| nāpyayaṃ pāka
iṣṭasādhanaṃ pākatvādityanumānajanyaḥ, dharmasiddheḥ
sādhyasya pākasya pratyakṣānumānābhyāmanavagamāt| pacatinā ca
kevalena sādhyapākāpratīteḥ|yatkiṃcitpākasya
ceṣṭasādhanatvānumāne .api madhyamavṛddhasya
sādhyapākagocarapravṛttyanupapatteḥ pratyayena kevalena
bhāvanāmātramabhidadhatā sādhyapākānavagamāt|
prakṛtipratyayābhyāṃ ca kṛtijanyeṣṭasādhanapākānavagame
.api pākasyeṣṭasādhanatvānumānaṃ vyarthaṃ prāgeva
śabdādiṣṭasādhanatvāvagamāt| etenāyaṃ pāka iṣṭasādhanaṃ
praiṣagocaratvādityapi nirastam, pakṣadharmatvāsiddheḥ|
madhyamavṛddhenendriyādīnāṃ pākagocarāptābhiprāyānavagamāt|

liṅādiśabdādevāvagamyata iti cet---na, madhyamavṛddhena
liṅādīnāṃ tatra saṃgatyagrahāt, tasya vyutpattidaśāyāṃ
bādhakatvāt| bālena ca svātmadṛṣṭāntena ca
pravartamānamadhyamavṛddhasya
pravṛttijanakaprayatnajanakeṣṭasādhanatābodhānumāne .api
pravṛttiviṣayagocarābhiprāyānumānāt, svātmani
tathānavagamāt| na hi yathā balā[?]
svagatastanyanapānādipravṛttijanyeṣṭasādhanajanyecchājanyaprayatnapūrvakatvamanvayavyatirekābhyāmavagacchanti,
tathā pravṛttiviṣayagocareṣṭābhiprāyajñānapūrvakatvamapi| na
cānavagate praiṣādau liṅādivācyatvajñānaṃ sambhavati,
vācyānavagamasya tadvācakatvakāraṇatvopagamāditi|

athopadeśe yatra prayojyapuruṣagataṃ karmaphalaṃ tatra bhavatu
nāmeṣṭasādhanavācakatvam, liṅādeḥ
prayojyapuruṣavyāvṛtteriṣṭasādhanabodhapūrvakatvāt| yatra
punarna prayojyapuruṣagataṃ karmaphalaṃ kiṃ tu
prayoktṛpuruṣagatam, yathā sūpakāraṃ prati paceti mahāmātraṃ
prati gajamānayetyādau rājavākye sūpakārakṛtapākādeḥ
rājāpekṣitaudanādisādhanatvāttatra neṣṭasādhanatve
liṅādīnāṃ vyutpattiḥ, pravartamānasya sūpakārādeḥ
pākagocareṣṭasādhanabodhādananumānāt, pākasya
sūpakāratṛptisādhanavācakatvāt| kvacicca
puruṣābhiprāyavācakatvamiti vācaspatiprabhṛtayaḥ| tadapi na,
rājatṛptisādhanasyaudanasya rājñastu sūpakārasyāpi
vedanadātṛsvāmitṛptisādhanatayā iṣṭasādhanatvena pākasāpi
sūpakārasyeṣṭasādhanatvāt|

atha kathaṃ svāmitṛptisādhanaudanasya
sūpakāreṣṭasādhanatvamiti cet---svāmitṛpteḥ
sūpakāreṣṭatvādityavaihi| nanu na svāmitṛptiḥ
sūpakārasukhaṃ svāmisamavetatvāt| nāpi sūpakārasukhasādhanam,
anyagatasukhāderanyagatasukhādyasādhanatvāt| tatkathaṃ
sūpakārasyeṣṭeti| ucyate---svāmitṛpteḥ sukhasvarūpatayā
svabhāvata eva sūpakāreṣṭatvātsvagatasukhavat| svagatameva
sukhamiṣṭamiti cet---na, putrādisukhasyāpīṣṭatvāt| anyathā

putrajanmanimittāyāmiṣṭau pitrānuṣṭhīyamānāyāṃ
putragatapūtatvādeḥ phalatvaprasaṅgāt| svarūpeṇa
sukhasyeṣṭatve śatrorudāsīnasya ca sukhamiṣṭaṃ syāditi
cet---na, pratītiviṣayasukhasyeṣṭatvāt|
yathā hi sukhādabhīṣṭasādhanatvādātmādayaḥ putrādayaśca tathā vedanadātā svāmyapīti siddhaṃ svāmitṛptisādhanaudanasādhanapākasyeṣṭasādhanatvam|
anyathā sūpakārasya pākagocareṣṭasādhanavirahe tajjanyecchānutpattau tajjanyaprayatnānudayāt|

anicchannapi rājājñayā pravartata iti cet---na,
cetanapravṛttericchājanyatvāt| na
cāsyāśceṣṭasādhanatābodhajanyatvātkvacitpravṛttiriṣṭasādhanatābodhajanyecchājanyā,
kācitpraiṣādijanyeti cet---na,
prayoktṛpraiṣādiricchāviśeṣasya liṅā vyuccāraṇasya
prayatnādīnprāgeva vinaṣṭasya
pravṛttyavyavahitapūrvakṣaṇarahitapravṛttijanakānupapatteḥ|
prayoktṛpraiṣādijñānaṃ liṅādijanyaṃ pravṛttiheturna
svarūpeṇa praiṣādiriti cet---na, ananuvidheyapraiṣādyavagame
.apyapravṛtteḥ|

anuvidheyapraiṣādijñānaṃ pravṛttiheturiti cet---na, kiṃ
kuryāditi viṣayaviśeṣānavacchinnapraiṣādyavagame
.apyapravṛtteḥ|
viṣayaviśeṣānavacchinnānuvidheyapraiṣādijñānaṃ
pravṛttiheturiti cet---kiṃ
viṣayaviśeṣānavacchinnapraiṣādijñānaṃ svarūpeṇa
pravṛttihetuḥ, kiṃ veṣṭasādhanatvānuvidheyapraiṣasya
gocaratvaviṣayatayeṣṭasādhanatvānumitīcchājananadvārā|
nādyaḥ, mānābhāvāt, iyaṃ pravṛttiretatpraiṣajñānajanyā
tadanantarotpannatvāt, saṃskāravaditi cet---na,
vyavahitānantarotpanne vyabhicārāt|
avyavahitatadanantarotpannatvaṃ heturiti cet---na,
avyavahitatadanantarotpanne ghaṭādibhirvyabhicārāt| te .api
pakṣatulyā iti cet---na, apasiddhāntāt,
avyavahitaviśeṣaṇāsiddheśca|
praiṣādijñānādiṣṭasādhanatvajñānam,
iṣṭasādhanatvajñānādicchā, tataḥ pravṛttiriti svīkārāt|
tatra pravṛttitvena sopādhitvācca| na cātra
pakṣetaratvāśaṅkā|
praiṣādijñānājanyastanapānādipravṛtteḥ
vipakṣasya vyāvartanāt| iyaṃ pravṛttirna praiṣādijñānajanyā,
pravṛttitvāt, stanapānādipravṛttivaditi
pratipakṣatvācca|

na dvitīyaḥ,
anuvidheyapraiṣagocaratvajñānajanyeṣṭasādhanatvānumitijanyecchādita
eva pravṛtterutpannatvena praiṣādijñānasyākāraṇatvasthiteḥ
iṣṭasādhanatvajñānena praiṣādijñānasyecchotpattikāle
nāśātpravṛttyavyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavirahācca
prācīnapakṣadoṣācca|

astu vā praiṣādijñānasya pravṛttiviśeṣaṇahetutvaṃ
kāryasyeṣṭasādhanajñānajanyecchājanyatvaṃ durvāram| iyaṃ
pravṛttirjanyeṣṭasādhanajñānajanyecchājanyā pravṛttitvāt,
sampratipannavadityādeḥ sattvāt| na ca
svamandiradāhagocarapiśācādipravṛttau vyabhicāraḥ, tasyā api
piśācaniṣṭhadāhādiṣṭasādhanatvajñānecchāpūrvakatvāt|
nijagṛhadāhādinā piśācādyabhilaṣitasukhasyotpatteḥ|

na ca jalapavanādijanyāyāṃ pravṛttau vyabhicāraḥ,
jalādinudyamānapuruṣeṣu prayatnarūpapravṛtterabhāvāt|
taddeheṣu ca ceṣṭātvāparaparyāyapravṛttivirahāt| nāpi bhuvi
balātpāṣāṇapravṛttitaḥ puruṣapravṛttau vyabhicāraḥ, tasyā
apyaniṣṭasādhanapāṣāṇādivahanaparihārarūpapāṣāṇavahaneṣṭasādhanabodhapūrvake

icchājanyatvāt| pāṣāṇavahanasya kathamaniṣṭasādhanatvamiti
cet| ājñāviṣayapāṣāṇavahanābhāvasya duḥkhahetuka
bhighātahetutvāditi|

nāpi viṣabhakṣaṇodakhādanādipravṛttau vyabhicāraḥ, tasyā
apyavamānanādiduḥkhasādhanadehavināśeṣṭasādhanabodhajanyecchāpūrvakatvāt|
nāpi bhagavato mahādevasya svargādipravṛttau vyabhicāraḥ,
īśvaraprayatnakāryatvapakṣe tasyāpi pakṣatulyatvāt|
ajanyatvapakṣe kāryatvena
hetuviśeṣaṇājjīvanayonivyatiriktajanyaprayatnatvasya vā
pravṛttiśabdārthatvāditi| tasmātsarvatra
kāryeṣṭasādhanabodhasya pravṛttihetutvamiti siddhaṃ
liṅādīnāmiṣṭasādhanatvavācakatvamiti|

liṅādīnāmiṣṭasādhanatvāvācakatve
vidhinimantraṇāmantraṇādhīṣṭasampraśnaprārthaneṣu liṅ iti
pāṇinismṛtivirodha iti cet---na, vidhiśabdeneṣṭasādhanatvasya
preraṇāparaparyāyasyābhidhānāt| nimantraṇaśabdena ca
yatpratyākhyāne pratyavāyaḥ
tathāvidheṣṭasādhanatvapratipādanāt, āmantraṇaśabdena
yatpratyākhyāne na pratyavāyaḥ
tathāvidheṣṭasādhanatvavacanāt| adhīṣṭapadena ca
satkārapūrvakeṣṭasādhanatvābhidhānāt,
pāṇinismṛtervyutpāditanyāyatvenopapattau
mūlāntarakalpanānupapatteḥ|

santu vā praiṣādaya eva vidhyādiśabdavācyāḥ| tathāpi na
pāṇinismṛtivirodhaḥ| tena
praiṣāderliṅādivācyatvamabhidadhatāpīṣṭasādhanatvasya
vācyatvāniṣedhāt|
nyāyasiddheṣṭasādhanatvavācakatvasmṛtisiddhapraiṣādivācakatvayoravirodhāt,
pāṇininā ca praiṣādestajjñānasya pravartakatvānabhidhānānna
tanniṣedhe .api smṛtivirodhaḥ| etenāyaṃ vācaka iṣṭasādhanaṃ
preraṇāgocaratvādityapi nirastam, preraṇāśabdena
praiṣādyabhidhānavat śabdabhāvanābhidhāne .api
āśrayāsiddhivatpakṣadharmatvāsiddherapi vajralepāyamānatvāt|

madhyamavṛddhena pākagocaratvapravṛttyanukūlaśabdavyāpārānavagamāt|
tacca vyutpattivirahādyupapattidaśāyāṃ madhyamavṛddhasyāpi bālatvāt, bālena ca svabhāvadṛṣṭāntena madhyamavṛddhasya prayatnajanakecchājanakakāryeṣṭasādhanabodhānumāne .api pravṛttiviṣayagocaraśabdavyāpārabhidhādanumānāt|

svātmani tarhyapravṛttihetutvānavagamāt śabdāśravaṇe .api bālasya stanapānādau
pravṛtteḥ svarūpāsiddheśca pravṛttyanukūlaśabdavyāpāre mānābhāvāt|
ayaṃ liṅādipratyayaḥ pravṛttyanukūlavyāpāravānvyāpārātiriktapravṛttijanakatvāt, ātmavadityāderiṣṭasādhanabodhenārthāntaratvāt|
viśeṣyāsiddheśca śabdasya pravṛttijanakatve mānābhāvāt|
śabdaḥ pravṛttijanakaḥ, tadavyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavṛttitvāditi hetusiddhiriti cet---na, vyomādibhirvyabhicārāt|
avyavahitaviśeṣaṇāsiddheśca| śabdādiṣṭasādhanatvajñānam,
tata icchā, tataḥ pravṛttiriti svīkārāt| iyaṃ
pravṛttirliṅādijanyā, tadavyavahitottarakṣaṇotpannatvāt,
tajjñānajanyavaditi hetusiddhiriti cet---na,
tadavyavahitottarakṣaṇotpattau
ghaṭādibhirvyabhicārāt, ātmavadasiddheśca|
vyāpāretaratvaviśeṣaṇāsiddheśca liṅādeḥ
prayoktṛpuruṣavyāpāratvasambhavāt| śabdādeva
pravṛttyupapatteḥ na prayoktā pravṛttiheturiti na śabdasya
tadvyāpāratvamiti cet---na, śabdajñānajanyādeva
pravṛttyupapatteḥ śabdasyāpi pravṛttyajanakatve
vyāpārakalpanānupapatteḥ| etena
liṅādirvyāpāravānvyāpārātiriktakāraṇatvādātmavadityapi
nirastam|

atha laukikavākyeṣu tāvatpreraṇādijñānaṃ pravṛttiheturna
praiṣādiviśeṣaṇajñānaṃ vyabhicārāt| tathā ca svargakāmo
yajetetyādivākyaśravaṇānantaraṃ bhāvinī pravṛttiḥ
preraṇādijñānapūrvikā, pravṛttitvāt,
praiṣajñānajanyapravṛttivadityanumīyate| na
ceṣṭasādhanatvarūpapreraṇājñānena siddhasādhyatā, tasya
pravṛttiviṣayagocaratvābhāvāt| na hīṣṭasādhanatvaṃ
praiṣādivadviṣayanirūpyam| śabdavyāpārastu
viṣayanirūpyāvakalpita iti|

na cātra praiṣādiḥ sambhavati, tasya puruṣābhiprāyaviśeṣasya
apauruṣeye vede asambhavāt|

nanu bhavatu nāma
sākṣātkṛtasvargāpavargasādhanadharmādipadārthasārtho
dharmopadeṣṭā bhagavānīśvaraḥ| tataḥ kimityāśaṅkāyāṃ
tṛtīyaṃ sūtram---

tadvacanādāmnāyaprāmāṇyam|| 1-1-3||

iti| taditi sarvanāmnā prathamasūtragatadharmapadopātto
dharmopadeṣṭā sarvajñaḥ parāmṛśyate| vacanāditi ca
bhāvapradhāno nirdeśaḥ vacanatvādityarthaḥ| āmnāya iti vedo
vivakṣitaḥ| prāmāṇyamityatra

sādhanīyamiti viśeṣaḥ|
prayogastu---vedavākyaṃ pramāṇam, īśvaravākyatvāt,
sampratipannatadvākyavat| na ca dṛṣṭāntābhāvaḥ,
īśvarasādhakānumāneṣu
pramāṇavākyapraṇetṛviśeṣaṇopādānena dṛṣṭāntassiddheḥ|
atheśvarasādhakānumāneṣu
apramāṇavākyapraṇetṛviśeṣaṇopādānādīśvarapraṇītāpramāṇavākyasiddheḥ,
īśvaravākyatvasya anaikāntikatvamiti cet---na,
bhramarahitatvāpramāṇavākyapraṇetṛtvayoḥ
vyāghātenāpramāṇavākyapraṇetṛtvāsiddheḥ|
apramāṇavākyapraṇetṛbhrāntatvayoḥ
parasparāvinābhūtatvādabhrāntatvāpramāṇavākyapraṇetṛtvasiddhirapi
mā bhūditi cet---satyam, na bhavatyeva|
apramāṇavākyapraṇetṛtvaviśeṣaṇavat[tā]
bhrāntatvasiddhirapi[riti] tadvidhureṇa
punarabhrāntatvasiddhirbhavantī kena vāryate| atha
bhramasamānādhikaraṇadharmaviṣayatvarahitayośca
parasparābhāvāvinābhūtatvācca| tatrābhrāntatvaṃ na
tatrāpramāṇavākyapraṇetṛtvam, yathā ghaṭādau
bhramavattvābhāvasyābhrāntasya ghaṭādau
sattvādapramāṇavākyapraṇetṛtvasya
cāpramāṇavākyārthajñānatadvivakṣātatsamānaviṣayaprayatnādimattvasya
cābhāvāt|
apramāṇavākyapraṇetṛtvavyāghātādabhrāntatvasiddhirapi mā
bhūditi cet---satyam, na
bhavatyevāpramāṇavākyapraṇetṛtvaviśeṣaṇavatā
bhrāntatvasiddhirapi| tadvidhureṇa punarabhrāntatvasiddhirbhavantī
kena vāryate| atha
bhramasamānādhikaraṇadharmaviṣayatvarahitatvaviśeṣaṇarahitena
dharmo .apramāṇavākyapraṇetṛsākṣātkāragocaraḥ prameyatvāt
ghaṭavadityādinā pramāṇavākyapraṇetṛsiddhirbhavantī kena
vāryata iti cet---na, kenaciduttamapramāṇavākyapraṇetāro
.apyasmadādayo yogajadharmadaśāyāṃ dharmādisākṣātkārabhājo
bhavantīti īśvaraḥ pramāṇavākyapraṇetā puruṣatvādahamiveti
vā dṛṣṭāntasiddheḥ| īśvaro .apramāṇavākyapraṇetā
puruṣatvāt, anāptavaditi satpratipakṣatvamiti sampratipannavaditi
cet---na,
pramāṇavākyapraṇetṛtvāpramāṇavākyapraṇetṛtvayorasmadādau
sahānavasthitayoḥ
parasparavirodhātparasparaviruddhatvāparyavasāyitvācca|
mahāvidyāsādhyavatpramāṇavākyapraṇetṛtvābhāvasādhane ca
nāsmadādayo dṛṣṭāntaḥ, asmadādeḥ
pramāṇavākyapraṇetṛtvāt| ghaṭo dṛṣṭānta iti cet---na,
puruṣatvādeḥ sādhanavikalatvāt|
īśvarātiriktātmatvarahitatvādeścānātmatvena sopādhitvāditi,
īśvaro .apramāṇavākyapraṇetā puruṣatvādahamiveti siddhe
parameśvaravākya īśvaravākyatvamiti cet---na,
apramāṇavākyapraṇetṛtve bhrāntatvasyopādhitvāt| tasyāpi
sādhane dharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhāt|
tenābhrāntatvenaiveśvarasādhanāditi|
yadveśvarasādhakaprathamānumāne
.apramāṇavākyapraṇetṛniṣṭhadharmaviṣayatvarahitadharmopadeśapraṇetṛniṣṭhapadamupādāya
dharmo
.apramāṇavākyapraṇetṛniṣṭhadharmaviṣayatvarahitadharmopadeśapraṇetṛniṣṭhasākṣātkāragocaraḥ
prameyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi parameśvaraḥ sādhanīyaḥ| tathā ca
dharmigrāhakapramāṇenaiveśvarasyāpramāṇavākyapraṇetṛtvābhāvasiddherdharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhitamīśvarasyāpramāṇavākyapraṇetṛsādhanamiti
nānaikāntikamīśvaravākyatvamiti| na caivaṃ
dharmigrāhakapramāṇenaiva vedasyaprāmāṇyasiddhervedavākyaṃ
pramāṇamīśvaravākyatvādityasya vaiyarthyamiti vācyam|
apramāṇavākyapraṇetṛtvābhāvaviśiṣṭadharmopadeśapraṇetṛtvasādhakena
pramāṇatvaviśiṣṭadharmopadeśāsādhanāt| na
hyapramāṇatvābhāvādāvapramāṇatvam, api pramitisādhanatvamiti|
bhavatu vāpramāṇatvābhāva eva pramāṇatvam| sidhyatu ca
tadviśiṣṭatvaṃ dharmopadeśasya tathāpi vedaprāmāṇyaṃ tena na
sidhyati, tena vedasya
dharmopadeśatvāsādhanādyatkiṃciddharmopadeśenāpi tasya caritārthatvāditi vyāghātābhāvalakṣaṇayogyatvayorvirahācca|
vedavākyaṃ na pramāṇaṃ vedavākyānyapramāṇatvarahitatvādityasyāpi `agnirhimasya bheṣajam' ityādau vyabhicārāt|
sarveṣāṃ pratipakṣahetūnāṃ vedavākyatvābhāvena sopādhikatvācca|
na cāsya pakṣetaratā, `agnirhimasya bheṣajam' ityāderarthavādasya na yathāśrute .arthe prāmāṇyamiti dṛṣṭāntaḥ sādhyavikala iti cet---na,
bādhakābhāvena yathāśrute .arthe prāmāṇyasya durvāratvāt|
lakṣaṇayānyatra prāmāṇyābhyupagame .api
prāmāṇyāvivādābhāvena dṛṣṭāntasya
sādhyavikalatāparihārācceti| nanvevaṃ

sati `caityaṃ vandeta svargakāmaḥ' iti buddhavākyaṃ pramāṇam
buddhavākyatvāt, `ahiṃsā dharmasādhanam' iti vākyavaditi
buddhāgamānāmapi prāmāṇyaṃ syāditi| na,
nairātmyakṣaṇabhaṅgādipratipādakavākyeṣu buddhavākyatvasya
vyabhicārāt| nairātmyakṣaṇadharmādipratipādakavākyānāṃ ca
tatra tatra vakṣyamāṇabādhakabalenāprāmāṇyaniścayāt| etena
caityavandanādikaṃ vākyaṃ pramāṇaṃ sarvajñavākyatvāt,
`ahiṃsā dharmasādhanam'
iti vākyavaditi nirastam| anāptavākye vyabhicārācca| tasyāpi
yogadaśāyāṃ sarvajñatvāt|
kiṃcinniṣṭhasarvaviṣayakajñānajanyavākyatvamiti cet---na,
asyāpi pūrvadūṣaṇagata[gra]statvāditi| etena `keśolluñchanaṃ
svargasādhanam' iti vākyaṃ pramāṇaṃ
jinavākyatvātsarvajñavākyatvam| taccānāptavākye nāstīti
cet---na,
anāptavākyasyāpīśvaraniṣṭhasarvaviṣayajñānajanyatvāt|
buddhajñānasya kiṃcinniṣṭhatvānaṅgīkārācca
hetorviśeṣaṇāsiddhatvāt| sarvaviṣayajñānajanyavākya[tva]meva
sarvajñavākyatvam| na tu
kiṃcinniṣṭhasarvaviṣayajñānajanyavākyatvamiti cet---na, asyāpi
pūrvadūṣaṇagrastatvāditi| etena `keśolluñchanaṃ
svargasādhanam' iti vākyaṃ pramāṇaṃ jinavākyatvāt,
sampratipannavākyavaditi
jaināgamavākyaprāmāṇyasādhanapratipattirapi nirastaḥ|
atheśvaravākyatvamanāptavākye .anaikāntikam,
anāptavākyasyāpīśvarajanyatveneśvaravākyatvāditi cet---na,
īśvaraśarīratālvoṣṭhapuṭavyāpārajanyavākyasyeśvaravākyatvāt|
tasya cānāptavākye .asattvāt| atha kiṃ
sākṣādīśvaraprayatnādhiṣṭhitatvamīśvaraśarīratvam, kiṃ
veśvarabhogāyatanatvam, kiṃ veśvarajñānajanitendriyāśrayatvam|
prathame .anāptaśarīrasyāpīśvaratvam, sarvapadārthānāṃ
sākṣādīśvaraprayatnaviṣayatvāditi
taṭasthamevānaikāntikatvam| dvitīye parameśvare .api
sukhaduḥkhāpattiḥ| tṛtīye parameśvare
kāryajñānādyāpattiriti cet---na,
parameśvaragirijāsambhogaviyogajanyasukhaduḥkhakāryajñānādyaṅgīkārātpurāṇādau
girijāsambhogaviyogajanyasukhaduḥkhendriyajñānādiśālino
vigrahavata eva candraśekharasya śravaṇāt| dharmādharmābhāve
kathamīśvarasya sukhaduḥkhādīnīti cet---na, īśvare
dharmādharmayorapyaṅgīkārāt| purāṇādau parameśvarasya
tapojanyadharmabrahmaśiraśchedajanyādharmayoḥ śravaṇāt|
sukhaduḥkhādimattve parameśvarasyāsmadādibhyaḥ [na] ko .api
viśeṣa iti cet---na,
abhrāntatvasṛṣṭisaṃhārakartṛtvavedanirmātṛtvasakalabhuvanasvāmitvādikamityavehi|
anyathā sukhaduḥkhāviśeṣe rājño .api pāmarādibhyo viśeṣo na
syāt| deśasvāmyaviśiṣṭavanitāsambhogajanyasukhādayaḥ
pāmarādibhyo rājño viśeṣā iti cet---na, evaṃ tarhi
viśvasvāmyagirijāsambhogajanyasukhādayo rājādibhyaḥ
parameśvarasya viśeṣā iti saṃtoṣṭavyam| etena vedādinirmāṇe

parameśvarasya prayojakābhāva iti pratyuktam| parameśvare
vedādinirmāṇajanyadharmasukhāderaṅgīkārāt|

ātreyācāryāstu parameśvare dharmatajjanyaśarīrasukhādīni
manyante, na tvadharmatajjanyaśarīraduḥkhādīnīti| tadayuktam,
dharmasukhādisādhakadharmigrāhakānumānādipurāṇāderadharmaduḥkhādāvapi
tulyatvāt| evaṃ smṛtihetusaṃskāradveṣāvapi
dharmigrāhakānumānādibalātparameśvare svīkartavyau|
tasmādbuddhisukhaduḥkhecchādveṣaprayatnadharmādharmasaṃskāravānparameśvara
iti| atha
kāryayatnakālīnatvarahitakriyājanyasaṃyogajanyadvyaṇukajanakaprayatnāśrayatayā

parameśvaraḥ sidhyanna kāryaprayatnavāneva
dharmigrāhakātsidhyatīti kathaṃ dharmigrāhakādeva
kāryayatnavataḥ parameśvarasya siddhiḥ|
kāryākāryaprayatnayorekatra virodhāditi cet---na,
vyāptipakṣadharmatābalātkāryākāryaprayatnavantamīśvaraṃ
sādhayatā dharmigrāhakapramāṇena
kāryākāryaprayatnayoravirodhāt| nanu vedavākyaṃ
pramāṇamityatra pramāṇatvaṃ pramitijanakatvaṃ sādhyam, tathā
ca kimādyasya vedavākyasya dharmitvam, tadārabdhādermadhyamasya
vā, śabdaḥ śabdāntaramārabhate iti nyāyena
śravaṇedeśotpannasyāntasya vā| na prathamadvitīyau, bādhāt,
prathamamadhyamavākyānāmaśrūyamāṇānāṃ pramityajanakatvāt|
na tṛtīyaḥ, antavākyasya
svaviṣayasākṣātkārapramityajanakatvenārthāntaragrastatvāt|
sākṣātkāriṇi jñāne viṣayasyāpi kāraṇatvena
tvayāṅgīkṛtatvāt| anaṅgīkāre .apyanenaiva tatsiddhirastviti
vaktuṃ śakyatvāditi cet---na, śrūyamāṇavākyasyaiva
dharmitvenopadeśāt|

na ca svaviṣayasākṣātkārī[ri]pramitijanakatvenārthāntaratā,
idaṃ vākyaṃ naitadviṣayasākṣātkārajanakam, vākyatvāt,
vākyāntaravaditi tanniṣedhāt|
etadvākyametadgocarasākṣātkārajñānajanakametadvākyatvāditi
vyatirekiṇā satpratipakṣatvamiti cet---na,
etadgocarasākṣātkārajanakendriyādeḥ sapakṣādasya
vyāvṛttatvenāsādhāraṇatvāt|
etadvākyametadvākyagocarasākṣātkārajanakametadvākyaśrotrānyataratvāt,
śrotravaditi cet---na, vākyatvābhāvena sopādhitvātt|

na cāsya pakṣetaratā, etadgocarasākṣātkārajanakatvena
vipakṣasya vākyāntaravyāvartyasya sattvāditi| yadvā,
arthāntaratānirāsārthametadvedavākyametajjanyasākṣātkāratvarahitapratītijanakamiti
sādhyanirdeśaḥ kartavyaḥ| athaitadvedavākyaṃ
svagocarasākṣātkāravyatiriktāṃ svakāraṇādigocarāṃ pramitiṃ
liṅgatvena janayatvityarthāntaratvaṃ tadavasthamiti cet---na, idaṃ
vedavākyaṃ kāraṇajanyaṃ pauruṣeyaṃ vā vākyatvādityādau
vākyasya dharmitvāt, tadgatavākya[tva]syaiva liṅgatvāt|
ākāśamitarebhyo bhidyata etadvākyatvāt yattvitarebhyo na bhidyate
na tattvetadvā[dvedavā?]kyam, yathā ghaṭa ityādāvidaṃ
vedavākyaṃ liṅgatvena pramitijanakamiti cet---na, ekaikasmādeva
varṇādākāśamitarebhyo bhidyata iti pramityutpattau
kramaviśeṣānavacchinnavarṇasamudāyarūpapadasamudāyasyākāṃkṣāsannidhiyogyatāsahitasya
tajjanakatve pramāṇābhāvāt| anyathā sarveṣāṃ
vyarthaviśeṣaṇānāmanumitijanakatvāpatteḥ dharmitvenaiva
vedavākyamanumitijanakaṃ bhaviṣyatīti cet---ekaikasmādeva
varṇādanumityupapattau
varṇsasamudāyarūpapadasamudāyasyākāṃkṣāsannidhiyogyatāsahitasya
dharmitvena tajjanakatve pramāṇābhāvāt|
dharmijñānādevānumityupapatteḥ dharmiṇo .anumitijanakatvāt|
evaṃ sati liṅgajñānādevānumityupapatterliṅgamapyanumitijanakaṃ

syāditi cet---na, dhūmādagniṃ pratyemīti dhūmasyānumitiṃ prati
kāraṇatvānubhavāt| parvatādagniṃ pratyemīti
dharmiliṅgamapyanumityajanakaṃ syāditi cet---na, dhūmādagniṃ
pratyemīti

athāto dharmaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ iti sūtreṇa
prameyamātraṃ
vyākhyeyatayā pratijñātam| tathā hi yadā
tāvaddharmādharmavyākhyānaṃ sūtrārthaḥ,
tadā
dharmādharmatayā tadutpādātayā vā,
dharmādharmapramityutpādakatayā vā, tadutpādakādyutpādakatayā
vā prameyamātraṃ vyākhyeyam| ātmā
tāvaddharmādharmayostatpramiteśca [samavāyi]kāraṇam|
ātmamanaḥsaṃyogaścāsamavāyikāraṇam| śarīrādayaśca
nimittakāraṇam|
ātmamanaḥsaṃyogajanakakriyāśarīrāvayavādayaśca kāraṇakāraṇam|
ātmamanaḥsaṃyogajanakapūrvasaṃyoganivṛttijanakavibhāgādayaśca
kāraṇakāraṇakāraṇam| evaṃ tatkāraṇatvena
dvyaṇukaparamāṇuparyantaṃ saṃgrāhyam| ākāśaṃ ca
dharmajanakapraṇavādidharmapramitijanakavedādikāraṇam| evaṃ
yāgahomadānajanakapuroḍāśādayaḥ kāraṇakāraṇam|
tadavayavāśca tattatkāraṇatvena saṃgrāhyāḥ|
rūparasagandhasparśāśca dharmajanakadevabrāhmaṇatṛptikāraṇam,
śuklamadhurasurabhimṛdunā bhaktādinā tṛptijananāt|
saṃkhyāparimāṇe dharmajanake,
gosahasramahatsuvarṇakalaśādidāne dharmajananāt| pṛthaktvaṃ ca
paramparayā dharmakāraṇam,
pṛthaktvajanyapṛthaktvaviśiṣṭavrīhyādisādhanāvagamasya
dharmajanakayāgādijanakatvāt|
vaidharmyānyonyābhāvaviśiṣṭavrīhyādibodhādeva
yāgādyanuṣṭhānaṃ na pṛthaktvaviśiṣṭavrīhyādibodhāditi
cet---na, pṛthaktvaviśiṣṭabodhāddravyānumānasambhave
vaidharmyānyonyābhāvaviśiṣṭabodhādevānuṣṭhānaṃ
bhaviṣyatīti niyantumaśakyatvāt| paratvāparatvayorapi
paramparayā dharmakāraṇatvam, sāmānyaparatvaviśiṣṭabodhavatā
parasmiṃśca paratvaviśiṣṭabodhavatā parābhivādinā
dharmajananāt, paratvāparatvaviśiṣṭabodhayośca
paratvāparatvajanyatvāddharmādharmādijanakadikkālādibodhajanakatvādvā
paratvāparatvayorvyākhyānam| buddhīchādveṣaprayatnānāṃ
dharmādharmakāraṇatvaṃ sākṣātparamparayā vā spaṣṭameva|
gurutvasya ca paramparayā dharmakāraṇatvam, galantikādijalena
gurutvādadhaḥsthitaliṅgopari patatā dharmajananāt| meghādijalena
gurutvādadhaḥpatatā vrīhyādidharmadādhanajananāt| dravatvasya ca
paramparayā dharmakāraṇatvam, dravatvātparitaḥ
syandamānābhiradbhirvrīhyādidharmasādhanajananāt| snehasya ca
paramparayā dharmakāraṇatvam, snehajanyasaṃgrahaṇavatā
vrīhyādiviśiṣṭe hi
puroḍāśādisādhanadharmajananāt| saṃskārasya ca paramparayā
dharmakāraṇatvam,
dharmajanakānuṣṭhāsyamānapadārthasmṛtijanakatvāt|
dharmādharmayośca paramparayā dharmakaraṇatvam,
darmādharmajanakaśarīrādikāraṇatvāt| dharmasya ca
dharmādharmajanakatve .api svargāpavargasādhanatvena phalavattvāt|
adharmasya ca dharmādharmajanakatve .api
mokṣopayogivairāgyajanakaduḥkhajanakatvena phalavattvāt|
śabdaśca praṇavādi[ḥ] dharmakāraṇam, vedādiśca
dharmādharmeśvarādipramitikāraṇam|

karmāṇi ca paramparayā dharmakāraṇam,
dharmasādhanavrīhyādijanakasaṃyogajanakatvāt|

sāmānyaṃ ca vrīhitvādi paramparayā dharmakāraṇam,
vrīhitvajanyavrīhitve[tva]viśiṣṭabodhasya
dharmajanakānuṣṭhānahetutvāt|

viśeṣāśca
mokṣajanakamohādiviviktātmasākṣātkārasādhanatvādvyākhyeyāḥ,
dharmājanakatve .api
dehādiviviktātmasākṣātkārajanakayogajadharmasahakāritvāt|

samavāyo .api
anuṣṭhānāṅgavrīhitvādiviśiṣṭavrīhyādibodhajanakadvāreṇa
dharmasādhanam, viśiṣṭabodhasya
viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyasambandhajanakatvāt|
dharmajanakapraṇavādidharmādharmeśvaramokṣādipramitijanakavedādisākṣātkārajanakatvācca
samavāyo vyutpādanīyaḥ| śabdaśrotrasamavāyasya
śabdasākṣātkārahetutvāt|

abhāvaḥ sākṣātparamparayā ca dharmādharmamokṣādisādhanam,
pratibandhābhāvaprāgabhāvayoḥ sarvakāryajanakatvāt|
pūrvasaṃyogapradhvaṃsasya
dharmādijanakavrīhyādijanakottarasaṃyogajanakatvācca|
duḥkhadhvaṃsasya ca
paramapuruṣārthamokṣasādhanatvātsutarāmabhāvo vyutpādanīyaḥ|

tasmādupayogitvāddravyaguṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyābhāvarūpaṃ
prameyaṃ vyutpādanīyamiti siddham| yadāpi vedavyākhyānaṃ
sūtrārthaḥ,
tadāpi vaidikapadārthatayā tatpratītyupayogitayā
[ca] dravyādiprameyamātraṃ vyākhyeyameva| tathā hi
vrīhigavādipadānāṃ jātivyaktitatsambandhavācakatvapakṣe
jātidravyasamavāyāḥ padārthāḥ|
vrīhitvāśrayavyaktivācakatvapakṣe vrīhyādidravyameva
padārthaḥ| śuklādipadānāṃ ca
jātivyaktitatsambandhavācakatvapakṣe jātiguṇasamavāyāḥ
padārthāḥ| vyaktivācakatvapakṣe tu śuklādiguṇa eva
padārthaḥ| gamanādipadānāṃ ca
jātivyaktitatsambandhavācakatvapakṣe jātikriyāsamavāyāḥ
padārthāḥ| vyaktivācakatvapakṣe tu karmaiva padārthaḥ|
samavāyasambandhādipadānāṃ ca samavāyaḥ padārthaḥ|
viśeṣādipadānāṃ ca viśeṣa eva padārthaḥ|
samavāyasambandhādipadānāṃ ca samavāyaḥ padārthaḥ|
nañādipadānāṃ cābhāvaḥ padārthaḥ| tasmādvedaṃ
vyācakṣatā vaidikapadārthā
dravyaguṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyābhāvāśca
vyutpādanīyā eva, tadanavagame tatra saṅgatigrahaṇāyogāt|
anavagatasaṃgatīnāṃ ca padebhyaḥ pratipattumaśakyatvāditi|

yadāpi dharmopadeṣṭṛvyākhyānaṃ sūtrārthaḥ,
tadāpi vedāparaparyāyadharmopadeśapadārthatayā
dravyādayo vyutpādanīyāḥ| evaṃ
dharmopadeśakartureva dharmopadeṣṭṛtvādvedasyaiva
dharmopadeśatvāt| anavagatapadārthānāṃ
vedāddharmapratipattyasambhavāditi|

yadāpi liṅgarūpadharmavyākhyānaṃ sūtrārthaḥ,
tadāpi dharmatayā tadāśrayatayā ca
dravyaguṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyābhāvarūpaṃ
prameyaṃ vyākhyeyameva|

evaṃ sūtravyākhyānāntare
.api dravyādiprameyamātraṃ vyākhyeyameveti||

tadvibhāgārthaṃ sūtram---

pṛthivyaptejo vāyurākāśaṃ kālo digātmamana iti
dravyāṇīti|| 1-2-4

atra ca prameyaṃ dvividham---sadasacceti vibhāgaḥ prathamaṃ
sūtrakārasyābhipretaḥ,
navamādhyāyavyatirikteṣu navādhyāyeṣu
sato vyutpādanāt| tadanantaraṃ sat ṣoḍhā dravyaṃ guṇaḥ karma
sāmānyaṃ viśeṣaḥ samavāya iti vibhāgaḥ
sūtrakārasyābhipretaḥ,
agre dravyādiṣaṭkasya vyutpādanāt|
idaṃ tu sūtraṃ
dravyavibhāgaparam| vibhāgasya kiṃ prayojanamiti
cet---viśeṣapratipattiriti brūmaḥ| na hi dravyamityukte
pṛthivyudakatejovā[yu]rityādiviśeṣapratipattirjāyate|
pṛthivyudakaṃ teja ityukte tu pṛthivyādiviśeṣapratipattirjāyata
iti| pṛthivyādiṣu dravyatvapratipattirvā vibhāgavākyaprayojanaṃ
na hi dravyamityukte pṛthivyādiṣu navasu
dravyatvapratipattirjāyate, vibhāgavākyāttu jāyata
iti| pṛthivyādiviśeṣalakṣaṇapravṛttiḥ,
dravyatvena
pṛthivyādiviśeṣaniṣṭhadharmānumite[ti]rvā| na
hyanavagate pṛthivyādau viśeṣe
lakṣaṇapravṛttiḥ, dravyatvena
tanniṣṭhadharmānumitirvā sambhavati|
yathāsambhavaṃ pratyakṣānumānābhyāṃ
pṛthivyādiviśeṣasiddheḥ
pṛthivyādiviśeṣaniṣṭhadravyatvasiddheśca
vyarthaṃ vibhāgavākyamiti cet---na,
aniścitāptabhāve sūtrakāravibhāgavākyasya
tattadviśeṣaniṣṭhadravyatvādigocarapramāṇe
jijñāsājananadvāreṇa
pratyakṣānumānaparyavasāyitvābhāvāvagamāt|
niścitāptabhāvasūtrakāravibhāgavacanasya
pratyakṣānumānajanyasākṣātkārānumitivijātīyapramitijanakatvena
saphalatvāt| anyathā vibhāgavacanādeva
pṛthivyādiviśeṣatanniṣṭhadravyatvāsiddheḥ
pratyakṣānumānayoḥ viphalatvasya
duṣparihāratvāt| sūtrakāravacanaṃ
na pramāṇamiti
cet---na
pratyakṣānumānāvagatārthapratipādakatve
vibhāgavacanasyāpramāṇatvānupatteḥ|
pratyakṣānumānāvagatārthapratipādakatve ca
vaiyarthyābhidhānasyonmattapralāpatvāt|

udayanādayastu
nyūnādhikasaṃkhyāvyavacchedārthaṃ vibhāgavacanamityāhuḥ|
tadayuktam, vibhāgavacane pṛthivyādiviśeṣaṇatvena
navasaṃkhyānupadānāttannyūnādhikasaṃkhyāvyavacchedāsambhavāt,
pṛthivyādiṣu navasaṃkhyāyā durghaṭatvācca|
ākāśakāladigvyatiriktānāmanantatvenaikatvānupapattau
vivakṣitanavasaṃkhyānupapatteḥ,
ekaikapṛthivyādivyaktidikkālākāśeṣu navasaṃkhyāsambhave .api
dvitīyapṛthivyādivyaktyapekṣayādhikasaṃkhyopapatteḥ|
adhikasaṃkhyāvyavacchedasambhavasya durghaṭatvāt| guṇādiṣu ca
saṃkhyāvārttāyā apyasambhavāt|

atha mā bhūnnyūnādhikasaṃkhyāvyavacchedo vibhāgaphalam,
dravyanyūnatvādikatvavyavacchedastu vibhāgaphalaṃ bhaviṣyatīti
cet---na, dravyanyūnatvādhikatvayorapramitau
tadabhāvasyāvagantumaśakyatvāt| pramitau tadabhāvāvagamasya
bādhitatvātpṛthivyādyanyatamasmindravyatvādyatyantabhāvasya
vyavacchedaḥ pṛthivyādivyatirikte ca dravyatvavyavacchedo
vibhāgaphalaṃ bhaviṣyati, dravyatvātyantābhāvadravyatvayoḥ
pramitatvena pṛthivyādyanyatamasmin

pṛthivyādivyatirikte ca niṣeddhuṃ śakyatvāditi cet---na,
dravyatvātyantavyavacchedaḥ kiṃ
dravyatvātyantābhāvātyantābhāvaḥ, kiṃ vā
dravyatvātyantābhāvātyantābhāvātyantābhāvavyavacchedaḥ, kiṃ
vā dravyatvātyantābhāvātantābhāvapratyayaḥ| na prathamaḥ,
dravyatvātyantābhāvātyantābhāvasyānāditve
vibhāgavākyaphalatvānupapatteḥ| dvitīye .api kiṃ
dravyatvātyantābhāvābhāvaḥ kiṃ dravyatvameva kiṃ vā
dravyātiriktaḥ| prathame sampratipattireva, pṛthivyādiṣu
manaḥparyanteṣu dravyatvapratipatteḥ
vibhāgavākyaphalatvenāsmābhirapyaṅgīkārāt| dvitīye .api
pṛthivyādyanyatamasminvipratipanno dravyatvātyantābhāvapratyayaṃ
vibhāgavākyaṃ janayatīti tasya vibhāgavākyaphalatvaṃ
vivakṣitam|
kiṃ vā vibhāgavākyāvagataṃ dravyatvaṃ janayatīti tasya vibhāgavākyaphalatvam vivakṣitam, na prathamaḥ,
vibhāgavākyasya pṛthivyādiṣu dravyatvapratyayacaritārthasya
pṛthivyādiniṣṭhadravyatvātyantābhāvātyantābhāvapratipattyajanakatvāt|
na dvitīyaḥ, sampratipattereva|
pṛthivyādivyatiriktadravyatvavyavacchedo vibhāgavākyaphalamityato
.api kiṃ vibhāgavākyaṃ pṛthivyādivyatirikto
dravyatvātyantābhāvātyantābhāvapratyayaṃ janayatīti tasya
vibhāgavākyaphalatvaṃ vivakṣitam, kiṃ vā vibhāgavākyāvagataṃ
pṛthivyādiniṣṭhadravyatvaṃ pṛthivyādyatirikte
dravyatvātyantābhāvapratyayaṃ janayatīti tasya tasya
vibhāgavākyaphalatvam| na prathamaḥ, vibhāgavākyasya
pṛthivyādidravyasaṃsargāvagamamātracaritārthasya
pṛthivyādivyatiriktadravyatvātyantābhāvasaṃsargābodhakatvāt,
pṛthivyādidravyatvasaṃsargasya prakārāntareṇa siddhatvāt|
pṛthivyādivyatiriktadravyatvātyantābhāvasaṃsargaparaṃ
vibhāgavākyamiti cet---na, prakārāntareṇa iti
kimuddeśavākyāditi vivakṣitam, kiṃ vā
pratyakṣānumānābhyāmiti| na prathamaḥ,
dravyaguṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyābhāvāḥ
prameyamityāderuddeśavākyasya
dravyatvādiprameyatvādisaṃsargamātrapratipādakatvena
pṛthivyādidravyatvasaṃsargatvāpratipādakatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
pratyakṣānumānābhyāṃ pṛthivyādidravyatvasaṃsargasya
avagatatvena vibhāgavākyasya tadapratipādakatve
pṛthivyādivyatiriktadravyatvātyantābhāvasaṃsargasyāpi
pratyakṣānumānāvagatatvena vibhāgavākyasya
tadapratipādakatvaprasaṅgāt| mukhye bādhakamantareṇa vākyasya
svārthapraityāgārthāntarakalpanayorniṣpramāṇakatvācca
vibhāgavākyādevoddeśākṣepe uddeśavākyabalena
vibhāgavākyasyānyathākartumaśakyatvācca| dravyāntaraṃ
pṛthivyādi (?) na lakṣaṇavākyādeva
pṛthivyādidravyatvasiddheḥ| vibhāgavākyaṃ
pṛthivyādivyatiriktaṃ dravyatvaniṣedhaparamiti cet---na,
pṛthivyādilakṣaṇavākyānāṃ
pṛthivyādilakṣaṇamātrapratipādakatvena
pṛthivyādidravyāpratipādakatvāt| agre pṛthivyādīnāṃ
guṇavattvapratipādanātpṛthivyādīnāṃ dravyatvasiddheḥ,
vibhāgavākyaṃ pṛthivyādivyatiriktaṃ[kte]
dravyatvaniṣedhaparamiti cet---na, tasya guṇatvapratipādakatvena
dravyatvāpratipādakatvāt| tatpratipāditaguṇa[tva]vattvameva
dravyatvaṃ pratipādayatīti cet---na, vibhāgavākyena
pṛthivyādīnāṃ dravyatvāpratijñāne
guṇavattvahetvabhidhānasyānavakāśāt| pratijñāne vā
vibhāgavākyasya pṛthivyādidravyapratipādakatvena
pṛthivyādivyatiriktadravyatvaniṣedhāpratipādakatvāt|
nāpi dvitīyaḥ,
vibhāgavākyāvagatapṛthivyādiniṣṭhadravyatvasya
pṛthivyādivyatiriktādravyatvasādhane .apakṣadharmatvāt,
viruddhatvācca| adravyatvasya
dravyatvātyantābāvatvāddravyatvatadatyantābhāvatvayośca
nirodhasyodbhūtatvāditi|

nanu pṛthivyāpastejo vāyurātmā manaḥ ityatra pṛthivyādipadāni
pṛthivītvādyāśrayavyaktiparāṇi kiṃ vā
pṛthivītvādijātiparāṇi| prathame
kimekaikapṛthivī[tvā]dyāśrayavyaktiparāṇi kiṃ vā
sarvapṛthivītvādyāśrayavyaktiparāṇi, ādye vibhāganyūnatvam,
ekaikapṛthivītvādyāśrayavyaktivyatiriktapṛthivītvādivyaktīnāmavibha[ñjanāt,
āpa iti bahuvacanānupapatteśca, ekasyāmudakavyaktau
bahutvāsambhavāt---bahuṣu bahuvacanam
iti pāṇinismaraṇāt|
ekasminnevedaṃ bahuvacanaṃ dārā itivadanuśāsanabalāditi
cet---na, āpa iti bahuvacanasyaikatve .anuśāsanābhāvāt|
āpaḥśabdo nityabahuvacanānna ityanuśāsanamastīti
cet---na, tasya niyamena bahutvavācakatvenāvyutpatteḥ|
bahuṣu bahuvacanam
ityanenaiva āpa iti bahuvacanasya bahutvavācakatvasiddhe
punarāpaḥśabdo bahuvacanātamiti vidhānamekasmindvayośca
bahuvacanaprāptyarthamiti cet---na, siddhe satyārambho niyamāyeti
nyāyena āpaḥśabdasya punarbahuvacanāntatvavidhānasya
bahuvacanānta evāyam, na dvivacanānto naikavacanānta iti
jñāpanārthatvāditi| na dvitīyaḥ, pṛthivī tejo
vāyurityādyekavacanānupatteḥ
pṛthivyādiprātipadikānāmanantavyaktiparatvāt| nāpi tṛtīyaḥ,
vibhāganyūnatvādhikatvayorāpatteḥ pṛthivyādipadānāṃ
pṛthivītyādijātiparatve pṛthivyādivyaktīnāmapratipādanāt|
vibhāganyūnatvaṃ dravyāṇāmapi pṛthivītyādijātīnāṃ
vibha[ñ]janādvibhāgādhikatvaṃ āpa iti bahuvacanānupapatteśca
aptvasyaikatvāditi---tanna, pṛthivyādipadaiḥ
pṛthivītvādyāśrayasamastavyaktipratipādanāt| na
caivamekavacanānupapattiḥ,
pṛthivītvādijātigataikatvapratipādakatvena tadupapatteḥ|
pṛthivītvādijātīnāṃ pratipādakārthābhāvānna tābhiḥ
pratyayārthasyaikatvasyānvaya iti cet---jāterapi
prātipadikārthatvāt| jāteraprātipadikārthatve
.apyupalakṣaṇatayā buddhisthayā jātyā
pratyayārthasyaikatvasyānvayo bhaviṣyati| prātipadikopāttānāṃ
tadvyaktibhirekatvasyānvayānupapatteḥ| upalakṣaṇabhūtayā
jātyā pratyayārthasyaikatvasyānvayo na dṛṣṭa iti cet---na,
asminvatsare ba[vrī]hireva niṣpanna ityādau darśanāt|
pṛthivītvādijātigataikatvapratipādane
sūtrakārasya
kiṃ prayojanamiti cet---sakalapṛthivyādivyaktisaṃgrahaḥ|
pṛthivītvānāmanekatve tu pṛthivīpadenaikapṛthivītvāśrayaḥ,
sakalavyaktisaṃgrahe
pṛthivītvāntarāśrayavyaktīnāmasaṃgrahāt, vibhāganyūnatvasya
tadavasthatvācca|

akṣaśabdapravṛttinimittākṣaśabdānāṃ
vibhītakatvendriyapātakaṭākṣatvarūpāṇāṃ
bhedasthitāvekasminprayoge .akṣaśabdena
sakalavibhītakatvāśrayasaṃgrahe .api
indriyādyāśrayāṇāmasaṃgrahasya sarvasampratipannatvāt|
itiśabdaḥ samāptau| tena samāptāni dravyāṇi, ito .atiriktāni
na santītyarthaḥ| etāvantyeva dravyāṇītyatra kiṃ pramāṇamiti
cet---sūtrakāravacanameva
tatpramāṇamiti cet---na,
abādhitānubhavajanakatvasya prāmāṇyasya kaṇādavacanatvena
agnyuṣṇakapratipādakakaṇādavacanavadanumānāt,
na cābādhitatvaṃ traikālikabādhābhāvarūpaṃ
duravadhāraṇamiti aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇateti vācyam| abādhitatvasya
kālatrayabādhavirahatvasyānaṅgīkārāt| tarhi kiṃ
[a]bādhitatvamiti cet---bādhitatvātyantābhāvaḥ| bādhitatvameva
kimiti cet---idaṃ bādhyamidaṃ bādhyamityanugatabiddhisiddhau
bādhyajñānaniṣṭho jñānatvāvāntaro jātiviśeṣaḥ,
upādhirvāstu| tadatyantābhāvaśca bādhyatvaṃ
mānasapratyakṣasiddhameveti kathamabādhyasya duravadhāraṇatā|
anyathā janitajñānasyāpyabādhitatvānavadhāraṇe
vedāntaprāmāṇyasyāpi
duravadhāraṇatvāpatteḥ|
abādhitānubhavajanakatvaṃ prāmāṇya upādhiriti cet---na, tasyaiva
tatrānupādhitvāt| pramātvajātitvasādhanatvaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ
sādhyamitaramabādhitānubhavajanakatvaṃ ca tato bhinnamupādhiriti
cet---na, sādhanavyāpakatvāt| āptoktatvamatropādhiriti cet---na,
pratyakṣādau sādhyavyāpakatvātteṣāṃ prāmāṇye
.apyāptoktatvābhāvāt| sādhanavyāpakatvācca|
sūtrakāravacanasya
pramāṇatve tata eva sakalapadārthasiddhau sūtrakārādīnāṃ
paramāṇvādisādhakapramāṇavyutpādanaṃ vyarthamiti cet---na,
mūlapramāṇavattvena
sūtrakāravacanasyāptavacanatvasiddhyarthatvātpramāṇāntarādapi
tatsiddhitvakhyāpanārthācca| na hyāgamasiddha ātmeti
tadgocarapratyakṣānumānavyutpādanaṃ vyarthamiti| api
caitāvantyeva dravyāṇīti kiṃ pramāṇamiti kimetāvatā
dravyatvasādhakapramāṇena guṇādivyāvṛttasyaiva dravyatvasya
supraśnaḥ kiṃ vā
etāvadvyatiriktānāmadravyatvasādhakapramāṇapraśnaḥ| prathame
dravyatvasādhakānumānamevetyuttaram| dvitīye .apyetadevottaram|
dravyatvasādhakapramāṇena guṇādivyāvṛttasyaiva dravyatvasya
sādhanāt| kiṃ ca etāvadvyatiriktasyādravyatve kiṃ
pramāṇamityatraitāvadvyatiriktapadena kiṃ
guṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyābhāvā vivakṣitāḥ
tadvyatiriktaṃ vā| prathame
guṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyābhāvā dravyatvarahitā
guṇavattvarahitatvāt| yanna dravyatvarahitaṃ na
tadguṇavattvarahitam, yathā ghaṭaḥ| guṇavattvarahitāśca
guṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyābhāvāḥ,
tasmāddravyatvarahitā ityuttaram|
dravyatvarahitasyāprasiddheraprasiddhaviśeṣaṇateti cet---na,
dravyatvarahitatvasyāprasiddhatve guṇādayo na dravyamityatra kiṃ
pramāṇamiti praśnasyaivānupapatteḥ| dravyatvaṃ
dravyatvānyonyābhāvavyatiriktaprāgabhāvapradhvaṃsatvarahitābhāvapratiyogi,
prameyatvāt, ghaṭavat| dravyatvamatyantābhāvapratiyogi jātitvāt,
gotvādivadi[vadityādi]nā viśeṣaṇatvaprasiddheḥ|
dravyatvasādhakena guṇādivyāvṛttadravyatva[a]prasiddheśca
nāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā| guṇādayo dravatvavantaḥ, meyatvāt,
ghaṭavaditi vyatirekiṇaḥ
satpratipakṣatvamiti cet---na, dravyatvasādhakapramāṇābādhāt|
guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitaguṇādivyāvṛttajāteḥ
dravyatvaśabdārthatvāt|

atha guṇavadvyāvṛttarahitatve sati guṇavyāvṛttajātitvāt
dravyatvaśabdārtho, na tu guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitatve sati
guṇakarmaviśeṣasamavāyābhāvavyāvṛttajātitvaṃ
karmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyābhāvā eva dharmatvenopādīyante|
tena na dravyatvasādhakapramāṇabādha iti cet---na, evaṃ sati
prameyatvasya guṇa eva vyabhicārāt| dravyatvarahitatvena guṇasya
vipakṣatvāt guṇatvarahitatvena heturviśeṣaṇīya iti cet---na,
guṇavattvena sopādhitvāt| dravyatvarahitatvena vipakṣādguṇādeva
vyāvṛttatvāt| dravyatvaṃ
dravyatvānyonyābhāvavyatiriktaprāgabhāvatvapradhvaṃsatvarahitakiṃcinniṣṭhābhāvapratiyogi
meyatvāt| dravyatvaṃ kiṃcinniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogi,
jātitvāt, gotvavadityādinā siddhe dravyatvātyantābhāvavati
meyatvasyānaikāntikatvācceti|

dvitīye tu praśnānupapattireva|
guṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyābhāvavyatiriktapadenobhayavādisiddhapṛthivyādidravyābhidhāne
tato dravyatvasādhakapramāṇapraśnasya vyāhatatvāt|
ubhayavādisiddhapṛthivyādidravyāvyatiriktābhidhāne ca tatra
pramāṇaṃ vaktavyam| aprāmāṇike
dravyatvābhāvasādhakapramāṇapraśnasyonmattapralāpatvāt|
guṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyābhāvavyatiriktatvaṃ
nobhayavādisiddhapṛthivyādidravyavyatiriktavṛtti
guṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyābhāvavyatiriktāvyāvṛttatvāt,
meyatvavaditi| na,
guṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyābhāvavyatiriktatvaṃ
nobhayavādisiddhapṛthivyādidravyavyatiriktavṛtti
guṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyābhāvāvyatiriktavṛttittvāt,
pṛthivītvavat ityādinā satpratipakṣatvāditi|

atha dravyamubhayavādisiddhapṛthivyādidravyavyatiriktavṛtti
dravyavyāvṛttatvātsattāvaditi dravyāntarasiddheretāvantyeva
dravyāṇītyupapannamiti cet---na, pṛthivyādiśabdena kimekaikā
pṛthivyādivyaktirvivakṣitā, kiṃ vā yāvatpratipannāḥ
pṛthivyādivyaktayaḥ, kiṃ vā sarvā eva pṛthivyādivyaktayaḥ,
kiṃ vā pṛthivyādijātīyam| prathame arthāntaratā|
ekaikapṛthivyādivyaktivyatiriktapṛthivyādivyaktisiddhyāsya
caritārthatvena dravyāntarāsādhakatvāt| dvitīye .api
arthāntaratā,
yāvatpratipannapṛthivyādivyaktivyatiriktasiddhimātreṇāsya
caritārthatvāt| tṛtīye .apyaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam,
sarvapṛthivyādivyaktīnāmavagantumaśakyatvena
tadvyatiriktavṛttitvasyāprasiddhatvāt| caturthe .api
yatkiṃcitpṛthivyādisiddhyārthāntaratā, yasya kasyāpi
pṛthivyādeḥ kiṃcitpṛthivījātīyādivyatiriktatvam|

atha pṛthivyādivyatiriktapadasthāne
pṛthivītvādirahitapadamupādīyate, tathā ca na
pṛthivyādīnāmarthāntaratvam, tasya pṛthivyādivyatiriktatve
pṛthivītvādyākrāntatve .api na
pṛthivītvarahitatvānupapatteriti manyase| na
pṛthivyantarādisiddhyarthāntaraparihāre
.apyākāśakāladigantaraiddhyārthāntarasya duṣparihāratvāt|
ākāśāntarāderapi prathamākāśādivyatiriktatvopapatteḥ|
ākāśāntarāderapyākāśāditvenākāśādivyatiriktatvānupapattiriti
cet---na,
ākāśāntarāderapyākāśāditve .api
prathamākāśādivyatiriktatvenākāśādivyatiriktatvopapatteḥ|
prathamākāśasyāpyākāśatvāt|

arthāntaratāparihārārthamākāśakāladigvyatiriktapadasthāne
.apyākāśatvakālatvadiktvarahitapadamupādīyate---tanna,
ākāśatvakālatvadiktvāni kiṃ jātayaḥ kiṃ
vākāśakāladiglakṣaṇāni, kiṃ
vākāśakāladikśabdapravṛttinimittāni| prathame
.aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam, ākāśatvādijātīnāmaprasiddhatvena
tadrahitavṛttitvasyāpyasiddhatvāt| dvitīye
ākāśādīnāmarthāntaratvam,
ākāśasyāpyākāśaśabdāśrayatvena ākāśalakṣaṇarahitatvāt|
na caivaṃ śabdasyāpakṣadharmatvamākāśaniṣṭhatvena
tadabhāvābhāvāt| dikkālayorapi
dikkālalakṣaṇaparatvaviśeṣajanakasaṃyogātyantābhāvāśrayatvena
dikkālalakṣaṇarahitatvāt| śabdasaṃyogādeḥ
svātyantābhāvasāmānādhikaraṇasyāvyāpyavṛtitvasya
svīkārāt| evaṃvidhāvyāpyavṛttitvaṃ meṣyata iti cet---na,
anevaṃvidhāvyāpyavṛttitvasiddhyāpyarthāntarasya durvāratvāt|
śabdasyāvyāpyavṛttitvānaṅgīkāre sarvaiḥ
sarvaśabdopalambhaprasaṅgāt| sarvaśabdānāṃ
sarvapuruṣaśrotravṛttitvasiddhyāpyasya caritārthatvenāsya
tādavasthyācceti| tṛtīye .apyākāśādīnāmarthāntaratvam,
ākāśāderapyākāśādiśabdapravṛttinimittākāśādirahitatvāt|
ākāśādiśabdapravṛttāvākāśādereva nimittatvāt|
vyabhicārābhāvena tadgatadharmāntarasya
nimittasyānaṅgīkārāditi| kiṃ ca
pṛthivyādivyatiriktavṛttitvādāvadravyatvamupādhiḥ| na cāsya
pakṣetaratā| pṛthivyādivyatiriktavṛttitvādisādhyābhāvavataḥ
pṛthivītvādereva vipakṣādvyāvṛttatvāddravyatvam|
pṛthivītvādivyatiriktavṛttītyādau ca dravyatvaṃ na
pṛthivyādivyatiriktavṛtti, adravyāvṛttitvāt,
pṛthivītvavadityādinā satpratipakṣatvamiti| etena
pṛthivyādivyatiriktaṃ dravyavṛtti
pṛthivyādivyatiriktāvyāvṛttatvāt, meyatvavaditi
dravyāntarasādhanaṃ pratyuktam| kiṃcitpṛthivyādivyatiriktatvasya
tadanyapṛthivyādidravyavṛttitvenārthāntaragrastatvātpṛthivyādivyatiriktatvasthāne
pṛthivītvāptvatejastvavāyutvātmatvamanastvarahitatvopādāne
ākāśāntarādidravyasiddhyārthāntarasya durvāratvāt|
ākāśakāladigvyatiriktatvapadasthāne .api
ākāśatvakālatvadiktvarahitatvopādāne
.apyākāśatvakālatvadiktvānāṃ jātitvapakṣe
āśrayāsiddhatvāt| ākāśatvakālatvadiktvajātīnāmasiddhatvena
tadrahitatvasyāpyasiddheḥ|
ākāśatvakālatvadiktvānāmākāśādilakṣaṇatvapakṣe ca
ākāśādīnāmarthāntaratvāt|
ākāśādīnāmākāśādilakṣaṇaśabdātyantābhāvavattvenākāśādilakṣaṇarahitatvāt|
ākāśādiśabdapravṛttinimittatvādirahitapakṣīkṛtākāśādilakṣaṇarahitatvāt|
ākāśādiśabdapravṛttinimittatvādirahitapakṣīkṛtākāśādidravyabhinnatva
ākāśādīnāmapyākāśādiśabdapravṛttinimittākāśādirahitatvāt|
dravyavṛttitve cākāśādivyatiriktatvarahitatvasyopādhitvācceti|
na cāsya pakṣetaratvam,
guṇatvakarmatvādidravyavṛttitvarahitatvasya vyāvartyasya sattvāt|
pṛthivyādivyatiriktatvaṃ na dravyavṛtti
pṛthivyādivyatiriktatvarahitāvṛttitvāt guṇatvavadityādinā
satpratipakṣatvācceti| athākāśaṃ
pṛthivītvāptvatejastvavāyutvātmatvamanastvadikkālalakṣaṇarahitadravyabhinnaṃ,

sattvāt, ghaṭavaditi dravyāntarasādhanam| na
cātrāpyaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ
pṛthivītvāptvatejastvavāyutvātmatvamanastvadikkālalakṣaṇarahitapakṣīkṛtākāśadravyabhinnatvasya
ghaṭādau prasiddhatvāt, na ca
pṛthivītvādirahitapakṣīkṛtākāśādidravyabhinnatvasiddhārthāntaratā|
pakṣīkṛtākāśasya pakṣīkṛtākāśabedasādhane
vyāghātabādhayordurvāratvāditi manyase---tanna,
dikkālalakṣaṇavyāvṛttatvasvīkāre
dikkālalakṣaṇarahitadikkāladravyānyatvasiddhārthāntarasya
tadavasthatvāt| ākāśāntarasiddhyāsya caritārthatvena
dravyāntarasādhakatvāt|

athaitadvyāvṛttyarthaṃ
pakṣīkṛtākāśavyatiriktākāśatvarahitatvaviśeṣaṇamupādīyate|
tanna, aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt|
pakṣīkṛtākāśavyatiriktākāśasya etadanumānapravṛtteḥ
pūrvamaprasiddhatvāt| athāyaṃ ghaṭa
etadanyapṛthivītvāptvatejastvavāyutvātmatvamanastvadikakālākāśalakṣaṇarahitadravyabhinnaṃ,
sattvāt, paṭavaditi dravyāntaraṃ sādhayatīti---tanna,
avyāpyavṛtti[ḥ]
