śabdādyākāśalakṣaṇarahitākāśādidravyabhinnatvasiddhārthāntarasya
tadavasthatvāt| ayaṃ ghaṭa
etadanyapṛthivītvāptvatejastvavāyutvātmatvamanastvadikkālākāśalakṣaṇarahitadravyabhinnatvarahitaḥ,
etattvāt na yadevaṃ na tadevamiti vyatirekiṇā
satpratipakṣatvācca| na cātrāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam,
etadanyapṛthivītvāptvatejastvavāyutvātmatvamanastvadikkālākāśalakṣaṇarahitadravyabhinnatvaṃ
svānyonyābhāvatvarahitaprāgabhāvatvapradhvaṃsatvarahitābhāvapratiyogi
meyatvādghaṭavaditi viśeṣaṇaprasiddheriti|| 1-1-4||

atha guṇavibhāgaparaṃ sūtram---

rūparasagandhasparśāḥ saṃkhyāḥ parimāṇāni pṛthaktvaṃ
saṃyogavibhāgau paratvāparatve buddhayaḥ sukhaduḥkhe
icchādveṣau prayatnaśca guṇāḥ iti|| 1-1-5||

rūparasagandhasparśā [ityatra] samāsasya kiṃ prayojanamiti
cet---vibhaktilāghavamiti brūmaḥ| yadi rūpaṃ raso gandhaḥ
sparśa iti vyastamupādīyeta, tadā pratyekaṃ vibhaktiprayogāt
catasro vibhaktayaḥ prayoktavyā iti vibhaktigauravam| samāsapakṣe
punarekaiva vibhaktiḥ prayujya iti| ātreyācāryāstu
rūparasagandhasparśā iti samāsasya rūparasagandhasparśānāṃ
nityānityatvapākajatvādisādharmyapratipādanaṃ
prayojanamityāhuḥ| tanna,
rūparasagandhasparśanityatvānityatvapākajatvānāmagre
sūtrakāreṇa
sākṣādeva pratipādanāttatsūcanasya
viphalatvāditi| evaṃ tarhi rūpādīnāṃ prayatnāntānāṃ
vibhaktilāghavārthaṃ samāsaḥ sūtrakāreṇa
kiṃ na kriyata iti
cet---avāntaraprayojanārtham| tacca pratipadaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ|

saṃkhyā iti bahuvacanaṃ
saṃkhyātvatadavāntaraikatvadvitvatritvādiparigrahārtham| tena
yatkaiściduktam---
ekatvameva saṃkhyā, dvitvādayaḥ punarekatvasamuccayaviśiṣṭā
iti tannirastam
dvitvatritvādijātiviśeṣavyatiriktasyaikatvasamuccayaviśeṣasyānirūpaṇāditi|

parimāṇānīti bahuvacanaṃ
parimāṇatvāvāntarāṇutvamahattvahrasvatvadīrghatvakhyāpanārtham|
tena yatkenaciduktam---
mahattvameva parimāṇam| apakṛṣṭaṃ ca mahattvamevāṇutvam|
tadeva ca hrasvatva[m]| mahattvaviśeṣa eva ca dīrghatvamiti
tannirastam| samarthayiṣyāmaścaitaditi|

pṛthaktvamityekavacanaṃ
pṛthaktvāvāntaradvipṛthaktvādijātinirāsārtham| dvau
pṛthagityādivyavahārasya dvitvāvacchinnapṛthaktvādevopapatteriti
kecit|
tanna, ekaikaniṣṭhapṛthaktvavyāvṛttānāṃ
dviniṣṭhatriniṣṭhapṛthaktvādijātīnāṃ
jātyantarasamānanyāyena siddhatvāt|
tasmātpṛthaktvamityekavacanaṃ
pṛthaktvajāterekatvakhyāpanārtham| atha rūpaṃ rasa
ityādyekavacanaṃ kiṃ na kṛtamiti cet---na, kvacidekavacanena
jātyekatve khyāpite tatsamānanyāyena rūpādijātīnāmekatvasya
nirdhārayituṃ śakyatvāditi|

saṃyogavibhāgau paratvāparatve ityādāvapi samāsaḥ pūrvavat
vibhaktilāghavārtham|

buddhaya iti bahuvacanaṃ buddhibahutvakhyāpanārtham| tena
buddhirekaiva buddhibhedavyavahārastu
candrabhedavyavahāravanmithyaiveti māyāvedāntasiddhānto
nirastaḥ| yadvā, buddhaya iti bahuvacanaṃ
buddhitvāvāntara[pramātṛ]sākṣātkāritvānumititvasaṃśayatvaviparyayatvādijātibahutvakhyāpanārtham|
tena jñānatvāvāntarajātayo na santīti vadantaḥ prābhākarā
nirastāḥ|

prayatnaśceti cakāro
.anuktagurutvadravatvasnehasaṃskāradharmādharmaśabdasamuccayārthaḥ|

anuktatvāviśeṣe .api kathametāvanta eva samuccīyanta iti
cet---anuktānāmetāvatāmeva guṇatvāt|
tasmātkaṇṭhoktarūpādayaḥ caśabdasamuccitāśca gurutvādayo
guṇā iti| kathametāvanta eva guṇā iti cet---na, etāvantyeva
dravyāṇīti nyāyena dattotaratvāditi|| 1-1-5||

atha karmavibhāgārthaṃ sūtram---

utkṣepaṇamavakṣepaṇamākuñcanaṃ prasāraṇaṃ gamanamiti
karmāṇi|| 1-1-6||

utkṣepaṇatvaṃ jātiviśeṣaḥ| tadāśraya utkṣepaṇam|
evamavakṣepaṇādayo .api nirvaktavyāḥ|
utkṣepaṇamityādyekavacanamutkṣepaṇatvādijātyekatvakhyāpanārtham|
utkṣepaṇādivyaktīnāmanantatvenaikatvānupapatteḥ|
utkṣepaṇādijātigataikatvapratipādanasya kiṃ prayojanamiti
cet---na, pṛthivītyatra jātigataikatvapratipādanasya prayojanasya
darśitatvāt| bhramaṇarecanasyandanordhvajvalanādīnāmapi
sadbhāvānnyūno vibhāga iti cet---na, bhramaṇādīnāṃ
gamanatvāt|
utkṣepaṇatvāvakṣepaṇatvākuñcanatvaprasāraṇatvarahitakarmatvasya
gamanalakṣaṇatvāt| uktalakṣaṇavanmātraniṣṭhajāteśca
gamanatvaśabdārthatvāt| tathā ca prayogaḥ---bhramaṇādayo na
gamanatvavantaḥ,
utkṣepaṇatvāvakṣepaṇatvākuñcanatvaprasāraṇatvarahitakarmatvāt,
sampratipannagamanavaditi| na

cātrāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam, gamanatvasyedaṃ gamanamidaṃ
gamanamityanugatapratyakṣasiddhatvāt| gamanaṃ
gamanalakṣaṇavadvṛttigamanalakṣaṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitagamanalakṣaṇavadvṛttitvarahitajātimat,
jātimattvāt, gotvavaditi viśeṣaṇaprasiddheḥ| na ca gamanaṃ
gamanalakṣaṇavadvṛttigamanalakṣaṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitagamanalakṣaṇavadvṛttigamanalakṣaṇarahitajātirahitam,
gamanatvarahitajātitvarahitatvāt, abhāvavaditi satpratipakṣatvaṃ
jātimattvarahitatvena sopādhikatvāt| tasyāpi sādhane bādhāt| na
cāsya pakṣetaratā, evaṃvidhajātimato .api pakṣāt
ghaṭādervyāvṛttatvāditi| gamanaśabdavācyatvaṃ vā sādhyam|
tathā ca bhramaṇādayo gamanaśabdavācyāḥ,
utkṣepaṇatvāvakṣepaṇatvākuñcanatvaprasāraṇatvarahitakarmatvāt,
sampratipannagamanavat| na ca bhramaṇādayo gamanatvavantaḥ, nāpi
gamanaśabdavācyāḥ sampratipannagamanātiriktatvāt,
ghaṭādivaditi pūrvānumānayoḥ satpratipakṣatvam,
utkṣepaṇatvāvakṣepaṇatvākuñcanatvaprasāraṇatvarahitakarmatvābhāvena
sopādhikatvāditi|
athavotkṣepaṇatvāvakṣepaṇatvākuñcanatvaprasāraṇatvarahitakarmatvameva
gamanatvam| tacca bhramaṇādau pratyakṣasiddhameva|
tadabhāvasādhane pratyakṣabādha eveti sarvamanavadyam|
utkṣepaṇāvakṣepaṇākuñcanaprasāraṇa[gamanānāṃ] samāso
vibhaktilāghavārthaṃ kiṃ [na] kṛta iti
cet---utkṣepaṇatvādijātigataikatvapratipattiprayojanānurodhena
vibhaktilāghavasyopekṣaṇāditi|| 1-1-6||

atha dravyādīnāṃ sādharmyavaidharmyapratipādanārthaṃ
sūtram---

sadanityaṃ dravyavatkāryaṃ kāraṇaṃ sāmānyaviśeṣavaditi
dravyaguṇakarmaṇāmaviśeṣa iti|| 1-1-7

saditi bhāvapradhāno nirdeśaḥ| sattvamityarthaḥ| evamanityamiti,
dravyavaditi, kāryamiti, kāraṇa[miti,]
sāmānyaviśeṣavadityādayo bhāvapradhānā eva nirdeśāḥ|
sāmānyaviśeṣavaditi sāmānyavat viśeṣavat
sāmānyaviśeṣavaditi vivecanīyam| tena
sattvānityatvadravya[va]ttvakāryatvakāraṇatvasāmānyavattvaviśeṣavattvāni
dravyaguṇakarmaṇāṃ sādharmyāṇītyuktaṃ bhavati| itiśabda
evaṃvidhābhidheyatvaprameyatvādiparigrahārthaḥ| sattvamastitvaṃ
sakalapadārthaniṣṭho dharmaviśeṣaḥ| tatra kiṃ pramāṇamastīti
cet---pratyakṣameva| athāstitvaṃ sattaiva, sā ca
dravyaguṇakarmasveva vartate| sāmānyādiṣu punarastīti pratyayo
bhrāntiriti cet---na, bādhakābhāvāt| na hi sāmānyādayo na
santīti bādhakamudeti| kālāntare bādhakaṃ bhaviṣyatīti
cet---na, ghaṭo .astītyādipratyayasyāpi kālāntare bādhakaṃ
bhaviṣyatīti vaktuṃ śakyatvāt| sāmānyādayo .astitvavantaḥ
prameyatvād, ghaṭavadityanumānaṃ pramāṇam|
sāmānyavattvamatropādhiriti cet---na, sādhanavyāpakatvāt,
pakṣetaratvācca| kevalānvayisādhyābhāvasyāprasiddhatve
sādhyābhāvavato vipakṣasyāvidyamāna tvena

vipakṣādravyāvṛttatvād (?)| na cāsya
kevalānvayisādhyābhāvastāprasiddhatvenāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāditi|
nanvastitvamastitve vartate vā na vā| prathame ātmāśrayaḥ|

dvitīye astitvasya sakalapadārthavṛttitvānupapattiḥ| svasmādeva
vyāvṛttatvāditi na, astitve .apyastitvāṅgīkārāt|
utpattipramitipratibandhakatvābhāvenātmāśrayasya prakṛte
dūṣaṇatvānaṅgīkārāt| astitve .astitvaṃ vartata ityatra kiṃ
pramāṇamiti cet---prameyatvādikameva| jātyādayo .astitvarahitāḥ
dravyatvaguṇatvakarmatvarahitatvāt, na yadevaṃ na tadevam, yathā
ghaṭa iti vyatirekiṇā satpratipakṣatvamiti cet---na,
aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt| atha yatra bhāvarūpaḥ sādhyam,
tatrāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ dūṣaṇam| pramātumaśakyatvena
tasya sādhanābhāvena vyāptergrahītumaśakyatvāt| prakṛte
punarabhāvarūpaṃ sādhyam| tatrāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ
dūṣaṇam| viśeṣaṇāprasiddhatve .api tadabhāvasya
bhūvarūpatayā pramātuṃ śakyatvena tasya
sādhanābhāvena[a]vyāptigrahaṇopapatteriti manyase---tanna,
viśeṣaṇāprasiddhau tena saha hetorvyāptergrahītumaśakyatvena
vyāpyatvāsiddherduṣparihāratvāt|
sādhyābhāvasya sādhanābhāvena vyāptigrahaṇasambhave 'apyapakṣadharmatvāt|
vyatirekavyāpteścānvayavyāptigrahaṇopāyatvena svīkārāt|
upapāditaṃ caitat purastāt|

aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatve ca sādhyasamarpakapadasyāvācakatvena nirarthake .antarbhāvācca|
dharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhitatvena vyatirekiṇo durlabhatvācca|
jātyādigrāhakapramāṇena teṣāmastitvena grahaṇāt|

athāstitvaṃ prameyādvyāvṛttaṃ jātitvādgotvavaditi siddhe
astitvābhāvavati prameye
.astitvasādhakaprameyatvasyānaikāntika[tva]miti manyate---tanna,
prameyādvyāvṛttatve sādhye sadvyāvṛttitvasyopādhitvāt|
tadabhāve satpratipakṣatvācca| na cāsya pakṣetaratā,
prameyādvyāvṛttatvarahitatvātprameyatvādervyāvṛttatvāt|
etenāstitvaṃ vyāvṛttaṃ jātitvādgotvavadityastitvābhāvasya
prasiddhatvāt jātyādayo .astitvarahitāḥ ityasya vyatirekiṇo
nāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇateti pratyuktam|

athāstitvaṃ jātirdharmāntaraṃ vā| jātiriti brūmaḥ| jātyādau
jāterasambhava iti cet---na, bādhakābhāvāt| jātau jātisvīkāre
.anavasthā bādhakamiti cet---na, dravyatvādāvastitvāṅgīkāre
.anavasthāyā nirūpayitumaśakyatvāt| astitve .apyastitvāntaraṃ
tatrāpyastitvāntaramityanavastheti cet---na, astitve
astitvānaṅgīkārānmūlakṣaterabhāvenaivaṃvidhānavasthāyā
dūṣaṇatvānaṅgīkārācca| samavāye jātisvīkāre
sambandhābhāvo bādhaka iti cet---na, samavāyābhāvayoḥ
viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvasambandhāṅgīkārāt| samavāye
jātisvīkāre samavāyābhāvo bādhaka iti cet---na, samavāye .api
samavāyasambhavāt| samavāyāntarasvīkāre tatrāpi
samavāyāntaramityanavastheti cet---na, tasyaiva samavāyasya
tatrāṅgīkārāt| ātmāśrayaḥ syāditi cet---astu,
utpattijñaptipratibandhakatvābhāva ātmāśrayasya
dūṣaṇatvānaṅgīkārāt| samavāyasya samavāyāśrayatvena
samavāyabhinnatvaprasaṅgo ghaṭavaditi cet---na,
viparyayāparyavasāyitvenāsya tarkābhāsatvāt| samavāyaḥ
samavāyānāśrayaḥ samavāyānāśritaśca
samavāyabhinnatvarahitatvādityādeḥ
sapakṣavṛttitvāniścayenānvayitvānupapatteḥ|
samavāyānāśritatvādabhāvātsamavāyānāśritācca ghaṭādeḥ
sapakṣādapi vyāvṛttatvena vyatirekitvānupapatteśca| astu vā
samavāye samavāyāntaram, tathāpi nānavasthā, dvitīye samavāye
prathamasamavāyasyaiva svīkārāt| na caivaṃ
parasparāśrayadoṣaḥ, utpattijñaptipratibandhakatvābhāve
parasparāśrayasyāpi doṣatvānaṅgīkārāt| yadyatrāśritaṃ na
tattasyāśrayo yathā paṭastantūnām| prathamasamavāyāśritaśca
dvitīyaḥ samavāyaḥ| tasmānna prathamasamavāyāśrita iti
cet---na, yattatśabdārthasyānugatasya vaktumaśakyatvāt|
prameyatvādau vyabhicārācca| piṇḍāśritapiṇḍāśraya
viṣāṇādau vyabhicārācca| yatprathamasamavāyāśritaṃ na
tatprathamasamavāyāśrayo yathā prathamasamavāyāśrito bhāvaḥ|
prathamasamavāyāśritaśca dvitīyaḥ samavāyaḥ tasmānna
prathamasamavāyāśraya iti cet---na, dvitīyasamavāyaḥ
prathamasamavāyāśrayaḥ prathamasamavāyānyabhāvatvāt
ghaṭavadityādinā satpratipakṣatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
samavāyasyāsiddhatvena prathamahetorāśrayāsiddhatvācca|
prathamasamavāyāśritābhāve .api prathamasamavāyāṅgīkārāt
dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatvācca| bhāvavadabhāve .api
samavāyasādhakapramāṇasya tulyatvāt, bādhakābhāvācca| astu
vā dvitīyasamavāye .api samavāyāntaram| tathā ca nānavasthā,
tṛtīyasamavāye prathamasamavāyasyaivāṅgīkārāt| na caivaṃ
cakrakadoṣaḥ, utpattijñaptipratibandhakatvābhāve
cakrakasyādūṣaṇatvāt| evaṃ caturthapañcamasamavāyābhyupagame
.api nānavasthā| astu vānavasthā, tathāpi nāsau doṣaḥ,
utpattijñaptipratibandhakatvābhāve .anavasthāyā
apyadūṣaṇatvāt| samavāyānantyaprasaṅga iti cet---na,
iṣṭatvāditi|

viśeṣeṣu sattāṅgīkāre svarūpahāniprasaṅga iti cet---na,
sattāyāḥ svarūpahānyā saha vyāptiviraheṇa prasaṅgasya
praśithilamūlatvāt| viśeṣeṣu sattāṅgīkāre
viśeṣalakṣaṇahāniḥ| sattārahitatve satyekadravyasamavetatvasya
viśeṣalakṣaṇatvāditi cet---na,
antyaviśeṣalakṣaṇatvānupapattāvapi guṇakarmarahitatve
satyekamātrasamavetasya viśeṣalakṣaṇatvopapatteḥ|
viśeṣa[ṇa]tvajātervā viśeṣalakṣaṇatvāt, vakṣyāmaśca
viśeṣalakṣaṇāntarāṇīti na kiṃcidetaditi|

abhāve sattāṅgīkāre .astītipratyayaviṣayatvam, na tu ghaṭo
.astīti pratyayaviṣayatvasya svīkārāt| ghaṭo nāstītyasyāpi
ghaṭābhāvāstitvagocaratvāt| ghaṭo netyetāvatā
ghaṭābhāvapratipādanāt| ghaṭa ityanena pratiyogino
ghaṭasyābhidhānāt| nañā cābhāvābhidhānādastīti ca
sattābhidhānāditi| abhāve sattāṅgīkāre navamādhyāye
sūtrakārasyāsattvāditi
vyavahāro viruddha iti cet---na,
abhāvatvābhiprāyeṇāsaditi vyavahāropapatteḥ| sattaiva
bhāvatvam, sattābhāvo .abhāvatvam| tatkathamabhāve
sattāṅgīkāre ghaṭata iti cet---na, sattāyā bhāvatvāṅgīkāre
dravyādiṣaṭkalakṣaṇānyatamatvasyaiva bhāvatvāt|
dravyādiṣaṭkalakṣaṇātyantābhāvasya cābhāvatvāditi|

jātiṣu sattāṅgīkāre

sāmānyaviśeṣeṣu sāmānyaviśeṣābhāvāttata eva jñānam|
iti sūtravirodha
iti cet---na, sāmānyaviśeṣābhāvādityanena
sāmānyaviśeṣamavāntaraṃ sāmānyaṃ niṣedhatā sattāyā
aniṣedhāt| anyathā sāmānyābhāvādityetāvataiva
sāmānyaniṣedhe siddhe
sūtragataviśeṣapadavaiyarthyāpatteriti|

tatsiddhaṃ sattājātirevāstitvaṃ sakalapadārthasādharmyamiti|
yaduktaṃ praśastapādabhāṣyakāraiḥ---sa cānuvṛttereva
hetutvātsāmānyameveti|

udayanastu
yadi sattā sāmānyādibhyo vyāvarteta tadā sāmānyādibhyo
vyāvṛttabuddherapi hetutvāt dravyatvādivatsāmānyaviśeṣa eva
syāt| na tu sāmānyamevetyanuvṛttereva
hetutvātsāmānyamevetyuktaṃ bhāṣyakṛteti
varṇayāṃbabhūva| tadayuktam, jātyādibhyo vyāvṛttāyāḥ
sattāyā jātyādibhyaḥ svāśrayavyāvartakatve
guṇādivyāvṛttasyāpi dravyāderguṇādibhyaḥ
svāśrayābhāvavyāvartakatvābhāvaprasaṅgāt|

atha dravyatvādikaṃ viśeṣeṇa svāśraye
svātyantābhāvavyāvṛttavatpratyayaṃ janayatīti
dravyatvādervyāvartakatvamiti cet---na, astitve .api
samānatvāditi|

anityatvaṃ vināśapratiyogitvaṃ
kāryadravyaguṇakarmaprāgabhāvaniṣṭho dharmaviśeṣaḥ|

dravya[va]ttvaṃ dravyāśritatvamṃ
kāryadravyaguṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyadravyapravṛtti
(?) sāmānyābhāvaniṣṭho dharmaviśeṣaḥ|

kāryatvaṃ
prāgabhāvapratiyogitvamanityatvamanityadravyānityaguṇakarmapradhvaṃsaniṣṭho
dharmaviśeṣaḥ|

kāraṇatvaṃ janakatvaṃ sakalapadārthaniṣṭho dharmaviśeṣaḥ|

nanu kimidaṃ janakatvam| na tāvatpūrvavṛttitvam,
ciravinaṣṭānāmapīdānīṃ
jāyamānaghaṭādikāraṇatvaprasaṅgāt|
nāpyavyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavṛttitvaṃ
ghaṭādyavyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavṛttīnāṃ rāsabhādīnāmapi
ghaṭādikāraṇatvaprasaṅgāt| nāpi
niyamenāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavṛttitvam, niyamena
tadghaṭapūrvakṣaṇavṛttīnāṃ
rāsabhādīnāmetadghaṭajanakatvaprasaṅgāt| nāpi
niyamenaitajjātīyāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavṛttijātīyatvam,
jātirahite pradhvaṃsādau jātirahitānāṃ prāgabhāvānāṃ
ghaṭādāvakāraṇatvaprasaṅgāt| śabda[bde]
gaganasyāpyakāraṇatvaprasaṅgāt| gaganasya nityatvena
pūrvakṣaṇavṛttijātīyatvābhāvāt|
niyamenāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavṛttijātīyatvasya ca kāraṇatvena
gaganādīnāṃ sarvakāryajanakatvaprasaṅgācca|
nāpyanvayavyatirekatvaṃ gaganādīnāṃ
śabdādāvakāraṇatvaprasaṅgāt| nityatvena kālato
vyatirekābhāvāt| vibhutvena deśato vyatirekābhāvācca|
tanturūpādīnāṃ ghaṭādijanakatvaprasaṅgācca|

kiṃ ca kāraṇatve pramāṇaṃ na tāvadindriyam,
prathamākṣasannipātena tadapratipatteḥ|
nāpyanvayavyatirekasahitam,
tadanusandhānarahitānāmanvayavyatirekasattvenāpi
kāraṇatvāpratīteḥ|
nāpyanvayavyatirekānusandhānasahitam,
anvayavyatirekānusandhānasahitendriyasannipāte .api tanturūpādau
gha[pa]ṭādikāraṇatvāpratīteḥ|
anvayavyatirekatadanusandhānavirahe .api gaganādiṣu
kāraṇatvapratīteśca| anvayavyatirekānusandhānādeva
kāraṇatvapratītyupapatterindriyasyākāraṇatvaprasaṅgācca| nāpi
liṅgam, tadabhāvāt| anvayavyatirekau liṅgāviti cet---na, tayoḥ
paṭājanakatanturūpādigatatvenānaikāntikatvāt|
ananyathāsiddhāvanvayavyatirekau liṅgamiti cet---na,
ananyathāsiddhatvaṃ kiṃ kāraṇaniṣṭhatvam, kiṃ vā
kāraṇatvārahita[a]niṣṭhatvam| nādyaḥ, tanturūpādīnāṃ
paṭakāraṇakāraṇatvādirahitatvena

tadanvayavyatirekayoḥ
kāraṇakāraṇādyaniṣṭhatvenānaikāntikatvasya tadavasthatvāt|
na dvitīyaḥ, asiddhatvāt, tantvādīnāṃ paṭakāraṇatvasiddheḥ
pūrvaṃ tantvādigatānvayavyatirekayoḥ
paṭakāraṇatvarahitāniṣṭatvāsiddheḥ| nāpi pramāṇāntaram,
anirūpaṇāt, tasmādaśakyavivecanaṃ kāraṇatvaṃ
niṣpramāṇakaṃ ceti|

atrocyate---kiṃ kāraṇatvamaśakyavivecanam, kiṃ vā tadviśeṣo
ghaṭādikāraṇatvam| nādyaḥ, janakatvasyaiva kāraṇatvāt|
janakatvameva kimiti cet---janakatvameva| janakatve pṛṣṭe
janakatvamevetyuttaramanupapannamiti cet---na, asyaivopapannatvāt|
arthāntarābhidhāne bādhāt, arthāntarāpatteśca| na hi kiṃ
paṭatvamiti pṛṣṭe paṭatvamityuttaraṃ bādhādarthāntaratve
atikrāmatīti sambhavati| kāraṇatvasya kiṃ lakṣaṇamiti
cet---[a]kāraṇatvātyantābhāvaḥ| kathamayaṃ kāraṇatvasya
lakṣaṇamiti cet---kāraṇatvavyatiriktāniṣṭhatve sati
kāraṇatvaniṣṭhatvāt| kāraṇatvasya kimāśraya iti
cet---dravyādikameva, kiyaditi cet---sarvameva| kāraṇatve kiṃ
pramāṇamiti cet---idaṃ kāraṇamidaṃ kāraṇamiti pratyakṣameva,
yathedaṃ sadidaṃ saditi pratyakṣaṃ sattāyāḥ pramāṇaṃ
tathedaṃ kāraṇamidaṃ kāraṇamiti pratyakṣaṃ kāraṇatve
pramāṇam| abādhitānubhavajanakatvasyobhayatrāviśeṣāt|
abādhitānubhava eva kathaṃ siddha iti cet---mānasapratyakṣatvāt|
anubhavabādhitatvātyantābhāvayoḥ mānasāvaseyatvāt| idaṃ
kāraṇamidaṃ kāraṇamityavisaṃvādivyavahārācca| gaganādau
kāraṇatve kiṃ pramāṇamiti cet---prameyatvādikameva| nāpi
dvitīyaḥ, ghaṭajanakatvasya ghaṭakāraṇatvātpaṭajanakasyaiva
paṭakāraṇatvāt| ākṣepāntarāṇāṃ ca pūrvavadeva
parihārāditi|

kāraṇalakṣaṇaṃ kimiti cet---kāraṇalakṣaṇaṃ kiṃ
kāraṇadharmo vivakṣitaḥ, kāraṇāvyāvṛtto vā,
kāraṇaśabdapravṛttinimittaṃ vā,
akāraṇāvṛttikāraṇāvyāvṛtto dharmo vā| prathame
dravyatvādikameva| dvitīye kāraṇatvāstitvaprameyatvādikameva|
tṛtīye kāraṇatvameva| caturthe pramāṇānupapatteḥ|
akāraṇāpramitau tadapravṛtteḥ dharmasyāpramitatvena
praṣṭumaśakyatvāt| śakyatve vā na kiṃcidityevottaram|
akāraṇavṛtterdharmasyāsmābhiranaṅgīkārāt|
athātyantamuktireva kāraṇatvam,
prasiddhādanantarabhāvikāryasyāsambhavena tasyāḥ
kāraṇatvānupapattiḥ| tadanantaraṃ ca kāryānupapattiḥ|
sarvotpattimannimittādṛṣṭaprayojanayorabhāvāditi
manyase---tanna, atyantamuktyanantaramapi
paramāṇukarmatajjanyasaṃyogāṅgīkārāt| tasya

ca bhogatatsādhanetaratvenādṛṣṭājanyatvāt,
prayojanarahitatvācca| antya[tyanta]mukteḥ kāraṇatve kiṃ
pramāṇamiti cet---antya[tyanta]muktiḥ kāraṇaṃ meyatvāt,
ghaṭavadityādi| na cātropādhisambhavaḥ|
vyāvartyavipakṣābhāvena yasya
kasyacidupādhitvenābhidhīyamānasya pakṣetaratvāt|
vipakṣābhāvaśca kevalānvayisādhyābhāvasyāprasiddhatvena
tadvato vipakṣasyāsambhavāt| [na cātra pratipakṣasambhavaḥ,
kevalānvayisādhyābhāvasyāprasiddhatvena tadvato
vipakṣasyāsambhavāt|] na cātra pratipakṣasambhavaḥ,
kevalānvayisādhyābhāvasyāprasiddhatvena tadabhāvavyāpyasya
kvacidapyavagantumaśakyatvāt| atyantamukterakāraṇatvapakṣe
.atyantamuktyanyatvasyaiva kāraṇalakṣaṇatvāditi|

atha kiṃ samavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇam---kāryāśritatvameva|
samavāyikāraṇaviśeṣalakṣaṇāni tu yathā saṃ[bhavaṃ]
draṣṭavyāni| tathā hi ghaṭāsaṃyuktatve sati kāraṇatvamiti
cet---na, rūpādisamavāyikāraṇaghaṭādāvativyāpakatvāt|
ghaṭādīnāmapi ghaṭarūpādikāraṇaghaṭāvayavasamavetatvena
tadgataghaṭāśrayatvaṃ ghaṭasamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇaṃ
paṭāsaṃyuktatve sati paṭāśrayatvaṃ ca
paṭasamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇaṃ ghaṭarūpādyāśrayatvaṃ ca
ghaṭarūpādisamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇam| yadvā, ghaṭa
ṭarūpasamavāyikāraṇakāraṇalakṣaṇamityādi|

atha kiṃ[kima]samavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇam| [na] na
samavāyikāraṇasamavetatvena kāraṇatve sati
pratyāsannatvāt[tvam]
sukhaduḥkhādijanakadharmādāvativyāpakatvācca|
dharmādharmādīnāṃ sukhādijanakatve sati
tatkāraṇātmasamavetatvāt,
kāraṇaikārthasamavāyavatkāryaikārthasamavāyasya
pratyāsattitvāt| paramāṇuparimāṇādāvativyāpakatvācca|
teṣāma[pi] viśiṣṭajñānādijanakatvena janakatvāt|
tryaṇukādijanakadvyaṇukakāraṇasamavetatvena pratyāsannatvācca|
sāmānyaviśeṣayorativyāpakatvācca|
tayorapyanuvṛttavyāvṛttatvabuddhijanakatvāt|
saṃyogādikāryaikārthasamavetatvācca|
ātmaviśeṣaguṇamātrajanakatvarahitatvena viśeṣaṇe cātmamanaḥsaṃyogo[ge] .avyāpakatvaprasaṅgāt|
atha yadyatpratyāsannatve sati yasya kāraṇatvaṃ tattasyāsamavāyikāraṇamiti cet---na, yattacchabdayoranugatārthaparatve lakṣaṇātivyāpakatvasya tadavasthatvāt|
tadviśeṣaparatve ca lakṣaṇasyāvyāpakatvāditi pratyāsattiśabdārthayoḥ kāraṇaikārthasamavāyakāryaikārthasamavāyayoranugatatvena lakṣaṇasyāvyāpakatvācca|
tasmādanupapannamasamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇamiti---tanna,
dravyatvarahitātma[guṇa]viśeṣaguṇatvarahitadhvaṃsādyātmaviśeṣaguṇamātrajanakatvarahitabhāvasyāsamavāyikāraṇasāmānyalakṣaṇatvāt|
na caivaṃ pūrvoktadoṣaḥ, ghaṭādīnāṃ dravyatvarahitagrahaṇena
vyudāsāt| dharmādharmādīnāṃ
cātmaviśeṣaguṇatvarahitagrahaṇena nirākaraṇāt|
dhvaṃsādyanyātmaguṇaviśeṣaguṇamātrajanakānāmanekasaṃkhyānekapṛthaktvaparimāṇasaṃyogavibhāgaparatvāparatvāntyāvayavirūparasagandhasparśasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyānāṃ
dhvaṃsādyātmaviśeṣaguṇamātrajanakatvarahitagrahaṇena
nirākaraṇāt| dhvaṃsānyātmaviśeṣaguṇavyatiriktajanakānāṃ
rūparasagandhasparśasaṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgagurutvadravatvasnehasaṃskārakarmaṇāṃ
samavāyikāraṇānāṃ saṃgrahācceti|
prāgabhāvapratibandhakābhāvāntyamuktānāṃ ca bhāvagrahaṇena

nirāsāt| na caivamātmamanaḥsaṃyogāvyāptiḥ, tasya dhvaṃsātmaviśeṣaguṇavyatiriktamanaḥkriyāhetutvāt|
manaḥkarmaṇyasamavāyikāraṇāntarābhāvāditi|
dravyatvarahitākāśā[di]dravyānārambhakaniṣṭhasnehadravyatvavyatiriktaviśeṣaguṇatvarahitadhvaṃsādyajñānamātrajanakatvarahitabhāvatvasya
samavāyikāraṇasāmānyalakṣaṇatvāt|
ākāśādidravyānārambhakaniṣṭhasnehadravyatvavyatiriktaguṇaviśeṣaguṇatvarahitagrahaṇenātmāvayaviviśeṣaguṇānāṃ
nirāsāt| api ca kimasamavāyikāraṇābhipreteṣu
taditaravyāvṛttamidamasamavāyikāraṇamidamasamavāyikāraṇamityanuvṛtto
vyavahāro 'asti na vā| asti cet---tarhi
tatsiddhamasamavāyikāraṇa[tvama]samavāyilakṣaṇaṃ bhaviṣyati|
nāsti
cet---kimasamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇenānugatavyavahārābhāvena
tatsiddhyarthaṃ lakṣaṇānapekṣaṇāt| anugatajāterabhāvena
tatsiddhyarthamasamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇānapekṣaṇāt|
itarabhedapratyayārthasamavāyikāraṇa[lakṣaṇa?]miti cet---na,
tattadvyaktilakṣaṇenaiva tasyā vyakteritarabhedapratyayopapatteḥ|
asamavāyikāraṇābhipretasarvavyaktīnāṃ
yugapaditarabhedapratyayārthamasamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇamiti
cet---na,
yugapadasamavāyikāraṇābhipretasakalavyaktogocaretaravyāvṛttapratyayasya
pramāṇaprayojanaviruddhatvenāṅgīkārāt|
idamatrāsamavāyikāraṇamidamatrāsamavāyikāraṇamiti
asamavāyikāraṇaviśeṣavyavahārasya
cāsamavāyikāraṇaviśeṣalakṣaṇādevopapatteḥ|
viśeṣaguṇalakṣaṇābhāvāttadadhīna[ma]samavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇamayuktamiti
cet---na,
rūpatvarasatvagandhatvasparśatvarahitapṛthivīniṣṭhaniṣṭhatvādhikaraṇaguṇaniṣṭhajātimattvasya
viśeṣaguṇalakṣaṇatvāt| na cedaṃ
sāṃsiddhikadravatvāvyāpakaṃ tasyāpi
rūpatvarasatvagandhatvasparśatvapṛthivīniṣṭhanaimittikadravatvādiniṣṭharahitaguṇatvāvāntaraśabdābhilapajātimattvāt|
nāpi taijasadravya[va]tve .ativyāpakam, tasya
rūpa[tva]rasatvagandhatvasparśatvarahitapṛthivīniṣṭhanaimittikadravatvādiniṣṭhatvarahitaguṇatvāvāntarajātirahitatvāt
naimittikadravatvaśabdābhilapyajāteḥ pṛthivīvṛttivṛttitve
satyapṛthivīvṛttivṛttitve
satyapṛthivīvṛttivṛttirahitaguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvasya vā
viśeṣaguṇalakṣaṇatvāt| na caivaṃ rūpādāvavyāptiriti
vācyam, rūpatvādīnāṃ pṛthivīvṛttivṛttitve
satyapṛthivīvṛttivṛttitve .api tadavāntarāṇāṃ
nīlatvapītatvādīnāṃ pṛthivīvṛttivṛttitve
satyapṛthivīvṛttitvarahitānāṃ pṛthivyādirūpādau
vidyamānatvāt nīlatvādīnāṃ pṛthivīvṛttivṛttitve
satyapṛthivīvṛttivṛttitvavirahāt| udakatejasoḥ
nīlapītādirūpavirahāt| pṛthivīśuklarūpe .api
udakatejorūpavyāvṛttiśuklatvāvāntarajātisvīkārāt|
pṛthivīrase ca
jalarasavyāvṛttavyaktamadhuratvakaṭutvādijātīnāṃ svīkārāt|
pṛthivīsparśe ca
jalādisparśavyāvṛttasparśāvāntarapākajasparśatvādijātisvīkārāt|
udakarūpe ca
pṛthivyādirūpavyāvṛttaśuklatvāvāntarasvacchatvasvīkārāt|
udakarase ca
pṛthivīrasavyāvṛttamadhuratvāvāntarāvyaktamadhuratvāṅgīkārāt|
tejorūpe ca
jalādirūpavyāvṛttaśuklatvāvāntarabhāsvaratvāṅgīkārāt|
nipratipannaṃ[nna] pṛthivīsparśādau
pṛthivīvṛttivṛttitve
satyapṛthivīvṛttivṛttitvarahitajātīnāṃ
sparśatvādinānumānāt| na ca paramamahattve .ativyāptiḥ| tatra
mahattvāvāntaraparamamahattvābhāvāt| bhāve vā tadatiriktatvena
viśeṣaṇāditi| asamavāyikāraṇaviśeṣalakṣaṇāni tu
ghaṭajanakaghaṭāvayavaghaṭasaṃyogatvaṃ
ghaṭāsamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇam| ghaṭarūpajanakarūpatvaṃ
ghaṭāvayavarūpatvaṃ vā ghaṭarūpāsamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇam|
ghaṭarasajanakarasatvādikaṃ ca
ghaṭarasādyasamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇam| ekajanakatvaikatvaṃ
caikatvāsamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇamekatvajātireva|
dvi[tvā]dyasamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇam|
ekapṛthaktvajanakaikapṛthaktvaṃ
caikapṛthaktvāsamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇam| ekapṛthaktvajātireva
dvipṛthaktvādyasamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇam|
paratvajanakasaṃyogatvaṃ ca paratvāsamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇa
ṣṭavadātmaparatvādhārasaṃyogādāvativyāpaka
ratvājanakatvāt| janakatve vā tasyāpyasamavāyikāraṇatvāt|
aparatvajanakasaṃyogatvaṃ cāparatvāsamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇam,
jñānajanakātmamanaḥsaṃyogatva
nādyasamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇam| śabdajanakatve
satyākāśaniṣṭhatvaṃ śabdāsamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇam|
karmāsamavāyikāraṇatvaṃ ca karmāsamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇam|
karmāsamavāyikāraṇatvaṃ cedaṃ karmāsamavāyikarāṇamidaṃ
karmāsamavāyikāraṇamityanugatavyavahārasiddham|
anugatavyavahārābhāve ca lakṣaṇasyaivānaṅgīkārāt| evaṃ
yatra yatra lakṣaṇaviśeṣāniruktiḥ, anugataśca vyavahāro .asti,
tatra tatrānugatavyavahārasiddho dharma eva lakṣaṇamiti
samarthanīyamiti|

nimittakāraṇasya kiṃ lakṣaṇamiti cet---kāraṇatvameva|
samavāyikāraṇatvāsamavāyikāraṇatvayorapi jñānādyutpattau
nimittakāraṇatvāt| nimittakāraṇaviśeṣalakṣaṇaṃ tu
ghaṭasamavāyikāraṇaghaṭāsamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇarahitatve sati
ghaṭakāraṇatvamityādikamityalaṃ vistareṇeti|

sāmānyavattvamanuvṛttāśrayatvaṃ viśeṣavattvaṃ
sāmānyaviśeṣavattvamanuvṛttavyāvṛttāśrayatve| etacca trayaṃ
sarvapadārthasādharmyam, anuvṛttavyāvṛttadharmāṇāṃ sarvatra
vidyamānatvāt| prameyatvaṃ pramāviṣayatvam| jñeyatvaṃ
jñānaviṣayatvam| abhidheyatvamabhidhānaviṣayatvamiti yāvat|
etāni ca sakalapadārthasādharmyā[ṇi]| prameyatvādīnāṃ
svasmādeva vyāvṛttatvāt, na sakalapadārthadharmatvamiti cet---na,
prameyatvādīnāṃ prameyatvādyāśrayatvāt| prameyatvaṃ
prameyatvāśrayo .abhidheyatvāt| abhidheyatvamabhidheyatvāśrayaḥ,
prameyatvāt, ghaṭavadityādipramāṇasattvāt|
ātmāśrayasyotpattijñaptipratibandhakatvābhāvenādūṣaṇatvāt|
nanvanityatvakāryatvadravyāśritatvādīni na dravyāṇi,
guṇarahitatvāt| guṇāśritatvātkarmāśritatvācca| nāpi guṇaḥ
karma vā, guṇāśritatvācca| nāpi jātayaḥ, abhāvāśritatvāt,
dravyatvena jātisaṃkaraprasaṅgācca|
dravyatvasyānityatvakāryatvadravyāśritatvaparihāreṇa gaganādau
vartamānatvāt| anityatvakāryatvadravyāśritatvādīnāṃ ca
dravyatvaparihāreṇa karmādau vidyamānatvāt| guṇatvena
jātisaṃkaraprasaṅgācca| sāmānyavattvādīnāṃ ca sattayā
tulyavyaktidoṣagrastatvāt| na samavāyaḥ, abhāve .api
vidyamānatvāt, asambandhatvācca| na viśeṣaḥ, anekāśritatvāt|
nābhāvaḥ, pratiyogyanirūpyatvāt| nāpi padārthāntaram,
anaṅgīkārāditi|

ucyate| jātaya evaitāḥ, abhāvāśritatvasya ca sattāyāṃ
vyabhicārāt| jātisaṃkarasya cādūṣaṇatvāt| dūṣaṇatve vā
dravyatvasyāpyucchedāt| viśeṣagrahaṇābhāvāt| ye
parasparaparihāravattve sati samānādhikaraṇe na te jātī| yathā
bhūtatvendriyatve| parasparaparihāravattve sati samānādhikaraṇe
dravyatvānityatve| tasmānna jātīyamiti [jātī ta iti] cet---na,
dravyatvāṃśe bādhāt|

athānityatvādayo na jātayaḥ dravyatvasamānādhikaraṇatve sati
dravyatvābhāvatvaniṣṭhatvāt, rūpābhāvavaditi cet---na,
sattāyāṃ vyabhicārāt, sattānyatvena hetuviśeṣaṇatve ca
bādhāt| nityatve satyanekāśritabhāvatvasya nityatve
satyanekasamavetatvasya vā jātitvasya prakṛte pramāṇasiddhatvāt|
tulyavyaktitvasyāpi jātidūṣaṇatvānaṅgīkārāt| santu vā
nityatvadravyāśrita[tvā]dayaḥ padārthāntarāṇi,
dravyādilakṣaṇarahitatvāt|

na ca ṣaḍeva bhāvā iti niyamavyākopaḥ saptamabhāvasyāpi
pramitatvena ṣaḍeva bhāvā iti niyamasyānaṅgīkārāt| na caivaṃ
padārthānāmānantyam| prameyatvādīnāmanantatve .api
dharmatvenaiva kiṃ dharmapadārthasya lakṣaṇam|
dravyādiṣaṭkalakṣaṇarahitatve sati bhāvatvameva, santu vā
nityatvadravyāśraya[tva]ādayo bhāvaviśeṣā eva|
athānityatvadravyāśritatvādayo bhāvāḥ pratiyogyanirūpyatvāt,
ghaṭavaditi cet---na, bhāvatvaṃ kiṃ sattā, kiṃ vā
ṣallakṣaṇānyatamatvam, kiṃ vā bhāvo .ayaṃ bhāvo
.ayamityanugatapratītisiddho dharmaviśeṣaḥ,
pratiyogyanirūpyatvaṃ vā, niṣpratiyogitvaṃ vā| prathame
siddhasādhanam, abhāve sattāṅgīkārāt| dvitīye bādhaḥ|
tṛtīye .aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam, bhāvo .ayaṃ bhāvo
.ayamityanugatamaterasiddhatvena dharmaviśeṣaṇasyāpyasiddheḥ,
siddhatve .api hetorasiddhatvāt| caturthe sādhyaviśiṣṭatvam,
apakṣadharmatvaṃ bādhaśca prameyatvādinirūpakendriyādīnāṃ
svabhāvapratiyogitvena prameyatvādīnāṃ pratiyoginirūpyatvāt|
dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatvaṃ ca| ghaṭādīnāmapi
svabhāvapratiyogīndriyānirūpyatvāt| api ca prameyatvaṃ nāma
pramāviṣayatvam| tacca pramānirūpyaṃ ghaṭābhāva iva
ghaṭanirūpyaṃ tadavacchedena pratīteḥ| evaṃ jñeyatvaṃ
jñānanirūpyam| abhidheyatvamabhidhānaviṣayatvam| kāryatvaṃ
kāryanirūpyam| anityatvamanityanirūpyam| dravyāśritatvaṃ
dravyāśritanirūpyamiti| prameyatvādīnāṃ
pratiyogyanirūpyatvamasiddhamiti| pañcame .api hetorasiddhatvaṃ
bādhaśca, prameyatvādīnāṃ
pramādipratiyogikatvasyānubhavasiddhatvātprameyatvādīnāṃ kiṃ
bhāvatve pramāṇamiti cet---na, abhāvatvaṃ yadi
bhāvalakṣaṇakarmatvābhāvaḥ abhāvaśabdavācyatvaṃ vā tadā
dravyādiṣallakṣaṇātyantābhāvavattvameva tatra pramāṇam, atha
dravyādiṣallakṣaṇātyantābhāvavattvam, tadā tasya
ghaṭābhāvādau pratyakṣatve prakṛte .api pratyakṣameva|
anumeyatve .anumānameveti satoṣṭavyam| atha prameyatvaṃ
kasyābhāvaḥ| pramāyā eva| atha prameyatvaṃ pramāyā na
prāgabhāvaḥ anāśitvāt, na pradhvaṃsaḥ utpattirahitatvāt,
nātyantābhāvaḥ pramāsamānādhikaraṇatvāt,
nānyonyābhāvaḥ, pramāyāmapi vidyamānatvāditi manyase tanna,
prameyatvasya pramāyāmabhāvatvāṅgīkārāt|

anye tu,
prameyatvaṃ pramātyantābhāvaḥ, pramāniṣṭhatve sati
pramābhāvatvasya pramātyantābhāvalakṣaṇatvādityāhuḥ| tanna,
asya pramālakṣaṇasya pramānyonyābhāvagatatvenātivyāpakatvāt|
anyonyābhāvalakṣaṇaṃ hi tattvena lakṣaṇaṃ viśeṣaṇīyamiti
cet---na, tasyaiva vaktumaśakyatvāt| śakyatve vā prameyatvasya
pramātyantābhāva evāntarbhāvānaṅgīkārāditi|
evamabhidheyatvādayo .api nirvaktavyāḥ|

yadvā prameyatvaṃ pramātyantābhāvaḥ| atha
pramātyantābhāvaśabdena kimekaikasyāḥ pramāyā atyantābhāvo
vivakṣitaḥ kiṃ vā sarvapramāṇānāṃ, sarvātyantābhāvaḥ,
sarvapramāpratiyogika eko .atyantābhāvo vā| nādyaḥ,
ekaikapramātyantābhāvasya ekaikapramāśraya evāvidyamānatvena
prameyatvasya kevalānvayitvabhaṅgāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
sarvapramāpratiprayogisarvapramātyantābhāvānāṃ
kvacidapyavidyamānatvena kevalānvayitvabhaṅgāt| na tṛtīyaḥ,
sarvapramāpratiyogikasyaikasyātyantābhāvasyāsambhavātpratiyogibhedasyābhāvabhedatvāditi
manyase---tanna, ekaikapramātyantābhāvasyaiva
prameyatvenāṅgīkārāt| na caivaṃ prameyatvasya
kevalānvayitvabhaṅgaḥ, tattatpramāśraye .api tasyāstasyāḥ
pramāyā atyantābhāvāṅgīkārāt| jñānādīnāmapyavṛttitvāt|
evaṃ sati pramāvyaktīnāmānantyādanantāni prameyatvādīni
syuriti cet---satyametat,
anantapratiyogikānantapramātyantābhāvānāṃ
sarvairapyaṅgīkārāt| astu vā sarvapramāpratiyogika eko
.atyantābhāvaḥ prameyatvam| na cāsya kevalānvayitvānupapattiḥ,
katipayapramāvatsu sarvātmasu
sarvapramāpratiyogikaikātyantābhāvasambhavāt|

evaṃ jñānātyantābhāvo jñeyatvam| śabdātyanābhāvo
.abhidheyatvamiti| yadvā kālātyantābhāvaḥ prameyatvam|
digatyantābhāvo jñeyatvam| ākāśātyantābhāvo
.abhidheyatvamiti| yadvā pramātyantābhāvavattvaṃ prameyatvam|
jñānātyantābhāvavattvaṃ jñeyatvam|
śabdātyantābhāvavattvamabhidheyatvamiti|

nanu pramātyantābhāvavattvaṃ kiṃ pramāviśeṣātyantābhāvaḥ,
pramāviśeṣātyantābhāvāśrayatvaṃ vā,
sarvapramāpratiyogikāḥ sarve .atyantābhāvo vā, tadvattvaṃ vā,
sarvapramāpratiyogika ekaikātyantābhāvo vā, tadvattvaṃ vā|
nādyaḥ, gatārthatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ, vatyarthavaiyarthyāt| na
tṛtīyaḥ, pūrvavadeva dūṣitatvāt| na caturthaḥ, tata eva
vatyarthavaiyarthyācca| na pañcamaḥ, gatārthatvādeva| na
ṣaṣṭhaḥ, vatyarthavaiyarthyāditi| tanna,
pramāviśeṣātyantābhāvavanniṣṭhasya
pramātyantābhāvavattvasya prameyatvena vivakṣitatvāt| na ca
pramāvatyātmani
pramātyantābhāvāsambhavātpramātyantābhāvavattvaṃ
prameyatvaṃ na ghaṭa iti prameyatvasya
kevalānvayitvasyānupapattiriti vācyam|
devadattaniṣṭhapramāvatyapi
devadattayajñadattaniṣṭhapramātyantābhāvasambhavāt| yathā hi
devadattasya yajñadattādiniṣṭhapramāsambhave .api
devadattaniṣṭhapramāsambhavātpramāvattvam, evaṃ
devadattaniṣṭhapramātyantābhāvāsambhave .api
yajñadattaniṣṭhapramātyantābhāvasambhavāt|
pramātyantābhāvavattvamapi yajñadattaniṣṭhapramāṇaṃ
pramātvena tadatyantābhāvānāmapi pramātyantābhāvavattvāt|
tena pramāvatyapi pramātyantābhāvavattvaṃ prameyatvaṃ durvāram|
na ca vatyarthavaiyarthyam, sarveṣāmapi pramātyantābhāvānāṃ
svapratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatvāsambhavena
kevalānvayitvānupapatteḥ|
asminpakṣe vijñānādīnāmavyāpyavṛttitvānaṅgīkārādyadi na
pramātyantābhāvaḥ kevalānvayī, tarhi tadvattvamapi
kevalānvayitvam| tadvyāvṛttisthala eva tadvattvasyāpi
vyāvṛtteriti cet---na, ekapramātyanābhāvavyāvṛttisthale
tadvattvasyaiva vyāvṛttatve .api
pramātyantābhāvāntarasambhavena
pramātyantābhavavattvasyāvyāvṛttatvāt| ekadhūmābhāvavati
taddhūmavattvasya vyāvṛttatve dhūmāntarasambhavena
dhūmavattvāvyāvṛtteḥ sarvalokasiddhatvāt|
anyathaikadhūmavyāvṛttau parvate dhūmavattvasya vyāvṛttatvena
apakṣadharmatvaprasaṅgāt| mahānase caikadhūmavyāvṛttau
dhūmavattvasya vyāvṛttatvena vyāpyatvābhāvaprasaṅgācca|
pramātyantābhāvānāṃ vyāvṛttatve
pramātyantābhāvavattvamanuvṛttamityatra kiṃ pramāṇamiti
cet---ayaṃ pramātyantābhāvavān, ayaṃ pramātyantābhāvavāniti
pratyakṣameva| atha dhūmavānayaṃ dhūmavānayamiti pratyakṣamiva
dhūmavattvasyānivṛttatve pramāvattvapramātyantābhāvayorekatra
samāveśe pramāvattvatadatyantābhāvayorvirodhocchedaprasaṅga iti
cet---na, pramātyantābhāvavattvasya
pramāvattvātyantābhāvavattvasya
pramāvattvātyantābhāvavattvānaṅgīkārāt|
pramāvattvātyantābhāvasya ca pramāvattvenaikatra
samāveśānaṅgīkārācca| tarhi pramātyantābhāvavattvaṃ
tasyābhāva iti cet---na, pramātyantābhāvasyaiva dhūmavattvamiva
dhūmasya, ghaṭatvamiva ghaṭasyeti|

yadvā pramānyonyābhāvavattvaṃ prameyatvam|
jñānānyonyābhāvavattvaṃ jñeyatvam|
śabdānyonyābhāvavattvamabhidheyatvamiti| na ca
vatyarthavaiyarthyam, pramāviśeṣānyonyābhāvānāṃ
svapratiyogivyāvṛttatvena kevalānvayitvānupapatteḥ|
pramānyonyābhāvavattvasya cāyaṃ pramānyonyābhāvavānayaṃ
pramānyonyābhāvavānityanuvṛttapratyayavattvena
kevalānvayitvopapatteḥ| evaṃ śabdānyonyābhāvatvādāvapi
vācyamiti|

yadvā īśvarapramāviṣayatvaṃ prameyatvam,
īśvarajñānaviṣayatvaṃ jñeyatvam,
īśvarecchāviṣayatvamabhidheyatvamiti| yadā punarastitvaṃ
sattavyatiriktam, tadāstitvaṃ sadatyantābhāvavattvaṃ,
sadanyonyābhāvavattvaṃ vā, saṃyogātyantābhāvo vā,
īśvaraviṣayaprayatnaviṣayaṃ veti nirvaktavyamiti|

yadvā prameyatvaṃ pramābhāvavattvam, jñeyatvaṃ
jñānabhāvavattvam, abhidheyatvaṃ śabdābhāvavattvamiti| na ca
prameyatvādau kevalānvayitvānupapattiḥ|
ātmapramāprāgabhāvapradhvaṃsayoranyatra ca
pramātyantābhāvānyonyābhāvayośca vidyamānatvena
pramābhāvavattvasya sarvatra vidyamānatvāt| evaṃ
jñeyatvādāvapi vācyamiti|

evaṃ
pramāviṣayatvasaṃśayaviṣayatvaviparyayaviṣayatvānadhyavasāyaviṣayatvasmṛtiviṣayatvasākṣātkāraviṣayatvānumitiviṣayatvādayaḥ
sakalapadārthasādharmyāṇi|

dravyaguṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyānāṃ bhāvatvam| nanu
kimidaṃ bhāvatvam---pratiyoginirūpyatvābhāvo vā
ṣaḍanyatamalakṣaṇatvaṃ vā vidhipratyayaviṣayatvaṃ vā
nirvikalpaviṣayatvaṃ vā
pṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgaparatvāparatvasamavāyalakṣaṇarahitatve
sati savikalpakamātraviṣayatvābhāvo [vā]
dravyādiṣallakṣaṇarahitatvābhāvo vā, vibhāgo[bhāvo] .ayamiti
pratyayaviṣayatvaṃ vā dharmāntaraṃ vā| nādyaḥ, dravyādīnāṃ
svabhāvapratiyogīndriyādinirūpyatvena tadrahitatvāpatteḥ| na
dvitīyaḥ, dravyādiṣallakṣaṇātiriktatadanyatamavattvasya
nirvaktumaśakyatvāt| na tṛtīyaḥ, vidhipratyayasyānirūpaṇāt|
pratyayatvāvāntaro vā jātiviśeṣo vibhāgasya
vibhajyamānanirūpyatvena pṛthaktvaparatvāparatvānāṃ
cāvadhinirūpyatvena
pratiyoginirūpyatvābhāvavannirvikalpakaviṣayatvāsambhavāt|
abhāva[atha]vidhipratyaya(viṣay)tvamiti cet---na, tatra
pramāṇābhāvāt|

na caturthaḥ,
saṃyogavibhāgapṛthaktvaparatvāparatvasamavāyeṣvavyāpakatvātsaṃyogasamavāyayoḥ
sambandhinirūpyatvena vibhāgasya vibhajyamānanirūpyatvenābhāve
.ativyāpakatvācca| abhāvo nirvikalpakaviṣayaḥ, prameyatvāt,
ghaṭavadityanumānāt| na cāsya samavāyābhāvānaikāntikatvam,
teṣāmapi pakṣatulyatvāt| atha ghaṭābhāvādīnāṃ
pratiyogighaṭitamūrtitvena
savikalpakaviṣayatvaniyamānnirvikalpakaviṣayatvaṃ bādhitamiti
cet---na, pratiyogighaṭitamūrtitvaṃ kiṃ
pratiyogibhinnatvābhāvaḥ, pratiyogyavacchinnatvaṃ vā? na
prathamaḥ, pratiyogitadabhāvayorbhedasya pratyakṣasiddhatvāt|
dvitīye .api pratiyogyavacchinnatvaṃ kimabhāvadharmaḥ, svarūpaṃ
[vā]| nādyaḥ, tadapratītāvapyabhāvasya
nirvikalpakaviṣayatvasambhavāt| dharmānavacchedenāpi
ghaṭādernirvikalpakaviṣayatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
pratiyogyavacchinnasyābhāvadharmatvena tatsvarūpatve
pramāṇābhāvāt| ghaṭāpratiyogitvasya
sakalaghaṭābhāvaniṣṭhatvena
parasparavyāvṛttaghaṭābhāvasvarūpatvānupapatteśca|

na pañcamaḥ, abhāvasya nirvikalpakāviṣayatvena
savikalpakamātraviṣayatvāsambhavena
tadabhāvasyāsambhavagrastatvāt|
gurutvamahattvadīrghatvābhāvavyāpakatvācca|
gurutvamahattvāderidamasmādguru, idamasmānmahaditi
pratiyoginirūpyatvena
nirvikalpakāviṣayatvātsavikalpakamātraviṣayatvasambhavāt|
teṣāmavadhinirūpyatve .api nirvikalpaviṣayatvamiti cet---na,
pṛthaktvādīnāmapyavadhyādinirūpyāṇāṃ
nirvikalpakaviṣayatvasambhavena
tattadvyāvartakaviśeṣaṇānarthakyaprasaṅgāt|
sukhaśabdādyutkarṣanikarṣādijātāvavyāpakatvācca|
utkarṣanikarṣāderidamasmādutkṛṣṭam, idamasmādutkṛṣṭamiti
pratiyoginirūpyatvena nirvikalpakāviṣayatvāt|
savikalpakamātraviṣayatvāttadabhāvasambhavāt|

nāpi ṣaṣṭhaḥ, dravyādiṣallakṣaṇarahitatvasya sarvatra
tadabhāvasyāsambhavagrastatvāt|
dravyādīnāmekaikalakṣaṇavattve .api ṣallakṣaṇarahitatvāt|

nāpi saptamaḥ, bhāvo .ayamiti biddhiśabdena kiṃ
bhāvabuddhimātraṃ vivakṣitam, bhāvo .ayamiti buddhiviśeṣo vā|
nādyaḥ, parasparāśrayaprasaṅgāt| bhāvena buddhinirūpaṇāt,
tayā ca bhāvanirūpaṇāt| na dvitīyaḥ, avyāpakatvaprasaṅgāt,
atītānāgatabhāvānāmetadviṣayatvāsambhavāt,

asyā
buddheretadviṣayatvāsambhavācca jñānasvaprakāśatvaniṣedhāt|

nāpyaṣṭamaḥ, tatra mānābhāvāditi---tanna,
dravyādiṣallakṣaṇānyatamavattvasyaiva bhāvatvāt| na ca
dravyādiṣallakṣaṇānyatamavattvamaśakyanirūpaṇam,
dravyādiṣallakṣaṇapratiyogikaṣaḍanyonyābhāvavattvātyantābhāvasya
dravyādiṣallakṣaṇānyatamatvāt|
dravyādiṣallakṣaṇapratiyogikaṣaḍatyantābhāvavattvātyantābhāvo
vā bhāvatvalakṣaṇam| na cedaṃ dravyādāvabhāvavyāpakam,
dravyādiṣallakṣaṇapratiyogikaṣaḍatyantābhāvavattvasya
dravyādāvasambhavāt| astu vā bhāvatvaṃ dharmāntaram| na ca
pramāṇābhāvaḥ| bhāvo .ayaṃ bhāvo .ayamiti pratyakṣasyaiva
tatra pramāṇatvāt| na cedaṃ pratyakṣamapyasiddham, bhāvo
.ayamityanugatavyavahārādeva tatsiddheḥ| anumānaṃ vā tatra
pramāṇam| ayaṃ ghaṭa
etatpravṛttidravyāvṛttitvānadhikaraṇaitadvṛttiguṇavyāvṛttitvā[da]nadhikaraṇaitatpravṛttikarmavyāvṛttatvānadhikaraṇaitatpravṛttijātivyāvṛttatvānadhikaraṇaitadvṛttiviśeṣavyāvṛttatvānadhikaraṇaitatpravṛttyabhāvavyāvṛttatvasamavāyavyāvṛttatvānadhikaraṇaitadvṛttyadhikaraṇavat,
meyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi| na cātrāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam,
etadvṛttitve sati
dravyavyāvṛttatvānyatamasyaitadghaṭānyonyābhāvādeḥ paṭādau
prasiddhatvācca| na cātra tadghaṭatvamupādhiḥ, pakṣetaratvāt|
dravyavyāvṛttatvarahitaguṇavyāvṛttatvarahitakarmavyāvṛttatvarahitajātivyāvṛttatvarahitaviśeṣavyāvṛttatvarahitasamavāyavyāvṛttatvarahitābhāvavyāvṛttatvarahitādhikaraṇamityetāvataiva
bhāvatvasiddheḥ vyarthaṃ pratyekametatpravṛttigrahaṇamiti
cet---na, evaṃvidhadharmāprasiddhatvena
aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāpattau
tatparihārārthametatpravṛttipadasyopādānāt|
dravyādivyāvṛttatvābhāvavṛttitvarahitasyāprasiddhatve
.apyetadvṛttitvaviśiṣṭadravyavyāvṛttatvādirahitasyaitadvṛttitvaviśiṣṭābhāvavṛttitvarahitasya
caitadghaṭānyonyābhāvādeḥ prasiddhatvāt
dravyādiṣallakṣaṇānyatamavattvādinārthāntarateti cet---na,
abhāvavyāvṛttadravyādiṣaṭpadārthānugatadharme vipratipannaṃ
pratyanumānopanyāsāt|
dravyādiṣallakṣaṇānyatamatvadravyādiṣallakṣaṇātyantābhāvavattvātyantābhāvavyatiriktatvena
sādhyaviśeṣaṇācca|
abhāvavyāvṛttadravyādiṣaṭpadārthānugatadharmamātracaritārthasya
dravyādiṣallakṣaṇānyatamavattvādāvaidāsīnyācca|

na caivaṃ
dravyādidvitricatuḥpañcapadārthamātravṛttidharmasiddhiprasaṅgaḥ,
iṣṭatvāt| yadvā, ayaṃ ghaṭa
etadvṛttivyāvṛttatvarahitaitadvṛttijātimattva[sya]
vyāvṛttatvarahitaitadvṛttiviśeṣavyāvṛtta[tva]rahitaitadvṛttisamavāyavyāvṛttatvarahitābhāvavṛttitvarahitajātimān,
jātimattvāt, paṭavaditi bhāvatvāparaparyāyajātisādhanam| na
cātrāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam, paṭatvādau sādhya[a]prasiddheḥ|
na ca jātyādau sattāṅgīkāre jātimattvasya vyabhicāraḥ,
teṣāṃ pakṣatulyatvāt| jātitvaviśeṣatvādijātisvīkārāt| na
cābhāve sattāṅgīkāre jātimattvasya tatra vyabhicāraḥ, abhāve
sattvamabhyupagacchatā tatra ghaṭābhāvatvādisvīkārāt|
ghaṭatvapaṭābhāvatvādestulyayogakṣematvāt| na cānenaiva
nyāyena
dravyādidvitricatuḥpañcapadārthamātravṛttipṛthivyādidvitryādimātravṛttirūpādidvitryādimātravṛttijātyādisiddhiprasaṅgaḥ|

asati bādhe tatsiddheriṣṭatvāt| sati bādhe tatraiva
tannivāraṇāditi|

seyaṃ jātirbhāvatvam, tadabhāvaścābhāvatvamiti| dravyādīnāṃ
pañcānāṃ samavāyalakṣaṇarahitatve sati bhāvatvam|
dravyādīnāṃ pañcānāṃ samavāyitvamanekatvaṃ [ca]
sādharmyamiti praśastapādāḥ|
tanna| samavāyitvaṃ kiṃ samavāyaḥ, samavāyāśrayatvaṃ vā|
nādyaḥ, samavāyādāvapi

samavāyavīkārāt| na dvitīyaḥ, tata
eva samavāyasyaiva
sādharmyatvopapatterāśrayatvaviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyācca| anekatvaṃ
ca kiṃ saṃkhyā, svarūpabhedo vā| nādyaḥ, guṇādāvasambhavāt|
na dvitīyaḥ, svarūpabhedasya vyāvṛttatvenānugatatvānupapatteḥ|
dharmatvāsambhavācca|
guṇakarmasāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyābhāvānāṃ
dravyatvarahitatvaguṇatvarahitatvakāryāśrayatvarahitatvādiguṇābhāvasyaiva
dharmatvopapatteḥ vatyarthavaiyarthyamiti cet---na, guṇābhāvasya
dravyeṣvapi
vidyamānatvātsvagatakāryaguṇaprāgabhāvapradhvaṃsaguṇānyonyābhāvānāṃ
sarvatra sambhavāt, dravyāntaravartiguṇānāṃ dravyāntare
.atyantāsambhavācca|

anutpannakriyavinaṣṭadravyavyatiriktamūrtavyatiriktānāṃ
kriyāvattvābhāvaṃ sādharmyam|
nityadravyavyatiriktānāmāśritatvam| paramāṇūnāṃ
ghaṭādivadbhūtalāśritatvamastīti cet---na, pramāṇābhāvāt|
na tāvatparamāṇūnāmāśritatve pratyakṣaṃ
pramāṇamanaṅgīkārāt| nāpyanumānam, liṅgābhāvāt|
paramāṇava āśritāḥ, mūrtatvāt, paṭavaditi cet---na,
nityadravyatvābhāvena sopādhitvāt| paramāṇavo nāśritāḥ,
nityadravyatvāt, gaganavaditi satpratipakṣatvācca| yadvā
āśritatvaṃ sakalapadārthadharmaḥ, gaganādīnāmāśritatvāt,
kālāśritatvādvā| na cātra mānābhāvaḥ, gaganādaya
āśritāḥ, kālāśritā vā prameyatvāt,
kālaguṇavadityanumānāt| na cātra nityadravyatvābhāva upādhiḥ,
sādhyābhāvavato vyāvartyasyābhāvena pakṣetaratvāt| na ca kāla
eva vyabhicāraḥ, kālasyāpi kālāśritatvāt| na
cātmāśrayadoṣaḥ, utpattijñaptipratibandhakatvābhāve
ātmāśrayatvasya dūṣaṇatvābhāvāditi| tanna, kālānāśrite
kālānyonyābhāvakālaguṇātyantābhāvādau vyabhicārāt|
bhāvatvena hetuviśeṣaṇe vyarthaviśeṣyatvāpātāt| bhāvatvameva
heturatreti cet---na, śabdajñānādau vyabhicārāt| te .api
kālāśritā eveti cet---na,
gaganādilakṣaṇānāmabhivyāpakatvaprasaṅgāt|
tasmādgaganādīnāṃ kālāśritatvaṃ niṣpramāṇakam,
āśritatvaṃ ca sapramāṇakamiti siddham| gaganādayaḥ
kutrāśritā iti cet---mūrteṣveva| ata eva gaganādīnāṃ
sarvagatatvamācāryā manyante|

dravyādīnāṃ trayāṇāmeva sattāsambandhaḥ
sāmānyaviśeṣavattvaṃ svasamayārthaśabdābhidheyatvaṃ
sādharmyamityapi na yuktam, sattāsambandhasya sattāyāmapi
vidyamānatvāt, sambandhasyobhayaniṣṭhatvāt, gotvādāvapi
vidyamānatvācca| sattāniṣṭhasamavāyasya tatrāpyaṅgīkārāt|
sattāśrayatvaṃ sambandhaḥ, na tu sattāsamavāya iti cet---na,
tasyāpi jātyādau

svīkārāt, sambandhapadavaiyarthyācca| sattaiva sādharmyamastīti
cet---na, tasyāḥ sakalapadārthadharmatvāt| athāyaṃ ghaṭa
etadvṛttidravyavyāvṛttatvarahitaitadvṛttiguṇavyāvṛttatvarahitaitadvṛttikarmavyāvṛttatvarahitaitadvṛttidravyatvaguṇatvakarmatvarahitajātimān
jātimattvāt, paṭavadityanumānasiddhā jātiḥ sattā| sā ca
dravyādīnāṃ trayāṇāṃ sādharmyamiti manyase---tanna, tasyāḥ
sattvavyavahārāhetutvena sattāśabdavā[cyatvā?]naṅgīkārāt|
sadvyavahārahetutvaṃ ca tadabhāve .api jātyādau
sadvyavahāradarśanāt| anyathā dravyāderapi
sadvyavahārahetutvāpattau sattāśabdavācyatvāpatteḥ|

sāmānyaviśeṣavattvamapi na trayāṇāmeva| jātyādāvapi
jātitvaviśeṣatvādisāmānyaviśeṣasambhavāt|

svasamayārthaśabdābhidheyatvamapi na yuktam,
pūrvopapāditadravyāditrayamātravṛttinimittatvopapatteḥ|
sūtrakāreṇa evaṃvidhasamayasyākṛtatvācca|
sūtrakāraśiṣyaiḥ evaṃvidhaḥ samayaḥ kṛta
iti cet---na, pramāṇābhāvāt| pravṛttinimitta[a]sambhave .api
samayakaraṇasyonmattapralāpatvāt|
dravyāditrayamātravṛttinimittāsambhave .api
 dravyatvaguṇatvakarmatvanimittasambhavenārthaśabdasyākṣādipadavadanekārthopapatteśca|
dharmajanakatvaṃ dharmajanakānāmeva|
adharmajanakatvamapyadharmajanakānāmeva|

kāryatvaṃ kāraṇavatāmeva| anityatvaṃ vināśakāraṇavatāmeva|

sāmānyādīnāmeva buddhilakṣaṇatvaṃ sādharmyamiti
praśastapādāḥ|
tanna, buddhau sāmānyādiniṣṭhatvābhāvena
sāmānyādilakṣaṇatvānupapatteḥ,
ātmaniṣṭhatvenātivyāpakatvācca| buddhiśabdena
buddhiviṣayatvaṃ vivakṣitamiti cet---na, dravyādāvativyāpatvāt|
athānuvṛttabuddhiviṣayatvaṃ sāmānyalakṣaṇam,
vyāvṛttabuddhiviṣayatvaṃ viśeṣalakṣaṇam, ihedamiti
buddhiviṣayatvaṃ samavāyalakṣaṇamiti manyase---tanna,
anugatabuddhiviṣayatvādīnāṃ sāmānyānyaikaikaniṣṭhatvena
sāmānyaviśeṣatvādisamānadharmatvānupapatteḥ, upapattau vā
sāmānyādilakṣaṇatvānupapatteḥ|
athānuvṛttabuddhiviṣayatvavyāvṛttabuddhiviṣayatvenedamiti
buddhiviṣayatvānyatamatvaṃ buddhilakṣaṇatvaṃ
vivakṣitam---tadapi na, tasya dravyādāvapi sambhavāt| ghaṭo
.ayamityādyanuvṛttabuddheḥ ghaṭavyāvṛttādigrāhakatvāt
vyāvṛttaḥ paramāṇurityādeśca paramāṇvādigrāhakatvāt| iha
tantuṣu paṭa ityādibuddheśca tantupaṭādigrāhakatvāditi|
athānuvṛttabuddhiviṣayatvavyāvṛttaviṣayatvena idamiti
buddhiviṣayāvyatiriktalakṣaṇarahitatvaṃ
buddhilakṣaṇatvaśabdena vivakṣitamiti manyase---tanna,
sāmānyādilakṣaṇāntarāṇāṃ vakṣyamāṇatāditi|| 7||

dravyāṇi dravyāntaramārabhanta iti|| 1-1-8||

nanvatra dravyārambhakatvaṃ yadi dravyasamavāyikāraṇatvam, tadā
dravyāṇīti kiṃ sarvadravyāṇi vivakṣitāni, mūrtāni vā,
sparśavanti vā, antyāvayavitvarahitāni sparśavanti vā| nādyaḥ,
ākāśakāladigātmamanasāṃ ghaṭādīnāṃ ca
dravyasamavāyikāraṇatvaprasaṅgāt| na dvitīyaḥ, manaso .api
dravyasamavāyikāraṇatvaprasaṅgāt| na tṛtīyaḥ,
ghaṭādīnāmapi dravyasamavāyikāraṇatvaprasaṅgāt| na
caturthaḥ, antyāvayavitvānirukteḥ, dikkālātmanāṃ
dravyajanakatvaprasāṅgācceti---tanna, yadā dravyārambhakatvaṃ
dravyasamavāyikāraṇatvaṃ tadā
dravyāṇītyanenāntyāvayavitvarahitasparśavatāṃ parigrahāt| na
cāntyāvayavitvāniruktiḥ,
avayavatvarahitāvayavitvasyāntyāvayavitvāt| yadā tu dravya
kāśamanovyatiriktadravyaparigrahāt, mā bhūdvā
dravyāṇītyupāttānāṃ dravyāṇāmupasaṃgrāhakāntaram,
tathāpi dravyasamavāyikāraṇatvaṃ tadā
dravyāṇītyanenāntyāvayavitvarahitasparśavatāṃ parigrahāt| na
cātyantāvayavitvāniruktiḥ,
avayava[tva]rahitāvayavitvasyāntyāvayavitvāt| yadā tu
dravyārambhakatvaṃ dravyakāraṇatvaṃ vā sādharmyaṃ ghaṭata
eva, tatkeṣāmiti cet---tantvādīnāmiti brūmaḥ| kiyatāmiti
cet---na, saṃkhyāviśeṣa[pa]rijñānasyādūṣaṇatvāt|
kimupasaṃgrāhavatāmiti cet---na,
upasaṃgrāhakāntarānaṅgīkārāt| kimatra pramāṇamiti cet---na,
idaṃ dravyasamavāyikāraṇamidaṃ dravyasamavāyikāraṇam, idaṃ
dravyakāraṇamidaṃ dravyakāraṇamiti pratyakṣameva| yadi ca
dravyakāraṇatve gaganamano.anyaddravyatvamupasaṃgrāhakamiṣyate,
tadā guṇānāṃ dravyakāraṇatvaṃ na syāt|
upasaṃgrāhakasyāpyupasaṃgrāhakāntarāpekṣayāyāmanavasthānāt|
upasaṃgrāhakāntaramantareṇāpi tadavagamasambhave
dravyasamavāyikāraṇatvāderapi tathaivāgamasambhavāt
mūlakṣaterabhāvāt neyamanavasthā dūṣaṇamiti cet---na,
pūrvapūrvopasaṃgrāhakānavagame taduttarānavagamāt
mūlakṣaterāvaśyakatvāt| dravyāṇi dravyamārabhanta
ityetāvataiva caritārthatve vyarthamantaragrahaṇamiti cet---na,
ārabhyārambhakadravyayorbheda iti pratipādanārthatvāt|

sāṃkhyā hi
kāryakāraṇayorabhedamicchanti| pramāṇayanti ca tantavaḥ
paṭānna bhidyante, paṭakāraṇatvāt| yatpaṭādbhidyate na
tatpaṭakāraṇaṃ, yathā ghaṭa ityādi| tanna, paṭakāraṇatvasya
kuvindādau vyabhicārāt| te .api pakṣatulyā iti cet---na,
pratyakṣabādhāt| paṭādbhinnāḥ kuvindādaya
ityaparokṣānubhavasya sarvalokasiddhatvāt| kiṃ ca ghaṭādīnāṃ
paṭabhinnatvamapramitaṃ pramitaṃ vā| nādyaḥ,
vyatirekagrahaṇābhāvaprasaṅgāt| na dvitīyaḥ, tenaiva nyāyena
tantūnāmapi paṭabhedasiddherdurvāratvāt| paṭakaraṇatvasya
paṭabhinnarahitatvādvyāvṛttatvenāsādhāraṇatvācca| etena
paṭastantubhyo na bhidyate, tantukāryatvāt, yattantubhyo bhidyate
na tattantukāryam, yathā ghaṭa ityādayaḥ prayogā nirastāḥ|

ārabhyārambhakayorbhede kiṃ pramāṇamiti cet---ayaṃ
tanturetasmātpaṭādbhinnaḥ ayaṃ paṭa etasmāttantorbhinna ityādi
pratyakṣamanumānaṃ vā| ayaṃ tanturasmātpaṭādbhidyate,
tantutvāt [tantvantaravat]| ayaṃ paṭa etasmāttantorbhidyate,
paṭatvāt, paṭāntaravadityādi| so
.ayamārabhyārambhakayorbhedāntaragrahaṇena jñāpya iti
saphalamantaragrahaṇamiti| ārabhyārambhakabhāvasya
pratyakṣānumānābhyāmevāvagateḥ vyarthamantaragrahaṇamiti
cet---na, ārabhyārambhakabhāvasyāpi pratyakṣānumānāvagatatvena
tatpratipādaka(syāntaragrahaṇasya vyarthatve)
sūtrasyāpi
vyarthatvāditi||

guṇāśca guṇāntaram|| 1-1-9||

ārabhanta ityanuṣajyate| atra ca guṇāścetyekaṃ
sūtram|
guṇāntaramiti dvitīyamiti sūtrakārasya
vivakṣitam| tena na
kevalaṃ dravyāṇi dravyāntaramārabhante, api tu guṇā apīti
prathamasūtrārthaḥ,
dravyārambhakasaṃyogeśvarajñānādīnāṃ
paṭādidravyajanakatvāt| cakāro
.anuktadravyaprāgabhāvapratibandhakābhāvasamuccayārthaḥ,
dravyaprāgabhāvapratibandhakābhāvayorapi dravyakāraṇatvāt| tena
dravyajanakatvaṃ dravyajanakadravyaguṇābhāvānāṃ sādharmyamiti
sūtradvayatātparyam|
yadā tu guṇāśca guṇāntaramityetāvadekaṃ
sūtram,
ārambhakatvaṃ ca janakatvam,
cakāraścānuktadravyādisamuccayārthaḥ, tadā sarvapadārthānāṃ
guṇajanakatvaṃ sādharmyamiti sūtrārthaḥ|
sarvapadārthānāṃ
viśeṣaṇatvena viśeṣyatvena vā viśiṣṭajñānajanakatvena
guṇajanakatvāt| tadā tvārambhakatvamatrāsamavāyikāraṇatvaṃ
cakāraśca karmasamuccayārthaḥ, tadā
guṇakarmaṇorasamavāyikāraṇatvaṃ sādharmyamiti
sūtrārthaḥ|
athārambha itivadantarapadasyāpi
pūrvasūtragatasyātrāpyanvayasambhavāt,
antaragrahaṇaṃ
vyarthamiti cet---na, anantarapadasya dravyapadānupapatteriti| yadvā
guṇāśca ityayaṃ cakāro
guṇāntaramityasyānantaramāvartanīyaḥ| tena guṇāścetyatra
cakāro .anuktasamuccayārthaḥ| guṇāntaramityatra ca
karmasamuccayārthaḥ| tena yathā guṇadravyayoḥ (dravyajanakatvaṃ
tathā) guṇakarmajanakatvamapīti sūtrārthaḥ
sampadyate|| 9||
yathā dravyajanakatvaṃ dravyāṇāṃ sādharmyam, guṇajanakatvaṃ
[ca] guṇānām, tathā karmajanakatvaṃ karmaṇāṃ kiṃ na syāt|
yathā vā dravyaguṇayoḥ karmajanakatvaṃ tathā (karmaṇo .apyastu
iti) karmaṇānvayaparihārārthaṃ sūtram---

karma karmasādhyaṃ na vidyate|| 1-1-10||

iti| na tāvatkarmaṇaḥ karmajanakatve pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇam, idaṃ
karma karmajanakamityaparokṣānubhavavirahāt| nāpyanumānaṃ
liṅgābhāvāt| atha prathamakarma karmajanakam, niyamena
tadavyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavṛttitvāt śaravaditi cet---na,
karmatvābhāvena sopādhitvāt| na cāsya pakṣetaratvam,
janakatvarahitātyantakarmaṇo vipakṣādvyāvṛttatvāt| karmatvena
satpratipakṣatvācca| (prayoga)śca karmaṇaḥ karma[a]janakatve
pramāṇam| ādyaṃ karma na karmajanakaṃ karmatvāt
antyakarmavaditi| atha karma karmasādhyaṃ netyetāvataiva
caritārthatvādvidyata iti vyarthamiti cet---na,
pramāṇapratipādakatvāt| kuto na karma
karmasādhyamityapekṣāyāṃ vidyate tatra pramāṇaṃ
karmatvādikamiti sūtravyākhyānācca|
atha karma karmasādhyaṃ na
vidyate iti na yuktam, karmaṇaḥ karmajanakatvaniṣedhāvasare
karmaṇaḥ karmajanaka[nya]tvaniṣedhasyāprastutatvāt| niṣedhe
arthātkarmaṇaḥ karmajanakatvaniṣedho .api sidhayatīti cet---na,
sākṣādeva karmaṇaḥ karmajanakatvaniṣedhasya
kartuma[m]śakyatvena karmaṇaḥ karmajanaka[nya]tvaniṣedhamukhena

tatpratipādanasya[a]garīyastvāt| tasmātkarma karmajanakaṃ na
vidyata iti [sūtraṇīya]miti---tanna,
sūtrārthāparijñānāt|
karma sādhyaṃ yasya tatkarmasādhyaṃ karmajanakam| tena
karmajanakamiti sākṣādeva karmajanakatvaniṣedhaḥ sidhyatīti|
yadvā sadanityaṃ dravyavatkāryam [1-1-7][ityādinā]
dravyādīnāṃ kāraṇatvādisādharmyamuktam| tadupapādanārtham
dravyāṇi dravyāntaramārabhante [1-1-8] guṇāśca guṇāntaram
[1-1-9] iti sūtradvayam|
nanu bhavatu dravyādīnāṃ kāraṇatvaṃ
dravyādijanakatvāt| karmaṇastu na kā[ryam]| na tāvatkarmaṇaḥ
[na] karma kāryaṃ karmatvādeva| nāpi kāryāntaram,
avidyamānatvādityāśaṅkāparihārārthaṃ
sūtram---
karma karmasādhyaṃ na vidyate [1-1-10] iti| yadyapi karma karmasādhyaṃ
na, tathāpi saṃyoga iti karmaṇaḥ kāraṇatvopapattiriti
sūtrārthaḥ|| 10||

kāryāvirodhi dravyaṃ kāraṇāvirodhi ca|| 1-1-11||

kāryamavirodhi avināśakaṃ yasya tatkāryāvirodhi|
kāryāvināśyamityarthaḥ| kāraṇamavirodhi yasya
tatkāraṇāvirodhi| tadanena kāryakāraṇa[a]virodhitvaṃ
dravyāṇāṃ sādharmyamuktamiti bhāṣyakārādayaḥ| !!
tadayuktam, sarvakāryadravyāṇāṃ
samavāyikāraṇāsamavāyikāraṇavināśavināśyānāṃ
kāryavināśyatvena tadvirahānupapatteḥ|
samavāyikāraṇāsamavāyikāraṇavināśayoḥ
kāryatvātsvakāryavināśyatvābhāvaḥ kāryāvināśyatvamiti
cet---na, svaśabdena kiṃ kāraṇamātraṃ vivakṣitam,
samavāyikāraṇaṃ vā, dravyasamavāyikāraṇaṃ vā,
samavāyikāraṇaviśeṣo vā| na prathamaḥ, tata eva
samavāyikāraṇāsamavāyikāraṇavināśayorapīśvarakālādidravyajanyatvāt|
na tṛtīyaḥ, tata eva dravyasamavāyikāraṇavināśasyāpi
dravyasamavāyikāraṇajanyatvāt| vināśe pratiyogino .api
kāraṇatvāt| na caturthaḥ, tatkāryavināśitvasya
kvacidaprasiddhatve tadabhāvasyāprasiddhatvena
samānadharmatvānupapatteḥ| tatkāryasaṃyogavināśasya karmādau
ca prasiddhatve tadabhāvasya śabdādāvapi vidyamānatvena
śabdādīnāmapi kāryāvirodhitvaprasaṅgāt|

etena kāraṇāvirodhitvaṃ dravyāṇāṃ sādharmyamiti pratyuktam|
sarvakāryadravyāṇāṃ
samavāyikāraṇāsamavāyikāraṇavināśavināśyānāṃ
kāraṇavināśyatvena tadvirahānupapatteḥ|
samavāyikāraṇavināśayorapi svakāryakāraṇatvāt|
svasvakāraṇadravyāvināśyatvaṃ kāraṇāvirodhitvamiti cet---na,
sarvakāryadravyāṇāṃ svasvakāraṇeśvarādidravyavināśyatvena
tadvirahānupapatteḥ|
īśvaradikkālavyatiriktasvasvakāraṇadravyavināśyatvābhāvaḥ

kāraṇāvirodhitvamiti cet---na,
īśvaradikkālavyatiriktasvasvakāraṇadravyavināśyatvasya
kvacidaprasiddhatvena tadabhāvasyāvagantumaśakyatvena
samānadharmatvānupapatteḥ
bhavadabhimatakāraṇavirodhyantyaśabdādau vidyamānatvācca|
ghaṭādivadantyaśabdasyāpi ghaṭādikāraṇavināśyatvāt
śabdakāraṇāvināśyatvaṃ kāraṇa[a]virodhitvam| taccāntyaśabde
nāstīti cet---na, sarvakāryadravyāṇāṃ
śabdakāraṇeśvarādivināśyatvena tadvirahānupapatteḥ|
īśvarādivyatiriktaśabdakāraṇāvināśyatvaṃ
kāraṇāvirodhitvamiti cet---na, tasyāntyaśabde .api sambhavāt|
antyaśabda īśvarādivyatiriktena svakāraṇenopāntyaśabdena
nāśyata iti cet---na, nirākariṣyamānatvāditi||

sūtram---

ubhayathā guṇaḥ|| 1-1-12||

ubhayatheti kāryavirodhī kāraṇavirodhī ceti vyākhyeyam| na tu
kāryāvirodhī kāraṇāvirodhī ceti| kāryāvirodhitvasya
prathamādiśabde jñānādijanakajñānādau ca bādhāt|
kāraṇāvirodhitvasya copāntyaśabdavināśyāntyaśabde bādhācca|

kāryāvirodhitvakāraṇa[a]virodhitvapratiyogitvena
kāryā[rya]virodhitvakāraṇavirodhitvayorapi prakṛtatvāditi
praśastapādaśiṣyāḥ|
tanna, kāryavirodhitvasya dravyeṣvapi sambhavāt|
samavāyikāraṇāsamavāyikāraṇavināśayorapi kāryatvāt|
svasvakāryavināśyatvasya ca tattadviśeṣaniṣṭhatvena
śabdabuddhyādisamānatvānupapatteḥ| kāraṇavināśyatvasyāpi
dravyeṣvapi sambhavāt, dravyavināśyasyāpi kāraṇatvāt,
svasvakāraṇavināśyatvasyāpi dravyeṣvapi sambhavāt,
kāryadravyaguṇānāṃ svasvakāraṇeśvarādivināśyatvāt|
svasvakāraṇasamavāyikāraṇavināśyatvasya ca
tadviśeṣamātraniṣṭhatvena samānadharmatvānupapatteḥ|
guṇeśvasambhavācca| antyaśabdasyopāntyanāśyatve
pramāṇābhāvāt| antyaśabdaḥ śabdavināśyaḥ śabdatvāt
ādyaśabdavaditi cet---na,
svavināśyatvasiddhyāsyopapannatvenārthāntaragrastatvāt|
antyaśabdasya svavināśyatvena svotpattyanantarameva
vināśātkṣaṇikatvaprasaṅga iti cet---na,
antyaśabdasyopāntyavināśyatve .apyasya durvāratvāt| antyaśabdo
.antyaśabdavyatiriktavināśyaḥ śabdatvād, ādiśabdavaditi
cet---na, upāntyaśabdavyabhicārāt| upāntyaśabdastenaiva
tatpūrvabhāvinā vā śabdena vināśyata iti cet---na,
svotpattyanantarakṣaṇotpannavināśakānapekṣāyāmupāntyaśabdasya
kṣaṇikatvāpatteḥ| tatkāryaśabdāpekṣāyāṃ ca tata eva
upāntyavināśasiddhestayorakāraṇatvavyavasthiteriti
antyaśabdavyatiriktaśabdanāśyatvena
śabdajanakatvasyopādhitvācca| tadvirahasya pratipakṣatvācca|

na cāsya pakṣetaratā|
antyaśabdavyatiriktaśabdanāśyatvarahitatvādrūpādereva
vyāvṛttatvāt| athāyamantyaśabdaḥ etadvyatiriktaśabdavināśyaḥ
śabdatvāt ādyaśabdavaditi| na cātra śabdajanakatvamupādhiḥ,
antyaśabdāntareṇa sādhyāvyāpakatvāt|
tasya svavināśyatvenaitacchabdavyatiriktaśabdanāśyatve .api
manyase---tanna, antyaśabdāntarasyāpi svavināśyatvābhāvāt|
vināśakāntarānapekṣāyāṃ tasya[a]kṣaṇikatvāpatteḥ|
apekṣāyāṃ ca tata eva tadvināśopapattau tasya
kāraṇatvavyavasthiteḥ| kṣaṇikatvasyāpratipannatvena kathaṃ
tadāpādanamiti cet---na,
etadutpattikṣaṇāntarakṣaṇotpannavināśapratiyogitvasyāpādyatvāt|
tasya caitadanantarakṣaṇavinaṣṭaghaṭādiṣu prasiddhatvāt| na
caivaṃvidhakṣaṇikatvamantyaśabdādāviṣṭameveti vācyam,
tasyāpi svotpattikṣaṇavyatiriktakṣaṇasambandhitvasya
prameyatvenānumānāditi|
etadvyatiriktayatkiṃcicchabdavināśyatvenāsyopapannatvenopāntyaśabdavināśyatvāsādhakatvācca|
antyaśabdavināśakaṃ śabdāntaraṃ nopalabhyata iti cet---na,
upāntyaśabdasyāpyanupalambhāt| deśāntaragatatvādupāntyaśabdo
nopalabhyata iti cet---tulyaṃ śabdāntare .api| ayamantyaśabdo
naitadupāntyavināśyaḥ, tadajanakatvāt, rūpavaditi
pratipakṣagrastatvācca| na cāsyopāntyaśabde vyabhicāraḥ, tasya
tadajanakatve .api tadvināśyatvābhāvāt|
etenāyamantyaśabdavināśaḥ etacchabdavyatiriktaśabdajanyaḥ
śabdavināśa[ka]tvāt, ādyaśabdavināśavadityādayaḥ prayogāḥ
nirastāḥ
etadvyatiriktayatkiṃcicchabdajanyatvenāsyopapannatvenopāntyaśabdajanyatvāsādhakatvāt|
mahāpralayakālīnaśabdavināśe vyabhicārācca| tasyaiva
tacchabdavyatiriktaśabdajanyatvābhāvāt, tadanantaraṃ
śabdānutpatteḥ pratiyoginaśca svavināśakāraṇatvābhāvāt|
upāntyaśabdādeścāntyaśabdavināśakatvāsambhavāt| anyathā
kṣaṇabhaṅgāpatteriti| ayamantyaśabdavināśo
naitadupāntyaśabdajanyaḥ, etajjanakaśabdavināśatvāt,
sampratipannavaditi satpratipakṣatvācca|
upāntyaśabdasyāntyavināśakatvābhāve kiṃ pramāṇamiti
cet---upāntyaśabdo nāntyaśabdavināśahetuḥ
śabdatvādādyaśabdavat| ayamupāntyaśabdo
naitadantyaśabdavināśanahetuḥ, śabdatvāt,
ādyaśabdavadityādikamanumānam| kaḥ
punarantyaśabdavināśaheturiti
cet---sparśavaddravyasaṃyogaviśeṣa ityātreyācāryāḥ|
yathā hi śaragato vegaḥ sparśavatkuḍyādiśarasaṃyogena
vināśyate, tathā śabdo .api kuḍyādivyavahitena
śabdānupalabdheriti|

tadayuktam, vipratipannaḥ sparśavaddravyasaṃyogo na
śabdavināśahetuḥ saṃyogatvāt sampratipannavat| vipratipanno
naitadantyaśabdavināśahetuḥ, saṃyogatvāt,
sampratipannavadityādinā pratiṣedhāt,
sparśavaddravyasaṃyogaviśeṣasya antyaśabdavināśakatve
pramāṇābhāvācca| pariśeṣaḥ pramāṇamiti cet---na, tatrāpi
pratiṣedhasyoktatvāditi| tarhi ko .antyaśabdavināśaheturiti
cet---īśvarajñānecchāprayatnadikkālādṛṣṭāni|
īśvarādivyatiriktaḥ ko .antyaśabdavināśaheturiti
cet---ayamantyaśabda īśvarādivyatiriktahetujanyaḥ, kāryatvāt,
ghaṭavadityanumānasiddhaḥ padārthaviśeṣaḥ| ayamasāviti
viśeṣato nirdiśyatāmiti cet---na, prayojanābhāvāt, viśeṣataḥ
tannirdhārakapramāṇābhāvācca|
tarhi na siddhyediti cet---na, kiṃ sāmānyena[na?] siddhye[dhye]diti
vivakṣitam| viśeṣato na sidhyediti vā| nādyaḥ,
pūrvoktānumānādeva tatsiddheḥ| na dvitīyaḥ, iṣṭatvāt, idaṃ
dravyamantyaśabdavināśakaṃ na bhavati dravyatvāt,
sampratipannavat| ayaṃ guṇo nāntyaśabdavināśahetuḥ, guṇatvāt,
sampratipannavadityādinā
dravyādisakalapadārthānāmantyaśabdavināśahetutvaniṣedhāt|

prakṛtamanumānamabādhitamiti cet---na,
prakṛtānumānasiddhavyatiriktānāṃ dharmitve suddhasādhanāt|
prakṛtānumānasiddhasya dharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhāt|
prakṛtānumānasiddhasya ca dravyatvādinirdhārakapramāṇābhāvena
dravyatvaguṇatvādīnāṃ sandigdhāsiddhatvācca| sarveṣāmeṣāṃ
prakṛtānumānasādhyābhāvāsādhakatvenārthāntaragrastatvācca|
kiṃ ca yāvatpratipannaṃ
dravyaguṇādīnāmantyaśabdavināśahetutvapratiṣedhe ca
prakṛtānumānasya tadvyatirikte paryavasānāt| tadvyatiriktānāṃ
ca pratipannatvena dharmitvenopādātumaśakyatvācceti|
tasmāt---ubhayathā guṇaḥ [1-1-12] iti sūtraṃ
kāryakāraṇavirodhitvasādharmyapratipādanārthamiti|
kastarhi---kāryāvirodhi dravyaṃ kāraṇāvirodhi ca [1-1-11]
ubhayathā guṇaḥ [1-1-12] iti sūtrārthaḥ|
ucyate---sadanityaṃ
dravyavatkāryaṃ kāraṇaṃ sāmānyaviśeṣavaditi
dravyaguṇakarmaṇāmaviśeṣaḥ [1-1-7] iti sūtre
dravyaguṇakarmaṇāmanityatvamuktam| anityatvaṃ ca vināśitvam|
vināśaḥ kāryatvātkāraṇajanyaḥ iti
dravyādivināśakāraṇacintāyāṃ kiṃ dravyādivināśe
tattaddravyādikāryaṃ hetuḥ, kiṃ vā tattaddravyādikāraṇam,
kiṃ vā tattaddravyādikāraṇavināśaḥ, kiṃ vā
tattaddravyādikāryakṛtivināśa iti| tatra
prathamapakṣanirākaraṇārthaṃ sūtram---
kāryāvirodhi dravyam
[1-1-11] iti| dvitīyatṛtīyapakṣanirākaraṇārthaṃ
sūtram---
kāraṇāvirodhi ca [1-1-11] iti| atra kāryaśabdena svasvakāryamabhipretam, kāraṇaśabdena svasvakāraṇamīśvarajñānecchāprayatnadikkālādivyatiriktamabhipretam|
kāryādīnāṃ dravyavināśakatvamiti cet---sādhakamānābhāvāt|
evameva tadvinā janakamiti pratyakṣeṇa
dravyanāśakāraṇatvābhāvāvadhāraṇācca, dravyāṇāṃ ca
svasvakāryavināśyatve paramāṇordvyaṇukena, tantvādīnāṃ
paṭādibhirdvitīyakṣaṇa eva vināśaprasaṅgāt|
svasvakāraṇavināśyatve ca gha[pa]ṭādīnāṃ
dvitīyakṣaṇa eva tantvādervināśaprasaṅgāt,
apekṣaṇīyāntarābhāvāt| bhāve vā tata eva
gha[pa]ṭādivināśasiddheḥ tantvādīnāṃ vināśakatvāsiddheḥ|
cakāraḥ kāryakāraṇakāryanāśavyatiriktānāṃ
dravyavināśākāraṇatvasamuccayārthaḥ| tantupaṭādīnāṃ
yatkiṃcidvināśyatve dvitīyakṣaṇa eva vināśaprasaṅgāt|
tattaddravyavināśe tattatkāryakāraṇayorahetutvamupapādya
tattadguṇavināśe .api
tattatkāryakāraṇayorahetutvopapādanārthaṃ
sūtram---
ubhayathā
guṇaḥ [1-1-12] iti| ubhayatheti kāryāvirodhi kāraṇāvirodhi
cetyarthaḥ| atra ca guṇaśabdena
svasvakāryavināśyaśabdātmaviśeṣaguṇavyatirikto guṇo
vivakṣitaḥ| ādyaśabd[o] buddhyādīnāṃ
svasvakāryaśabdabuddhyādivināśyatvāt|

caturthapakṣasamarthanārthaṃ
sūtram---
kāraṇābhāvātkāryābhāvaḥ [1-2-1] iti|

pañcamapakṣaniṣedhārthaṃ sūtram---
na tu kāryābhāvātkāraṇābhāvaḥ [1-2-2] iti|
anekasūtravyavahitayordvitīyāhnikasūtrayoḥ

kathamatrānvaya iti cet---ākāṅkṣitatvādyogyatvācceti brūmaḥ|
ākāṅkṣādivatsannidhirapyanvayakāraṇam| sa cātra nāstīti
cet---na, buddhisthatvasya sannidheratrāpi vidyamānatvāt|
avyavahitoccāraṇasyāpi kartuṃ śakyatvāt| atraiva tarhi
sūtre
paṭhanīye iti cet---na, tatrāpi prayojanasambhavāt| tatrātra ca
paṭhanīye iti cet---na, ekatra paṭhane
.apyanyatrāpyanvayasambhavāt| atra kiṃ na paṭhite iti cet---na,
atra pāṭhe [tatra] kiṃ na paṭhite iti vaktuṃ śakyatvāditi|
astu vā---kāryāvirodhi dravyaṃ kāraṇāvirodhi ca [1-1-11]
ubhayathā guṇaḥ [1-1-12] iti sūtrāṇāmeva
kāraṇavināśātkāryadravyaguṇakarmaṇāma[pi] vināśa ityatra
tātparyam| guṇasya
guṇavināśakāraṇatvamagnisaṃyogarūpādivināśapratipādakena---agnisaṃyogācca
[7-1-5] iti sūtreṇa
siddham| karmaṇo guṇavināśyatvamuttarasūtra
eva bhaviṣyati|
tasmāddravyavināśe samavāyikāraṇavināśo
.asamavāyikāraṇavināśo vā kāraṇam| guṇavināśe
samavāyikāraṇavināśe vā samavāyikāraṇavināśo vā guṇo vā
kāraṇam| karmavināśe samavāyikāraṇavināśaḥ saṃyogo vā
kāraṇamiti vyavastheti| samavāyikāraṇavināśāddravyavināśa
iti na yuktam, pramāṇābhāvāt| kāryasya ca
samavāyikāraṇavināśajanyāsamavāyikāraṇavināśādevopapatteḥ|

tathā ca vipratipanno dravyavināśo .avayavasaṃyogavināśajanyaḥ
dravyavināśatvāt, tantusaṃyogavināśajanyapaṭavināśavaditi
cet---na, tantuvināśātpaṭavināśa iti pratyakṣavirodhāt|
avirodhe vā sarvatrāvayavasaṃyogavināśasyaiva
dravyavināśakāraṇatvāṅgīkārāt| kāryavirodhi utpannaṃ karma
vibhāgasaṃyogavegānārabhya nivartata iti pratyakṣasiddham| sā ca
karmanivṛttirnāśrayavināśajanyā, āśrayasya
pratyakṣasiddhatvāt| nāpyasamavāyikāraṇavināśajanyā, satyapi
gurutvadravatvavegādāvasamavāyikāraṇe

karmanivṛtterupalambhāt|
gurutvādijanyakarmanivṛtterasamavāyikāraṇavināśajanyatve .api
nodanābhighātajanyakarmanivṛttirasamavāyikāraṇajanyā
bhaviṣyatīti cet---na, gurutvādijanyakarmanivṛtteḥ
saṃyogajanyatvasiddheśca| vimatā karmanivṛttiḥ na
nodanābhighātakarmanivṛttijanyā, karmanivṛttitvāt,
gurutvājanyakarmanivṛttivat| vimatā nodanābhighātanivṛttirna
karmanivṛttikāraṇam, saṃyoganivṛttitvāt, sampratipannavat| iyaṃ
karmanivṛttirnaitannodananivṛttijanyā, karmanivṛttitvāt,
anyakarmanivṛttivat| iyaṃ nodananivṛttiḥ
naitatkarmanivṛttikāraṇam, nodananivṛttitvāt,
anyanodananivṛttivadityādinā pratiṣedhāt|

nāpi vibhāgajanyasaṃyogātpūrvasaṃyoganivṛttikṣaṇe nivṛtteḥ
tadanantaramuttarasaṃyogānudayaprasaṅgāt|
pūrvasaṃyoganivṛttisahito vibhāgaḥ karmanivṛttiheturiti na
pūrvasaṃyoganivṛttyudayakṣaṇe karmanivṛttiriti cet---na,
pūrvasaṃyoganivṛtteḥ karmanivṛttyajanakatvāt| janakatve vā tata
eva tadupapattau vibhāgasyākāraṇatvaprasaṅgāt| ayaṃ vibhāgo
naitatkramanivṛttikāraṇam, vibhāgatvādanyavibhāgavat| iyaṃ
karmanivṛttirnaitadvibhāgajanyā karmanivṛttitvāt,
anyakarmanivṛttivaditi pratiṣedhācca| vibhāgāduttarasaṃyoga iti
cet---na, ayaṃ vibhāgo naitatsaṃyogahetuḥ, vibhāgatvāt,
anyavibhāgavat| ayaṃ saṃyogo naitadvibhāgajanyaḥ, saṃyogatvāt,
anyasaṃyogavaditi pratiṣedhāt| vibhāga evottarasaṃyogaḥ
karmaṇā janyata iti cet---na,
vibhāgajanyapūrvasaṃyoganivṛtterapyuttarasaṃyogahetutvasya
pratyakṣasiddhatvāt| iyaṃ pūrvasaṃyoganivṛttiḥ
naitaduttarasaṃyogahetuḥ saṃyoganivṛttitvāt,
anyasaṃyoganivṛttivat| ayaṃ saṃyogo naitatsaṃyoganivṛttijanyaḥ,
saṃyogatvāt, anyasaṃyogavaditi cet---na, etatsaṃyogahetutvasya
kvacidapramitau tadabhāvasyāprasiddhatvāt| pramitau vā tenaiva
nyāyena pūrvasaṃyoganivṛtteruttarasaṃyogahetutvasiddherbādhāt|
evaṃ dvitīyānumāne .api| pūrvasaṃyoganivṛttirapi
vibhāgakṣaṇa eva karmaṇā janyate iti vibhāgānantarakṣaṇa eva
vibhāgātkarmanivṛttiḥ, karmaṇaścottarasaṃyogo bhaviṣyatīti
cet---na, pūrvasaṃyogo na karmavināśyaḥ, guṇatvāt, rūpavat|
ayaṃ pūrvasaṃyogo naitatkarmavināśyaḥ, saṃyogatvāt,
anyasaṃyogavat| etatkarmaitatsaṃyogavināśakam, karmatvāt,
anyakarmavaditi pratiṣedhāt|

nāpi vegaḥ, vibhāgā[t] pūrvasaṃyoganivṛttikṣaṇa eva
vegātkarmanivṛttau tadanantaramuttarasaṃyogānudayaprasaṅgāt|
ayaṃ vego naitatkarmanivṛttihetuḥ, vegatvāt, anyavegavat|
iyaṃ karmanivṛttirnaitadvegajanyā karmanivṛttitvāt,
anyakarmanivṛttivaditi pratiṣedhācca|

nāpi rūpādayi vibhāgakṣaṇa eva rūpādibhyaḥ
karmanivṛttāvuttarasaṃyogānudayaprasaṅgāt|
tasmāduttarasaṃyogaḥ karmanivṛttihetuḥ pariśeṣāt| ayaṃ
saṃyogo naitatkarmanivṛttihetuḥ saṃyogatvāt, anyasaṃyogavat|
iyaṃ karmanivṛttiḥ naitatsaṃyogajanyā
karmanivṛttitvādanyakarmanivṛttivaditi cet---na,
karmanivṛttihetirasiddhau tadabhāvasyāprasiddhatvāt| siddhau vā
tenaiva nyāyenottarasaṃyogasyāpi karmanivṛttihetutvasiddheḥ
bādhāt| uttarasaṃyogānnivṛttaṃ
karmetyaparokṣānubhavabādhācca| vibhāgādīnāṃ
karmavināśakatvābhāvasādhanānyapi pratyakṣabādhitānīti
cet---na, vibhāgānivṛttaṃ karmetyādyaparokṣānubhavābhāvāt|
bhāve vā vibhāgavegayorapi karmavināśakatvābhyupagamāt| na
caivaṃ vibhāgānantarakṣaṇa eva
karmanivṛtteruttarasaṃyogānupapattiḥ|
vibhāgajanyapūrvasaṃyoganivṛtterapi
karmavināśakatvābhyupagamāt| ata eva bhagavān---
 kāryavirodhi karma|| 1-1-13||

ityasūtrayat, na tu saṃyogavirodhi karmeti|
anyakarmanivṛttiretajjanakāsaṃyogatvarahitā janyā kāryatvāt,
paṭavaditi siddhāyāṃ saṃyogajanyakarmanivṛttau
karmanivṛttijanakasaṃyoge cānaikāntikatvācca| karmanivṛtteśca
saṃyogajanakatve pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇamanumānaṃ ca| iyaṃ
karmanivṛttiretatsaṃyogavyatiriktaitajjanakatvarahitajanyā
kāryatvāt, paṭavaditi|

yadvā,
etatkarmāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇotpannametatkarmanivṛttijanakam|
etatsaṃyoga etatkarmanivṛttyavayavahitapūrvakṣaṇotpannai[nna
e]tajjanakatvāt| na yadevaṃ na tadevaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ| ayaṃ
saṃyoga etatkarmanivṛttyavyavahitapūrvakṣaṇotpanna
etajjanakakarmanivṛttijanakaḥ| na yadevaṃ na tadevam, yathā paṭa
iti vyatirekiṇā| na
caitatkarmanivṛttyavyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavṛttyetajjanakāsiddherāśrayāsiddhiḥ|
prathamānumānasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ ca dvitīyānumāna iti
vācyam| iyaṃ karmanivṛttiretadavyavahitapūrvekṣanotpannajanyā
etatkṣaṇotpannatvāt, etatkṣaṇotpannapaṭavaditi tatsiddheḥ|
anyathaitatkarmanivṛtteḥ pūrvameva janmaprasaṅgāt|
avyavahitapūrvakṣaṇotpannasyāpekṣaṇīyasyābhāvāt,
vidyamānasva[sa]pakṣatvādasādhāraṇatvamanayoriti cet---na,
vidyamānasapakṣatve .api hetostadvyāvṛttatvāniścayāt|
sapakṣavyāvṛttatvaniścayena cāsādhāraṇasya dūṣaṇatvāt|
anenaiva nyāyena vibhāgavegapūrvasaṃyoganivṛttīnāmapi
karmanivṛttikāraṇatvasiddheriti cet---na, asati bādhe
tatsiddheriṣṭatvāt| sati tu tatsiddhinivāraṇāditi|| 13||

atha dravyādilakṣaṇaprakaraṇam| atra
dravyalakṣaṇasūtram---
 kriyāvadguṇavatsamavāyikāraṇamiti dravyalakṣaṇam||

iti| sādharmyāvasare lakṣaṇakathanamayuktamiti cet---na,
dravyādilakṣaṇānāmapi sakaladravyādisamānadharmatvāt|

dravyādivibhāgānantaraṃ taddharmajijñāsāyāṃ
sādharmyavaidharmyasyāpi prastutatvācca| pūrvaṃ kimiti
vaidharmyaṃ na pratipāditamiti cet---na, pūrvaṃ
vairdharmyāpratipādane .apīdānīṃ tatpratipādanāvirodhāt|
kevalānvayivyatiriktapūrvapratipāditasakalasādharmyāṇāṃ
vairdharmyarūpatvācca| kriyāvadguṇavatsamavāyikāraṇamiti
bhāvapradhāno nirdeśaḥ| kriyāvattvaṃ guṇavattvaṃ
samavāyikāraṇatvamityarthaḥ| kriyāvattvaṃ na dravyalakṣaṇam,
ākāśādivyāvṛttatvenāvyāpakatvāditi cet---na, kriyāvattvasya
kriyāvaddravyalakṣaṇatvāt| na caivaṃ
dravyalakṣaṇatvānupapattiḥ kriyāvaddravyāṇāmapi dravyatvena
tallakṣaṇasya dravyalakṣaṇatvāt| na caivaṃ
pṛthivyādilakṣaṇānāṃ dravyalakṣaṇatvāpattiḥ, iṣṭatvāt|
yadvā, kriyāśabdena na karmābhipretam, kiṃ tu utpattivināśau,
sādhyatvāt| dhātvarthe ca vaiyākaraṇānāṃ
kriyāśabdaprayogāt| utpattivināśayoḥ kathaṃ dhātvarthatvamiti
cet---utpadyate vinaśyatītyādiprayogadarśanāt| utpattiśca
prāgabhāva eva (iti saddravyaṃ kathaṃ) kriyāvaditi cet---kriyā
vidyate .asminniti saptamyarthe vatiḥ tena kriyāvaditi
prāgabhāvādhikaraṇaṃ pradhvaṃsādhikaraṇaṃ cetyarthaḥ syāt|
tathā ca prāgabhāvādhikaraṇaṃ dravyam, pradhvaṃsādhikaraṇaṃ
dravyamiti lakṣaṇadvayaṃ sidhyati| na cānayoravyāpakatvaṃ
sarvadravyāṇāṃ saṃyogādiprāgabhāvapradhvaṃsādhikaraṇatvāt|
nāpyativyāpakatvam, prāgabhāvapradhvaṃsayoḥ
samavāyikāraṇamātraniṣṭhatvāṅgīkārāt| guṇavattvaṃ
guṇatve .avyāpakam, tasyāpi guṇasamavāyitvena guṇavattvāt|
tadasyāstīti
sambandhamātre vatyanuśāsanāditi cet---na, guṇavattvaśabdena
guṇāśrayatvasya vivakṣitatvāt| tadasminnastīti
tadāśrayatve .api vatyanuśāsanāt| guṇatvasya ca
guṇāśrayitvena guṇāśrayatvavirahāt| guṇāśritatve .api
guṇatvaṃ guṇāśrayo .apīti guṇāśrayatvamiti
vyāpakamityātreyācāryāḥ|
tanna, guṇatvasya guṇāśrayatve pramāṇābhāvāt śuklo ghaṭaḥ
saṃyukto .ayaṃ ghaṭa itivat śuklaguṇatvasaṃyuktaṃ
guṇatvamityananubhavāt| liṅgābhāvācca| guṇatvaṃ guṇāśrayaḥ
guṇasamavāyitvāt, dravyavaditi cet---na, guṇasamavāyitvaṃ kiṃ
guṇaniṣṭhasamavāyaḥ, kiṃ vā guṇaniṣṭhasamavāyāśrayatvam,
kiṃ vā guṇāśrayatvam| na prathamaḥ, guṇeṣu vyabhicārāt|
guṇasamavāyasya guṇatvavat guṇeṣvapi vidyamānatvāt|
samavāyasyobhayaniṣṭhatvātkarmādau vyabhicārācca|
samavāyasyaikatvena guṇasamavāyasya tatrāpi vidyamānatvāt| na
dvitīyaḥ, tata eva vyāvṛttyabhāvenāśrayatvasya vyarthatvācca|
na tṛtīyaḥ, sādhya[a]viśiṣṭatvāt guṇatvamaguṇamiti
pratyakṣavirodhācca| tasmānnātivyāpakaṃ guṇatvam|

ātreyācāryāḥ
punarguṇavatā[ditya]sya guṇatve .ativyāptiparihārārthaṃ
kriyāvaditi viśeṣaṇaṃ sūtrakāreṇopāttamityāhuḥ
tanna, pratyakṣabādhamullaṅghya guṇavattve
guṇatvamabhyupagacchatā kriyāvattvasyāpi
niṣedhumaśakyatvenātivyāptestadavasthatvāt| kriyāvattvena
guṇavattve .ativyāptiparihāre .api
sattāyāmativyāptestadavasthatvācca| tatparihārārthaṃ
samavāyikāraṇamiti
viśeṣaṇīyamiti cet---na, sattāyāmapi guṇādisamavāyitve sati
svaviśiṣṭabodhakāraṇatvena samavāyikāraṇatvāt|
kāryāśrayatvaṃ samavāyikāraṇatvam| tacca sattāyāṃ nāstīti
cet---na, guṇāśrayatvavatkāryāśrayatvasyāpi sattāyāṃ
sambhavāt| guṇeneva ca kāryeṇāpi sattāyāḥ
samavāyā[ya]sambhavāt| kāryāśritā sattā, na tu kāryāśraya
iti cet---na, tarhi guṇāśritā sattā na guṇāśraya iti
guṇāśrayatvasyātivyāptiparihārārthaṃ kāryāśrayatvaṃ
nopādeyamiti| tasmādyathā kāryāśrayatvaṃ dravyalakṣaṇam,
tathā guṇāśrayatvamapīti siddham|

itiśabda evaṃlakṣaṇāntaraparigrahārthaḥ| tadyathā
saṃkhyāśrayo dravyam| parimāṇāśrayo dravyam| pṛthaktvāśrayo
dravyam| saṃyogāśrayo dravyam| vibhāgāśrayo dravyam|
saṃkhyāyāmapi ekatvāśrayo dravyam| dvitvāśrayo dravyam|
tritvāśrayo dravyamityādi| pṛthaktve .api ekapṛthaktvāśrayo
dravyam| dvipṛthaktvāśrayo dravyam| tripṛthaktvāśrayo dravyam|
tripṛthaktvāśrayo dravyamityādi| pṛthaktvāśrayo
dravyamityetāvataiva caritārthatvādvyarthamekatvādigrahaṇamiti
cet---na,
atraikapṛthaktvaśabdābhyāmekatvapṛthaktvayoranabhidhānāt
dvipṛthaktvaśabdābhyāṃ ca dvi[tva]pṛthaktvayoranabhidhānāt|
kiṃ cākhaṇḍena dvipṛthaktvādiśabdena
dvipṛthaktvādya[na]abhidhānāt| yadi hi pṛthaktvapadaṃ
pṛthaktvamabhidadhyāttarhi pṛthaktvāśrayo dravyamityetāvataiva
dravyalakṣaṇasiddheḥ dviśabdābhihitaṃ dvitvaviśeṣaṇaṃ
vyarthaṃ syāt| yadā punardvipṛthaktvaśabdena
pṛthaktvāvāntarajātirvi[vi]śeṣavadabhidhīyate na tadā
vyarthaviśeṣaṇatvam| dviśabdena dvitvasyānupādānācca|
pṛthaktvaśabdena pṛthaktvānupādānācca| atha dvayoḥ
pṛthaktvaṃ dvipṛthaktvam, tena dvipṛthaktvāśrayo dravyamiti
lakṣaṇaṃ[ṇa] vākyārthaḥ syāditi dviṣu(tva?)viśeṣaṇam|
 tvamiti cet---na, asya vyutpattimātratvāt| dviṣu
pṛthaktvajātimātravṛttijātimadāśrayatvasya
dravyalakṣaṇatvāt| anyathā paṅkajatvaṃ kamalalakṣaṇam,
paṅkājjātaṃ paṅkajamiti vyutpattimāśritya
bhekādāvativyāpakaṃ syāt| gotvaṃ ca gavāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ
gacchatīti gauriti vyutpattimāśritya ghaṭādāvativyāpakaṃ
syāt| atha paṅkājjātaṃ paṅkajamiti gacchatīti gauriti ca
vyutpattimātramātram, paṅkajatvajātiḥ kamalānāṃ
gotvajātirgavāṃ lakṣaṇamiti vivakṣitam, tarhi dvayoḥ
pṛ[tha]ktvamiti vyutpattimātramapi
dvipṛthaktvamātravṛttijātimadāśrayadravyalakṣaṇam|
pṛthaktvamātravṛttijātimadāśrayatvasya
dravyalakṣaṇatvopapattervyarthaṃ dviṣṭhaviśeṣaṇamiti cet---na,
dviṣṭhapṛthaktvamātravṛttijātipadena
jātiviśeṣasyopalakṣaṇāttadvadāśrayatvasya dravyalakṣaṇatvāt
jātiviśeṣopalakṣakadviṣṭhapṛthaktvamātravṛttijātimadāśraya[padāntayaṃ]
ca pṛthaktvapadārthāśrayatvasya dravyalakṣaṇatvopapatteḥ
dviṣṭhamātravṛttijātimatyada[dityetad]vaiyarthyaṃ tadavasthamiti
cet---na, dviṣṭhapṛthaktvamātravṛttijātimadāśrayo
dravyamityatra dviṣṭhapadasamabhivyāhṛtapṛthaktvapadena
pṛthaktvāvāntarajātiviśiṣṭavācinā
pṛthaktvajātiviśiṣṭānabhidhānāt| abhidhāne vā
pṛthaktvāśrayasyāpratīteḥ| pṛthaktvapadasya pūrvottarābhyāṃ
dviṣṭha padābhyāṃ
nirākāṅkṣasyāśrayapadenāśrayapadasya ca
jātimatpadanirākāṅkṣasya
pṛthaktvapadenānvayābhāvāt|
jātimadāśrayo dravyamityetāvataiva dravyalakṣaṇopapatteḥ|
dviṣṭhapṛthaktvamātravṛtti iti vyarthamiti cet---na,
dviṣṭhapṛthaktvamātravṛttipadasamabhivyāhṛtajātipadasya
jātiviśeṣaparatvena jātimātra[trā?]samarpakatvāt| anyathā
guṇāśrayo dravyamityatrāpi huṇaśabdena
sāmānyapadaguṇakarmatvarahitābhidhāne sāmānyapadāśrayo
dravyamityetāvataiva dravyalakṣaṇopapatteḥ guṇakarmatvarahiteti
vyarthaṃ syāt| guṇaśabdena guṇatvavato .abhidhāne ca
guṇaśabdena sāmānyaguṇakarmatvarahitamātravṛttijātyabhidhāne
ca jātimadāśrayo dravyamityetāvataiva dravyalakṣaṇopapatteḥ sāmānyapadaguṇakarmatvarahitavṛttīti vyarthaṃ syāt| atha
sāmānyavadguṇakarmatvarahitamātravṛttipadasamabhivyāhṛtajātipadena
jātiviśeṣo vivakṣitaḥ, na tu jātimātramiti noktadoṣa iti
manyase, tarhi tulyaṃ prakṛte .apīti santoṣṭavyam| yattu
sadāśrayo dravyam, jātimadāśrayo dravyamiti
lakṣaṇadvayam---tanna, guṇakarmādāvativyāpakatvāt|
guṇatvakarmatvādīnāṃ sadāśrayatvāt jātimadāśrayatvācca|
sattāvāntarajātimadāśrayo riti dravyalakṣaṇamastīti
cet---na, guṇatvā(dī)nāṃ sattāvāntarajātitvādijātimattvena
guṇādāvativyāpakatvasya tadavasthatvāt| vipratipannaṃ
guṇatvādayo .avāntarajātimanto meyatvāt, ghaṭavadityanumānāt|
guṇakarmāvṛttijātimaddravyamityapi na dravyalakṣaṇam,
sāmānyādāvativyāpakatvāt| teṣāṃ
guṇakarma[a]vṛtti[jāti]jātitvādijātimattvāt|
guṇāvṛttigaganavṛttijātimaddravyam,
guṇāvṛttikālavṛttijātimaddravyamityādyapi na dravyalakṣaṇam,
karmādāvativyāpakatvāt| tathā ca gaganaṃ
gaganavṛttikarmavyāvṛttatvarahitagaganavṛttiguṇavṛttitvarahitajātimajjātimattvāt
ghaṭavadityādi| na cātra satpratipakṣatvādisambhavaḥ,
kevalānvayitvāt| etena karmāvṛttigaganavṛttijātimaddravyam,
karmāvṛttikālavṛttijātimaddravyamityādi dravyalakṣaṇam
nirastam| gaganavṛttisattāvāntarajātimaddravyamityapi na
dravyalakṣaṇam, guṇādāvativyāpakatvāt| tathā ca gaganaṃ
gaganavṛttiguṇavyāvṛttatvarahitasarvavyāvṛttajātimat,
jātimattvāt, prameyatvācca, ghaṭavaditi|
gaganavṛttiguṇavyāvṛttatvarahitatvācca| gaganavṛttitvarahitā
vā, guṇavyāvṛttatvarahitā vā| ādyā gagane vyāhatā,
guṇavyāvṛttatvarahitā ca| sattā vā tadavāntarā vā| ādyā
sadvyāvṛttagrahaṇena nirastā|
tasmādgaganirguṇavyāvṛttatvarahitasattāvāntarajātisiddhiriti
ceyamapi siddhāntanirastā| guṇagaganavṛttijātivirahasya
sūtrakāreṇāpratipādanāt
guṇagaganavṛttijātiḥ sūtrakāreṇa
na pratipāditeti tadvyutpādano .apasiddhanta iti cet---na,
ghaṭatvādivyutpādane .apasiddhāntaprasaṅgāt|
sūtrakāravyutpāditaviparītābhidhāne
.apyasiddhānto na tu
sūtrakārāpratipādita
iti cet---tulyam| tasmāt dravyaguṇayoḥ
karmavyāvṛttāddravyakarmaṇorguṇāvyāvṛttaguṇakarmaṇoḥ
dravyavyāvṛttā satāvāntarajātiranu bhyupagamyata eveti|
yadvāyaṃ guṇaḥ
etadvṛttigaganavyāvṛttatvarahitasattvavyāvṛttijātimān
jātimattvāt ghaṭavaditi|
etena gaganavṛttisattāvyatiriktajātimattvaṃ dravyamiti dravyalakṣaṇaṃ nirastam|
dravyākāśapadasthāne kālādipadaprakṣepeṇa pravṛttāni dravyalakṣaṇāni nirākāryāṇi|

kriyāsamānādhikaraṇasattāsākṣādvyāpyajātiyogyadravyamityapi
na dravyalakṣaṇam, guṇādāvativyāpakatvāt| tathā ca
guṇādayaḥ kriyāsamānādhikaraṇāḥ
sattāsākṣādvyāpyajātimanto jātimattvādghaṭavaditi| guṇādau
kriyāsamānādhikaraṇasattāsākṣādvyāpyajātisiddheḥ sattāyā
api sattāsākṣādvyāpyatvena guṇādīnāṃ
kriyāsamānādhikaraṇa sākṣādvyāpyajātimattvācca|
kathaṃ sattā sattāvyāpyeti
cet----sattātyantābhāvavadvṛttitvābhāvasya sattāvyāpyatvasya
sattāyāmubhayavādisiddhatvāt| astu sattā sattāvyāpyā, na tu
sākṣāditi cet---na, sadvyāvṛttijātivyāpyatvābhāvasya
sākṣāttvasya satāyāmapi vidyamānatvāt| anyasya ca
sākṣāttvasyānirukteḥ| yeṣāmapi na sattāvyāpyeti na
guṇādāvatiprasaṅgaḥ, teṣāmapi sākṣādgrahaṇam vyarthameva|
kriyāsamānādhikaraṇasattāvyāpyajātimattvasyāvyāptyativyāptiparihṛtatvena
viśeṣa[ṇa]antarānapekṣaṇāditi| astu tarhi
kriyāsamānādhikaraṇasattāsākṣādvyāpyajātiyogi dravyamiti
dravyalakṣaṇamiti cet---na,
sattātyantābhāvavadvṛttitvābhāvasya sattāvyāpyasya sattāyāṃ
vidyamānatvena guṇādāvativyāpakatvāt|
sattāvyatiriktatve sati sattātyantābhāvavadvṛttitvābhāvaḥ sattāvyāpyatvam, tacca
sattāyāṃ nāstīti cet---na,
sattātyantābhāvavadvṛttitvābhāvaviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyāt|
kriyāyāḥ samānādhikaraṇasattāvyāpyajātiyogitvasya
dravyalakṣaṇopapatteriti saṃyogavṛttisattāvāntarajātiyogi
dravyamityapi na lakṣaṇam, pūrvoktanyāyena
guṇakarmādāvativyāpakatvāt| saṃyogavaddravyamityetāvataiva
dravyalakṣaṇatvopapattau
tadvṛttisattāvāntarajātiyogigrahaṇavaiyarthyācca|
jātimanniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogyākāśavṛttijātiyogi
dravyamityapi na dravyalakṣaṇam, guṇādāvativyāpakatvāt| tathā
caguṇādayo
jātimanniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogyākāśavṛttijātiyoginaḥ,
jātimattvāt, paṭavaditi| etena
kāryasamānādhikaraṇasatāvāntarajātyadhikaraṇaṃ dravyam,
guṇasamānādhikaraṇasattāvāntarajātyadhikaraṇaṃ dravyamityādi
dravyalakṣaṇaṃ nirastam| kāryādhikaraṇaṃ dravyam,
guṇādhikaraṇaṃ dravyamityetāvataiva dravyalakṣaṇopapattau
samānādhikaraṇasattāvāntarajātigrahaṇavaiyarthyācca|
pṛthivyādinavānyatamatvamapi na dravyalakṣaṇam|
pṛthivyādinavavyaktivivakṣāyāṃ tadatiriktadravyāvyāpakatvāt|
pṛthivyādinavalakṣaṇavivakṣāyāṃ ca sarvadravyeṣu
vidyamānatvāt| dravyatvarahiteṣu navalakṣaṇeṣu
vartamānatvācca|
tasmātpṛthivyādinavalakṣaṇānyatamādhikaraṇaṃ dravyamiti
dravyalakṣaṇam| navalakṣaṇānyatamatvaṃ ca
navalakṣaṇapratiyogikanavānyonyābhāvavattvātyantābhāvaḥ|

saṃyogavaditarānadhikaraṇasaṃyogavaddharmādhikaraṇaṃ
dravyamiti vidyādharamiśrāḥ|
tanna, saṃyogavaditarānadhikaraṇatvaṃ kiṃ
saṃyogavyatiriktānadhikaraṇatvaṃ vivakṣitam, kiṃ vā
saṃyogavatsaṃyogavaditarānadhikaraṇatvam| na prathamaḥ
saṃyogavaddharmagrahaṇavaiyarthyāt| sarveṣāṃ saṃyogavatāṃ
saṃyogavaditaratvena dharmāṇāṃ saṃyogavaditarānadhikaraṇatvena
tadrahitatvānupapatteḥ, lakṣaṇasyāsambhavācca| na dvitīyaḥ,
saṃyogavadgrahaṇasaṃyogavaddharmagrahaṇayoḥ vaiyarthyāt|
saṃyogavaditarānadhikaraṇatvasyaiva dravyalakṣaṇatvopapatteḥ|
sarveṣāṃ saṃyogavatāṃ
svānyasaṃyogavaditaratvena taddharmāṇāṃ
saṃyogavatsaṃyogavaditarānadhikaraṇatvena
tadrahitatvānupapatteḥ, lakṣaṇasyāsambhavācca|
saṃyogavaditarapadena saṃyogatvarahitaṃ vivakṣitam| tena
saṃyogatvarahitānadhikaraṇaṃ dravyamiti dravyalakṣaṇamiti
cet---na, saṃyogavattvasya dravyalakṣaṇatvopapattau
rahitānadhikaraṇādhikaraṇagrahaṇavaiyarthyāt|
saṃyogādhikaraṇaṃ dravyamityetāvataiva dravyalakṣaṇopapattau
vatipratyayarahitānadhikaraṇagrahaṇavaiyarthyāt| etena
saṃyogapadasthāne
vibhāgasaṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvaguṇakāryādipadaprakṣepeṇa
dravyalakṣaṇāni nirastāni|
saṃyogavattvarahitāvṛttimadadhikaraṇaṃ dravyamityapi na
dravyalakṣaṇam| saṃyogādhikaraṇaṃ dravyamityetāvataiva
dravyalakṣaṇopapattau
vatitvapratyayarahitāvṛttimadgrahaṇavaiyarthyāt|
vatitvapratyayāntasya saṃyogapadasyādhikaraṇapadenānvayāsambhava
iti cet---na, niṣprayojanatvenāpasāritavatipratyayasya
saṃyogapadasyādhikaraṇapadenānvayasambhavāt| asambhave
vaivamādīnāṃ dravyalakṣaṇatvasvīkārāt| etena
saṃyogapadasthāne
vibhāgasaṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvapadaprakṣepeṇa
dravyalakṣaṇāni nirastāni| yadā tu na
dravyaguṇakarmavyatiriktānāṃ jātimattvaṃ tadā sadāśrayo
dravyam, jātimadāśrayo dravyam, guṇakarmāvṛttijātimaddravyam,
guṇāvṛttigaganavṛttijātimaddravyam,
guṇāvṛttikālavṛttijātimaddravyam,
guṇāvṛttidigvṛttijātimaddravyam,
guṇāvṛttighaṭavṛttijātimaddravyam| evaṃ yāvantyo
dravyavyaktayaḥ tāvanti dravyalakṣaṇāni|

tathā gaganavṛttisattāvāntarajātimaddravyam|
gaganavṛttisattāvyatiriktajātimaddravyam, gaganapadasthāne kālādipadaprakṣepeṇa yāvantyo dravyavyaktayaḥ tāvanti dravyalakṣaṇāni|

tathā kriyāsamānādhikaraṇasattāvāntarajātimaddravyam,
dravyasamānādhikaraṇasatāvāntarajātimaddravyam,
pūrvopapāditavaiyarthyāddhāraṇasāmarthye ca
kāryasamānādhikaraṇaṃ sattāvāntarajātimaddravyam,
guṇasamānādhikaraṇasattāvāntarajātimaddravyam, evaṃ
