kriyāpadasthāne rūpādipadaprakṣepeṇa
tattadrūpādivyaktipadaprakṣepeṇa ca guṇapadasthāne ca
saṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgapadaprakṣepeṇānantāni
dravyalakṣaṇāni| tathā sattāvāntarapadasthāne
sattāvyatiriktapadaprakṣepeṇānantāni dravyalakṣaṇāni| tathā
jātiniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratoyogyākāśavṛttijātimaddravyam,
ākāśādipadasthāne ca kālādipadaprakṣepeṇa yāvantyo
dravyavyaktayaḥ tāvanti dravyalakṣaṇāni draṣṭavyāni|

asmanmate tu kāryāśrayo dravyam, guṇāśrayo dravyam,
prāgabhāvāśrayo dravyam, pradhvaṃsābhāvāśrayo dravyam,
saṃkhyāśrayo dravyam, parimāṇāśrayo dravyam, pṛthaktvāśrayo
dravyam, saṃyogāśrayo dravyam, tathā saṃkhyādipadasthāne
ekatvādipadapadaprakṣepeṇānantāni dravyalakṣaṇāni| tathā
pṛthivyādinavalakṣaṇānyatamāśrayo dravyamiti|

kecittu
manyante kāryatvarahitāvṛttimadāśrayo dravyam,
guṇatvarahitāvṛttimadāśrayo dravyam,
saṃkhyātvarahitāvṛttimadāśrayo dravyam,
parimāṇarahitāvṛttimadāśrayo dravyam,
pṛthaktvarahitāvṛttimadāśrayo dravyam,
saṃyogatvarahitāvṛttimadāśrayo
dravyam, vibhāgatvarahitāvṛttimadāśrayo dravyam,
saṃkhyāpṛthaktvasthāne
caikatvaikapṛthaktvādipadaprakṣepeṇānantāni dravyalakṣaṇāni|
tathā kāryavattvarahitāvṛttimaddravyam,
guṇatvarahitāvṛttimaddravyam| tathā guṇapadasthāne
saṃkhyādipadaprakṣepeṇānantāni dravyalakṣaṇāni
draṣṭavyānīti|

kāryatvaṃ guṇavattvaṃ ca utpattikṣaṇe ghaṭādau
nāstītyavyāpakamiti cet---na, ghaṭādīnāṃ
kāryaguṇaprāgabhāvāśrayatve .api
kāryavattvaguṇavattvātyantābhāvarahitatvāllakṣyatvābhimataniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvasya
cāvyāpakaśabdārthatvāt| kāryaguṇayośca
dravyalakṣaṇatvānaṅgīkārāt| anaṅgīkāre vā
ghaṭādyutpattikṣaṇe paṭaniṣṭhakāryaguṇayoḥ
prāgabhāvasambhave
.apyatyantābhāva[a]sambhavenāvyāpakatvānupapatteḥ|
lakṣyatvābhimataniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvasyāvyāpakatvaśabdārthatvāt|
ghaṭādāvutpattikṣaṇe
kāryaguṇaprāgabhāvavatkāryavattvaguṇavattvayorabhāvo .astyeva|
sa cātyantābhāva eva, kāryavattvaguṇavattvayoranāditvena
prāgabhāvāsambhavāt| tataśca lakṣaṇasyāvyāpakatvamiti
cet---na, atyantābhāvasya pratiyogisamānadeśatvānupapatteḥ|
tatkiṃ prathamakṣaṇe .api ghaṭādau kāryavattvaṃ guṇavattvaṃ
cāstyeva, bāḍhaṃ, kāryaguṇayorabhāve kathaṃ kāryavattvaṃ
guṇavattvaṃ ceti cet---kāryaguṇābhyāmavyāpakatvāditi sarvaṃ
sundaram|

nanu lakṣaṇasya prayojanaṃ kiṃ dravyajñānam,
dravyaśabdavācyatvajñānaṃ vā, dravyāpahāro vā,
itara[dra]vyāvṛttadravyajñānaṃ vā,
guṇatvādirahitatvaviśiṣṭajñānatvaṃ vā| nādyaḥ,
dravyatvasākṣātka[a]raṇasyendriyādevopapatteḥ|
dravyatvānumiteśca liṅgādevopapatteḥ| dravyalakṣaṇameva
dravyatvaliṅgamiti cet---na,
dravyatvādi[pra]siddhāvaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt, siddhau vā
sādhanavaiyarthyāt| na dvitīyaḥ, dravyaśabdavācyatvāsiddhau
aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt, siddhau vā sādhanavaiyarthyāt,
dravyaśabdavācyādravyatvādvyāvṛttatvenāsādhāraṇatvācca|
dravyatvamapi pakṣa eveti cet---na, tatra guṇāvasyāprasiddhatvāt|
prasiddhavyavahārādeva dravyaśabdavācyatvasiddheśca| na
tṛtīyaḥ, dravyajñānādeva dravyārthinaḥ pravṛttisiddheḥ
dravyaśabdajñānādeva dravyaśabdaprayogatajjanyajñānopapatteḥ|
na caturthaḥ, itaraśabdena na dravyavatīṃ
lakṣaṇavatīmapyupādāne vyāghātāt, bādhācca|
adravyalakṣaṇarahitopādāne ca
dravyalakṣaṇarahitarasādivyāvṛttimato rūpādeḥ sapakṣādapi
vyāvṛttatvena dravyalakṣaṇasyāsādhāraṇatvaprasaṅgāt|

dravyalakṣaṇarahitapadena sarvaṃ dravyalakṣaṇarahitaṃ
vivakṣitamiti cet---na, sarvaśabdena
tvadadhigatamātravivakṣāyāṃ

tvadadhigatadravyalakṣaṇarahitavyāvṛttimato
.asmadādyadhigatadravyalakṣaṇarahitatvāt| sapakṣādapi
vyāvṛttatvena dravyalakṣaṇatvasyāsādhāraṇatvaprasaṅgāt|
tvadadhigatānadhigatavivakṣāyāṃ ca | anadhigate
vivakṣānupapatteḥ| anadhigatavyāvṛtteḥ
adhigantumaśakyatvenāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvācca|
dravyalakṣaṇarahitaṃ sarvaṃ mayādhigamyata iti cet---na,
asannikṛṣṭe tvadindriyāpravṛtteḥ| ananusaṃhitaliṅge ca
tavānumi[tyasambhavāt]|
 [a]pi ca kimiha
dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktipratiyogikaikāvyāvṛttiḥ sādhyate,
dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktipratiyogikāḥ sarvā vyāvṛttayo
vā| nādyaḥ, pratiyogi dravyalakṣaṇarahita
[prati]yogikaikavyāvṛtterasambhavāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
sarvavyāvṛttivirahāṇāmekatrāsambhavena sādhyābhāvasya
sādhanābhāvena vyāptyasambhavāt|

atha dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktivyāvṛttivirahāṇāmekatra
a[nā]sambhava iti cet---sarveṣāmapi dravyalakṣaṇarahita
svasvavyāvṛttirahitatve .api svānyasravyarahitavyāvṛttimattvena
tadrahitatvāsambhavāt| etasya ca
dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktipratiyogikasarvavyāvṛttivirahasya
niṣpramāṇakatvāt| pratiyogibhedasyābhāvabhedaniyatatvācca|
udayanādimate
cābhāvasya bhāvatvena
dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktivyāvṛttivirahāṇāṃ
dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktivyatiriktatvābhāvena
dravyalakṣaṇarahite sādhyābhāvasya sādhanābhāvena
vyāptyasambhavācca| api ca dravyalakṣaṇrahitaḥ sarvavyaktibhedaḥ
kvacidapramitaḥ pramito vā| nādyaḥ aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt|
dvitīye .api pakṣādanyatra, pakṣe vā| nādyaḥ, tatra
hetorvṛttāvanvayasambhavena kevalavyatirekitvabhaṅgāt| vyāvṛttau
ca sapakṣādapi vyāvṛttatvenāsādhāraṇatvāpātāt| na
dvitīyaḥ sādhanavaiyarthyaprasaṅgāt|
pratyakṣadravyeṣvitarabhedasya pratyakṣasiddhatvāt [|]
nāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam, apratyakṣadravyeṣvapi
itarabhedasādhanānna sādhanavaiyarthyamiti cet---na,
pratyakṣadravyeṣveva guṇavattvādīnāmitarabhedānvayaniścayāt,
kevalavyatirekitvabhaṅgāt| anumānapravṛtteḥ
pūrvamanvayaniścayābhāvasya
vyatirekiniścayājanyānvayaniścayābhāvasya vā
kevalaśabdārthatvāt| sapakṣe .anvayaniścayābhāvaḥ
kevalaśabdārtha iti cet---na, sapakṣaviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyāt,
sapakṣānirukteśca| niścitasādhyaḥ sapakṣa iti cet---na,
sādhyavattvena viśeṣaṇaprasiddhisthalasyāpi sapakṣatvāt|
pakṣavyatirikto niścitasādhyavānsapakṣa

iti cet---na, pakṣavyatiriktatvaviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyāt|
asminnapakṣatvena sopādhitvācca| viśeṣaṇaprasiddhisthala eva
sādhyavyāpakatvaniścayāt,
dravyalakṣaṇavadapratyakṣadravyavyāvṛttatvena
sādhanāvyāpakatvāt|
itarabhedarahitaparamāṇurūpādivyāvṛttatvena
pakṣetaratvābhāvācca| pratyakṣadravyeṣu pratyakṣeṇa
dravyalakṣaṇarahitapratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttiniścayāsambhavācca|
pratyakṣadravyeṣu
dravyalakṣaṇarahitapratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttayaḥ pratyakṣeṇa
dravyalakṣaṇarahitāpratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttayaścānumānena
niścīyanta iti cet---na,
dravyalakṣaṇarahitāpratyakṣānantavyaktivyāvṛttiniyatānantaliṅgānanusandhānāt|
pratyakṣadravyeṣvapi
dravyalakṣaṇarahitapratyakṣāpratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttīnāṃ
pratyakṣānumānābhyāmeva niścayopapattervyatirekivaiyarthyasya
tadavasthatvācca| apratyakṣaniṣṭho [.a]bhāvo na pratyakṣa iti
cet---na, apratyakṣagaganādiniṣṭhaghaṭābhāvādīnāṃ
bhūtalādau pratyakṣatvāt| apratyakṣe dharmiṇi bhāvaḥ (?)
pratyakṣa iti cet---na, vāyau rūpābhāvapratyakṣatvasya
udayanādibhirapyaṅgīkārāt|

adhikaraṇajñānapratiyogijñānendriyasannikarṣādipratyakṣapratiyogikābhāvapratyakṣasāmagrīsampattāvapi
pratyakṣānupapatterunmattapralāpatvācca| apratyakṣadravyeṣu
dravyalakṣaṇarahitapratyakṣāpratyakṣavyāvṛttīnāmapratyakṣatve
.api dravyavyāvṛttiniścaye .api
dravyalakṣaṇarahitapratyakṣavyaktitvarahitāpratyakṣavyāvṛttivadanumātuṃ
śakyatvācca pratyakṣadravyeṣu vyatirekivaiyarthyasya
tadavasthatvācca|

sarvadravyapakṣīkaraṇe
dravyatvasyetarabhedavyāpyatvamitarabhedasya dravyatvavyāpakatvaṃ
vyatirekiṇā sidhyatīti na vyatirekivaiyarthyamiti cet---na,
itarabhedenaiva dravyatvasyetarabhedavyāpyatvenetarabhedasya ca
dravyatvavyāpakatvena vyatirekiṇo vyāptivirahāt|
pakṣadharmāyāśca vyāpakapakṣasaṃsargātiriktasādhakatvāt|
sarvaśabdāvacchinnadharmiviśeṣaṇaṃ vidhīyamānadharmavyāpyaṃ
yathā, sarvaḥ paṭo rakta ityatra paṭatvaṃ raktatvavyāpyam|
vidhīyamānadharmatvasarvapaṭāvacchinnadharmaviśeṣaṇavyāpako
yathā raktaḥ paṭa ityatra raktatvaṃ paṭa ityasyeti cet---na, asya
mānāntaratvāt, sarvadravyapakṣīkaraṇe sarvadravyasambandha
itarabhedasya pramāṇāntarānavagato vyatirekiṇā sidhyatīti na
vyatirekivaiyarthyamiti cet---na,
sambandhidravyabhedenetarabhedasarvadravyasambandhānāmanekatvāt|
sarvadravyasambandhā evetarabhedasya sambandhasya ca
guṇavattvādereva
pratyakṣadravyāvyavasiterabhedānvayātsiddherayugapatsakaladravyānāmitarabhedo
na vyatirekiṇamantareṇa

sidhyatīti na vyatirekivaiyarthyamiti cet---na, avyāpakasya
vyāpakatvasaṃsargātiriktasya ca yogapadasya
vyatirekiṇāpyasiddheḥ
asakaladravyapakṣīkaraṇapravṛttavyatirekiṇamantareṇetarabhedaviśiṣṭasakaladravyagicaraikā
pratītirna jāyata iti na dravyavyatirekivaiyarthyamiti cet---na,
vyatirekiprameyasya pramāṇāntarādavagatatvena
pratigatasyaikatvasya ca niṣprayojanatve tadvyatirekivaiyarthyasya
tadavasthatvāt| ekaikasyaiva
dharmitvenaikaikadravyaparyavasitetarabhedapratītīnāmanekatvenetarabhedaviśiṣṭasakaladravyagocaraikapratītyasambhavācca|

pratyakṣadravyeṣvitarabhedasākṣātkārasya pratyakṣādutpannatve
.api ma vyatirekivaiyarthyamitarabhedānutpatteḥ phalasya sattvāditi
cet---na, pratyakṣadravyāṇāṃ sandigdhasādhyatvābhāvena
pakṣatvānupapattau taddharmasyāpakṣadharmatvena
anumitiphalatvānupapatteḥ| sandigdhasādhyatvaṃ na pakṣatvam, kiṃ
tu vipratipattigocaratvamiti cet---na, niścitetarabhedeṣu
pratyakṣadravyeṣvitarabhedavipratipatterabhāvāt|
dharmitvenopāttatvam, na tvanyaditi cet---na,
ubhayavādisampratipannasādhyavattvena pratyakṣadravyāṇāṃ paraṃ
prati dharmitvenopādānānupapatteriti cet---na,
pratyakṣadravyāṇāṃ sandigdhasādhyā[tvā?]bhāvena
pakṣatvānupapattau
taddharmasyāpakṣadharmatvenānumitiphalatvānupapatteḥ
arthāntarasya durvāratvāt| svārthānumāne ca
dharmyupādānābhāvena hetorapakṣadharmatvaprasaṅgāt|
dharmitvameva pakṣatvamiti cet---na, pratyakṣanirṇītavahnau
mahānasādau dhūmavattvena vahnyanumityanudayāt| upapadyata eva
tatra vahnyanumitiriti cet---na, pramāṇābhāvāt| mahānase ca
vahnimanuminomītyanuvyavasāyābhāvādvahnivyavahārasya

ca pratyakṣādevopapatteḥ| vahnyanumitisāmagryā tadanumitiriti
cet---na, sādhyasandehasya sādhyanirṇayasya
vānumitikāraṇasyāsambhavāt|
dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktivyāvṛttīnāmanyonyadravyavyāvṛttalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktiṣveva
prasiddhatvānnāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam, dravyeṣu vā sādhanānna
sādhanavaiyarthyamiti cet---na, itaravyāvṛttīnāṃ prasiddhatve
.api tadvirahāṇāmekatrāsambhavena sādhyābhāvasya
sādhanābhāvena vyāptyasambhavāt
itaravyaktīnāmitaravyāvṛttigrahaṇakāla
evetaravyāvṛttigrahaṇapramāṇena
dravyāṇāmapītaravyāvṛttigrahaṇasambhavena
vyatirekivaiyarthyasya tadavasthatvācca|

dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktipratiyogikasarvavyāvṛttimattvaṃ
sādhyam, tadabhāvasya caikatvātsādhanābhāvena vyāptirghaṭata
iti cet---na,
dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktipratiyogikasarvavyāvṛttimattvasyāprasiddhatvāt|
itarabhedasyāprasiddhatve .api tadabhāvasya bhāvatvena
vyatirekavyāpti [u]papatternāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvamatra
doṣa iti cet---na,
itarabhedapadābhilapyadravyalakṣaṇrahitasarvavyaktipratiyogikasarvavyāvṛttivirahāṇāmekatrāsambhavena
sādhanābhāva pañcamaḥ
guṇatvakarmatvajāti[tva]viśeṣatvasamavāyatvābhāvatvavirahābhāvānāṃ
guṇatvādīnāmekatvābhāvena sādhyābhāvasya sādhanābhāvena
vyāptyasambhavāt|
guṇatvakarmatvajātitvaviśeṣatvasamavāyatvābhāva[vat]tvapratiyogikaṣaḍatyantābhāvavattvavirahasya
ca guṇatvādivyatirekiṇo bhāvasya pratiyogiprasiddhimantareṇa
grahītumaśakyatvena sādhanābhāvena vyāptigrahaṇāsambhavāt|
dravyaguṇatvādirahitamiti pratijñāvākyādeva sādhyama[na]vagamya
sādhyābhāvasya sādhanābhāvena vyāptimavadhārya
pratipāditavyatirekiṇā pakṣe sādhyamanuminotīti cet---na,
pratijñāpadābhyāṃ
parasparāsaṃsṛṣṭidharmisādhyaparatayoccaritābhyāṃ
saṃsargānavagamāt| saṃsargāvagame .api
vaikaikaguṇatvādivirahasaṃsargasyaivāvagamāt|
guṇatvādivirahaṣaṭkavattvasādhyānapa[va]gamāt| a[va]game .api
vā pratijñāvākyāvagatasādhyasya niṣpramāṇakatvena
tadabhāvasyāpi niṣpramāṇakatvāt| pratijñāvākyasya ca
pramāṇavattve vyatirekivaiyarthyāt| aprasiddhameva sādhyaṃ
vyatirekī sādhayati| aprasiddhameva sādhyasaṃsargamanvayīti
cet---na, sādhyāprasiddhau tadabhāvasyāprasiddhatvena
vyatirekagrahaṇāsambhavāt|
aprāmāṇikasādhyapratiyogikābhāvasya ca sarvatra vidyamānatvena
sādhanapratipakṣa eva sādhanābhāvena vyāptibhaṅgācca|
vyatirekipramāṇatvātsādhyasya tadabhāvenāprāmāṇika iti
cet---na, vyatirekiprāmāṇyasiddheḥ|

śrīkarastu
guṇatvātyantābhāva[vaḥ]
karmatvātyantābhāvādipañcakasamānādhikaraṇatvātiriktaguṇatvātyantābhāvaniṣṭhatvarahitatvāt[vān]
prameyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi guṇatvātyantābhāvasya
karmatvātyantābhāvapañcakasamānādhikaraṇatvaṃ prasādhya
dravya[vyaṃ]karmatvātyantābhāvapañcakasamānādhikaraṇaguṇatvātyantābhāvavat,
guṇatvāt| na yadevaṃ na tadevamiti prayoktavyamityāha---tanna,
karmatvātyantābhāvapañcakasamānādhikaraṇaguṇatvātyantābhāvavataḥ

karmādeḥ sapakṣādapi vyāvṛttatvena guṇavattvādeḥ
sādhāraṇatvasya durvāratvāt| pakṣe
karmatvātyantābhāvapañcakasiddheḥ sādhanavaiyarthyācca| yatra
kvacana guṇatvābhāvasya
karmatvātyantābhāvapañcakasamānādhikaraṇatva .api
karmatvātyantābhāvapañcakasamānādhikaraṇatvasya
viśeṣaṇasādhakapramāṇādeva siddhatvācca| pakṣe
guṇatvātyantābhāvapakṣasya ca
guṇatvātyantābhāvavadityetāvataiva siddheḥ
karmatvātyantābhāvapañcakasamānādhikaraṇatvaviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyādguṇatvātyantābhāvavattve
sādhye karmādeḥ sapakṣādapi vyāvṛttatvena
guṇavattvāderasādhāraṇatvaṃ syāditi cet---na,
etadviśeṣaṇopādāne .apyasādhāraṇatvasya tadavasthatvena
sādhyagataviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyasya vajralepāyamānatvāt|
guṇātyantābhāvaḥ
karmatvātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvātyantābhāvātiriktaguṇatvātyantābhāvaniṣṭhatvarahitavān
prameyatvāt, paṭavaditi viśeṣaṇasādhakasya
satpratipakṣatvācca|

śivādityamiśrāstu
guṇatvātyantābhāvasya
karmatvātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvānumānena
dravyaviśeṣānavacchinnaṃ
guṇatvādipratiyogikaṣaḍatyantābhāvatvaṃ prasādhya dravyaṃ
guṇatvādipratiyogikaṣaḍatyantābhāvavat, guṇatva[a][va]t| na
yadevaṃ na tadevamiti vyatirekiṇaṃ pravartayati| tadapi na,
viśeṣaṇasādhakānumānasya
satpratipakṣatvādisakaladoṣagrastatvāt| prapañcitaṃ
caitanmahāvidyāviḍambane|
tasmānniṣprayojanāni lakṣaṇapratipādakāni
sūtrāṇi
nārambhaṇīyānīti| tadasundaram, dravyatvasiddheḥ
dravyalakṣaṇaprayojanatvātpratyakṣadravyeṣu
dravyatvasākṣātkārasya guṇavattvādyavagamasahitendriyajanyatvam,
asati guṇavattvādyavagame satyapīndriyāderghaṭapaṭādau dravyaṃ
dravyamityanugatāparokṣapratītyanudayāt| apratyakṣadravyeṣu
dravyatvānumiteranvayavyatirekiguṇavattvādidravyalakṣaṇajanyatvāt|
na cātra pṛthivītvagandhavattvarasavattvaśukla[vat]tvādayaḥ
upādhayaḥ pratyakṣāvāsitadravyatve calanādau
sādhya[a]vyāpakatvabhaṅgāt| nāpi
rūpavattvasparśavattvabhūtatvendriyatvaviśeṣaguṇavattvakāryavattvāsamatpratyakṣatvādayaḥ,
kālo dravyatvātiriktakālaniṣṭhatvarahitavān, meyatvāditi
kālaniṣṭhadravyatvasiddheḥ kāle sādhyavyāpakatvabhaṅgāt| na
caivaṃ kāle pūrvānumānavaiyarthyaṃ tadatiriktānāmeva
dharmitvenopādānāt| yadvā kālo
dravyatvātiriktakālaniṣṭhatvarahitavān, guṇatvāt,
paṭavadityādinā kevalānvayinā
dravyalakṣaṇenāpratyakṣadravyeṣu dravyatvānumānāt| na
cātrāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam,
dravyatvātiriktatvaviśiṣṭakālatvarahitasya rūpāderghaṭādau
pratyakṣasiddhatvāt| na ca sādhanavaiyarthyam, apratyakṣadravyeṣu
pratyakṣeṇa dravyatvāsiddheḥ| na cāsya sopādhitvādisambhavaḥ
kevalānvayitvādeva| astu vā pratyakṣadravyeṣu dravyatvasiddhiḥ
kevalavyatirekiṇā dravyalakṣaṇena| na cātra
vidyamānasapakṣatvādasādhāraṇatā, guṇavattvādeḥ
sapakṣādavyāvṛtteḥ| nāpyanvayasambhavena vyatirekibhaṅgaḥ,
sādhyaprasiddherevānvayaniścayānupajīvyatvena
kevalavyatirekipravṛtteḥ
pūrvamavaśyambhāvenānvayaniścayadaśāyāṃ
kevalavyatirekitvopapatteḥ| astu vā
sarvadravyasiddhi[r]dravyalakṣaṇādeva kevalavyatirekiṇaḥ| na
cāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam,
sādhyāprasiddheranumānāṅgavaikalyarūpatvābhāvāt,
anumānāṅgavaikalyānāpādakatvācca| sādhyāprasiddhiḥ
sādhyasaṃdehaṃ vighaṭayantī pakṣadharmatābhaṅge paryavaśyati
sandigdhasādhyasa[sya]pakṣatvāditi cet---na,
sādhyasandehasyānumānā[na]ṅgatvāt,
sādhya[a]nirṇayādevānumānopapatteḥ, anirṇītasādhyasyaiva
pakṣatvāt| anyathā vyutpannaviparyastayoranumityudayaprasaṅgāt|
sādhyāprasiddhau sādhyābhāvasya sādhanābhāvena
vyāptyanavagamāt vyatirekyapravṛttiriti cet---na,
sādhyābhāvaniṣṭhasādhanābhāvavyāpteḥ sādhananiṣṭhatvena
sādhyānumityajanakatvasya pūrvamevāvi[ve]ditatvāt|
sādhananiṣṭhasādhyavyāptereva sādhyānumitijanakatvāt|
vyatirekavyāptyanavagatāpya[vapyu]pāyābhāvāt guṇavattvasya
dravyatvena vyāptigrahaṇāsambhava iti cet---na,
pratyakṣasyevopāyatvāt guṇavattvaniṣṭhadravyatvavyāpteḥ
pratyakṣasyānvayādinā pratyakṣasyānvayādināpyaṅgīkārāt|
dravyatvāgrahe drāvyatvena guṇatvasya vyāptiḥ pratyakṣeṇa na
gṛhyata iti cet---kiṃ dravyāśritatvāttadagrahe na gṛhyata iti
vivakṣitam,
dravyatvānavacchinnatvāddravyajñānatvajñānajanyajñānaviṣayatvādvā,
dravyatvajñānājanyajñānāviṣayatvādvā,
dravyatvaviṣayajñānaviṣayatvādvā,
dravyatvāviṣayajñānāviṣayatvādvā| nādyaḥ, dravyatvavyāpteḥ
dravyatvavyāpyatvāparaparyāyaguṇavattvaniṣṭhāyā
dravyatvānāśritatvāt| dvitīye .api dravyatvāvacchīnatvaṃ kiṃ
dravyatvaviśiṣṭatvaṃ dravyatvavaiśiṣṭhyarūpatvaṃ vā|
nādyaḥ, dravyatvaviśiṣṭānāmapi ghaṭādīnāṃ dravyatvāgrahe
.api grahaṇāt| na dvitīyaḥ, dravyamātravṛtte dyāt|
dravyaguṇapañcavṛttervyāpyatvasya bhinnatvāt| na tṛtīyaḥ,
dravyajñānajanyadravyatvaviśiṣṭājñānaviṣayasyāpi
ghaṭāderdravyatvāgrahe .api grahaṇāt| na caturthaḥ,
mānābhāvādasiddheḥ, dravyatvavyāpyatvaṃ
dravyatvajñānājanya prameyatvāt, paṭavadityanumānācca| na
pañcamaḥ, ghaṭāderiva dravyatvaviṣayajñānaviṣayasyāpi
dravyatvaviṣayajñānaviṣayatvāt| na ṣaṣṭhaḥ,
mānābhāvādasiddheḥ| dravyatvavyāpyatvaṃ
dravyatvāviṣayajñānaviṣayaḥ dravyatvānyatvāt, paṭavat|
dravyatvāvyāpyatvāviṣayajñānaviṣayaḥ, meyatvāt,
paṭavadityanumānācca| na caivamādīnāṃ dravyatvābhāve
vyabhicāraḥ, tasya pakṣatulyatvātsapakṣābhāvātkutra
vyāptirgṛhyata iti cet---na, vyāptyāśrayapraśne guṇavattva
ityuttaram, grahaṇāśrayatvapraśne cātmanītyuttaram|
guṇavattvaṃ pakṣaḥ sapakṣo veti cet---na,
sādhyadravyatvarahitatvena tasya vipakṣatvāt, sapakṣe
vyāptirgṛhyate na tu pakṣa iti cet---na, anvayitvapakṣe .api
dravyatvābhāva iti guṇavattva eva vyāpyatvagrahaṇāt| anyatra
sattvāt| sapakṣe vyāptirgṛhyate iti prasiddheśca
sapakṣāvagatasādhananiṣṭhatvagrahaṇābhiprāyeṇopapatteḥ|
tathāpi sapakṣāvagatasādhananiṣṭhatayā vyāptirgṛhyate| na tu
pakṣāvagatatatsādhananiṣṭhatayeti cet---na,
avagatasādhananiṣṭhatayā vyāptigrahaṇopapattau
sādhanagatasapakṣatvasyānupayogitvāt| dravyatvavyāpyatvaṃ nāma
dravyatvavanmātravṛttitvaṃ
dravyatvātyantābhāvavṛttitvātyantābhāvo vā| tatra dvayamapi
dravyatvāvagamenāvagantumaśakyamiti cet---na, uktottaratvāt|
kevalānvayinyasambhavena cānayorvyāpyatvarūpānaṅgīkārācca|
kiṃ tarhi dravyatvavyāpyatvamiti cet---dravyatvānumitijanako
guṇavattvaniṣṭho dharmaviśeṣaḥ| sa ca dravyatvānavagame
.apyavagantuṃ śakyata eva| parvataniṣṭhavahniviśeṣānavagame

.api
parvataniṣṭhavahniviśeṣānumitijanakaparvataniṣṭhadhūmaviśeṣaniṣṭhaparvataniṣṭhavahnivyāpyatvavat|
anyathā parvataniṣṭhadhūmaviśeṣeṇa kevalavyatirekiṇā
parvataniṣṭhavahniviśeṣānumityudayaprasaṅgāt| anvayinā
dhūmavattvena
pakṣadharmatābalātparvataniṣṭhavahniviśeṣaṇasiddheḥ
nirākariṣyamāṇatvāt|

yadvā sādhyaviśeṣasamarpakatayā dravyalakṣaṇaṃ
dravyatvānumitiṃ janayati, na liṅgatayā| ayaṃ ghaṭa
etadvṛttidravyalakṣaṇavyāvṛttatvarahitaitadvṛttidravyalakṣaṇarahitavṛttitvānadhikaraṇajātimān,
jātimattvādityādau sādhyasya dravyalakṣaṇagatatvāt| na ca
prakārāntareṇāpi pratyakṣasiddherapyavaiyarthyam,
tasyānupajīvyatvāt| anyathānenāpi prakāreṇa dravyatvasiddheḥ
prakārāntareṇa vaiyarthyasya duṣparihāratvāt| yadapi dravyatvaṃ
dravyalakṣaṇam, tadāpi dravyatvasiddhirlakṣaṇaprayojanam,
dravyatvanirvikalpakasya dravyatvajanyatvāt| dravyatvasavikalpakasya
ca dravyatvatadavagamajanyatvāt| apratyakṣadravyeṣu ca
dravyatvānumiteḥ dravyatvāvagamajanyatvācca|
dravyaśabdavācyatvādisiddhirvā prayojanam,
tasmāddravyatvasiddhirdravyalakṣaṇaprayojanam|

yadvā, dravyaśabdavācyatvasiddhirdravyalakṣaṇaprayojanam| tathā
ca pṛthivyādayo dravyaśabdavācyāḥ, guṇavattvāt| na yadevaṃ na
tadevam, yathā rūpamityādi| na cātrāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam,
dravyapadaṃ vācyavat, padatvāt, gopadavaditi
viśeṣaṇaprasiddheḥ| na caivaṃ
vidyamānasapakṣatvāddravyalakṣaṇasyāsādhāraṇatvaṃ
sapakṣāvyāvṛttyaniścayāt| nāpyanvayasambhavena
kevalavyatirekitvabhaṅgaḥ, sapakṣe hetorvṛttyaniścayāt| nāpi
vyatirekivaiyarthyam, pakṣe dravyaśabdavācyatvasya
viśeṣakasādhanānumānenāniścayāt| nāpi vṛddhavyavahārādeva
dravyaśabdavācyatvasiddheḥ vyatirekivaiyarthyam,
tasyānupajīvyatvāt| anyathā vyatirekiṇaiva
dravyaśabdavācyatvasiddheḥ vṛddhavyavahāravaiyarthyasya
duṣparihāratvāt| astu vā pratyakṣadravyeṣu
dravyaśabdavācyatvasiddhiḥ vṛddhavyavahārāt|
apratyakṣadravyeṣu na dravyaśabdavācyatvasiddhiḥ,
kevalavyatirekiṇo dravyalakṣaṇatvāt| na cātra
vidyamānasapakṣatvenāsādhāraṇatā tato hetorniścayāt|
nāpyanvayasambhavena kevalavyatirekitvabhaṅgaḥ
viśeṣaṇapravṛtterivānvayaprasiddheranupajīvyatvena
tadabhāvadaśāyāṃ kevalavyatirekitvasambhavāt| nāpi
sādhanavaiyarthyam, apratyakṣadravyeṣu vṛddhavyavahārābhāvāt|
bhāve .api vā tasyānupajīvyatvāt| na cātra pūrvoktā
upādhayaḥ pūrvoktādeva nyāyāditi|

astu vā pratyakṣadravyeṣu
dravyaśabdavācyatvasiddhiranvayavyatirekiṇo dravyalakṣaṇāt| na
cātrāsmatpratyakṣādaya upādhayaḥ, pūrvoktādeva nyāyāt| na
copādhiviraheṇa satpratipakṣatvam, dravyalakṣaṇābhāve .api na
sopādhitvāditi| astu vā pratyakṣadravyeṣu
dravyaśabdavācyatvasiddhiḥ, kevalānvayino dravyalakṣaṇāt|
paramāṇudravyaśabdavācyatvavyatiriktaparamāṇuniṣṭhatvarahitavān,
guṇavattvāt ghaṭavadityādeḥ sarvatra pratyakṣadravyeṣu

sulabhatvāditi bhedāparaparyāya
dravyalakṣaṇarahitavyāvṛttimattvasiddhiḥ|

na ca dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktīnāmavagantumaśakyatvena
tadvyāvṛttimattvasyāprasiddheḥ aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā,
yāvatpratipannadravyalakṣaṇarahitavyaktivyāvṛttimattvasyaiva
sādhyatvāt| nāpi
yāvatpratipannadravyatvalakṣaṇarahitavyaktivyāvṛttimattvasya
kvacidaprasiddhatvādaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā, ghaṭādāveva
prasiddhatvāt|
dravyalakṣaṇarahitapratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttimattvasyānumānasiddhatvāt|
na ca svayaṃ
pratyakṣānumānāvagatadravyalakṣaṇarahitavyaktivyāvṛttimantaḥ
parāvagatadravyalakṣaṇarahitānsapakṣādapi
vyāvṛttatvādasādhāraṇatā, tasya svayaṃ
sādhyavattva[tta]yānavagatatvena tato hetuvyāvṛttyaniścayāt| na
ca ghaṭe
dravyalakṣaṇarahitānantapratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttiniyataliṅgāsambhavaḥ,
ghaṭadravyalakṣaṇarahitānantāpratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttimattvamabhyupagacchatā
pareṇāpi tanniyataliṅgasyāvaśyaṃ vaktavyatvāt| nāpi
sādhanavaiyarthyam,
ghaṭādiniṣṭhadravyalakṣaṇarahitapratyakṣāpratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttimattvasādhanapratyakṣānumānābhyāṃ
pratyakṣadravyeṣu
dravyalakṣaṇapratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttimattvāniścayātpratyakṣadravyaniṣṭhadravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvapratyakṣāpratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttatvaniścayasya
cānupajīvyatvāt|

astu vā dravyalakṣaṇarahitapratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttimattvameva
sādhyam| na ca cāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā, ghaṭa eva
sādhyaprasiddheḥ| na ca vidyamānasapakṣatvādasādhāraṇatā,
hetoḥ sapakṣādavyāvṛttatvāt| nāpi sapakṣe
anvayaniścayasambhavena kevalavyatirekitvabhaṅgaḥ,
sādhyaprasiddherivānvayaniścayasyānupajīvyatvena
kevalvyatirekipravṛtteḥ pūrvamavaśyaṃbhāvābhāvena
tadabhāvadaśāyāṃ kevalavyatirekipravṛtterdurvāratvāt| nāpi
sādhanavaiyarthyam, pratyakṣadravyeṣu
dravyalakṣaṇrahitasarvavyaktivyāvṛttimattvasādhakapratyakṣeṇāpratyakṣadravyeṣu
dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvapratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttimattvāniścayāt|
apratyakṣadravyeṣu
dravyalakṣaṇarahitapratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttimattvasya[a]pratyakṣeṇāgrahaṇācca|
grahaṇe .api vā tasyānupajīvyatvāt| nāpi ghaṭādau
vyatirekivaiyarthyam, teṣāṃ dharmitvenānupajīvyatvāt| astu vā
ghaṭavyatiriktapratyakṣadravyāṇāṃ gharmitvam, tathāpi
sādhanavaiyarthyam,
ghaṭaniṣṭhadravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvapratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttimattvāparokṣānubhavasya
ca
ghaṭādiniṣṭhadravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvapratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttimattvāgocaratvāt|
astu vā ghaṭasya dharmitvam, tathāpi na sādhanavaiyarthyam,
ghaṭaniṣṭhasādhyaprasiddheranupajīvyatvāt|
ghaṭādiniṣṭhasādhyatvaprasiddheścopajīvyatve .api
ghaṭaniṣṭhasādhyāgocaratvāt| na ca
dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktīnāmavagantumaśakyatvena
tadvyāvṛttimattvasyāprasiddheraprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā
dravyalakṣaṇrahitasarvavayaktīnāmanavagame .api
tadvyāvṛttīnāmindriyasannikṛṣṭānāṃ ghaṭaniṣṭhatvena
grahaṇopapatteḥ vināpi pratiyogigrahaṇamabhāvarūpasya
upapāditatvāt|
parapratyakṣāvagatadravyalakṣaṇarahitavyaktivyāvṛttimattvasyaiva
vā sādhyatvāt| na ca
dravyalakṣaṇarahitapratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttimattvasādhane
paramāṇurūpādeḥ sapakṣādapi vyāvṛttatvena
dravyalakṣaṇasyāsādhāraṇatā|
apratyakṣadravyavadapratyakṣaguṇānāmapi aniścitasādhyavattvena
sapakṣatvāsambhavāt, pramāṇāntarāt
tatsādhyavattvaniścaye ca tataḥ pūrvaṃ vyatirekipravṛttena
durvāratvāt| na ca
dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvapratyakṣavyaktivyāvṛttivirahāṇāmekatra
sambhavādvyatirekānupapattiḥ|
dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktivyāvṛttimattvasya sādhyatvāt|
tadabhāvasyaikasya guṇādau sarvatra sambhavāt| etena
dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktivyāvṛttivirahāṇāṃ
dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktirūpāṇāmekatrāsambhavādvyatirekānupapattirityapi
na yuktam| dravyalakṣaṇarahitapratyakṣasarvavyaktipratiyoginyekā
vyāvṛttiḥ sādhyate| na
caivaṃvidhasādhyāprasiddheraprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā, iyaṃ
rūpavyāvṛttiḥ
etadanyadravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktipratiyogikatvarahitadravyalakṣaṇarahitavyāvṛttyanyā
prameyatvāt, ghaṭavadityādinā sādhyaprasiddheḥ| santu vā
dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvapratyakṣavyaktipratiyoginyaḥ
sarvavyāvṛttayaḥ sādhyāḥ| na caivamaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā,
dravyalakṣaṇarahitapratyakṣasarvavyaktivyāvṛttīnāmekatrāprasiddhatve
.api
parasparavyāvṛttadravyalakṣaṇarahitapratyakṣavyāvṛttiṣveva
pratyakṣasiddhatvāt, na cāsya vyatirekāsambhavo doṣa[ḥ]
[tasyānumā]nānaṅgatvāt|
dravyalakṣaṇarahitapratyakṣasarvavyaktipratiyogikasarvavyāvṛttivyāpyatvasya
ca pratyakṣeṇāvagantumaśakyatvāt, sādhyaprasiddheḥ sambhavāt|
sādhyāprasiddhāvapi tadvyāpyatvagrahaṇasyopapāditatvācca| na
cātra pūrvoktā upādhayaḥ pūrvoktādeva nyāyāt|
dravyalakṣaṇarahitapratyakṣasarvavyaktipratiyogikaikavyāvṛtteśca
dharmaviśeṣānavacchedenaiva siddherupādheḥ
sādhyavyāpakatvāniścayācca|
dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktipratiyogikasarvavyāvṛttīnāmekatrāsambhavenopādheḥ
sādhyavyāpakatāniścayācca| na cātra prameyatvādibhiḥ
satpratipakṣatvam, sādhyaprasiddhisthale ghaṭādau vyabhicārāt|
nāpyasmatpratyakṣādiviraheṇa satpratipakṣatvam,
dravyalakṣaṇaviraheṇa sopādhitvāt| na cāsya pakṣetaratā,
sādhyaprasiddhisthalādghaṭādervipakṣavyāvṛttatvāditi|
tasmādapratyakṣadravyāṇi
dravyalakṣaṇarahitasarvavyaktipratiyogikasarvavyāvṛttimanti
guṇatvāt, na yadevaṃ na tadevam, yathā rūpamityādi
pratiyoktavyamiti|

yadvā
guṇatvakarmatvajātitvaviśeṣatvasamavāyatvābhāvatvapratiyogikaṣaḍatyantābhāvavattvasiddhirdravyalakṣaṇaprayojanam|
na cātrāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā, ghaṭādau sādhyaprasiddheḥ|
nāpi sādhanavaiyarthyādayaḥ, pūrvoktādeva nyāyāt| astu vā
guṇavattvātyantābhāvādirekaika eva sādhyaḥ| na
cātrāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ doṣāya, sādhyāprasiddhāvapi
tadabhāvasya guṇatvādeva tavāvagantuṃ śakyatvena
vyatirekagrahaṇopapatteḥ| nāpi vyatirekāsambhavaḥ,
guṇatvātyantābhāvātyantābhāvasya
guṇatvāderguṇādiṣvaikaikasya sambhavāt| nāpi
guṇatvātyantābhāvasādhanenaikadharmādeḥ sapakṣādapi
vyāvṛttāddravyalakṣaṇasyāsādhāraṇatā,
karmāderapyanirṇītasādhyatayā pakṣatulyatayā
sapakṣatvāsambhavāt| nāpi sādhanavaiyarthyam|
apratyakṣadravyeṣu guṇatvābhāvasya pratyakṣeṇānavagamāt|
pratyakṣadravyeṣvapi
guṇatvābhāvasādhakapratyakṣasyānupajīvyatvena
tadabhāvadaśāyāṃ
guṇatvābhāvavyavahārasādhakapratītijanakatvena vyatirekiṇaḥ
saphalatvāt| guṇatvāpratyakṣatvapakṣe ca guṇatvābhāvasya
ghaṭādāvapratyakṣatvācca|
pratyakṣapratiyogitvābhāvasyaiva
pratyakṣatvāt| na cāsya guṇatvasādhakaprameyatvādinā
satpratipakṣatvam,
ghaṭaniṣṭhaguṇatvābhāvapratyakṣabādhitatvena durbalatvāt| na
caivaṃ guṇatvābhāvasādhakapratyakṣeṇaiva sādhyaprasiddheḥ
vyatirekavaiyarthyam, pūrvameva nirastatvāt| na
cāsatpratipakṣatvopapādakatayāsyopajīvyatvaṃ
ghaṭaniṣṭhaguṇatvābhāvasādhakapratyakṣasyopajīvyatve .api
tena paṭādau guṇatvābhāvaprasādhanāt| guṇatvaśabdena ca kiṃ
guṇalakṣaṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitā jātirvivakṣitā,
guṇalakṣaṇavadvyāvṛtte[ttatve] sati sadvyāvṛttā vā| nādyaḥ,
pratipakṣahetoḥ sattayārthāntaraprasaṅgāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
vyāvṛttisthala eva prameyatvādervyabhicārāt| astu vā
sarvadravyeṣu guṇatvādipratiyogike .api ṣaḍatyantābhāvatvaṃ
sādhyaṃ vyatirekiṇaḥ| na cātrāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam,
guṇatvātyantābhāvapañcakasamānādhikaraṇatvātiriktaguṇatvātyantābhāvaniṣṭhatvarahitatvān
prameyatvāt, ghaṭavadityādinā tatsiddheḥ| na ca
pakṣatvādisambhavaḥ kevalānvayisādhyābhāvasyāprasiddhatvena
tena saha kasyacidapi hetorvyāptigrahaṇāsambhavāt|
arthāntarasādhane cārthāntarasya durvāratvāt|
mahāvidyādūṣaṇāntarāṇāṃ
ca yathāsthānamuddharaṇāt| tasmāt

yatra yatrāsmadādiṣu dūṣaṇoddhārasambhavaḥ|

taṃ taṃ panthānamāśritya vyatirekī samarthyatām||

tasmāddravyajñānajanakatvāddravyaśabdavācyatvajñānajanakatvādguṇādivyāvṛttadravyajñānajanakatvādguṇatvādirahitadravyajñānajanakatvācca
dravyalakṣaṇaṃ prayojanavaditi yuktaḥ sūtrārambhaḥ
iti| dravyatvajñānaṃ ca guṇādivyāvṛttadravyajñānajanakameva,
dravyaṃ guṇādibhyo bhidyate dravyatvāt, na yadevaṃ na tadevamiti
vyatirekipravṛtteḥ| tathā guṇatvādirahitadravyajñānamapi
guṇādivyāvṛttadravyajñānajanakameva| dravyaṃ guṇādibhyo
bhidyate
guṇatvarahitatvādityanumānapravṛtterguṇādivyāvṛttajñānaṃ ca
guṇādivyāvṛttanididhyāsanahetuḥ|
guṇādivyāvṛttadravyanididhyāsanaṃ ca
guṇādivyāvṛttadravyasākṣātkārahetuḥ|
guṇādivyāvṛttadravyasākṣātkāraścāpavargaheturiti|
pṛthivyādyaṣṭadravyādiguṇādivyāvṛttātmasākṣātkāro
muktihetuḥ| [tacca]
pṛthivyādyaṣṭadravyaguṇādivyāvṛttātmanididhyāsanajanyam|
tacca pṛthivyādyaṣṭadravyaguṇādivyatiriktātmamananajanyam|
taccātmalakṣaṇādeveti vyarthaṃ dravyalakṣaṇamiti cet---na,
guṇādivyāvṛttātmasvarūpadravyasya dravyalakṣaṇādavagatau
pṛthivyādyaṣṭadravyavyatiriktatvasya cānumānāntarādavagatau
pṛthivyādyaṣṭadravyalakṣaṇādivyatiriktātmasvarūpanididhyāsanasyopapatteḥ,
dravyalakṣaṇasyāpyarthavattvāditi|

dravyāśrayī na guṇavān saṃyogavibhāgeṣvakāraṇamanapekṣa iti
guṇalakṣaṇam|| 1-1-15||

āśrayo .asyāstīti āśrayī āśrita iti yāvat| dravye āśrayī
dravyāśrayī dravyāśritaḥ| yadvā dravyaśca tadāśrayaśca
dravyāśrayaḥ| so .asyāstīti dravyāśrayī dravyāśrita
ityarthaḥ| tena dravyāśrito guṇarahitaḥ
saṃyogavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahito
guṇa iti guṇalakṣaṇam, dravyāśritaviśeṣaṇena
dravyānāśritagaganādivyudāsāt, guṇarahitaviśeṣaṇena ca
guṇavadghaṭādinirākaraṇāt,
saṃyogavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahitaviśeṣaṇena
saṃyogavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇakarmavyāvartanāditi kecit|
tanna, dravyāśritasāmānyasamavāyābhāveṣvativyāpakatvāt,
dravyāśritaviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyācca| gaganādīnāṃ
guṇarahitagrahaṇenaiva vyudāsāt| atha lakṣaṇavākyagatena padena
sāmānyādivyāvartakaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ samarpanīyam,
tasyaivāpekṣitatvāt| sāmānyavattvaṃ ca sāmānyādivyāvartakam|
tacca na yathāśrutena dravyādipadena śakyaṃ samarpayitumiti
dravyāśritapadaṃ sāmānyavattvaṃ lakṣayati tena
dravyāśritapadavaiyarthyam| nāpi sāmānyādau lakṣaṇasya
vyāvartakaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ samarpaṇīyam, tasyaivāpekṣitatvāt|
sāmānyavattvaṃ ca sāmānyādivyāvartakam, tacca na yathāśrute
[.a]vyāptiriti manyase, tadapi na,
saṃyogavibhāgakāraṇatvarahitatvenaiva karmavyāvṛttisiddhau
anapekṣatvaviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyāt|
saṃyogavibhāgakāraṇatvarahitameva viśeṣaṇamiti cet---na, evaṃ
satu guṇatvarahitaviśeṣaṇa[vaiyarthyam,
saṃyogavibhāgānapekṣa]kāraṇatvarahitatvenaiva dravyāṇāṃ
vyudāsāt| adṛṣṭeśvarajñānecchāprayatne .api tattvenaiva
karmavyāvṛttisiddhā saṃyogavibhāgakāraṇatvarahitatvameva
viśeṣaṇamiti cet---na, evaṃ sati
guṇarahitaviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyāt|
saṃyogavibhāgakāraṇatvarahitatvenaiva dravyāṇāṃ vyudāsāt|
adṛṣṭeśvarajñānecchāprayatneṣu lakṣaṇasyāvyāpakatvācca|
teṣāṃ sarvotpattimajjanakatvena saṃyogavibhāgakāraṇatvāt|
atheśvarajñānādyavyāptiparihārārthaṃ
karmaṇyativyāptiparihārārthaṃ ca
saṃyogavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahitaviśeṣaṇa
īśvarajñānādīnāṃ saṃyogavibhāgakāraṇatve .api
saṃyogavibhāgasāpekṣakāraṇatvena
saṃyogavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahitatvāt| karmaṇaśca
saṃyogavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahitatvaviśeṣaṇāt| na ca
guṇarahitatvaviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyam, dravyāṇāṃ
saṃyogavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahitatvena
nirākartumaśakyatvāditi manyase---tadapi na,
vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahitatvenaiva
karmaṇyativyāpterīśvarajñānādau [a]vyāpternirākaraṇasiddhau
saṃyogānapekṣakāraṇatvaviśeṣa[ṇa]vaiyarthyam,
sāmānyādīnāmapi sāmānyavattvasya
vyutpāditatvenātivyāptestadavasthatvācceti|

ātreyācāryāstu
saṃyogavibhāgayorakāraṇaṃ guṇa ityetāvataiva lakṣaṇam| paraṃ
śeṣasvarūpakathanārthamityāhuḥ| tadapi na,
saṃyogavibhāgakāraṇatvasya
sāmānyādāvativyāpakatvāttadvyudāsārthaṃ
kāraṇatvaviśeṣaṇamupādeyamiti cet---na, sāmānyādīnāmapi
svaviśiṣṭabodhanatvakāraṇatvāt| viśiṣṭajñānasya
viśeṣaṇajanyatvātsāmānyādayaḥ kāraṇāḥ bhāvatvāt,
ghaṭavadityanumānācca| atha
jñānatvarahitaghaṭādikāraṇatvaviśeṣaṇamupādeyam, na
caivamantyāvayaviparimāṇādyavyāptiḥ, asyāpi
jñānatvarahitatvapradhvaṃsādikāraṇatvāt| nāpi
paramāṇuparimāṇāvyāptiḥ, tasyāpi karmakāraṇatvāt|
mūrtaparimāṇasyānvayavyatirekibhyāṃ
karmahetutvāvadhāraṇāditi manyase---tanna,
jñānatvarahitaghaṭādijanakaprāgabhāvapratibandhādau
paṭarūpādivināśajanakapaṭapradhvaṃsādau cātivyāpakatvāt|

tadvyāvṛttyarthaṃ bhāvatvaviśeṣaṇamupādeyamiti cet---na,
evamativyāptiparihāre
.apyadṛṣṭeśvarajñānecchāprayatneṣvavyāpterduṣparihāratvāt|
teṣāṃ sarvakāryajanakatvena
saṃyogavibhāga[a]janakatvānupapatteḥ|
gaganaparimāṇādāvavyāpakatvācca| teṣāṃ
saṃyogavibhāgājanakatve .api jñānatvarahitājanakatvāt|

anye tu
dravyāśrayītyetanna
guṇavānsaṃyogavibhāgeṣvanapekṣakāraṇamityaparamiti
sūtrārthaṃ
varṇayanti| tadapi na, dravyāśritatvasya
karmādāvativyāpakatvāt| karmavyāvṛttyarthaṃ
sarvadravyāśritatvaṃ vivakṣitamiti cet---na,
sattādravyatvādāvativyāpakatvāt| adravyānāśritatve sati
sakaladravyāśritatvaṃ vivakṣitamiti cet---na,
dravyatva .ativyāpakatvāt| dravyatvavyatiriktatve satīti cet---na,
dravyalakṣaṇāntareṣvativyāpakatvāt|
dravyalakṣaṇavyatiriktatvaṃ viśeṣaṇamupādeyamiti cet---na,
evamativyāptiparihāre .api
sakalaguṇāvyāpterdurvāratvātsarvadravyāśritatvasya
kvacidapyasambhavāt| sarvadravyavṛttiniṣṭhāvāntarajātimattvaṃ
sarvadravyavṛttitvamiti cet---na,
ākāśavṛttiniṣṭhāvāntarajātimattvāderlakṣaṇatvopapattau
sarvapadopāttadravyāntaravṛttiniṣṭhatvānna vaiyarthyāt|
guṇarahitaḥ saṃyogavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahito guṇa ityapi
na lakṣaṇam, sāmānyādāvativyāpakatvāt| sāmānyavattvaṃ
viśeṣaṇamupādeyamiti cet---na, sāmānyāderapi sāmānyavattvena
lakṣaṇātivyāpakatvasya tadavasthatvāt|
sāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyābhāvavyatiriktatve satīti cet---na,
adhyāhāraprasaṅgāt| evamativyāptiparihāre .api lakṣaṇasya
duravagamatvācca| jñāte guṇarahitatve guṇalakṣaṇajñānam,
guṇalakṣaṇe ca jñāte guṇarahitatvajñānamiti
parasparāśrayāt||

anye tu
guṇavānityatra guṇaviśeṣasparśo vivakṣitaḥ| tena
dravyāśritasparśarahitaḥ
saṃyogavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahito guṇa iti lakṣaṇaṃ
varṇayanti| tadapi na, dravyāśritasāmānyādāvativyāpakatvāt|
tadvyāvṛttyarthaṃ samānyaviśeṣasamavāyavyatiriktabhāvavattvaṃ
viśeṣaṇamupādeyamiti cet---na, adhyāhāraprasaṅgāt|
dravyāśrita ityatra na [ca] dravyaviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyācca|
āśritatvenaivānāśritagaganādivyudāsāt|
saṃyogavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahita ityatra
saṃyogavibhāgāpekṣakāraṇatvarahitatvaviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyācca|
vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvenaiva karmavyudāsāt|
saṃyogānapekṣakāraṇatvarahitatvena karmaṇo
nivartayitumaśakyatvācca| karmaṇaḥ
saṃyogānapekṣakāraṇatvavirahāt|
vibhāgātpūrvasaṃyoganivṛttyanantaraṃ karmaṇā saṃyogajananāt|
tasmādanupapannaṃ guṇalakṣaṇaṃ sūtramiti|
atrocyate---dravyamāśrayo yasyāsau dravyāśrayaḥ|
dravyaguṇakarmavṛttijātyādivyāvṛttyarthamarthatvāparaparyāyasāmānyaṃ
yadudayanādibhiḥ
sattetyucyate, dravyāśrayo .asminnastīti dravyāśrayī,
arthatvavānityarthaḥ| upapāditaṃ cārthatvam| na guṇavānityatra
guṇaśabdena guṇaviśeṣāḥ
saṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgā gṛhyante|
saṃyogavibhāgeṣvakāraṇamanapekṣa iti
saṃyogavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahitatvaṃ vivakṣitam,
kāraṇapadasyānapekṣapadenānvayāt| nañā
samānārthasyākāraṇasyānapekṣakāraṇena sambandhāt| akārasya
ca
nañā samānārthatvamamānonā pratiṣedhā ityanuśāsanāt| na
kāraṇamakāraṇamiti nañaḥ samāsābhyupagame ca
saṃyogavibhāgayorakāraṇamiti guṇalakṣaṇasya
īśvarajñānādyavyāpakatvaprasaṅgāt| īśvarajñānādīnāṃ
saṃyogavibhāgakāraṇatvāt, saṃyogavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvaṃ
ca saṃyogakāraṇatve sati vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvam,

anapekṣapadasya vibhāgenaivānvayāt| saṃyogenāpyanvaye
vibhāgakṣaṇa eva saṃyogodayaprasaṅgāt|
saṃyogakāraṇatvamātreṇa lakṣaṇopapattau
anapekṣatvaviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyācca|
vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇarahitatvenaiva karmaṇyativyāpteḥ
īśvarecchāprayatnādṛṣṭeṣu avyāpteśca parihārādvyarthaṃ
saṃyogakāraṇatvagrahaṇamiti cet---na,
śarīrakuḍyavibhāgajanakahastakuḍyādivibhāgādāvavyāptiparihārārthatvāt
hastakuḍyavibhāgādeḥ śarīrakuḍyavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvena
tadrahitatvānupapatteḥ| saṃyogakāraṇatve sati
vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahitatvasya ca
śarīrakuḍyavibhāgajanakahastakuḍyādivibhāgādāvapi sambhavāt|
na caivamitaretarāśrayaḥ, guṇarahitatvasya
guṇalakṣaṇaviśeṣaṇatānaṅgīkārāt| saṃkhyādeśca
saṃkhyātvādinaiva nirūpaṇāt|

tasmādarthatvāśrayaḥ saṃkhyā[dya]nāśrayaḥ saṃyogakāraṇatve
sati vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahito guṇa iti lakṣaṇaṃ
sidhyati| evaṃ saṃkhyāpadasthāne
parimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgapadaprakṣepeṇa
lakṣaṇacatu[ṣṭayaṃ] dra[ṣṭa]vyam|

yadvā, guṇavānityatra guṇarahitatvābhāvena dravyatvābhāvo
lakṣyate| tathā ca na parasparāśrayaḥ, guṇarahitatvasya
lakṣaṇaviśeṣaṇatvābhāvāt| dravyatvasya ca guṇamantareṇāpi
samavāyikāraṇatvenaiva nirūpaṇasambhavāt| tasmādarthatvāśrayo
dravyatvarahitaḥ saṃyogakāraṇatve sati
vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahito guṇa iti sūtrārthaḥ|

yadvā, na guṇavāniti guṇaviśeṣasparśābhāvo vivakṣitaḥ,
anāśritagaganādivyudāsārthaḥ|
dravyāśritatvaviśeṣaṇalābhāya ca dravyāśrayīti padaṃ
punarāvartanīyam| dravyaṃ ca tadāśrayaśca dravyāśrayaḥ| so
.asyāstīti dravyāśrayīti dravyāśritatvalābhāt| na ca
dravyalakṣaṇasya vyāvartyābhāvaḥ, tasya
lakṣaṇāntargatatvābhāvāt| na caivaṃ dravyagrahaṇavaiyarthyaṃ
guṇānāmadravyavṛttitvaśaṅkānirāsārthatvāt| tathā ca
netaretarāśrayaḥ, guṇarahitatvasya
lakṣaṇaviśeṣaṇatvābhāvātsparśasya ca sparśenaiva
nirūpaṇāt| tasmādarthatvāśraya āśritasparśarahitaḥ
saṃyogavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahito guṇa iti lakṣaṇaṃ
sūtrārthaḥ|

yadvā, na guṇavāniti guṇarahitakarmatvameva lakṣaṇaviśeṣaṇam|
na caivamitaretarāśrayaḥ| guṇena guṇarahitatvanirūpaṇe .api
guṇarahitatvādilakṣaṇena guṇānirūpaṇāt,
tasyetarabhedamātranirūpakatvācca| guṇena
guṇarahitatvādilakṣaṇanirūpaṇe .api
guṇarahitatvādilakṣaṇamantareṇāpi guṇatvenaiva
guṇanirūpaṇācca| guṇatvasya ca
rūpādivyāvṛttatvarahitarūpatvādicaturviṃśatijātirahitāvṛttijātitvena
nirvacanāt| guṇarahitatvādiguṇalakṣaṇānavagame
.api rūpaṃ
rūpavṛttirūpavyāvṛttatvarahitarūpavṛttirasādivyāvṛttatvarahitarūpavṛttirūpatvādicaturviṃśatijātirahitāvṛttijātimat,
jātimattvāt, ghaṭavadityādinā guṇatvasyāvagantuṃ
śakyatvācca| astu vā guṇalakṣaṇenaiva guṇatvanirūpaṇam,
tathāpi netaretarāśrayaḥ,
guṇarahitatvādivarjitenārthatvāśrayaḥ saṃyogakāraṇatve sati
vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahita ityādinā guṇanirūpaṇādevaṃ
sati etadevāstu kiṃ teneti cet---na, tasyāpi nirdoṣatvena
lakṣaṇatvānapāyāt|
parasparāśrayaparihārārthamavaśyopajīvyenānenaiva
itarabhedādisiddhervyarthaṃ guṇarahitatvādilakṣaṇamiti cet---na,
guṇalakṣaṇāntarājanyetarabhedānumitijanakatvenāsyāvyarthatvāt|
etadvyatiriktenāpi guṇalakṣaṇena
guṇanirūpaṇasiddherasyāvaśyopajīvyatvābhāvācca| na caivaṃ
sati tadevāstī[stvi?]ti vācyam, lakṣaṇāntareṇāpi
guṇanirūpaṇasiddheḥ tasyopajīvya
nirūpaṇārthamupajīvyamiti cet---na, uktanyāyena
sarveṣāmavaśyopajīvyatvābhāvasiddheḥ| saṃyogavibhāgeṣviti
bahuvacanaṃ svarūpakathanārtham| na tu lakṣaṇāntargataṃ
vyāvartyābhāvāt|

yadvā [saṃ]yogavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahitatvaṃ
saṃyogavibhāgāsamavāyikāraṇatvarahitatvaṃ vivakṣitam|
saṃyogavibhāgāsamavāyikāraṇarahitatvaṃ ca kāraṇatve sati
vibhāgāsamavāyikāraṇatvābhāvaḥ, vibhāgakāraṇatve sati
saṃyogāsamavāyikāraṇatvābhāvo vā, ubhayābhāvo vā|
ubhayatrāsamavāyikāraṇavivakṣāyāṃ viśeṣaṇavaiyarthyāt|
śarīrakuḍyavibhāgāsamavāyikāraṇahastakuḍyādāvavyāptiparihārārthaṃ
saṃyogakāraṇatve satīti viśeṣaṇam|
śarīrakuḍyasaṃyogāsamavāyikāraṇahastakuḍyasaṃyogādāvavyāptiparihārārthaṃ ca vibhāgakāraṇatve satīti viśeṣaṇam|
atra cāsamavāyikāraṇaniruktiparyālocanaprayuktadūṣaṇaparihāre ca prayatnaḥ kartavyaḥ|
upekṣaṇīyaṃ veti|

itiśabdaśca sūtre
evaṃvidhe lakṣaṇāntaraparihārārthaḥ|
tathā ca, dravyakarmāvṛttimānartho guṇaḥ|
dravyatvakarmatvarahito .artho guṇaḥ|
saṃkhyāvadvṛttitvarahitakarmavṛttitvarahitādhikaraṇamartho guṇaḥ|
saṃkhyāvadvṛttitvarahitatvotkṣepaṇākuñcanaprasāraṇagamanāvṛttimānartho
guṇaḥ| evaṃ saṃkhyāpadasthāne
parimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgapadaprakṣepeṇa
karmavṛttitvarahitatvaviśeṣaṇopādāne catvāri lakṣaṇāni|
tathā karmavṛttitvarahitapadasthāne
utkṣepaṇāvakṣepaṇākuñcanaprasāraṇagamanavṛttipadaprakṣepeṇa
catvāri lakṣaṇāni draṣṭavyāni|
saṃyogavadvṛttitvarahitakarmavṛttikarmavṛttitvarahitādhikaraṇamartho
guṇaḥ|
saṃyogavadvṛttitvarahitotkṣepaṇāvakṣepaṇākuñcanaprasāraṇagamanā[t]
vṛttimānartho guṇaḥ|
vibhāgavadvṛttitvarahitakarmavṛttitvarahitādhikaraṇamartho
guṇaḥ|
vibhāgavadvṛttitvarahitakarmavṛttitvarahitādhikaraṇamartho
guṇaḥ|
parimāṇavṛttitvarahitakarmavṛttitvarahitādhikaraṇamartho
guṇaḥ|
parimāṇavadvṛttitvarahitotkṣepaṇāvakṣepaṇākuñcanaprasāraṇagamanāvṛttimānartho

guṇaḥ| pṛthaktvavadvṛttitvarahitakarmāvṛttimānartho guṇaḥ|
guṇavadvṛttitvarahitotkṣepaṇāvakṣepaṇākuñcanaprasāraṇagamanāvṛttimānartho
guṇaḥ| na cānayoritaretarāśrayatvaṃ doṣaḥ,
guṇalakṣaṇamantareṇāpi guṇatvena nirūpaṇasambhavāt| na ca
sarvatrārthagrahaṇavaiyarthyam, sāmānyādivyudāsārthatvāt|

yattu vidyādharamiśrādibhiruktam---
utkṣepaṇāvṛttisaṃyogāsamānādhikaraṇasattāsākṣādvyāpyajātimadguṇa
iti [tat] na yuktam, jātyādāvativyāpakatvāt utkṣepaṇāvṛtteḥ
saṃyogasamānādhikaraṇatvarahitāyāḥ sattāsākṣādvyāpyāyā
jātitvaviśeṣaṇatvādijāteḥ jātyādāvapi sattvāt
kṣaṇarahitāvṛttijātimatī prameyatvādityādeḥ durvāratvāt|
avakṣepaṇādāvativyāpakatvādutkṣepaṇāvṛtteḥ
saṃyogasamānādhikaraṇatvarahitatvasya
sattāsākṣādvyāpyasyāvakṣepaṇādereva tatra sattvāt|
avakṣepaṇa[tva]adikaṃ sattasākṣādvyāpyaṃ na bhavatīti
cet---na, sattāsākṣādvyāpyatvaṃ
kimaparokṣasattāsākṣādvyāpyatvamabhipretam,
sattāśrayavyāvṛttajātyavyāpyatve sati sattāvyāpyatvaṃ vā,
sattāśrayavyāvṛttajātivyāpyatvarahitatve sati sattāvyāpyatvaṃ
vā| nādyaḥ, avakṣepaṇāderapi sattāvyāpyasyāparokṣatvāt| na
dvitīyaḥ, avakṣepaṇāderapi
sattāśrayavyāvṛttaghaṭatvādyavyāpyatvāt| na tṛtīyaḥ,
guṇavattvasyāpi sattāśrayavyāvṛttaguṇatvavyāpyatvena
sattāśrayavyāvṛttajātivyāpyatvarahitatvānupapattau
guṇalakṣaṇasyāsambhavāpatteḥ| dravyāderapi
sattāśrayavyāvṛttadravyatvādivyāpyatvena tadrahitatvānupapattau
sattāsākṣādvyāpyatvābhāvaprasaṅgācca|
guṇatvavyatiriktasattāśrayavyāvṛttajātivyāpyatvarahitatve sati
sattāvyāpyatvaṃ sattāsākṣādvyāpyatvaṃ guṇatve ghaṭata iti
cet---na,
karmatvāderguṇatvavyatiriktasattāśrayavyāvṛttakarmādivyāpyatvena
tadrahitatvānupapattau
sattāsākṣādvyāpyatvābhāvasambhavādekamastviti cet---na,
utkṣepaṇāvṛttitvaviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyaprasaṅgāt| karmatvasya
sākṣādvyāpyatvaviśeṣaṇenaiva vyāvṛttatvāt| etena
sattāvāntarajātyavāntarajātitvarahitatve sati
sākṣādvyāpyatvaṃ sattāsākṣādvyāpyatvamityapi nirastam|
sattāśrayavyāvṛttajātyāśrayavyāvṛttatvasyaiva
sattāvāntarajātyavāntaraśabdārthatvāt| tasya ca pūrvameva
dūṣitatvāt|
guṇatvakarmatvānyatamatvasattāsākṣātvyāpyatvamiti cet---na,
guṇatvavānguṇaḥ ityetāvataiva guṇalakṣaṇasiddhau
viśeṣaṇāntaravaiyarthyasya ca vajralepāyamānatvāditi|
avakṣepaṇādāvutkṣepaṇavṛttisaṃyogasamānādhikaraṇāvakṣepaṇarūpādivṛttisattāsākṣādvyāpyajātisambhavācca|
utkṣepaṇāvakṣepaṇavṛttitvarahitasaṃyogasamānādhikaraṇāvakṣepaṇavṛttitvarahitarūpādivyāvṛttāvakṣepaṇāvṛttitvarahitajātimat,
prameyatvāt, ghaṭavadityanumānāt| jātigrahaṇavaiyarthyācca|
utkṣepaṇāvṛttisaṃyogasamānādhikaraṇasattāsākṣādvyāpyarūpāvakṣepaṇātiriktātyantābhāvavatā
avakṣepaṇādinātivyāptiparihārārthaṃ

jātigrahaṇamiti cet---na, jātitvaṃ kiṃ jātiḥ, nityatve
satyanekasamavetatvādikaṃ
vā| nādyaḥ bhavadbhiranabhyupagamāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
samavāyatvenaivātivyāptiparihāropapatteḥ, anityatve
satyanekāśritatvasya vyarthatvāt| samavāyatve
satyāśritatvasyaiva samavetapadārthatvāt| etena
ghaṭāvṛttisaṃyogājanyasaṃyogāsamavāyikāraṇavṛttisattāsākṣādvyāpyajātimānguṇa
iti lakṣaṇaṃ nirastam|
saṃyogajanyatvarahitasaṃyogāsamavāyikāraṇāvṛttisaṃyogāsamānādhikaraṇajātimānguṇa
ityapi na lakṣaṇam, jātyādāvativyāpakatvāt,
pūrvoktavaiyarthyācca|

saṃyogāsamavāyikāraṇatvaṃ ca kiṃ saṃyogasamavāyitvarahitatve
sati saṃyogakāraṇatvam, asamavāyikāraṇatve sati
saṃyogakāraṇatvaṃ vā| nādyaḥ,
guṇalākṣaṇsyeśvarajñānecchāprayatnādṛṣṭāvavyāpakatvaprasaṅgāt|
īśvarajñānādīnāṃ saṃyogāsamavāyitve sati
saṃyogakāraṇatvena tanniṣṭhajātīnāṃ saṃyogāsamavāyitve
saṃyogakāraṇāvṛttitvānupapatteḥ| na dvitīyaḥ,
dravyatvarahitātmāviśeṣaguṇatvarahitatvaṃ
sādhyātmaviśeṣaguṇamātrajanakatvarahitabhāvatvādyasamavāyikāraṇatvalakṣaṇasya
guṇānarhatvena guṇena guṇalakṣaṇanirūpaṇe guṇalakṣaṇena ca
guṇanirūpaṇe
parasparāśrayaparihārārthamasamavāyikāraṇagarbhāṇi
guṇalakṣaṇāni praṇayatāṃ navīnatarkikāṇāṃ
tadevopasthitamiti mahānpramādaḥ|

asmanmārgeṇa
parasparāśrayaparihāre
.apyasamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇāntargatādyaviśeṣalakṣaṇatvaparyālocanayā
vaiyarthyasya duṣparihāratvāt| svātmaniṣṭhatve sati
viśeṣaguṇatvasyātmaviśeṣaguṇaśabdārthatvāt| tathā
cātmaniṣṭhatvarahitasaṃyogājanyasaṃyogakāraṇāvṛttisaṃyogāsamānādhikaraṇa
iti nirastam|
vegājanyavegāsamavāyikāraṇāvṛttivegāsamānādhikaraṇajātimānguṇa
iti na yuktam, pūrvoktadoṣāt| ātmani ativyāpakatvācca|
vegājanyavegāsamavāyikāraṇavṛtteḥ
vegasamānādhikaraṇasyātmatvasyātmani sambhavāt|
sattāsākṣādvyāpyaviśeṣaṇasya ca prāgeva dūṣitatvāt|
vegājanyavegāsamavāyikāraṇāvṛttisaṃyogāsamānādhikaraṇajātimānguṇa
ityapi na yuktam, dikkālādāvativyāpakatvāt|
anekadikkālābhyupagamapakṣe dikkālatvādijātisvīkārāt|
tasyāśca saṃskārāsamānādhikaraṇatvāt| dikālākāśeṣu ca
jñānarahitaparamamahattvavyaṅgyajātisvīkārāt| dikālayośca
tadviśeṣaguṇarahitamahattvasya na jātīsvīkārāt| kālaḥ
saṃskārasamānādhikaraṇakālaniṣṭhatvarahitavegājanyavegāsamavāyikāraṇavṛttikālaniṣṭhatvarahitajātimān,
prameyatvāt, ityāderna durvāratvāt| pūrvoktadoṣācca,
jñānāsamavāyikāraṇāsamavāyikāraṇāvṛttijñānāsamavāyikāraṇajātīyāsamānādhikaraṇajātimānguṇa
ityapi na lakṣaṇam, pūrvoktadoṣāt,
abhighātakarmaṇyativyāpakatvāt
janyamanaḥkarmavyāvṛttābhighātajanyavṛttijñānāsamavāyikāraṇajātīyāsamānādhikaraṇajātimattvāt|
mānasaṃ ca sparśarahitatvenābhighātāsambhave manaḥkarmaṇo
.abhighātājanyatvāt| vipratipannaṃ pratyabhighātajaṃ vā
cca vṛttitvarahitajātimān
jātimattvāt ghaṭavadityanumānam,
saṃyogāsamavāyikāraṇatvaviśiṣṭavibhāgāsamavāyikāraṇāvṛttitvenaiva
karmavyāvṛttisiddheḥ|

ātmaniṣṭhasattetarajātimānguṇa ityapi na
lakṣaṇam, viśeṣādāvativyāpakatvāt|
teṣāmapyātmaniṣṭhaviśeṣādiniṣṭhaviśeṣatvādijātimattvāt|
ghaṭāvṛttirūpavṛttijātimānguṇa ityapi na
yuktam, karmaṇyativyāpakatvāt|
dravyāvṛttiguṇakarmavṛttijāterupapāditatvāt,
jātyādāvativyāpakatvācca|
teṣvapyadravyamātravṛttijātisvīkārāt|
saṃskāravṛttyaparajātimānguṇa ityapi na
lakṣaṇam, dravyakarmaṇorativyāpakatvāt,
guṇakarmamātravṛttijāteḥ saṃskāravartinyāḥ
karmaṇyapi svīkārāt| evamanyānyapi
jātipuraskāreṇa navīnatārkikotprekṣitāni
dūṣaṇāy nirupādi (?)|

caturviṃśatyanyatamo guṇa ityapi na yuktam,
sarvarūpavyaktīnāṃ jñātumaśakyatvena
tadanyatamasyāpi jñātumaśakyatvāt|
yāvadadhigatarūpādyanyatamatvasya
cānadhigatarūpādyavyāpakatvāt|
rūpādidharmavyatiriktadharmavānguṇa ityapi na
lakṣaṇam| sarvarūpādīnāṃ jñātumaśakyatvena
taddharmavyatiriktavyatiriktatvasyāpi
jñātumaśakyatvāt|
yāvadavagatarūpādidharmavyatiriktavyatiriktadharmatvasya
ca bhavadanadhigatarūpādyavyāpakatvāt|
rūpatvādicaturviṃśatijātivyatiriktatvarahitādhikaraṇaṃ
guṇa iti tu guṇalakṣaṇaṃ samyageva|
tasmādidamatra rahasyam,

yatra
yatrāsmadabhī[dādī]ṣṭadūṣaṇoddhārasambhavaḥ|

tattadastu yathākāmaṃ guṇānāmatra lakṣaṇam||

lakṣaṇaprayojanaṃ coktameveti sarvaṃ
ramaṇīyamiti|| 15||

ekadravyamaguṇaṃ
saṃyogavibhāgeṣvanapekṣakāraṇamiti
karmalakṣaṇam|| 1-1-16||

ekadravyamāśrayo .asyāstīti ekadravyam,
ekadravyavyāpyatvamiti yāvat| na guṇaḥ aguṇam
[a]guṇatvamiti yāvat| kāraṇapadaṃ ca
saṃyogavibhāgābhyāṃ sambadhyate|
saṃyogavibhāgeṣviti bahuvacanaṃ karmaṇo
bahusaṃyogavibhāgajanakadravyaguṇādibhiḥ
bahusaṃyogavibhāgajanakatvasāmarthyapratipādanārtham|
vyāvartyābhāvena saṃyogavibhāgabahutvasya
lakṣaṇāntargatatvānupapatteḥ| anapekṣapadaṃ tu
avibhāgenaiva sambadhyate| saṃyogenāpi sambandhe
karmaṇaḥ saṃyogānapekṣakāraṇatvena
vibhāgakṣaṇa eva saṃyogodayaprasaṅgāt|
lakṣaṇānupayogini saṃyogānapekṣakāraṇatve
sūtrakārasya
tātparyābhāvācca|
tasmādekatvavyāpyaṃ vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇaṃ
karma| guṇatvarahitaṃ vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇaṃ
karma| saṃyogakāraṇatve sati
vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇaṃ karmeti lakṣaṇatrayaṃ
sūtrārthaḥ|
saṃyogakāraṇatve sati
vibhāgakāraṇaṃ karmetyadevāstu, kimanapekṣatvena
vibhāgaviśeṣeṇeti cet---na,
īśvarajñānecchāprayatnādṛṣṭādiṣvativyāptiparihārārthatvāt|
nanu kimidaṃ vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvam| na
tāvatkāraṇāntaranirapekṣatve sati
vibhāgakāraṇatvaṃ
samavāyikāraṇā[na]pekṣaṇāt| nāpi
samavāyikāraṇādiṣva[vya]tiriktakāraṇānapekṣatve

sati
vibhāgakāraṇatvamīśvarajñānecchāprayatnādṛṣṭāderapekṣaṇāt|
nāpi
samavāyikāraṇeśvarajñānecchāprayatnādṛṣṭadikkālātmavyatiriktakāraṇānapekṣayatki
ghaṭādikarmaṇyativyāpakatve sati
vibhāgakāraṇatvam,
vibhāgaprāgabhāvasyāpekṣaṇāt|
ubhayakarmajavibhāge ca karmaṇā dvitīyakarmaṇo
.anapekṣatvācca| nāpi
dravyatvarahiteśvarajñānecchāprayatnādṛṣṭaprāgabhāvakarmavyatiriktakāraṇānapekṣatve
sati vibhāgakāraṇatvam,
kālasamavāyikāraṇeśvarādīnāmapi
vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvaprasaṅgāt| teṣāmapi
dravyatvarahiteśvarajñānecchāprayatnādṛṣṭaprāgabhāvakarmavyatiriktakāraṇānapekṣatvāt|
nāpi
dravyatvarahiteśvarajñānecchāprayatnādṛṣṭaprāgabhāvadvitīyakarmavyatiriktakāraṇanapekṣatve
sati vibhāgakāraṇatvaṃ samavāyikāraṇādīnāmapi
vibhāgānapekṣatvaprasaṅgāt| nāpi
svotpattyanantarakṣaṇotpannakāraṇānapekṣatve sati
vibhāgakāraṇatvam| ca[sva]śabdena
vyaktiviśeṣānabhidhāne
lakṣaṇasyānanugamāpatteḥ| karmābhidhāne
.asamavāyikāraṇeśvarecchājñāne sati
vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvaprasaṅgāt| teṣāmapi
vibhāgajanane
karmānantarotpannakāraṇānapekṣatvāt| nāpi
vibhāgacaramakāraṇatvam, tasyāpyanirūpaṇāt|
vibhāgacaramakāraṇatvaṃ kiṃ vibhāgacaramatve sati
kāraṇatvam, caramatve sati vibhāgakāraṇatvaṃ vā,
vibhāgāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇotpannatve sati
vibhāgakāraṇatvaṃ vā| nādyaḥ,
vibhāgātpūrvamutpannatvena
vibhāgacaramatvāsambhavāt| paścādbhāvasya
caramaśabdārthatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ, yataḥ
kutaściccaramatve ghaṭāderapi
vibhāgacaramakāraṇatvāpatteḥ|
vibhāgakāraṇacaramatve ca
vibhāgakāraṇaghaṭānantarotpannavibhāgakāraṇapaṭāderapi
vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvaprasaṅgāt|
sakalavibhāgakāraṇacaramatvasya cāsambhavāt|
karmaṇaḥ svacaramatvānupapatteḥ
vibhāgakāraṇacaramatvāsambhavāt| nāpi tṛtīyaḥ,
ghaṭādāvativyāpakatvāt| teṣāmapi
ghaṭādyavyavahitottarakṣaṇotpannastambhādivibhāgāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇotpannatve
sati svagatavibhāgakāraṇatvāditi| tadayuktam,
vibhāgakurvadrūpatvāparaparyāyavibhāgajananaśīlatvasya
karmaviśeṣasyānapekṣakāraṇatvaśabdārthatvāt|
tasya ca padārthāntaravyāvṛttasya
vibhāgājanakākāraṇa
kāraṇavibhāgakāryatvarahitavṛttya
tadvṛttitvarahitādhikaraṇaṃ prameyatvāt,
ghaṭavadityanumānāt| vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvaṃ
ca jātireva| na ca guṇādinā jātisaṃkaraḥ|
tasyādūṣaṇatvāt| pattiḥ,
prameyatvādivattasyābhāvāntarbhāvāt| astu tarhi
vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇaṃ karmalakṣaṇam, kiṃ
saṃyogakāraṇatvāditi cet--na, śarīrasya
vibhāgājanakahastakuḍyavibhāga
gānapekṣakāraṇatvāditi|

yadvā, saṃyogavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvaṃ
karmatvaṃ vā, tathā ca karma karmeti lakṣaṇam| na
caivaṃ karmaṇaiva karmanirūpaṇādātmāśrayaḥ|
karmaṇaśca karmavṛttitvānupapatteḥ tvasya
lakṣaṇatvāt| karma karmeti karmatvāśrayo karmeti
vyākhyānāt| na ca karmatvena karmanirūpaṇam,
karmaṇā karmatvanirūpaṇamiti parasparāśrayaḥ|
karmatvena karmanirūpaṇe .api karmaṇā
karmatvānirūpaṇatvā vṛttajātitvaṃ
karmatvam| tacca
karmaṇā nirūpyata iti cet---na,
utkṣepaṇāvakṣepaṇākuñcanaprasāraṇagamanavyāvṛttatvarahitatve
utkṣepaṇāvakṣepaṇatvākuñcanatvaprasāraṇatva
karmatvanirūpaṇāt| karmatvena karmānirūpaṇācca|
na hi lakṣaṇe lakṣyaṃ nirūpyate kiṃ
tasyetarabhedādityuktatvāt| karmatvasya
karmāśrayatvena karmalakṣaṇatvopapatteriti|

yadvā, e karmaṇo dravyatvaniṣedhārthamiti|
tathā ca karma na dravyam, ekadravyatvāt,
guṇarahitatvācca rūpavat| nanvekadravyatvaṃ
yadyekatvādhikaraṇadravyāśritatvam, tadā
ghaṭādāvanaikāntaḥ, teṣāma śritatvāt|
athānekadravyānāśritatvam, tadā
gaganādāvanaikāntaḥ,
teṣāmanāśritatvenānekāśritatvāt|
athānekānāśritatve sati dravyānāśritatvam, tadā
asiddhiḥ| karmaṇo niṣedhārthatvāt|

kecidāhuḥ
ghaṭe calati rūpādayaścalantīti tannirāsārthaṃ
karmaṇo dravyāśritatvagrahaṇam| tathā ca
rūpādayo na karmavantaḥ, adravyatvāt, karmavaditi|
karma dravyaṃ sāmānyavattve sati
saṃyogavibhāgajanakakāraṇatvāt, ghaṭavaditi
satpratipakṣatvamiti cet---na,
vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvābhāvena sopādhitvāt|
tatsādhane ca bādhanāditi karma dravyasādhana
vṛttatvarahitatve sati
pṛthivyādinavalakṣaṇarahitāvṛttijāterdravyaśabdārthatvāt|
guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitatve
nirguṇāvṛttijātitvaṃ dravyatvam| tacca na
bādhitamiti cet---na, nirguṇe karmaṇi
ṇatvāditi|

yadvā saṃyogavibhāgāveva karmeti kecit|
tannirāsārthamuktamekadravyamaguṇamiti|
saṃyogavibhāgāvanekadravyau, guṇau ca| karma
punarekadravyam, guṇatva vibhāgau
naitatkarmasaṃyogavibhāgau| tena
tatkarmasaṃyogavibhāgatvāt
anyasaṃyogavibhāgavaditi|

yadvā, karma guṇa iti bhūṣaṇaḥ|
tannirāsārthamuktamaguṇamiti, na guṇa aguṇamiti
vyākhyānāt| sati tantuniṣṭhatvātpaṭavat|
karmaguṇavṛttikarmavṛttitvarahitādhikaraṇam,
meyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi| karmavadvā guṇaḥ,
vibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvāt, vibhāgavat|
sāmānyavattve satyekāśritatvāt, rūpavaditi
sapratipa bhipretaṃ jātirvā| nādyaḥ,
saṃyogavibhāgānapekṣakāraṇatvarahitatvādiguṇalakṣaṇasādhane
bādhāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
guṇalakṣaṇavanmātravṛttijātisādhane bādhādeva|
dravyāvṛttiguṇavṛttijāti
karmavṛttijātisvīkārāt| asvīkāre .api
tathāvidhajātisādhyārthāntarasya durvāratvāt|

itiśabda evaṃvidhalakṣaṇāntaraparigrahārthaḥ|
tathā ca dravyatvaṃ guṇatvarahito .arthaḥ| karma
dravya
vakṣepaṇatvākuñcanatvaprasāraṇatvagamanatvavyatiriktatvādhikaraṇaṃ
karmetyādi|
saṃkhyāvṛttitvarahitasaṃyogāsamānādhikaraṇasattāsākṣādvyāpyajātimatkarmeti
vidya pteḥ teṣāṃ
saṃkhyāvyāvṛttitvarahitasaṃyogasamānādhikaraṇarahitatvaparimāṇatvādijātimattvāt|

nanu tadvyāvṛttyarthaṃ
sattāsākṣādvyāpyagrahaṇamiti cet---na, prāgeva
dūṣitatvāt|
ṇavṛttisaṃyogāsamānādhikaraṇajātimatkarmetyapi
na lakṣaṇam, sāmānyādāvativyāpakatvāt,
rūpādavativyāpakatvācca| teṣvapi
saṃyogasamānādhikaraṇatvarahitsaṃyoga
raṇakarmavṛttijātimattvāt|
guṇakarmaṇorapyavyāvṛttajātisvīkārāt| etena
vibhāgājanyavibhāgasamavāyikāraṇavṛttivibhāgāsamānādhikaraṇajātimatkarma,
vegājanyavegāsamavāyikāraṇa jātimatkarmeti
lakṣaṇadvayaṃ nirastam|
ghaṭasamavāyikāraṇāsamavāyikāraṇavṛttighaṭāvṛttijātimatkarma
ityapi na lakṣaṇam, pūrvoktadoṣāt,
rūpādāvativyāpakatvācca| ghaṭā
vāvayavasaṃyogasyāpi kāraṇatvasambhavāt|
tadvṛtteśca guṇatvasya ghaṭāvṛtte
rūpādāvasiddhatvāt|
saṃyogāsamavāyikāraṇatvaviśiṣṭavibhāgāsamavāyikāraṇavṛttijātimadvyavṛtta
doṣāt|
saṃyogāsamavāyikāraṇatvaviśiṣṭetyatrāsamavāyiviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyācca|
nityapṛthivīrūpāsamavāyikāraṇasamavāyikāraṇavṛttiparamāṇvavṛttijātimatkarmetyapi
na lakṣaṇam, pū vaiyarthyācca|
nityarūpāsamavāyikāraṇatvenaivāpekṣitasaṃyogābhāvāt,
paramagrahaṇavaiyarthyācca| paramāṇvavṛttitvenaiva
sattānirāsāt, rūpādāvativyāpakatvācca|
paramāṇurūpāsamavāyikāraṇa
pārthivaparamāṇuvahnyavayavasaṃyogasyāpi
kāraṇatvasambhavāt| tadvṛttiguṇatvasya ca
paramāṇuvṛtte rūpādiṣu sattvāt|
kālāvṛttyutkṣepaṇāvakṣepaṇavṛttijātimatkarmetyapi
na lakṣaṇam| kāryadravyaguṇeṣvativyāpakatvāt|
teṣvapi kāryatvasya
kāryamātravṛttijātisvīkārāt| tasya ca
kālāvṛttitve satyutkṣepaṇāvṛttitvāt| etena
sattetarotkṣepaṇavṛttijātimatkarmetyapi lakṣaṇaṃ
nirastam| kāryadravyaguṇeṣu
kāryavyaṅgyajātisvīkārāt| vipratipannaṃ prati
ghaṭaḥ
kāryavyāvṛttighaṭavyāvṛttitvarahitakāryavṛttighaṭavṛttitvarahitajātimān
jātimattvāt, paṭavadityanumānāt|
anityadravyaguṇeṣvativyāpakatvāt|
anityamātravṛttijāterapyanityamātravṛttitve sati
sattāsākṣādvyāpyatvāt|
sattāsākṣādvyāpyatvasya prāgeva dūṣitatvācca|

idamatra rahasyam---

yatra yatrāsmadādīṣṭadūṣaṇoddhārasambhavaḥ|

tattadastu yathākāmaṃ karmaṇāmatra lakṣaṇam||

yadvā, dravyatvaṃ dravyalakṣaṇam|
guṇatvaṃ guṇalakṣaṇam| karmatvaṃ karmalakṣaṇam|
ākāśakāladigvyatiriktapṛthivyādidravyaviśeṣalakṣaṇāni
pṛthivītvodakatvādijātayaḥ|
rūpādiguṇaviśeṣāṇāṃ rūpatvādijātayo
lakṣaṇāni| karmaviśeṣotkṣepaṇādīnāṃ
cotkṣepaṇatvādijātayo lakṣaṇāni sāmānyasya ca
jātimattvameva| tatra jātirupādhirvā| samavāyasya
ca nityasambandhatvam, saṃyogavyatiriktasambandhatvaṃ
vā, [a]dravyasambandhatvaṃ vā, guṇasambandhatvaṃ
vā, karmasambandhatvaṃ vā, jātisambandhatvaṃ vā,
viśeṣasambandhatvaṃ vā| sambandhatvaṃ ca
jātireva, jātiviśeṣasamavāyeṣvapi
jātīsvīkārāt| abhāvasya cābhāvatvameva
lakṣaṇam| prāgabhāvasya
prāgabhāvatvam, pradhvaṃsasya ca pradhvaṃsatvam,
anyonyābhāvasyānyonyābhāvatvam,
atyantābhāvasyātyantābhāvatvaṃ lakṣaṇam|
prāgabhāvatvādayaśca jātaya eva
gotvāditulyayogakṣematvāditi|

yadvā, pṛthivyā gandhavattvam, śītatve sati
gurutvāśrayatvam, uṣṇatvaśītatvarahitatve sati
rūpavattvam, vāyuvyatiriktatve
satyanuṣṇāśītasparśavattvaṃ lakṣaṇam| udakasya
ca śītasparśavattvam, snehavattvam,
sāṃsiddhikadravatvādhikaraṇatvam, nirgandhatve sati
rasavattvam, nirgandhatve sati gurutvādhikaraṇatvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| tejasastūṣṇatvam, bhāsvararūpavattvam,
rasarahitatve rūpavattvam, gurutvarahitatve sati
rūpavattvaṃ lakṣaṇam| vāyostu nīrūpatve sati
sparśavattvam, nirgandhatve
satyanuṣṇāśītasparśavattvam, agurutve
satyanuṣṇāśītasparśavattvam,
sparśavyañjakasparśavattvaṃ lakṣaṇam| ākaśasya
śabdaḥ vibhvindriyatvam, śrotratvam,
ākāśānyatvātyantābhāvaśca lakṣaṇam| kālasya
ca nityaniṣṭhaparatvajanakatvasaṃyogarahitatve
satyaparatvajanakasaṃyogavattve sati vibhutvam, tattve
nityaniṣṭhāparatvajanakasaṃyogarahitatve sati
paratvajanakasaṃyogatve sati vibhutvādivyatiriktatve
sati viśeṣaguṇarahitatve sati mahattvam,
kālānyatvātyantābhāvaśca lakṣaṇam| diśastu
paramāṇuniṣṭhāparatvajanakasaṃyogavattve sati
vibhutvam, viśeṣaguṇarahitatve sati
kālānyamahattvam| viśeṣaguṇa]rahitatve sati]
kālānyavibhutvam, [diganya]tvātyantābhāvaśca
lakṣaṇam| ātmanaśca jñānāśrayatvādīni
lakṣaṇāni, [tathā] ākāśavyatiriktatve sati
viśeṣaguṇavattve sati vibhutvaṃ lakṣaṇam|
manasastu sukhagrāhakendriyatvam,
duḥkhagrāhakendriyatvam grāhakendriyatvam,
smṛtijanakendriyatvam, sparśarahitaparamāṇutvam,
viśeṣaguṇarahitaparamāṇutvam,
jñānādijanakasaṃyogāśrayatve satyaṇutvaṃ
lakṣaṇam|

rūpasya ca sparśāgrāhyatve sati
cakṣurgrāhyaguṇatvam,
sparśanāgrāhyacakṣurgrāhyajātimattvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| rasasya rasānāgrāhyaguṇatvam,
tvagindriyāgrāhyarasanajātimattvaṃ lakṣaṇam|
gandhasya ghrāṇagrāhyaguṇatvam,
tvagindriyāgrāhyaghrāṇagrāhyajātimattvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| sparśasya tu
cakṣuragrāhyatvagindriyagrāhyaguṇatvam,
cakṣuragrāhyatvagindriya[grāhya]jātimattvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| saṃkhyāyāstu
pṛthaktvāvṛttyekatvavṛttijātimattvam,
saṃyogāvṛttyekatvavṛttijātimattvam,
vibhāgāvṛttyekatvavṛttijātimattvam,
pṛthaktvāvṛttidvitvavṛttijātimattvam,
saṃyogāvṛttidvitvavṛttijātimattvam,
vibhāgāvṛttidvitvavṛttijātimattvam,
pṛthaktvāvṛttibahutvavṛttijātimattvam,
saṃyogāvṛttibahutvavṛttijātimattvam,
vibhāgāvṛttibahutvavṛttijātimattvaṃ lakṣaṇam|
parimāṇasya tu
saṃkhyāvṛttimahattvavṛttijātimattvam,
saṃkhyāvṛttyaṇutvavṛttijātimattvam,
saṃkhyāvṛttihrasvatvavṛttijātimattvam,
saṃkhyāvṛttidīrghatvavṛttijātimattvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| pṛthaktvasya tu
saṃyogavibhāgāvṛttyanekavṛttijātimattve sati
guṇatvaṃ lakṣaṇam| saṃyogasya tu
saṃkhyāpṛthaktva[a]vṛttyanekaniṣṭhajātimattve

satyadravyatvam,
saṃkhyāpṛthaktvavibhāgāvṛttyanekaniṣṭhajātimattve
sati guṇatvam,
saṃkhyāvṛttitantusaṃyogavṛttijātimattvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| vibhāgasya tu
pṛthaktvasaṃyogasaṃkhyāvṛttyanekavṛttiniṣṭhajātimattve
satyadravyatvam,
pṛthaktvasaṃyogasaṃkhyāvṛttyanekaniṣṭhavṛttijātimattve
sati guṇatvaṃ lakṣaṇam| paratvasya tu
saṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgavegāparatvakarmāvṛttimanoniṣṭhajātimattvam,
saṃyogāparatvavyatiriktaviśeṣaguṇarahitavibhusaṃyogajanyatvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| aparatvasya tu
saṃkhyāparimāṇasaṃyogavibhāgave[ga]pṛthaktvaparatvakarmatvāvṛttimanoniṣṭhajātimattvam,
saṃyogaparatvavyatiriktavibhuviśeṣaguṇarahitasaṃyogajanyatvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| buddhestu
icchāvṛttijñānavṛttijātimattvaṃ lakṣaṇam|
evamicchāpadasthāne prayatnādipadaprakṣepeṇa
bahūni buddhilakṣaṇāni draṣṭavyāni| sukhasya
tvadṛṣṭāvṛttijñānavyatiriktabhojanajanyātmaguṇavṛttijātimattvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| duḥkhasya
tvadṛṣṭāvṛttijñānavyatirikta
janyātmaguṇavṛttijātimattvaṃ lakṣaṇam|
icchāyāstu
adṛṣṭāvṛttighaṭajanakadevadattaguṇavṛttijātimattvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| dveṣasya
tvadṛṣṭāvṛttivirodhihiṃsājanakadevadattaguṇajanakadevadattaguṇavṛttijātimattvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| prayatnasya
tvadṛṣṭāvṛttighaṭajanakadevadattaguṇavṛttijātimattvaṃ
[lakṣaṇam]| gurutvasya
cādṛṣṭāvṛttighṛtamuktāphala
phalaguṇavṛttijātimattvaṃ lakṣaṇam| dravatvasya
tvadṛṣṭāvṛttyanyasyandanajanakajalavṛttijātimattvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| snehasya
tvadṛṣṭaprayatnadravatvāvṛttisaṃrambhajanakajanyaguṇavṛttijāti

saṃgrahajanakasaṃyogadravatvavyatiriktodakaguṇatvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| saṃskārasya
tvadṛṣṭāvṛttibhāvanāvṛttijātimattvaṃ
lakṣaṇam|
bhāvanāyāstvadṛṣṭavyatiriktasmṛtijanakajñānavyatiriktakāryajanakaviśeṣaguṇatvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| vegasya tu
sthitisthāpakabhāvanā[nya]saṃskāratvam,
sthitisthāpakavyatiriktakarmajanakasaṃskāratvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| sthitisthāpakasya tu
vegājanyakarmajanakasaṃskāratvaṃ lakṣaṇam|
adṛṣṭasya
tvasmatpratyakṣāvṛttisākṣādguṇatvāvāntarajātimadātmaguṇatvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| dharmasya
duḥkhajanakāvṛttisukhajanakavṛttijātimattvaṃ
lakṣaṇam,
guṇatvavyāvṛttaduḥkhajanaka[a]vṛttijātirahitasukhajanakaguṇatvaṃ
vā lakṣaṇam| adharmasya tu
sukhajanakāvṛttiduḥkhajanakavṛttijātimattvam,
guṇavyāvṛttasukhajanakāvṛttijātirahitaduḥkhajanakaguṇatvaṃ
vā lakṣaṇam| śabdasya tu śrotragrāhyaguṇatvam,
tvagindriyāgrāhyaśrotragrāhyajātimattvaṃ vā
lakṣaṇam|

utkṣepaṇasya
tūtkṣipāmītīcchājanyaprayatnajanyakarmatvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| avakṣepaṇasya
tvavakṣipāmītīcchājanyaprayatnajanyakarmatvam|
ākuñcanasya
tvākuñcāmītīcchājanyaprayatnajanyakarmatvam|
prasāraṇasya tu
prasārayāmītīcchājanyaprayatnajanyakarmatvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| gamanasya
tūtkṣepaṇāvakṣepaṇākuñcanaprasāraṇavyatiriktakarmatvaṃ
lakṣaṇam|

sāmānyasya tu nityatve satyanekāśritatve sati
samavāyitva[m], nityatve satyanekāśritatve sati
samavāyavyatiriktābhāvatvaṃ lakṣaṇam|

viśeṣasya tvanekāśritasattārahitasamavāyitvam,
anekānāśritasattārahitabhāvatvaṃ vā lakṣaṇam|

abhāvasya tu bhāvatvātyantābhāvaḥ|
prāgabhāvasya tvanāditve sati vināśitvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| pradhvaṃsasya tu kāryatve
satyavināśitvam, prāgabhāvapratiyogyabhāvatvaṃ
vā lakṣaṇam| atyantābhāvasya tu
prāgabhāvapradhvaṃsābhāvānyonyābhāvavyatirikta[a]bhāvatvaṃ
lakṣaṇam| anyonyābhāvasya tvanyonyābhāvatvaṃ
lakṣaṇam|

na cānyonyābhāvatve pramāṇābhāvaḥ,
ghaṭānyatvapaṭānyatvādīnāmanyonyābhāvatvāt|
paṭānyatvādiṣu
cedamanyatvamidamanyatvamityanugatānubhavasiddhasyānyatvasya[a]pratyakṣasiddhatvāt|
yathā paratvā[paratvā]diṣu idaṃ paratvamidaṃ
paratvamityanubhavasiddhaṃ paratvādikaṃ
dharmaviśeṣaḥ| tathā ghaṭapaṭānyatvādiṣu
anyatvadharmaviśeṣaḥ pratyakṣasiddho .artho
(dura)pahnavaḥ| anyathā paratvādiṣvapi
paratvadidharmāṇāmapahnavaprasaṅgāt|

na caiṣāṃ lakṣaṇānāṃ sūtrānārūḍhatvamiti
śaṅkanīyam| iti dravyalakṣaṇam, iti
guṇalakṣaṇam, iti karmalakṣaṇamiti
caśabdenaivaṃvidhalakṣaṇasaṃgrahāt|
itiśabdasyaivaṃprakāravācitvāt|
dravyaviśeṣaguṇaviśeṣakarmaviśeṣāṇāṃ ca
dravyaguṇakarmatvāt|
sāmānyādilakṣaṇānāmapyevaṃprakāratāt| na
dvitīyādhyāyādīnāṃ
lakṣaṇapratipādakānāṃ punaruktatvam, teṣāṃ
lakṣaṇaparīkṣaparatvāditi|| 16||

 iti
śrīharakiṅkaraparamapaṇḍitanyāyācāryabhaṭṭavādīndraviracite
vaiśeṣikasūtravārtike prathamādhyāye
prathamāhnikam|

dvitīyāhnikam|

atha kāraṇasāmānyaprakaraṇam|

 dravyaguṇakarmaṇāṃ dravyaṃ kāraṇam
sāmānyam|| 1-1-17||

dravyāṇi ca guṇāśca karmāṇi ca
dravyaguṇakarmāṇi, teṣāmeka[ṃ] dravyaṃ
kāraṇam| yathaikaḥ pārthivaḥ paramāṇuḥ
krameṇānantāni dvyaṇukādikrameṇānantā[ni
dravyāṇi, anantāṃ]śca
dravatvavegaparatvāparatvasaṃyogavibhāgānekasaṃkhyānekapṛthaktvāni
ca guṇānkrameṇānantāni ca karmāṇyārabhate,
tathāpyataijasavāyavīyāśca paramāṇavaḥ
krameṇānantāni
dvyaṇukasaṃskāraparatvāparatva
vibhāgānekasaṃkhyanekapṛthaktvakarmāṇyārabhante,
taijasāḥ paramāṇavaḥ naivamiti dravatvādīni ca
kāryadravyāṇi ca, dvyaṇukatantvādīni krameṇa
bahūni dravyāṇi rūpādīṃśca guṇānkarmāṇi
cārabhante, ye .api tantvādayo dve eva
dvitantu[kā]dike ekameva vā dvitantukādikaṃ
janayitvā dvitantukādikamajanayitvaiva vā
nivartante, teṣāmapi bahudravyajanakatvaṃ
tantutvādevānumeyam| bahudravyānutpattistu
tantuvināśādeva| yadvā, ye ca tantvādayo dve eva
dravye janayitvā nivartante, teṣāṃ
dvidravyajanakatvameva| ye tvekameva janyitvā
nivartante, teṣāmekadravyatvameva| ye tu
dravyamajanayitvaiva nivartante teṣāṃ
dravyājanakatvameva, antyāvayavimamānāṃstu[nāṃsi
tu] guṇakarmāṇyārabhante| ākāśaṃ tu guṇāneva|
saṃsāriṇastu
buddhisukhaduḥkhecchādveṣaprayatnadharmādharmabhāvanānekasaṃkhyānekapṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgānārabhante,
ghaṭādidravyaguṇakarmāṇi ca|
tasmādyadghaṭadravyaṃ yāvanti kāryāṇi janayati,
teṣāmekaṃ kāraṇamiti sūtrārthaḥ|

sādharmyāvasare
sādhāraṇakāraṇanirūpaṇamasaṅgatam, kāraṇasya
kāryadharmatvānupapatteriti cet---na,
sādhāraṇakāraṇanirūpaṇasya
tatkāryāṇāmatatkāryajanakatvadharmapratipādanārthatvāt|
tathā ca yaḥ paramāṇuryāvanti dravyaguṇakarmāṇi
ārabhante[te] tāvatāṃ tatparamāṇujanyatvaṃ
sādharmyamityādisamānadharmapratipādanāvasare
samānakāraṇasyāpi prasaṅgena
nirūpaṇasambhavācca|

kāraṇamapi kāryadharma evetyanye
idamatra kāraṇamiti pratīteḥ| anyathā dvaṇukādau
kāryatveneśvaraparamāṇvādikāraṇānanumānabhāvaprasaṅgāt|
kāraṇasya pakṣaniṣṭhatvāsambhavena pakṣe
sādhyabādhāt| sapakṣaniṣṭhatvāsambhavena
kāraṇena ca kāryatvasyānvayābhāvaprasaṅgācca|
īśvaraparamāṇvādīnāṃ
dvyaṇukānāśritatvāttaddharmatvāsambhava iti
cet---na, tadanāśritatve .api tadviśeṣaṇatvāt|
tadviśeṣaṇasyaiva dharmalakṣaṇatvāditi|

dravyāṇi dravyāntaramārabhante|| 1-1-8|| iti
sūtreṇa
idaṃ gatārthamiti cet---na, tasya
dravyārambhakaniṣṭhadravyārambhakatvādisādharmyapratipādakatvādasya
tattaddravyaviśeṣajanyadravyaguṇakarmaniṣṭhatattaddravyaviśeṣajanyatvādisādharmyapratipādanārthatvāt|
evamuttarasūtre
.api| guṇāśca guṇanataram [1-1-9]
ārabhante ityanena paunaruktyaṃ

pariharaṇīyamiti|| 17||

tathā guṇaḥ|| 1-1-18||

yathā dravyaṃ dravyaguṇakarmaṇāṃ sādhāraṇaṃ
kāraṇam, tathā guṇo
.apīśvarajñānecchaprayatnadharmādharmaiḥ
dravyaguṇakarmajananāt| tūlapiṇḍayorvartamānena
pracayanoda[na]acca saṃyogena guṇena
dvitūlakatadgatamahattvatūlapiṇḍakarmaṇāṃ
jananācca| yathā hi tūlapiṇḍayorvartamānaṃ
pracayanodanākhyasaṃyogastūlapiṇḍayordvitūlakaṃ
dravyamārabhate, ārambhakasaṃyogatvāt| tathā hi
dvitūlake mahattvaṃ pracayāt| apra
tulyamahattvadvayārabdhamahattvāt
dvitūlakatritūlakamahatvasyotkṛṣṭatvāt| tathā
tūlapiṇḍāntare karma nodanāditi tasmādyo guṇo
yāni dravyaguṇakarmāṇi janayati sa teṣāṃ
sādhāraṇamiti sūtrārthaḥ|

yastu dravyaguṇāveva janayati sa tayoreva
sādhāraṇaṃ kāraṇam| yathā
samavāyikāraṇasparśagatasparśayoḥ, sparśasya
dravyārambhakatvam, asparśānāṃ manasāṃ
dravyānārambhakatvāt| ya[va]stu guṇo guṇakarmaṇī
eva janayati sa tayoḥ sādhāraṇaṃ kāraṇam| yathā
avayavagurutvadravatvamahattvāni, teṣāmavayavini
gurutvadravatvamahattvārambhakatvāt, svāśraye ca
karmāramabhakatvāt| mūrtaparimāṇasya
karmajanakatvāt, amūrteṣu gaganādiṣu
karmānudayāt vego .api svāśraye karmārabhate,
vegavajjalāvayavārabdhe jalāvayavini vegamiti
praśastapādācāryāḥ|
tadātreyācāryā
nānumanyante, pramāṇābhāvāt|
vegavajjalāvayavārabdhe jalāvayavini vega eva tatra
pramāṇamiti cet---na, tasya
dravatvajanyakarmaṇaivopapatteḥ| tathā sati
dravatvakāla eva vego na syāditi cet---satyam, na
jāyata eva pramāṇābhāvāt| vegavajjalamiti
pratyakṣasya ca
dravatvajanyakarmāntarotpannavegenānyathāsiddhatvāt|
avayavavegasyāvayavivegājanakatve
vegarahitāvayavārabdhe .api tādṛśavegaprasaṅgaḥ
iti cet---na, iṣṭatvāt| nodanādijanyakarmaṇo .api
tādṛśavegodayāt| vegatvaṃ vegajanyavṛtti
vegavṛttitvāt, sattāvat| vegatvaṃ vegajanyavṛtti
vegāvyāvṛttatvāt, sattāvadityanumānaṃ
pramāṇamiti cet---na, avegavṛttitvena sopādhitvāt|
tadabhāvena satpratipakṣatvāt| jalāvayavivegasya
jalāvayavegajanyatvāsādhakatvenārthāntaragrastatvāt
ayaṃ vego na vegajanyo, vegatvāt, ityanumānācca| na
cātra pratipakṣasambhavaḥ, vegājanyatvena
sopādhitvāt| karmājanyatve
vegatvābhāvaprasaṅgācca| anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ

karmaṇo vegajanakatvāvadhāraṇāditi| yastu guṇo
guṇāneva janayati, sa guṇānāṃ sādhāraṇaṃ
kāraṇam| yathā paramāṇurūpaṃ
tajjanyānekadvyaṇukarūpāṇām, rasādayaśca
rasādīnāmiti| ādyaḥ śabdaḥ sarvatodiśānāṃ
śabdānām, bhāvanā ca smṛtīnām, vegaśca
karmaṇāmityādiḥ|| 18||

saṃyogavibhāgānāṃ karma|| 1-1-19||

kāraṇaṃ sāmānyamityanuvartate, upalakṣaṇaṃ
caitat| vege .api karmaṇaḥ kāraṇatvam| tena
yāvantaḥ saṃyogavibhāgavegāḥ yena karmaṇā
janyante, tāvatā tatkarma sādhāraṇam|
tajjanyatvaṃ ca teṣāṃ sādharmyamiti
sūtrārthaḥ|

idaṃ sūtramanārambhaṇīyameva,
saṃyogavibhāgaviśeṣāṇāṃ
saṃyogavibhāgatvāttatkarmaviśeṣajanyatvamatrādhikaṃ
pratipādyata iti cet---na, tasyāpi tata eva siddheḥ|
karmaviśeṣāṇāṃ saṃyogavibhāgaviśeṣajanakatve
pratipādite saṃyogavibhāgaviśeṣāṇāṃ
karmaviśeṣajanyatvasyārthasiddhatvāt| teṣāṃ
karmaviśeṣājanyatve karmaviśeṣāṇāṃ
tajjanakatvānupapatteḥ| tatsūtraṃ
karmaṇaḥ saṃyogavibhāgakāraṇatvapratipādakam|
idaṃ tu saṃyogavibhāgānāṃ karmakāryatve prati na
tasyāpyatatsiddhatvātsaṃyogavibhāgānāṃ
karmajanyatve karmaṇastajjanakatvāsambhavāt|
śabdapunaruktatvapadārthapunaruktatvasyāpi
dūṣaṇatvāt| saṃyogavibhāgānāṃ karmajanyasyāpi
karmaṇaḥ
saṃyogavibhāgakāraṇatvapratipādakatvācca|
karmaṇaḥ
saṃyogavibhāgakāraṇatvapratipādakamapīdaṃ
punaruktatayā saṃyogavibhāgānāṃ
karmakāryatvaparatayā vyākhyāyata iti cet---na,
tathāpyarthapunaruktatvasya tadavasthatvāt|
karmaṇaḥ saṃyogavibhāgakāraṇatvapratipādakatve
tasyāpi pūrvasūtrasya
saṃyogavibhāgānāṃ
karmajanyatvaparatayā vyākhyānāsambhavācca|
pūrvasūtrasyobhayaparatve
vākyabhedo doṣa iti
cet---na sūtreṣu
tasyādūṣaṇatvāt| saṃyogavibhāgāḥ karmaṇām
(1-1-28) ityanena gatārthatvaprasaṅgācceti na
saṃyogavibhāgadravyāṇāṃ karmakāraṇamiti
vaktavyam|| 19||

anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ paramāṇvādikarmaṇo
dvyaṇukādikāraṇatvāśaṅkāparihārārthaṃ
sūtram---

na dravyāṇāṃ vyatirekāt|| 1-1-20||

vyatirekāditi
dravyaguṇābhāvavyatiriktatvādityarthaḥ| tathā ca
karma na dravyajanakam,
dravyatvaguṇatvarahitabhāvatvāt, sāmānyavaditi| na
cātra sāmānyavattvābhāva upādhiḥ, gaganādau
sādhya[a]vyāpakatvabhaṅgāt| nāpi sāmānyavattvena
satpratipakṣatvam, gaganādau vyabhicārāditi|

yadvā, karma na dravyakāraṇam, vyatirekāt,
tadabhāve .api dravyasyodayāt|
yathai[dai]katantusaṃyogavināśādvinaṣṭe paṭe
.avasthitasaṃyogebhyaḥ paṭāntaramutpadyate, tadā
pūrvakṣaṇe
karmaṇo .abhāvānna tatpaṭajanakatvam|
yadā ca tantusaṃyuktasyāṃśoraṃśvantareṇa saṃyogāttantvantaramutpadyate tadanantaraṃ tvaṃśutantusaṃyogāttantoḥ saṃyogastadanantaraṃ ca dvitantukamutpadyate, na tadā dvitantukapūrvakṣaṇe karmāsti, tantvorniṣkriyatvāt|
aṃśukarmaṇaścāṃśudravyasaṃyogena
tantūtpattikṣaṇe avayavanāśāttena karmābhāve
.api dravyotpatterna karma dravyakāraṇam|

yadvā na karma dravyakāraṇam, vyatirekāt| satyapi
tasmin dūradeśasthitatantubhyāṃ
dvitantukānutpatteḥ| satyapi
tasminsaṃyogapūrvakṣaṇe dvitantukānutpatteśca|
svajanyasaṃyogādyārambhaḥ karmaṇāpekṣyata iti
cet---na, saṃyogādeva dravyasiddhau karmaṇaḥ
kāraṇatve pramāṇābhāvāt|
anvayavyatirekayorasaṃyogajanakatvenānyathāsiddhatvāt|
anyathā karmajanakanodanāderapi
dravyakāraṇatvaprasaṅgāt|

yadvā vyatiricyate dravyādibhyo bhidyate karmāneneti
vyatirekaḥ karmatvam, tasmātkarma na dravyakāraṇam|
tathā ca vimataṃ karma na dravyakāraṇam,
karmatvāt, ghaṭakarmavaditi|

yadvā vyatiricyate manaādibhyo bhidyate
dravyamaneneti vyatireko dravyatvam, (na) tasmātkarma
na dravyakāraṇam| tathā ca vimataṃ dravyaṃ na
karmajanyaṃ dravyatvāt, gaganavaditi|

yatpunarātreyācāryavyākhyānam---karma na
dravyakāraṇaṃ vyatirekāt vināśāt|
ārambhakasaṃyogādvinaṣṭe karmaṇi dravyodayāditi
tadayuktam, ārambhakasaṃyogāt, dravyotpattikṣaṇe
karmavināśe .api dravyapūrvakṣaṇe .api karmaṇo
vidyamānatvāt| vinaśyadavasthaṃ na kāraṇamiti
cet---na, vinaśyadavasthasyāpi dvitvādeḥ
dvitvaviśiṣṭadravyajñānādikāraṇatvāt| na
vinaśyadavasthānā[ma]samavāyikāraṇatvamiti
cet---na, pratyāsannasya
svāvadhṛtasāmarthyasyāsamavāyikāraṇatvaniṣedhe
bādhāt|
asamavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇasyātivyāpakatvācceti||

athādyaṃ karma uttarakarmaṇāṃ sādhāraṇaṃ kiṃ
na syādityāśaṅkāparihārārthaṃ sūtram---
 guṇavaidharmyānna karmaṇām|| 1-1-21||

karmaṇo guṇavaidharmyaṃ guṇatvābhāvaḥ| tasmānna
[karma] karmakāraṇam, tathā ca karma na
karmāsamavāyikāraṇam,
guṇa[tva]rahitatvādghaṭavaditi| karma na
dravyatvarahitābhāvatve sati karmakāraṇam,
guṇa[tva]rahitatvādghaṭavaditi| na cātra
pratipakṣasambhavaḥ, akarmatvena sopādhitvāditi|

yadvā, guṇavaidharmyāditi
karmāsamavāyikāraṇagurutvadravatvasaṃskārasaṃyogaguṇavaidharmyāditi
sūtrārthaḥ|
tadvaidharmyaṃ ca
gurutvadravatvasaṃskārasaṃyogarahitatvameva| tathā
ca karma na karmāsamavāyikāraṇam,
gurutvadravatvasaṃskārasaṃyogarahitatvādghaṭavaditi|
ādyapatanaṃ patanāsamavāyikāraṇam,
patanāsamavāyikāraṇa[ka]m, ādyapatanavyatiriktatve

sati gurutvasamavāyikāraṇajanyatvāt,
avayavigurutvavaditi satpratipakṣatvamiti cet---na,
prathamānumāne ādyapatanavyatiriktasyaiva karmaṇo
dharmitvena pratipakṣasyārthāntaragrastatvāt|
akarmatvena sopādhitvācca| ādyapatanatvaṃ ca kiṃ
patanajanakapatanamanyadvā| nādyaḥ,
hetorasiddhatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ, punaruktereveti,
etenādyasyandanaṃ syanadanajanakam,
anyasyandanānyatve sati
dravatvāsamavāyikāraṇajanyatvāt,
avayavavidravatvavaditi pratipakṣo nirastaḥ|
tasmātkarma na karmaṇāṃ sādhāraṇaṃ
kāraṇamiti|

idaṃ ca sūtramanārambhaṇīyameva
karma karmasādhyaṃ na vidyate [1-1-10] ityanenaiva
gatārthatvāt| tatra karmasādhyatvaṃ niṣidhyate,
atra punaranekakarmakāraṇatvaṃ niṣidhyata iti
cet---na, karmaṇaḥ
karmakāraṇatvaniṣedhādevānekakarmakāraṇatvaniṣedhāsiddheḥ,
karmajanakasyānupapatteḥ| atra pratijñāmātram, na
hetura[bhi]dhīyata iti na gatārthateti cet---na, atra
na karmaṇāmiti pratijñāyā avidyamānatvāt| tatra
karmatvena karmaṇaḥ karmakāraṇatvaṃ niṣidhyate,
atra tu guṇavaidharmyeṇeti cet---na, tatra
karmatvasya hetu(tve)nānabhidhānāt| tatra karmatvaṃ
ca hetutvamabhipretamiti cet---na, karmatvasyāpi
guṇavaidharmyānatirekāt| guṇavaidharmyaviśeṣaḥ
karmatvamatra hetutvenābhipretam| tadvyatiriktaṃ
guṇavaidharmyamatreti cet---na,
guṇavaidharmyamātrasya tatra hetutvavivakṣayā
sarvopapattāvetatsūtrapraṇayanavaiyarthyasya
tadavasthatvāt| tatra karmaṇaḥ karmājanakatve
pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇamabhipretam, iha tvanumānamiti
cet---na, pratyakṣeṇa karmaṇaḥ
karmājanakatvānanubhavāttatra
sādhakapramāṇābhāvo vivakṣitaḥ, atra
bādhakamanumānamabhidhīyata iti cet---na,
bādhakābhidhānādeva sādhakābhāve siddhe sati
sādhakasya bādhakatvānupapatteriti|| 21||

atha [kārya]sāmānyaprakaraṇam||

dravyāṇāṃ dravyaṃ kāryaṃ samānyam|| 1-1-22||

na sāmānyamekamaviśiṣṭamiti yāvat| tathānyaṃ
yaddravyaṃ yairdravyairjanyate, tatteṣāmekaṃ
kāryam| yathā dvyaṇukaṃ
paramāṇudvayadikkāleśvarāṇām| tryaṇukaṃ ca
dvyaṇukatrayadikāleśvaraṇāmityādi| yadā
dravyasamavāyikāraṇānāmiti vivakṣyate,
tadā dravye ca dravyāṇi ceti vigrahāśrayaṇātkiṃciddravyaṃ dvayordravyayoḥ kāryam|
yathā paramāṇvordvyaṇukam|
kiṃcidbahūnāṃ dravyāṇāṃ kāryam| yathā
trayāṇāṃ dvyaṇukānāṃ tryaṇukamiti|
tadanupapannam| tatra dravyāṇāṃ
dravyārambhakatvaṃ sādharmyaṃ vyutpādyate, atra tu
dvayordravyayoḥ bahūnāṃ vā dravyāṇāṃ
dravyārambhakatvaṃ vyutpādyata iti cet---na,
dvayorbahūnāṃ vārambhakatvasya tenaiva
gatārthatvāt| dravye dravyāṇi ceti vigrahasya
tatrāpi sambhavāt| punaruktaprasaṅgācca| tatra
yathā na vigraha iti cet---na,
tatraivaṃvidhavigrahāśrayāṇāṃ
vaitatsūtraprayojanasiddhāvetatsūtrārambhasya
niṣprabhāvatvāt| dvayorbahūnāṃ ca
dravyārambhakatvavyutpādakenānenaiva
dravyārambhakatvasiddheḥ pūrvasūtravaiyarthyasya
durvāratvācca|

tasmādanyathā sūtraṃ
vyākhyāsyate| kāryadravyaṃ dravyāṇāṃ
sāmānyaṃ sādharmyam| tathā
dvyaṇukaṃ

paramāṇvordvyaṇukaṃ ca trayāṇāṃ
dyaṇukāṇāmāśritatvasyaiva dharmalakṣaṇatvāt|
na caivaṃ pūrvasūtreṇa
gatārthatā, tena kāryadravyāṇāṃ
samānadharmatvāpratipādanāt| dravyāṇāṃ
dravyasamavāyikāraṇatvavyutpādanādarthasiddhaṃ
kāryadravyasyānekadravyādharmatvam,
svasamavetakāryārambhakatvasyaiva
samavāyikāraṇalakṣaṇatvāditi cet---na, tena
dravyasamavāyikāraṇatvāpratipādanāt,
dravyakāraṇatvamātrasya pratipādanāt| na
cānenānekadravyasamavāyikāraṇajanyatvaṃ
kāryadravyāṇāṃ vyutpādayatā dravyāṇāṃ
dravyakāraṇatvavyutpādanāt---dravyāṇi
dravyāntaramārabhante [1-1-8] ityasya gatārthatvam|
anena dravyāṇāṃ
dravyasamavāyikāraṇatvavyutpādanātkāryadravyāṇāṃ
samānadharmatvapratipādanāditi|| 22||

 tathā guṇāḥ|| 1-1-23||

yathā dvyaṇukādidravyaṃ
paramāṇudvayadikāleśvarādīnāṃ dravyāṇāṃ
samānaṃ kāryam, tathā dvyaṇukādirūpādayo .api
dvyaṇuka[adi]dikkāleśvarādidravyāṇāṃ samānaṃ
kāryamityarthaḥ|

ātreyācāryāstu
dvyaṇukarūpādīnāmekaikadvyaṇukādidravyakāryatvānupapatteḥ,
dvitvādīnāṃ cānekadravyakāryatvasya
dvitvaprabhṛtayaśca saṃkhyā ityanenaiva
gatārthatvādidaṃ sūtramaphalamiti
manvānā upekṣitavantaḥ| tadayuktam,
rūpādiviṣayasyopapādakatvāditi|| 23||

dvitvaprabhṛtayaśca saṃkhyāḥ
pṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgāśca|| 1-1-24||

kāryaṃ sāmānyamityanuvartate| dvitvādayaḥ
saṃkhyā dvipṛthaktvādīni pṛthaktvāni
sarvasaṃyogavibhāgāśca dravyāṇāṃ [anekeṣāṃ
samavāyināṃ] kāryaṃ sāmānyam| tathā [hi],
ghaṭapaṭa[dvi]tvaṃ ghaṭapaṭayoḥ kāryam|
ghaṭapaṭastambhatritvaṃ ca ghaṭapaṭastambhānāṃ
kāryam| ghaṭapaṭapṛthaktvaṃ ca ghaṭapaṭayoḥ
kāryam| ghaṭapaṭastambhapṛthaktvaṃ ca
ghaṭapaṭastambhānāṃ kāryamiti| saṃyogavibhāgau
ca dvayordvayoreva kāryamiti sūtrārtha
iti| tadanupapannam, asy sūtrasya
tathā guṇaḥ [1-1-18] ityanena gatārthatvāt|
anekasamavāyikāraṇajanyatvamena pratipādyata iti
cet---na pūrveṇaiva gatārthatvāt|
dikkāleśvarāṇāmapi samavāyikāraṇatvena
rūpādīnāmapyanekasamavāyikāraṇajanyatvāt| asya
pūrvasūtrasya
ca guṇāśca guṇāntaram [1-1-9] ityanenaiva
gatārthatvācca|

tasmādanyathedaṃ vyākhyeyam| yathā kāryadravyaṃ
dravyāṇāṃ sādharmyam, tathā tritvādayaḥ
saṃkhyāḥ pṛthaktvādīni ca| yathā
dvyaṇukādidravyaṃ dravyayoḥ sādharmyam, tathā
dvitvadvipṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgāśceti| na caivaṃ
guṇāśca guṇāntaram [1-1-9] ityādinā
gatārthatvena
dvitvādīnāmanekadravyasamānadharmatvāpratipādanātsarvamidamanupapannam,
ghaṭādivyatiriktasaṃkhānāmasattvāt guṇe
.apyakāryatvāt| kāryatve .api
kāraṇānirūpaṇāditi cet---na, ekatvasya prāgeva
vyutpādanāt| dvitvādīnāṃ ca dve
trīṇītyādipratyakṣasiddhatvāt| dvau
ghaṭāvityādipratyakṣeṇa ghaṭādiniṣṭhasya
dvitvāderanubhavāt| idaṃ dvitvametadghaṭādbhinnam,
etadviśeṣaṇatvādrūpavadityanumānācca| na
cāyamasiddhaḥ śuklo ghaṭa iti[vat] dvau
ghaṭāvityādipratyakṣasya sarvalokasiddhatvāt| na
caitaddvitvametadghaṭabhinnatvarahitam, etattvāt| na
yadevaṃ na tadevam, yathā paṭa ityādi vyatirekeṇa
satpratipakṣatvāt| etadghaṭabhinnatvasya
kvacidaprasiddhau
tadabhāvasyāprasiddhatvenāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt|
pramitau vā tenaiva nyāyena
dvitvasyācetanaghaṭabhinnatvasiddhervyatirekiṇo
bādhitatvāt| ghaṭādbhinnatve .api paṭatvasya
ghaṭeneva

paṭenāpi abhinnatvasvīkārāt| na caikatvadvayameva
dvitvam| idaṃ dvitvametadekatvadvayavyatiriktaṃ
dvitvāt, anyadvitvavaditi na[ca?] vyatirekasiddheḥ|
na caitaddvitvametadekatvadvayātmakamekatvāditi
vyatirekiṇā satpratipakṣatā|
etadekatvadvayātmakasya kvaciddravyasya
prasiddhatvenāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt| na
caitadghaṭapaṭadvitvametadghaṭaikatvātmakam,
etattvāditi vyatirekiṇā satpratipakṣatvam|
yadetadghaṭaikatvātmakaṃ na bhavati na
tadetaddvitvamiti vyatirekavyāpteḥ paṭaikatve
bhaṅgādbhavatā ghaṭapaṭadvitvasya
ghaṭapaṭaikātmakatvasvīkārāt| na
caitadghaṭapaṭadvitvametadghaṭapaṭaikatvavyatiriktatvarahitametattvāditi
pratipakṣaṇīyam, vikalpānupapatteḥ| tathā hi kiṃ
ghaṭapaṭaikatvapratiyogikaṃ yadetadvyatiriktatvaṃ
tadabhāvaḥ sādhyate, kiṃ vā
ghaṭapaṭaikatvapratiyogike dvitvavyatiriktatve
tayoreko bhāvaḥ sādhyate, kiṃ vā
ghaṭapaṭaikatvapratiyogike ye dve vyatiriktatve
tayoryadabhāvadvayaṃ tadvattā sādhyate, kiṃ vā
ghaṭapaṭaikatvapratiyogike ye dve vyatiriktatve
tayorabhāvadvayaṃ sādhyate, kiṃ vā
ghaṭapaṭaikatvapratiyogike ye dve vyatiriktatve
tadvattābhāvaḥ sādhyate| na prathamaḥ,
aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt| pratiyogibhedasya
vyaktibhedaniyatatvena
ghaṭapaṭaikatvapratiyogikaikatvavyatiriktatvānupapattau
tadabhāvasyāpyaprasiddhatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
ghaṭapaṭaikatvapratiyogike ye dve vyatiriktatve
tayorekasyābhāvasyānupapatteḥ
pratiyogibhedasyābhāvabhedaniyatatvāt| na
tṛtīyaḥ, aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvādeva|

ghaṭapaṭaikatvapratiyogike ye dve vyatiriktatve
tayoryadabhāvadvayaṃ tadvattvasya
kvacidapyaprasiddheḥ|
ghaṭaikatvapaṭaikatvapratiyogikavyatiriktaḥ
yavyatiriktatadabhāvadvayasya vyāhatatvāt|
ghaṭaikatve ca
ghaṭaikatvavyatiriktatvābhāvopapattāvapi
paṭaikatvavyatiriktatvābhāvānupapatteḥ|
vyatiriktatvadvayābhāvadvayasya bādhitatvāt| evaṃ
ghaṭaikatve .api| na caturthaḥ, viruddhatvāt
vyatiriktatvābhāvadvayādhikaraṇatvarūpaviruddhatvadharmāparaparyāyaghaṭapaṭaikatvabhedasādhakatvāt
ghaṭapaṭaikatvādiṣu| na ca
ghaṭapaṭaikatvavyatiriktatvābhāvadvayādhikaraṇatvasya

viruddhadharmatvāt| anapekṣitasādhakatve
padasyāpi viruddhatvāt| aviruddhatve vā
hetvābhāsāntaratvāt| aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ
punaḥ caturthapakṣe na sambhavatyeva|
ghaṭapaṭaikatvavyatiriktatvābhāvadvayasya
ghaṭapaṭaikatvaniṣṭhasya prasiddhatvāt|
abhāva dhikaraṇyamaprasiddhamiti cet--na,
sāmānādhikaraṇasya vyāpakāṃśaniveśabahāvāt|
na pañcamaḥ, arthāntaratvāt| na
ghaṭapaṭaikatvavyatiriktatvadvayādhikaraṇatvābhāvasya
prathamānumānavyāpakaghaṭapaṭaikatvavyatiriktatvadvayābhāvatvavirahāt,
tadvyāpyatvavirahāt| anyathā
ghaṭapaṭaikatvavyatiriktatvadvayādhikaraṇatvābhihite
ghaṭapaṭaikatve ca
ghaṭaikatvavyatiriktatvadvayābhāvadvayaprasaṅgāt|
na ca
ghaṭapaṭaikatvavyatiriktatvadvayādhikaraṇatvābhāvaḥ
pakṣīkṛtaḥ
