ghaṭapaṭaikatvavyatiriktatvadvayābhāvadvayaṃ
vinānavayavasya nityatvātsatpratipakṣatvamiti
vācyam| ghaṭapaṭaikatvādāviva tena vināpi
paryavasānāt
ghaṭapaṭaikatvavyatiriktatvadvayābhāvadvayaparyavasāne
ca pūrvopapāditaviruddhatvasya durvāratvācca| kiṃ
ca ghaṭapaṭaikatvaviruddhadharmatvena
ghaṭapaṭaikatvavyatiriktatvaṃ kvacidapramitaṃ vā|
nādyaḥ, aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt|
ghaṭapaṭaikatvavyatiriktatvāpramitau
tadabhāvasyāprasiddhatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
ghaṭapaṭaikāniṣṭhobhayavṛttitvarūpaviruddhadharmavattvena
dvitvabhāvighaṭapaṭaikatvavyatirekasiddheḥ
dvitvasyobhayavṛttitvaṃ ca dvāviti
pratyakṣāsiddham, saṃyuktāviti
pratyakṣasiddhatvamiva saṃyogasya|
etadghaṭavyatiriktavṛttitvaitatpaṭavyatiriktavṛttitvayoścaitadghaṭapaṭaikatvāniṣṭhatvena
tadviruddhadharmayoretadghaṭapaṭadvitve
pratyakṣasiddhatvācca| kiṃ
caitaddvitvametadghaṭavyatiriktavṛttitvayorādhāraḥ
dvitvādanyadvitvavaditi| na
caitaddvitvametadghaṭavyatiriktavṛttitvaitatpaṭavyatiriktavṛttitvayornādhāraḥ,
saṃyogatvavibhāgatvapṛthaktvarahitaitadghaṭaguṇavadrūpavaditi
satpratipakṣatvamiti vācyam, ayaṃ ghaṭaḥ
saṃyogatvavibhāgatvapṛthaktvarahitatvāneta(?)nniṣṭhaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ
dravyatvāt, paṭavaditi siddhe
guṇapratipakṣahetoranaikāntikatvāt| na
caitaddvitvametadghaṭapaṭaikatvavyatiriktamityatra
etaddvitvaśabdena kimetadghaṭapaṭaikatve eva
vivakṣite na tadvyatiriktapaṭe| ādye vyāghātaḥ,
dvitīye tvāśrayāsiddhiriti,
etadghaṭapaṭaikavyatiriktatvāsambhavācca|
vyatiriktatvābhyāṃ vipratipannasya dvāviti
pratyakṣasiddhasya dvitvasya dharmitvāddvitvaśabdena
kimetadghaṭapaṭaikatve eva vivakṣite
tadvyatiriktatvaṃ vā| anyathā vahnimānparvata
ityatra kiṃ vahnirahitaḥ parvato dharmaḥ
vahnimānvā| ādye vyāghātaḥ| dvitīye
tvāśrayāsiddhiriti vaktuṃ śakyatvāt| nanu
mūlānumānamayuktam,
dharmidṛṣṭāntadvitvabhedāsambhavāt|
tadasambhavaśca dvitvādīnāṃ jātitvena
sarvatraikatvāt| na ca dvitvādīnāṃ jātisaṃkramo
bādhakaḥ, tasyādūṣaṇatvāt| dvitvatritvādīnāṃ
sakaladravyapravṛttitvena
vyāpyavyāpakabhāvasambhavācca| nāpi
dvitvatritvādīnāṃ jātitve tulyavyaktitvaṃ
bādhakamaṅgīkārādeva| na ca dvitvatritvādīnāṃ
tu tayordravyaguṇādivyatiriktatvāt, gotvavaditi
cet---na, asiddheḥ,
guṇavyatiriktatvasādhakapramāṇābhāvāt,
guṇavisādhakatvasya vakṣyamāṇatvācca| nāpi
dvitvādayo jātayaḥ

nityatve satyanekasamavāyatvādit
vācyam, asiddhereva, dvitvādīnāṃ nityatve
pramāṇābhāvācca, anityatvasādhakasya
vakṣyamāṇatvācca| tasmādupapannaṃ
pūrvānumānamiti|

evaṃ tritvādayo .apyekatvavyatiriktāḥ
samarthanīyāḥ| nekasaṃkhyāyāḥ
pramāṇāntarasattvācca| ayaṃ ghaṭaḥ
kramasaṃyogavibhāgājanyapṛthaktvalakṣaṇākrāntāne(?)tanniṣṭhaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ
dravyatvāt, paṭavat| ayaṃ ghaṭaḥ
saṃyogatvavibhāgatvapṛthaktvānanyākrā
[dra]vyatvāt, paṭavat| ayaṃ ghaṭaḥ
anetanniṣṭhasaṃkhyādhikaraṇaṃ dravyatvāt,
paṭavat|

ayaṃ ghaṭaḥ kālaniṣṭhasaṃkhyādhikaraṇaṃ
dravyatvāt, kālavat| ākāśaṃ
pṛthaktvalakṣaṇānākrāntasaṃyogarahitakālaniṣṭhaguṇa
bhūsaṃyogenārthāntaratāparihārārthaṃ
saṃyogatvarahitagrahaṇam| na tu
vibhāgenārthāntaratātvasyobhayavādisiddhatvāt|
nāpi
pṛthaktvalakṣaṇānākrāntasaṃyogatvarahitakālaniṣṭhaguṇasambhavāt|
paṭasyā bhāgasya paṭe sambhavāt| na
caivamanekasaṃkhyāmātrasiddherdvitvādyasiddhiḥ,
anekasaṃkhyāsādhakānumānasādhyeṣu
bahuvṛttitvādhikaraṇatvabahutvajātyanadhikaraṇatvādiviśeṣaṇopādā
pṛthaktvalakṣaṇānākrāntākāśatvaniṣṭhaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ
dravyatvāt, ghaṭavat| ayaṃ ghaṭa
ākāśaghaṭaniṣṭhasaṃkhyādhikaraṇaṃ
dravyatvāt, paṭavadityādinā

ca bahutvasiddheḥ| bahutvasādhakānumānasādhyeṣu
ca mānena dvitvasiddheḥ| ayaṃ stambhaḥ
pṛthaktvalakṣaṇā(nā)krāntākāśaghaṭapaṭaniṣṭhaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ
dravyatvāt, ghaṭavadityādinā catuṣṭvasiddheḥ|
ayaṃ stambhaḥ
ākāśaghaṭapaṭaniṣṭhasaṃkhyādhikaraṇaṃ
saṃkhyāsiddhiḥ| ayaṃ stambhaḥ
etatpratyetatstambhākāśaghaṭapaṭavyatiriktavṛttitvarahitākāśaghaṭapaṭaniṣṭhapṛthaktvarahitaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ
dravyatvāt, ghaṭavat| ayaṃ stambhaḥ
etatpratyetatstambhākāśaghaṭa[paṭavyatiriktavṛttitvarahitākā]śaghaṭapaṭaniṣṭhasaṃkhyādhikaraṇaṃ
dravyatvāt ghaṭavadityādinā catuṣṭvasiddheḥ|
anenaiva nyāyenottarāṇāṃ tattatsaṃkhyāsiddhiḥ|

parārdhātiriktānekasaṃkhyā nāstīti
praśastaḥ|
yadātre[yaḥ saṃ]khyā
tu dvitvādikā parārdhānteti tadanupapannam,
parārdhādisaṃkhyāyā api pūrvoktasiddhatvāt|
saikaṃ parārdhamityanubhavasiddhatvācca| anyathā
parārdhādisaṃkhyāyā adṛśyarūpatvāt|
athaikatva kaṃ parārdhamiti vyavahāropapatteḥ
na parārdhādhikasaṃkhyāsiddhiriti manyase, tanna,
ekatvadaśasaṃkhyādinaiva
ekādaśādivyavahāropapatterekādaśasaṃkhyāvilayaprasaṅgāt|
parārdhaṃ kiṃcitsaṃkhyāvyū mānācca|
yataḥ paramā na saṃkhyā tatparārdhamiti
dharmigrāhakapramāṇena siddhau vyāghāto veti
cet---na, evaṃsiddhaparārdhasya
niṣpramāṇakatvenāsmābhirdharmitvenānupādānāt|
ekaṃ daśakamityādi
daśottaravṛddhisiddhasahasrādi
saṃkhyāyāśca dharmitvāt| tasmādanantaiva
saṃkhyā na parārdhānteti|

dvitvādiṣu ca saṃkhyātvaṃ pratyakṣasiddhameva,
ekatvaṃ saṃkhyā dvitvaṃ saṃkhyeti
pratītiprayogayoḥ sarvalokasiddhatvāt| asti
caikatvadvitvādiṣu gaṇanāvyavahāraḥ|
saṃkhyātvaṃ na [tvasya] ca jātitvaṃ
gotvasamānanyāyasiddham|
ekatvamekatvavṛttyanekatvavyāvṛttatvarahitaikatvavṛttyanekatvarahitajātimat,

jātimattvāt, ghaṭavat gotvavadityanumānācca|

na ca dvitvādiguṇatve pramāṇābhāvaḥ|
dvitvā mātrapramāṇatvāt|
paṭamahattvajanakatantubahutvaṃ guṇaḥ
parimāṇāsamavāyikāraṇatvāt, apaṭatve
satyadviṣṭhapaṭaguṇajanakatantusamavetatvāt|
apaṭatve sati
mahattvajanakatantusamavetatvātpracayavadityanu[mānāt
]tvena mahattvajanakabahutvavat guṇatvānumānācca|

nanu saṃkhyā na guṇaḥ
sampratipannaguṇānatiriktatvāt, paṭavat, vimataṃ
bahutvaṃ na guṇaḥ
parimāṇatvasaṃyogatvarahitāmahattvajanakatantuniṣṭhatvāt,
paṭavat| mahattvājanaka
mahattvajanakasaṃkhyāvadbahutvaṃ na guṇaḥ,
pṛthaktvātiriktabahusamavetatvāt, gotvavaditi
satpratipakṣaviraheṇa sopādhitvamiti cet---na,
vikalpāsahatvāt| [na] guṇa iti kiṃ
guṇatvābhāvaḥ sādhyate, guṇalakṣaṇābhāvo vā,
[dravyatvakarmatvarahi]tajātimattvābhāvo vā,
guṇaśabdapravṛttinimitta[tva]abhāvo vā,
[karmatvarahitāsamavāyikāraṇatvābhāvo,
asamavāyikāraṇatvābhāvo vā]| na prathamaḥ,
aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvātprābhākaranyāyabhūṣaṇavṛttikārādīnāṃ
guṇatvāsiddheḥ tadabhāvasyāprasiddhatvāt| na
dvitīyaḥ,
[saṃkhyāvyāvṛttaguṇalakṣaṇābhā]vasādhane
siddhasādhanātsaṃkhyāvyāvṛttatvarahitaguṇalakṣaṇābhāvasādhane[ca]
bādhāt| arthāntaratvācca| guṇalakṣaṇābhāvasya
guṇatvābhāvatvavirahāt|
guṇatvānaṅgīkāravādināṃ
guṇalakṣaṇābhāvasya
[guṇatvābhāvavyāpyatvānaṅgī]kārācca|
guṇatvāsiddhau tadabhāvasyāsiddhatvena
tadvyāpyatvasyāpyasiddheḥ| na tṛtīyaḥ,
arthāntaratvāt| balācca (?)
dravyakarmatvarahitajātimattvasya
pratyakṣasaṃkhyayāṃ pratyakṣasiddhatvācca| na
caturthaḥ, pū[rvopapāditārthāntaragrastatvāt],
sidhasādhanācca, saṃkhyāguṇavādināpi
saṃkhyāyāṃ
dravyatvakarmatvasaṃkhyātvarahitajātimattvābhāvasyāṅgīkārāt|
na pañcamaḥ, pūrvavadarthāntaragrastatvāt,
guṇatvābhāvena sopādhitvācca| na cā[nyaḥ
pūrvavadarthāntaratvāt| ]siddhau
guṇatvābhāvasyāpyasiddhatvena tasya
sādhanavyāpakatvānupapatteḥ| na ca guṇatvāsiddhau
guṇatvābhāvasyāpyasiddherupādheḥ
svarūpa[a]siddhatvamapi vācyam,
asyābhinnaguṇatvasya sa ratvāt| guṇatvasya
guṇaśabdapravṛttinimittatvābhāvamātrapracaritārthatvenāsya
saṃkhyātvasya
guṇaniṣedhasādhakatvenāpyarthāntaratvāt|
guṇatvābhāvena sopādhitvācca| na cāsya
sādhanavyāpya na saṃkhyātvaṃ na guṇajātiḥ
saṃkhyāvṛttitvāt, meyatvavadityādayaḥ
pratipakṣā nirastāḥ|
guṇaśabdārthānirūpaṇātsaṃkhyātvasya
jātitvābhāvamātrasiddhasya caritārthatvena
saṃkhyāyā guṇatvābhāvā [na] ca vādino
.api saṃkhyāguṇatvasādhane
aprasiddhaviśeṣa[ṇa]tvam, asmābhirguṇatvasya guṇaśabdapravṛttivyutpādanāditi|
na ca dvitvādīnāṃ kāryatve pramāṇābhāvaḥ ghaṭādivat,
ghaṭādiniṣṭhadvitvābhāvamātrasiddha mapi
kāryatvasya pratyakṣataḥ siddhatvāt| anyathā
ghaṭādīnāmapi kāryatvābhāvaprasaṅgāt|
anumānācca| ghaṭaniṣṭhamanekatvaṃ
kāryaṃ kāryaguṇatvāt anekaguṇatvāt
ghaṭapaṭaguṇatvāt saṃyogavaditi| na cāsya
sādhanavyāpakatvam,
ghaṭaikatvabhāvakāryaguṇatvasyaikatvābhāvavyabhicārāt|
anaikāntikaṃ ca kāryaguṇatvaṃ ghaṭaikatve
ghaṭaikatvaṃ na diti
ghaṭaikatvasyākāryatvasiddheriti cet---na,
ekatvamekatvānekavṛttirahitasaṃkhyātvarahitāvṛttikāryaniṣṭhajātimat,
saṃkhyātvāt, dvitvavaditi siddhe kāryaikatve
upādheḥ sādhyavyāpakatvabhaṅgāt vyabhicāra[ḥ]
sādhakānumānasyānaikāntikatvācca|

na ca nityamātraniṣṭhadvitvādīnāṃ kāryatve
pramāṇābhāvaḥ, dharmigrāhakapramāṇādeva
tatsiddheḥ| yasya kasyāpi
nityamātraniṣṭhadvitvādisādhakasya
dvitvādikāryatvasādhakatvāt
nityamātraniṣṭhadvitvādi
kāryamanekasaṃkhyātvāt, ghaṭabahutvavaditi|

na ca nityamātravṛttidvitvādikaṃ na kāryaṃ
nityamātravṛttisaṃkhyātvāt, akāśaikatvavaditi
satpratipakṣatvam| kālo
.anityavṛttitvarahitasaṃyogatvavibhāgatvapṛthaktvarahitānetanniṣṭhakāryaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ
nityadravyatvādātmavaditi
siddhāyāmanityavṛttitvarahitakāryasaṃkhyāyāṃ
vyabhicārāt| na cārthāntaratā,
saṃyogatvavibhāgatvarahitapṛthaktvarahitākālaniṣṭhakālaguṇatvasya
kālaniṣṭhānekasaṃkhālakṣaṇatvāt
anityavṛttitvarahitasaṃyogatvavibhāgatvapṛthaktvarahitākālakālaniṣṭho
guṇaḥ
anekasaṃkhyāsaṃyogatvavibhāgatvapṛthaktvarahitā
kālaguṇatvāt, kālaniṣṭhadvitvavaditi manyase|
tasyānekasaṃkhyātvasiddhatve ca kālo
nānityavṛttitvarahitasaṃyogatvavibhāgatvapṛthaktvarahitānetanniṣṭhakāryaguṇādhikaraṇam,
ātmatvarahitākāśānyamūrtatvāt, rūpavaditi
satpratipakṣatvam| pratipakṣahetorāśritatvena
sopādhitvāt|

na ca dvitvādīnāmanityatve pramāṇābhāvaḥ,
kāryatvasādhakādeva tatsiddheḥ| kāryatve
cānumānam| na ca dvitvādīnāṃ
kāraṇatvānupapattiḥ| dravyāṇāṃ
samavāyikāraṇatvāt, ekatvādīnāṃ
cāsamavāyikāraṇatvāt, apekṣābuddhyādīnāṃ ca
nimittakāraṇatvāt| na cātra pramāṇābhāvaḥ,
dravyādīnāṃ dvitvādijanakatvasya
pratyakṣasiddhatvāt| anekasaṃkhyā ekatvajanyā
kāryasaṃkhyātvāt ghaṭaikatvavadityanumānācca| na
cānekasaṃkhyā naikatvajanyā anekaniṣṭhatvāt,
saṃyogavibhāgavaditi satpratipakṣatvam| paramāṇuḥ
saṃyogatvavibhāgatvapṛthaktvarahitaikatvajanyamahattvaniṣṭhaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ
dravyatvāditi siddhāyāḥ
paramāṇumahanniṣṭhasaṃkhyāyā api dharmitve
dharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhāttadvyatiriktānekasaṃkhyāyāśca
dharmitve tatraivānaikāntikatvāt|

na cātrārthāntaratā,
saṃyogatvapṛthaktvarahitamahatparamāṇuniṣṭhaguṇatvasya
mahatparamāṇuniṣṭhānekasaṃkhyālakṣaṇatvāt,
saṃyogatvavibhāgatvapṛthaktvarahitaikatvajanyamahatparamāṇuniṣṭho
guṇaḥ
mahatparamāṇuniṣṭhānekasaṃyogavibhāgatvapṛthaktvarahitamahatparamāṇuguṇatvāt,
mahatparamāṇuniṣṭhadvitvavadityanumānācca| na
caivaṃvidhe sthale pratipakṣasambhavaḥ
prativādinaścāśrayāsiddherityuktam|

ghaṭapaṭadvitvādīnāmapekṣābuddhijanyatve
pramāṇam ghaṭapaṭadvitvaṃ
ghaṭapaṭaikatvagocarabuddhijanyam, kāryatvāt,
saṃskāravat| īśvarāpekṣābuddhijanyatve[na]
pramāṇam---ghaṭapaṭadvitvaṃ
ghaṭapaṭaikatvagocareśvara[apekṣābuddhi]janyaṃ
kāryatvādityādi| na cātra dṛṣṭāntāsambhavaḥ,
ghaṭapaṭaikatvagocareśvarabuddhiḥ kāryajanikā
buddhitvāt, asmadbuddhivaditi dṛṣṭāntasiddheḥ| na
cāsyātyabuddhau vyabhicāraḥ, tasyā api
pradhvaṃsajanakatvā[t| na ca]
ghaṭapaṭaikatvagocaratvāsiddheratrāśrayāsiddhatvam,
ghaṭapaṭaikatvādikamīśvarabuddhiviṣayo
meyatvāditi īśvarabuddheḥ
ghaṭapaṭaikatvagocaratvasiddheḥ| na cātrāpi
ghaṭadṛṣṭāntābhā siddhiḥ kiṃviṣayiṇī
buddhitvāditi tatsiddheḥ īśvarabuddheḥ
kiṃcitkāryatvakiṃcidviṣayatvayoḥ
dharmigrāhakapramāṇādeva siddheśca|

asmadāsyapekṣābuddhijanyatve dvitvādīnāṃ
pramāṇam---ahaṃ
ghaṭapaṭa[dvi]tvajanakaghaṭapaṭaikatvagocarasākṣātkāravān,
ātmatvāt, īśvaravadityādi| na
cātreśvaratvamupādhiḥ,
kṣityādikartṛtvanityajñānatvādirūpeśvaratvasya
vipakṣarūpaṃ vyāvartyaṃ hi tadviśeṣaṇatvena
pakṣetaratvāt|
īśvaraniṣṭhāntyaviśeṣaikatvādīnāṃ ceśvaro
ghaṭapaṭadvitvajanakaghaṭapaṭaikatvagocarasākṣātkāravadanyohamayatvāt
[anyo meyatvāt] ghaṭapaṭavaditi siddhe
ghaṭapaṭadvitvajanakaghaṭapaṭaikatvagocarasākṣātkāravati
sādhyāvyāpakatvenānupādhitvāt|

na ceśvaratvenāsya satpratipakṣatvam, upādheḥ
sādhyavyāpakatvabhaṅgasthale vyabhicārāt| na ca
nāhaṃ
ghaṭapaṭadvitvajanakaghaṭapaṭaikatvagocarasākṣātkāravānmadanyacetanarahitarahitavānghaṭavaditi
satpratipakṣatvam, anātmatvena sopāhikatvāt|

kiṃ ca ghaṭapaṭaikatvagocaro matsākṣātkāraḥ
anekatvajanakaḥ
ghaṭapaṭaikatvagocarasākṣātkāratvāt,
īśvarasākṣātkāravaditi| na cāsya
vinaśyadavasthaghaṭapaṭaikatvagocarasākṣātkāre
vyabhicāraḥ, tasyāpi dvitvajanakatvāt|
tadanantarakṣaṇe ca ghaṭapaṭadvitvānupapatteśca
ghaṭapaṭavināśādevopapatteḥ| na ca
ghaṭapaṭaikatvagocaro manniṣṭhasākṣātkāro
nāśikatva(?)janako matsākṣātkāratvāt,
anyamatsākṣātkāravaditi satpratipakṣataṃ
prācīnānumānasiddhadvitvajanakaghaṭapaṭaikatvagocaramanniṣṭhasākṣātkāre
vyabhicārāt| etadghaṭapaṭadvitvasya
etadghaṭapaṭaikatvagocaramatsākṣātkārajanyatve
pramāṇam---ahametaddvitvajanakaitadghaṭapaṭaikatvagocarasākṣātkāravān,
yogadharmājanyaitaddvitvasākṣātkāratvāt,
īśvaravaditi| na ca
yogadharmājanyatvaviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyam,
etaddvitvajanakaitadghaṭapaṭaikatvagocarasākṣātkāravirahitasya
taddvitvasākṣātkāravaditi
ciramukterbhaviṣyanmukteśca
vyabhicāraparihārārthatvāt|

na ca
nāhametaddvitvajanakaitaghaṭapaṭaikatvagocarasākṣātkāravān,
dvitvāt, sampratipannavadityādinā
satpratipakṣatvamīśvara
etaddvitvajanakaitadghaṭapaṭaikatvagocarasākṣātkāravadanyo
meyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi siddheḥ|
etaddvitvajanakaitadghaṭapaṭaikatvagocarasākṣātkāravatīśvaravyatirikte
cetanatvena duḥkhādhikaraṇe vyabhicārāt|
idametadghaṭapaṭaikatvagocaraṃ
jñānametaddvitvajanakam,
etadanyakāryatvarahitajñānatvāt,
īśvarajñānavaditi| na ca nedametaddvitvajanakaṃ
jñānatvātsampratipannavadityādinā
satpratipakṣatvam,
prācīnānumānasiddhaitaddvitvajanakatanniṣṭhasākṣātkāre
vyabhicārāt| na caitaddvitvaṃ
naitadghaṭapaṭaikatvagocarasākṣātkārajanyam,
dvitvāt, anyadvitvavaditi satpratipakṣatvam,
prathamānumānavyāpakābhāvādvyāpyatvāt| na
cārthātsatpratipakṣatvam,
prathamānumānasādhyābhāvamantareṇa
dvitīyānumānasādhyāparyavasānasya
nirvaktumaśakyatvāt| idaṃ ghaṭapaṭaikatvagocaraṃ

jñānaṃ dvitvajanakam,
etadanyakāryatvarahitajñānatvāditi siddhe[ḥ],
etajjñānajanyadvitve vyabhicārācca|
tanniṣṭhaitadghaṭapaṭaikatvagocaraitatsākṣātkārajanyadvitvavyatiriktadvitvānāmasmadādiniṣṭhāpekṣābuddhijanyatve
pramāṇam---manniṣṭhaikatvagocarasākṣātkārajanyadvitvavyatiriktāsmadādipratyakṣadvitvādiyogajadharmājanyajanyaikatvagocarasākṣātkārajanyādiyogajadharmājanyasākṣātkāragocaradvitvāt,
ghaṭapaṭaikatvagocarasākṣātkārajanyadvitvavat|

yadvā dvitvasāmānyamekatvasyāparajñānajanyavṛtti
dvitvāvyāvṛttatvāt, sattāvat| na cātra
dvitvavṛttitvamupādhiḥ, asiddhaviśeṣasya
vipakṣarūpavyāvartyarahitatvenāsya pakṣetaratvāt|
avṛttīnāṃ pravṛttigrahaṇenaiva vyudāsāt|
dvitvamātravṛttīnāṃ ca dvitva
sāmānyavyatiriktānāṃ pakṣatulyatvāt|
dvitvasāmānyasya ca pakṣatvāditi|

na ca dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatvam, satāyā
ekatvajñānajanyaikatvaviśiṣṭadravyajñānādivṛttitvāt|
na cāsya ekadvitvāpekṣābuddhijatvamātreṇa
caritārthatvāt| na
sarvadvitvānāmapekṣābuddhijatvamasiddhiriti
vācyam, vipratipannāni
dvitvānyekatvagocarajñānajanyāni dvitvāt,
ekatvavaditi siddheḥ| evaṃ dvi[tri?]tvādiṣvapi|

kiṃ ca dvitvādīni ekatvagocarajñānajanyāni
anekasaṃkhyātvāt, dvitvavaditi| yadvā
sarvadvitvādīni ekatvagocarajñānajanyāni
ekatvajñānajanyaitatpradhvaṃsābhāvasādhya[a]vyāpakatvāt,
ekatvajñānajanyasaṃskāre .api
sādhyāvyāpakatvācca|

ekatvaviśiṣṭadravyajñānajanyaḥ saṃskāraḥ, na
tvekatvajñānajanya iti cet---na,
ekatvaviśiṣṭadravyajñānasyāpyekatvagocaratvenaikatvajñānatvāt|
na ca kāryatvasya ghaṭādau vyabhicāra(sta)syāpi
ekatvagocareśvarajñānajanyatvāt| na cātra
saṃkhyātvābhāva upādhiḥ,
ekatvagocareśvarajñānajanyatvaghaṭaikatvādau
sādhyāvyāpakatvāt| dvitvādikaṃ
naikatvajñānajanyaṃ saṃkhyātvādekatvavaditi
cet---na, ghaṭādiniṣṭhaikatvasya
dṛṣṭāntatve ghaṭādiniṣṭhaikatvavyabhicārāt|
tasyeśvaraniṣṭhaikatvavyabhicārāt|
tasyeśvaraniṣṭhaikatvagocarajñānajanyatvāt|
ghaṭādiniṣṭhaikatvaṃ
pakṣatulyamīśvarāsiddheriti cet---na, bhagavataḥ
sarvajñasya vyutpāditatvāt| akāryatvena
sopādhitvāt|
pūrvopapāditaikatvajñānajanyadvitvādau
vyabhicārācca|

yadvāyaṃ ghaṭa
etadghaṭapaṭaikatvagocarajñānājanyaitanniṣṭhatvarahitadvitvādhikaraṇaṃ
dvitvādhikaraṇatvāt, paṭavaditi| yadvāyaṃ ghaṭo
ghaṭapaṭaikatvagocara[ma]jñānājanyaghaṭaniṣṭhatvarahitadvitvādhikaraṇaṃ
sparśādhikaraṇatvāt, paṭavadityādi| yadvāhaṃ
dvitvājanakamanniṣṭhatvarahitaikatvajñānavān,
ātmatvāt, anyavat|
yadvāhametaddvitvājanakamanniṣṭhaikatvarahitaikatvajñānavān,
ātmatvāt, anyātmavat| yadvāyaṃ
ghaṭapaṭaikatvagocarasākṣātkāraḥ
etaddvitvaparamakāraṇam, ekatvāt, na yadevam na
tadevam, yathā gaganamiti| na
cātraitaddvitvaparamakāraṇatvāniruktirdoṣāya|
etaddvitvajanakaprāgabhāvasamānakālīnatvarahitaitaddvitvakāraṇatvasya
kṣaṇākāraṇatva
etadvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇotpannā kāraṇatvasya
kṣaṇakāraṇatvasya kṣaṇakāraṇatve ca
kṣaṇavyatirikta
ekatvavyavahitapūrvakṣaṇotpannaitatparamakāraṇatvāt|
idaṃ dvitvametadvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇotpannajanya
etadutpattikṣaṇotpanna
etadutpattikṣaṇotpannapaṭavaditi sādhyaprasiddheḥ|
etaddvitvacaramakāraṇasyobhayavādisiddhatvācca|

na cāyaṃ ghaṭapaṭaikatvasākṣātkāro
naitaddvitvaparamakāraṇam, sākṣātkāratvāt,
sampratipannavaditi satpratipakṣatvam|
ahametaddvitvāccaramakāraṇamanniṣṭhatvarahitasākṣātkāravān,
ātmatvāt, anyātmavaditi siddhe
etaddvitvacaramakāraṇasākṣātkāre vyabhicārāt,
na cātra sākṣātkāratvābhāva upādhiḥ,
pratipakṣahetorvyabhicārasthale
sādhyavyāpakatvābhāvāt| sādhyāvyāpakatvādiśca
etaddvitvacaramakāraṇasya
sākṣātkāritvābhāvāniścayāditi| na ca
dvitvādīnāṃ vināśakāraṇānupapattiḥ,
apekṣābuddhivināśāśrayavināśayoḥ sambhavāt|
tathā
caitaddvitvajanakaikatvagocarasākṣātkāranāśa
etaddvitvanāśacaramakāraṇametattvāt, na yadevaṃ
na tadevam, yathā gaganamityādi|

na cātrāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam, pūrvoktādeva
nyāyāt| nāpyayamekatvagocarasākṣātkāravināśo
naitaddvitvavināśacaramakāraṇam, vināśatvāditi
satpratipakṣatvam|
ahametaddvitvavināśacaramakāraṇatvarahitamanniṣṭhaikatvarahitaikatvasākṣātkāranāśavān,
ātmatvāt, anyātmavaditi siddhe
etaddvitvavināśacaramakāraṇavināśe vyabhicārāt|

na ca prakṛte vināśatvābhāva upādhiḥ,
pratipakṣahetorvyabhicārasthale
sādhyavyāpakatvabhaṅgāt,
sādhyavyāpakatvāniścayācca|
etaddvitvatvavināśacaramakāraṇasya
vināśatvābhāvāniścayāt|
etattvagocarasākṣātkāranāśasya
etaddvitvavināśajanakatve pramāṇam|
ahametaddvitvavināśajanakatvasākṣātkāravināśavān,
ātmatvāt, anyavaditi anyadvitvasya
dvitvanimittakāraṇavināśavināśyatve pramāṇam|
idaṃ dvitva[tvaṃ]
nimittakāraṇavināśadravyavināśavināśyam,
dvitvāt,
īśvarāpekṣābuddhijaparamāṇudvitvavaditi|

na ca dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatvam,
īśvarāpekṣābuddhijaparamāṇudvitvasya
tannimittakāraṇādṛṣṭavināśavināśyatvādvipratipannaṃ
pratīśvarāpekṣābuddhijanyaṃ paramāṇudvitvaṃ

dvitvakāraṇavināśavināśyam, dvitvāt,
āśrayavināśavināśyadvitvavadityanumānāt| na
cātra samavāyikāraṇavināśasambhavaḥ
paramāṇutadekatvayoravināśitvāt|
āśrayavināśavināśyaghaṭapaṭadvitvādīnāṃ
dvitvādikāraṇavināśavināśyatve .apīdameva
pramāṇamiti|

nanu dvitvatritvādīnāṃ bhedānupapattiḥ,
kāraṇabhedāsambhavāt| dvitvasya tu dve
samavāyikāraṇe tritvasya trīṇītyapi na yuktam|
dvitvatritvotpatteḥ pūrvaṃ dve
trīṇītyasambhavāttritvasya dvitve
asamavāyikāraṇa[tva]m| tritvasya
ekatvānyasamavāyikāraṇānītyapi na yuktam,
uktadoṣāt| ekatvādau dvitvāderasambhavācca|
ekatvadvyajñānaṃ dvitve kāraṇam|
ekatvatrayajñānaṃ ca tritve kāraṇamityapi na
yuktam| pūrvoktadoṣāditi| tadayuktam,
vikalpānupapatteḥ| kimatra kāraṇabhedābhāvena
bhedābhāva āpadyate, anumīyate vā| nādyaḥ,
mūlaśaithilyāt| dvitvājanakakāraṇajanyatve .api
nityeṣu dvitvabhedavattvāt|
dvitvājanakakāraṇajanyatvena
dvitvabhinnakāryatvābhāva āpadyata iti cet---na,
dvitvādīnāṃ
dvitvājanakakāraṇajanyatvābhyupagamāt|
ghaṭapaṭastambhatritvādīnāṃ
ghaṭapaṭadvitvājanakastambhagataikatvatadviṣayabuddhīnāmapi
kāraṇatvāt|
ghaṭapaṭastambhatritvājanyajanakakāraṇājanakatvena
ghaṭapaṭadvitvena
ghaṭapaṭastambhatritvādijanakakāryatvābhāva
āpādita iti cet---na, ghaṭapaṭadvitve .api
ghaṭapaṭatritvājanakastambhagataikatvāviṣayāsmadādibuddheḥ
kāraṇatvāt|

paramāṇudvayatadatiriktadravyaniṣṭhatritvādanyajanakakāraṇājanyatvena
paramāṇudvayatadatiriktaniṣṭhatritvādabhinnakāryatvābhāva
āpadyata iti cet---na, paramāṇudvitve .api
paramāṇudvayatadatiriktaniṣṭhaikatvāviṣayaparamāṇudvayaniṣṭhaikatvaviṣayaparameśvarabuddheḥ
kāraṇatvābhyupagamāt| īśvarajñānasya
sarvaviṣayatvāt|
paramāṇudvayavyatiriktaniṣṭhaikatvāviṣayatvena
ghaṭata iti cet---na, īśvare
sarvaviṣayajñānasarvapadasarvaviṣayajñānābhyupagamāt|
na caivamīśvare sarvajñatvabhaṅgaprasaṅgaḥ,
sarvaviṣayajñānasyābhyupagamāt|
sarvavastusarvaviṣayajñānābhyupagame .api
sarvajñatvopapatteḥ| na ca
sarvaviṣayaikajñānasyābhāvāpattirdoṣāya,
iṣṭatvāt| na
ceśvarāpekṣābuddhernityatvātparamāṇudvutvasya
sarvadotpattiprasaṅgaḥ, iṣṭatvādeva|
īśvarāpekṣābuddheranityatvācca| na caivamīśvare
[.a]nityajñānāpattiḥ iṣṭatvāt| na caivamīśvare
nityajñānābhyupagamavirodhaḥ
nityajñānasyānabhyupagamāt| na

caivaṃvidhāpekṣābuddhau pramāṇābhāvaḥ,
etatparamāṇvekatvaviṣayaitaddvitīyaparamāṇvekatvāviṣayatvarahitaitadekatvaviṣayaitatparamāṇudvyaikatvavyatiriktaikatvaviṣayatvarahitakāryasākṣātkāraviṣayo
meyatvāt, ghaṭavadityanumānāt| na ca
śarīrādikāraṇābhāvātparameśvare
kāryajñānānutpattiḥ, śarīrādeḥ abhyupagamāt|
tathā ca---

bhagavantamumākāntaṃ candracūḍaṃ trilocanam|

śrutismṛtipurāṇāni vadanti parameśvaram||

na ca smṛtyādau [sṛṣṭyādau]
śarīrādyasambhavātparameśvarasya
smṛtyādi[sṛṣṭyādi]dvyaṇukaparimāṇājanakadvitvajanakāpekṣābuddhyasambhavaḥ|
etadbrahmāṇḍavartiśarīrāsambhavātsarvabrahmāṇḍapralayānantaraṃ
kā gatiriti cet---na, tadānīṃ
paramāṇudvitvotpattera[na]ṅgīkārātsṛṣṭyādidvyaṇukaparimāṇasya
pralayapūrvakālīnaparameśvarapekṣābuddhijanyadvitvajanyatvābhyupagamācca|
na ca pralayapūrvakālīnadvitvasya
yāvatsamanuvṛttyasambhava iti vācyam,
bādhakābābhāvāt| na cedaṃ
dvitvamāśutaravināśi [dvi]tvādanyadvitvavaditi
vācyam, pareṇāpi
paramāṇudvitvasyāpekṣābuddhijanyatvamicchatā
pralayapūrvakālīnasyāpi
yāvatsargamanuvṛttyaṅgīkārāt,
paramāṇudṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatvāt| pareṇa
ghaṭapaṭadvitvādīnāmapi
yāvadāśrayamanuvṛttyaṅgīkārātparamāṇudvitvasya
nityasarvaviṣayāpekṣābuddhijanyatve .api
paramāṇudvyavyatiriktaniṣṭhadvitvājanakadvyaṇukārambhakasaṃyogajanyatvasvīkārācca|
anyathā prāgapi tadudayāpatteḥ sāmagrīsattvāt|
na caivaṃ
satyanārambhakaparamāṇuniṣṭhatritvānutpattiprasaṅgaḥ,
iṣṭatvāt, anārambhakaparamāṇuniṣṭhadvitve
pramāṇābhāvāt| dvyaṇukaparimāṇādeḥ kāryasya
virahāt| [prapañcitaṃ] caitadasmābhiḥ
haraprasādanāmni kiraṇāvalīdarpaṇake|

astu vā paramāṇudvitve
paramāṇudvayatattadvyatiriktaniṣṭhatritvānyajanakaparamānudvayavyatiriktaniṣṭhaikatvānālambanaṃ
paramāṇudvayaikatvāla mutpādakam| na cātra
pramāṇābhāvaḥ|
etatparamāṇvekatvametatparamāṇvekaviṣayatvaviṣayaikadvitīyaparamāṇvekatvāviṣayatvarahitaitadekatvaviṣayaitatparamāṇudvayaikatvavyatiriktaikatvaviṣayatvarahito
.asmadādisākṣātkāravi prameyatvāt,
ghaṭavadityanumānāt| na cāsmadādīnāṃ
sargānyadvyaṇukaparimāṇajanakadvitvotpādakajñānāsambhavaḥ,
tadāsmadādiśarīravirahāditi vācyam|
asmadādīnāṃ
tadānīmetadbrahmāṇḍavartiśarīrasambhavāt| na
cāsmadādīnāṃ
paramāṇugataikatvasākṣātkārānupapattiḥ
yogajadharmabalādupapatteḥ|
tasmātsarvadvitvādīnāṃ
dvitvānyajanakakāraṇopapatterna tritvādibhirna
kāryatvāpattiriti\

nāpi dvitīyāyā[dvitīyaḥ] bādhāt,
dvitvatritvādibhedasya
pratyakṣasiddhatvātkāraṇa[a]bhedasyoktaprakāreṇāsiddhatvācca|
dvitvatritvādīnāṃ
dvitvaprāgabhāvādirupādikāraṇabhedasattvācceti|
na ca prathamamekatvasāmānyajñānaṃ
tadanantaramekatvaviśiṣṭānekatvajñānaṃ
tadanantaraṃ dvitvasāmānyajñānaṃ tadanantaraṃ
dvitvaguṇaviśiṣṭadravyajñānaṃ śuklo ghaṭa iti
jñānavaditi cet---na,
dvitvāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇotpannavyatiriktaikatvaviśiṣṭadravyajñānānāṃ
dharmitve siddhasādhanāt|
dvitvāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇotpannaikatvaviśiṣṭadravyajñānasya
dharmitve cāśrayāsiddheḥ| tathāvidhasya
bhavadbhiranabhyupagamāt|
abhyupagame vā dve dravye iti
jñānānutpattiprasaṅgāt|
ekatvaviśiṣṭadravyajñānena dvitvotpattikṣaṇa eva
bhavadabhimatāpekṣābuddhivināśāt
dvitvasāmānyajñānakṣaṇe
vāpekṣābuddhivināśena dvitvavināśāditi|
tasmādekatvaguṇaviśiṣṭadravyajñānavyatiriktāpekṣābuddhirniṣpramāṇikaivetyekatvaviśiṣṭadravyāhataṃ

[dravyajñānaṃ] savikalpakamevāpekṣābuddhiriti|

astu vā nirvikalpakamekatvasāmānyānekaikatvagocaraṃ jñānam apekṣābuddhiḥ|
tatra ca pramāṇam idam ekam idam ekatvaviśiṣṭadravyajñānam eva|
na tvanuvyavasāyaḥ|
dvitvotpattikṣaṇe tadutpādaikatvaviśiṣṭadravyajñānānutpādāddvitvotpattyanantarakṣaṇe 'anuvyavasāyotpāda eva, tadānīṃ
dvitvasāmānyajñānānutpādāt|
dvitvotpattyanantarakṣaṇe cānuvyavasāyotpāde
tadānīṃ dvitvasāmānyajñānānutpādāt
jñānayoryugapadutpattya[na]ṅgīkārāt|
tadanantarakṣaṇe ca dvitvasāmānyajñānotpāde
tadanantarabhāvidvitvaviśiṣṭadravyajñānānutpattiḥ,
dvitvakṣaṇotpannaikatvaviśiṣṭadravyajñānenānuvyavasāyakṣaṇe
.apekṣābuddhervināśāt| apekṣābuddhivināśena ca
dvitvasāmānyajñānakṣaṇe dvitvavināśāt|

na ca dvitvotpattikṣaṇe
ekatvaviśiṣṭadravyajñānotpattau tena
dvitvasāmānyajñānakṣaṇe apekṣābuddhivināśāt|
apekṣābuddhivināśena ca dvitvaguṇajñānakṣaṇe
dvitvavināśāt dravyajñānānutpattirbhavatāmapi
samānaiveti śaṅkā|
iyamasmābhirdvitvasāmānyajñānānantarakṣaṇa eva
dvitvaviśiṣṭajñānasvīkārāt|
nirvikalpakatvasāmānyajñānasya
dvitvaguṇālambanatvena
viśeṣaṇajñānatvopapatteḥ|
tasmādekatvasāmānyasahitānekaikatvagocaraṃ
nirvikalpakam,
tasmādanirdvitvasāmānyajñānānantaramevāpekṣābuddhirna
punaranekatvasāmānyaviśiṣṭānaikaikatvajñānam,
tatra pramāṇābhāvāt|
dvitvādeścaitanyasāmānyānekaikatvagocaranirvikalpakādevopapatteriti|

na cānekaikatvagocaraṃ nirvikalpakaṃ (na)
taddvitvajanakaṃ nirvikalpakatvāt,
sampratipannavaditi vācyam|
anekaikatvānālambanatvena sopādhitvena
pūrvopapāditanyāyena tasya dvitvajanakatvācca| tena
dvitvasāmānyajñānānantaraṃ
dvitvasāmānyaviśiṣṭaguṇajñānamapi pratyuktam,
pramāṇābhāvāt| na ca dve dravye iti jñānaṃ
viśeṣaṇajñānajanyaṃ viśiṣṭajñānatvāt
tri[tva]jñānavaditi vācyam|
nirvikalpakasāmānyajñānau (?) siddhasādhanāt|
tasyāpi dvi[tva]guṇālambanatvāt|
jātivyaktyorekālambanatvaniyamasyobhayavādisiddhatvāt|
dvitvasāmānyajñānasya dvitvālambanatvāsiddhāvapi
tenārthāntarasya durvāratvāt|
dvitvasāmānyajñānasya dvitvālambanamātreṇa
caritārthasya sādhanasya
dvitvasāmānyaviśiṣṭajñāne .aparyavasānāt|

atha dve dravye ityatra
dvitvasāmānyadvitvaviśeṣaṇaṃ na ca
nirvikalpakadvitvasāmānyajñānasya
dvitvasāmānyasya viśiṣṭaṃ dvitvaṃ viṣaya iti
tadvyatiriktadvitvajñānasiddhiriti cet---na,
kāryasya dvitvasāmānyaviśiṣṭadvitvavatā
taddravyaviśeṣaṇaṃ kiṃ vā dvitvasāmānyaṃ
tadviśiṣṭatā, kiṃ vā
dvitvasāmānyaviśiṣṭatāviśiṣṭadvitvamiti|

nādyaḥ, nirvikalpakadvitvasāmānyajñānasyāpi
dvitvaviśiṣṭādhāradvitvaviṣayatvena
siddhasādhanatārthāntaratayostadavasthatvācca| na
dvitīyatṛtīyau
dvitvasāmānyadvitvasāmānyaviśiṣṭayordvitvaniṣṭhayordravyaviśeṣaṇatvāsambhavāt|

sambhave vānaṅgīkārāt| na caturthaḥ,
pūrvavadvikalpya taddoṣaireva nirākaraṇāt,
tasmādanupapannā prācīnācāryāṇāṃ
dik|

asmākaṃ
tu
prathamai[mame]katvasāmānyaikatvaguṇadravyagocaraṃ
nirvikalpakam|
tadanantaramekatvasāmānyaviśiṣṭānekaikatvaviśiṣṭadravyajñānamapekṣābuddhiḥ|
tadanantaramapekṣābuddhyanuvyavasāyādapekṣābuddhivināśāddvitvasāmānyadvayaguṇadravyagocaraṃ
nirvikalpakaṃ ca|
tadanantaramapekṣābuddhivināśāddvitvavināśadvitvasāmānyaviśiṣṭadvitvaguṇa
dvitvaguṇaviśiṣṭadravyagocare dve dravye iti
savikalpakaṃ ceti prakriyā|

yadvā
prathamamanekatvasāmānyānekaikatvadravyagocaraṃ
nirvikalpakamapekṣābuddhiḥ|
tadanantaramekatvasāmānyaviśiṣṭaikatvaguṇaikatvaguṇaviśiṣṭadravyaṃ
dvigocaraṃ savikalpakaṃ dvitvaṃ ca| tadanantaraṃ
savikalpakādapekṣābuddhivināśakadvitvasāmānyadvitvaguṇadravyagocaraṃ
nirvikalpakaṃ ca|
tadanantaramapekṣābuddhivināśāddvitvavināśadvitvasāmānyaviśiṣṭadvitvaguṇatvasya
dvitvaguṇaviśiṣṭadravyagocaraṃ dve dravye iti
savikalpakaṃ ceti prakriyā|
dvitvasāmānyādigocaranirvikalpakatve savikapakameva
pramāṇam| savikalpakamevānuvyavasāya iti|

nanu dve dravye iti jñānasya
dvitvasāmānyaviśiṣṭaṃ dvitvam|
[a]dvitvaviśiṣṭe ca dravye viṣaya ityanupapannam|
dve dravye iti jñānakṣaṇe
dvitvasyābhāvādvartamānaviṣayatvācca
pratyakṣasya| yadi ca viśiṣṭajñānaṃ
viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyatatsambandhaviṣayaṃ, tadā
surabhi candanamiti jñānaṃ na syāt cakṣuṣaḥ
saurabhyāgrāhakatvena ghrāṇasya
candanāgrāhakatvena surabhi candanamiti
jñānajanakatvānupapatteḥ| na ca
viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvo .api padārthātirikto
bhāvarūpaḥ sambhavati, anabhyupagamāt|

na cāyaṃ samavāya eva| tasyātīndriyatvena
cakṣurjanyasākṣātkāragocaratvānupapatteḥ| na
cāyaṃ bhāva eva
pratoyogiviśeṣatastannirūpaṇāt| yuktam,
ayameko dvāvityanayoraviśeṣaprasaṅgāt|
viśeṣaṇātyantābhāvavadvyāvṛttiviśeṣye
viśiṣṭapratyayaviṣaya ityudayanādayaḥ|
tadapi na yuktam, vikalpānupapatteḥ|
viśeṣaṇātyantābhāva
viśeṣaṇātyantābhāvādhikaraṇaikatvavyaktyanyonyābhāvaḥ,
kiṃ vā
viśeṣaṇātyantābhāvādhikaraṇasarvavyaktipratiyogikaḥ
eko .anyonyābhāvaḥ, kiṃ vā
viśeṣaṇātyantābhāvādhikaraṇasarvavyaktipratiyogikāḥ
sarve anyonyābhāvāḥ kiṃ [vā]
viśeṣaṇātyantābhāvatyantābhāvaḥ, kiṃ [vā]
viśeṣaṇātyantābhāvādhikaraṇatvātyantābhāvaḥ,
kiṃ [vā] viśeṣyātyantābhāvaḥ|

nādyaḥ, ayamayaṃ ca na rūpam, etau
dvāvityaviśeṣaprasaṅgāt,
rūpasyāpyetaddvitvātyantābhāvādhikaraṇatvāt|

na dvitīyaḥ,
anekapratiyogikaikānyonyābhāvānupapatteḥ,
vakṣyamāṇadoṣācca|

na tṛtīyaḥ,
etaddvitvātyantābhāvādhikaraṇasarvavyaktyapratītau
tadabhāvānāṃ pratyetumaśakyatvāt|
etaddvitvātyantābhāvādhikaraṇātīndriyavyaktyanyonyābhāvānāṃ
sākṣātkāragocaratvānupapatteśca|
anantānyonyābhāvapratibhāsācca|
apratiyogijñānasambhavācca| taddhi na
tāvaddvitvasāmānyajñānakṣaṇe jāyate| nāpi
dvitvaguṇajñānakṣaṇe, yugapajjñānānupapatteḥ|
nāpi dvitīyotpattikṣaṇe| asmanmate
savikalpāpekṣābuddhipakṣe tadanuvyavasāyena
nirvikalpakāpekṣābuddhipakṣe
caikatvaviśiṣṭadravyajñānena
yugapadutpattiprasaṅgāt| udayanādimate
ca sāmānyajñānakāle vinaṣṭasya
pratiyogijñānasya
taduttarakālabhāvidvitvaviśiṣṭadravyajñānajanakatvānupapatteḥ|
nāpi dvitvaguṇajñānottarakṣaṇe
dvitvaviśiṣṭajñānena yadutpatti[?]prasaṅgāt|
yugapadutpannatvena
kāraṇabhāvābhāvaviśeṣayorālambanayoraviśeṣāt|
tasmāddvitvasyāpekṣābuddhivināśaprasaṅgācca|

nāpi caturthaḥ,
viśeṣaṇātyantābhāvātyantābhāvasya
viśeṣaṇasvarūpānatireke dve dravye iti jñānasya
dvitvagocaratvaprasaṅgāt| atireke ca
pūrvavatpratiyogijñānāsambhavena
tadgrahaṇānupapatteḥ| tata eva dvāviti
jñānotpattau dvitvāsiddhiprasaṅgāt|
dvitvasāmānyaviśiṣṭataddvitvaguṇajñānāddvitvasiddhiriti
cet---na, tasya prāgeva nirastatvāt|
dvitvanirvikalpakāddvitvasiddhiriti cet---na,
dvitvasavikalpakābhāve dvitvanirvikalpakāsiddheḥ
tasya savikalpakonneyatvāt|

dvitvāsiddhau tadatyantābhāvāderapyasiddheśca
nāpi pañcamaḥ|

tata eva nāpi ṣaṣṭhaḥ| tata eva etau dvāvetau
pṛthagetau saṃyuktāvetau vibhaktāviti
pratyayānāmaviśeṣaprasaṅgācca|
viśeṣyātyantābhāvāviśeṣyayorālambanayoraviśeṣāt|

tasmāddvitvasyapekṣābuddhivināśyatve dve dravye
iti pratyayānupapattiviṣayatvāditi| tadayuktam| dve
dravye iti pratyayasya
dvitvaviśiṣṭadravyādiviṣayatvāddve dravye iti
pratyayakāle .api naṣṭadvitvakṣaṇasya viṣaya iti
cet---na, bādhakābhāvāt| idaṃ dvitvaṃ
naitatsākṣātkāraviṣayam,
etatkālīnarahitatvādbhaviṣyadghaṭavaditi cet---na,
tattatsākṣātkāraviṣayatve dvitvasyaivāsiddheḥ|
āśrayāsiddhatvāt| etatsākṣātkāraviṣayatve ca
tadabhāvasādhanasya ca bādhitatvāt| kiṃ
caitatsākṣātkāraviṣayatvasya
kvacidaprasiddhāvaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt|
prasiddhau vānenaiva nyāyenāsya
dvitvasyaitatsākṣātkāragocaratvāsiddherbādhāt|
anyaddravyametatsākṣātkāragocaro dravyatvāt,
bhāvighaṭavadityāderdurvāratvādasiddhatvācca|

asmanmate
dvitvasāmānyadvitvaguṇagocaranirvikalpakena
dvitvaguṇaviśiṣṭadravyagocarasavikalpakakṣaṇe
apekṣābuddhivināśāttadanantarakṣaṇe
cāpekṣābuddhivināśena dvitvavināśācceti dve
dravye iti jñānaṃ
bādhakābhāvātkevalaviśeṣyālambanaṃ
viśiṣṭajñānatvātsurabhi candanamiti jñānavaditi
cet---na, anumānajñānādau vyabhicārāt|
tasyāpi kevalaviśeṣyālambanatvapakṣe
sādhyāsiddhiprasaṅgāt| pratyakṣatvena
hetuviśeṣaṇe
dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatvāt| surabhi
candanamityasyāpi
saurabhyaviśiṣṭadravyagrāhakatvāt|
saurabhyaviśiṣṭadravyasya ca ghrāṇenaivopagamāt|
ghrāṇarasanayorapi dravyagrāhakatvāt|
anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ
gandhaviśiṣṭadravyajñānasya
ghrāṇajanyatvāvagamāt|
rasaviśiṣṭadravyaj`nānasya ca
rasajanyatvāvagamāt| anyathā cakṣuḥsparśanayorapi
dravyāgrāhakatvaprasaṅgāt
ghrāṇajanyasaurabhyajñānasahakṛtacakṣuḥsparśanābhyāṃ
vā saurabhyaviśiṣṭadravyagrahaṇāt|

na caivaṃ gandhasyāpi cakṣuḥsparśanagrāhyatvāpattiḥ,
iṣṭatvāt| na caivaṃ ghrāṇavikalānāmapi
saurabhyaviśiṣṭadravyāvagamaprasaṅgaḥ,
ghrāṇajanyasaurabhyāvagamasyāpi sahakāritvābhyupagamāt| evaṃ
rasanajanyarasajñānasahakṛtābhyāṃ cakṣuḥsparśanābhyāṃ
rasaviśiṣṭadravyāvagamāt| dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatvāt|
surabhi candanamiti jñānasyānumānatvāt| idaṃ
candanametatsaurabhyavadetattvāt, na yadevaṃ na tadevaṃ, yathā
ghaṭa iti kevalavyatirekiprayuktena vā
prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvametatsaurabhyaghrāṇasiddhatvāt|

na caitatsaurabhyasya prasiddhāvapi
tadvattvasyāprasiddheraprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā, tasya
pakṣadharmāvaseyasya vyāpakatvānaṅgīkārāt| idaṃ saurabhyaṃ
kvacidāśritaṃ guṇatvāt, rūpavadityādi taddvitvasāpi
prasiddhatvāt| viśiṣṭatā kānubhavasiddhā
ṣaṭpadārthavyatirekiṇyā bhāvaviśeṣā eva prameyatvādivat|
dvitvamekatvāntarasahitaṃ tritvajanakam| tritvamekatvāntarasahitaṃ
catuṣṭayajanakaṃ ceti| dvitve catuṣkajanake ityādikaṃ na
syāditi cet---sādhakapramāṇābhāvādityavehi|

dvitvādikamanekasaṃkhyājanakaṃ saṃkhyātvādekatvavaditi
cet---na, ekatvāntarānanusandhānadaśāyāṃ tritvādikamanutpādya
naṣṭeṣu dvitvādiṣu vyabhicārāt|
āśrayavināśavinaṣṭadvitvādiṣu vyabhicārācca| bhavanmate
parārdhasaṃkhyāyāṃ vyabhicārācca| teṣāmapi dharmitve
bādhācca| dvitvādikaṃ nānekasaṃkhyājanakamanekasaṃkhyātvāt,
āśrayavināśavinaṣṭadvitvādivaditi satpratipakṣatvācca|
tritvādikaṃ nānekasaṃkhyājanyaṃ saṃkhyātvāt,
ekatvavadityanumānācca|

kiṃ cānekasaṃkhyākāraṇatvaṃ
kimanekasaṃkhyāsamavāyikāraṇatvam, anekasaṃkhyākāraṇatvaṃ
vā| nādyaḥ, ekatvena sopādhitvāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
anekaguṇasvabhāvenaiva sopādhitvāt| tritvādīnāṃ
dvitvādijanyatve niyatahetutvaprasaṅgācca| ghaṭapaṭadvitve
ghaṭapaṭaikatvajñānamapekṣābuddhiḥ ghaṭapaṭadvitve ca
ghaṭapaṭastambhaikatvajñānamapekṣābuddhiḥ
ghaṭapaṭadvitvastambhaikatvajñānaṃ vā
ghaṭapaṭastambhakuḍyacatuṣkeṣvadhikatvāt|
ghaṭapaṭastambhakuḍyeṣvekatvajñānamapekṣābuddhiḥ
ghaṭapaṭastambhatritvakuḍyaikatvajñānaṃ

vā ghaṭapaṭadvitvastambhakuḍyadvitvajñānaṃ veti|

na ca niyatahetukatvaṃ tritvādau jātibhedasvīkārāt| anyathā
daśa pañca pañcadaśetyādigaṇitavyavahāravilopaprasaṅgāt|
yatra
prācīnaikatvacakṣurādisannikarṣavirahādaśeṣaikatvagocaramekaṃ
jñānaṃ na jāyate, jāyate ca śatamityabādhitaṃ jñānaṃ tatra

navatisaṃkhyāsahitaikatvāntarajñānamapekṣābuddhiḥ,
antyaika[tvānubhava]janitasaṃskārāntaraṃ sakalaviṣayaṃ
smaraṇaṃ vā prakārāntarāsambhavāt|
antyaikatvānubhavatadanantarotpannaprācīnaikatvasmaraṇābhyāṃ
śatasaṃkhyotpattiriti tu na yuktam, prācīnaikatvasmaraṇena
śatasaṃkhyotpattikṣaṇe .antyānubhavaikatvānubhavanāśāt|
antyaikatvānubhavanāśena ca
śatasaṃkhyātvasāmānyajñānakṣaṇe
śatasaṃkhyānāśāttadanantarakṣaṇe śatamiti
jñānānutpattiprasaṅgāt|
prācīnaikatvābhāvajanitasaṃskārāntyaikatvasannikṛṣṭendriyābhyāmaśeṣaikatvagocaraṃ
grahaṇasmaraṇātmakaṃ jñānaṃ śatasaṃkhyotpādakaṃ jāyata iti
cet---na, grahaṇatvapramitau grahaṇatvenaiva
smṛtitvābhāvānumānāt| smṛtitvapramitau ca smṛtitvenaiva
grahaṇatvābhāvānumānāditi|

antyaikatvānubhavānantaramaśeṣaikatvagocaramanuvyavasāyātmakaṃ
sākṣātkārimānasajñānamutpannaṃ
śatasaṃkhyotpādakamityātreyācāryāḥ|
tanna, tādṛśajñāne pramāṇābhāvāt|
śatasaṃkhyāyāścoktaprakāreṇābhyupapatteḥ|
manaso bahirasvātantryācca|

śatasaṃkhyājñānaṃ ca ghaṭādau cākṣuṣameva|
na cāśeṣaśatasaṃkhyāśrayatvaṃ sannikarṣābhāvātkathaṃ cakṣuṣā śatasaṃkhyājñānaṃ janya[te] iti vācyam|
ekāśrayasannikarṣeṇāpi jātivattata eva grahaṇopapatteḥ|
śatamityanubhavasyā[na]pahnavatvāt|
evaṃ parārdhādisaṃkhyāsvapīti|

nanu pṛthaktvamanekeṣāṃ dharma iti na yuktam|
pṛthaktve pramāṇābhāvāt| pṛthagiti pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇamiti cet---na,
tasyānyonyābhāvālambanatvāt| pṛthaktvameva vinirūpyam|
ayamasmātpṛthagityeva, avadhau pañcamīprayogāt|
anyonyābhāvastu nāvadhinirūpyaḥ|
ayamayaṃ netyatrāpi pañcamīprasaṅgāditi cet---na, ayamasmādanyaḥ, ayamasmādbhinna ityādāvanyonyābhāvasyāpi avadhinirūpyatvāt|
anyabhinnādiśabdā api pṛthaktvavācina eveti cet---na,
pṛthaktvābhāve .api rūpādraso bhinnaḥ, anya ityādiprayogāt|
[prasādau] rasādau pṛthaktvāropādbhinādipadaprayoga iti
cet---na, bādhakābhāvāt| pṛthaktvaṃ na tu guṇavarti guṇatvāt,
rūpavaditi bādhakamiti cet---na, pṛthaktvasya guṇatvāsiddheḥ|
anyādiśabdānāmanyonyābhāvavyāpakatve ayamasmādanya ityādi
pañcamīprayogo na syāt, tasyāvadhinirūpyatvābhāvāditi
cet---na, tasyāpyavadhinirūpyatvāt| tarhi nāyamayamityatrāpi
pañcamīprasaṅga iti cet---na, atra sato
.apyavadhitvasyāvivakṣitatvāt| avadhitvavivakṣāyāṃ
prañcamīprasaṅga iti cet---na, ayamayaṃ na
ityetāvataivānyonyābhāvapratītyupapattau
niṣprayojanāvadhitvavivakṣāsambhavāt| avadhitvavivakṣāyāṃ
pṛthagādipadaprayoge .api pañcamī na syāditi cet---na, sattvaṃ
(?) ghaṭaḥ
pṛthagityādiprayogadarśanātpṛthaktvasyāpyavadhinirūpyatvābhāvācca|

ghaṭādīnāmavadhinirūpyatve pramāṇābhāvātpaṭādghaṭaḥ
pṛthagiti pañcamyevāvadhitvapramāṇamiti cet---na, tasyāḥ
pṛthaganyabhinnādyupapadatvalābhādanyonyābhāvapratiyoginyudayasambhavāt|
na caivaṃ ghaṭaḥ paṭo netyatrāpi pañcamīprasaṅgo
.anyonyābhāvapratiyogitvasambhave
.api pṛthaganyādyupapadābhāvāt nañaśca
pāṇiniśā[kaṭā]yanādibhirupapāditatvānabhidhānādanyatarāditaratallakṣaṇakatvādanyapadaparyāyopalakṣaṇatvānmayyapīdamupalakṣaṇamastīti
cet---ghaṭaḥ paṭo netyatrāpi pañcamīprasaṅgāt|
itaravadanārambhaprasaṅgācca| tasyāpyanyapadaparyāyatvāt|
tatsadṛśavācakamitarapadaṃ na tvanyapadaparyāya iti cet---na,
pramāṇābhāvāt| itarapadānārambha eva tatra pramāṇamiti
cet---na, tasyetarapadāyoge pañcamīprāptārthatvāt|
upapadavibhaktitve pañcamī nirarthikā syāditi cet---na,
anyonyābhāvapratiyogitvenārthavattvāt|
pṛthaganyabhinnādipadānāṃ pañcamīprayojakatve
gauravaprasaṅgādavadhitvaṃ kalpyata iti cet---na,
kḷptopapadamahimnaiva pañcamyupapattāvavadhitvakalpanasyaiva
garīyastvāt| anyathā sakalopapadavibhaktivilayaprasaṅgāt| na
tatrāpyavadhitvavadarthāntarakalpanopapatteravaśyaṃ vāsyā
upapadavibhaktitvamaṅgīkartavyam| anyathā anyārāditararta iti
sūtrānārambhaprasaṅgāt
avadhau pañcamīvidhānādeva ghaṭādanyaḥ paṭa ityādāvapi
pañcamīprāpteravadhitvasya nirvaktumaśakyatvācca|
nirūpakatvamevāvadhitvamiti cet---na, ghaṭa[ḥ] paṭo netyatrāpi
pañcamīprasaṅgāt| paṭāderapi
ghaṭaniṣṭhānyonyābhāvanirūpakatvānyonyābhāvapratiyogitvarahitatve
sati nirūpakatvamevāvadhitvamiti cet---na, evaṃvidhasyāsambhavāt|
yasya kasyāpi kutaḥ| kutaścidanyatvenānyonyābhāvapratiyogitvena
tadrahitatvānupapatteḥ| ghaṭātpaṭaḥ pṛthagityādau
pañcamyanudayaprasaṅgācca
ghaṭādīnāmanyonyābhāvapratiyogitvena
tadrahitābhyāmasambhavāt| pṛthaktvaṃ nirūpakatvamavadhitvamiti
cet---na, tasyāpyanirūpaṇāt| kiṃ ca paṭādyatrāpi (?)
pañcamīprasaṅgāt, paṭāderapṛthaktvanirūpakatvāt| anyathā
paṭādghaṭaḥ pṛthagityatrāpi pañcamyanudayaprasaṅgāt|
ayamasmātpara ityādau pañcamyanudayaprasaṅgācca|
pṛthaktvanirūpakasyāpratipādanātpṛthaktvenāvadhinirūpaṇam,
avadhinā ca pṛthaktvanirūpaṇamiti parasparāśrayaprasaṅgācca|
guṇanirūpakatvamavadhitvamiti cet---na, ghaṭaḥ paṭo netyatrāpi
pañcamīprasaṅgāt| ghaṭāderapi
pṛthaktvaparatvādiguṇanirūpakatvāt, ghaṭatvātparā sattetyādau
pañcamyanudayaprasaṅgācca| apādānatvamavadhitvamiti cet---na,
tasyāpyanirūpaṇāt|

kiṃ ca paṭādiniṣṭhāvadhitvāṅgīkāre .anyonyābhāvasyāpi
avadhinirūpyatvātpaṭādinā avadhinā
anyonyābhāvanirūpaṇātpaṭādiniṣṭhāvadhitvāṅgīkāre ca
pṛthaktvasyāpi
avadhinirūpyatvāsambhavātpaṭādīnāmavadhitvābhāvāt|
paṭādiniṣṭhenāvadhinā pṛthaktvaṃ nirūpyatve na
tvanyonyābhāva iti cet---na,
paṭādiniṣṭhatvāvadhitvanirūpyatvayorapi niṣpramāṇakatvāt|
etena pṛthaktvamanyonyābhāvādbhidyate| bhāvo vā guṇo
vāvadhinirūpyatvātparatvavadityanumānamapi nirastam|
pṛthaktvamanyonyābhāvaḥ pṛthaktvāt, na yadevaṃ na tadevam,
yathā ghaṭaḥ| ghaṭapṛthaktvaghaṭānyonyābhāvaḥ
anyonyābhāvatvāt| na yadevaṃ na tadevam, yathā ghaṭaḥ|
ghaṭānyonyābhāvaḥ paṭapṛthaktvaṃ ghaṭānyonyābhāvatvāt, na
yadevaṃ na tadevam, yathā ghaṭaḥ ityādi vyatireki
anyonyābhāvapṛthaktvādīnāṃ suprasiddhatvāt|
tadbhinnābhāvatvasyāpi suprasiddhatvācca|

tasmānnāsti pṛthaktvaṃ pramāṇābhāvāditi| tadayuktam,
vikalpāsahatvāt| tathā hi kiṃ pṛthaktve pramāṇābhā
anyonyābhāvavyatirekatve vā| nādyaḥ, paṭādghaṭaḥ
pṛthagityādipratyakṣasyaiva sattvāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
paṭapṛthaktvaṃ na paṭānyonyābhāvaḥ pṛthaktvāt,
ghaṭapṛthaktvādau sattvāt| na ca paṭapṛthaktvaṃ
paṭānyonyābhāvaḥ paṭapṛthaktvāt| na yadevaṃ na tadevam,
yathā ghaṭa iti vyatirekiṇā satpratipakṣatvam, aguṇavattvena
sopādhitvāt| na cāsya sādhanavyāpakatvam,
ghaṭapṛthaktvasyāsādhāraṇatvena guṇābhāvasādhakatvāt|
rūpāderguṇādivatkarmāderaguṇādapi vyāvṛttatvāt| na cāsya ca
pakṣetaratvam, paṭānyonyābhāvarahitādrūpādervipakṣādapi
vyāvṛttatvāt| na cāsya sādhyavyāpakatvam,
paṭānyonyābhāvatvasya guṇatvābhāvavyāpyatvaniścayāt|
guṇatvābhāvasya paṭānyonyābhāvatvavyāpakatvaniścayātpaṭaḥ
paṭapṛthaktvavyatiriktānyonyābhāvapratiyogī meyatvāt,
paṭavaditi siddhatvāt| paṭānyonyābhāvātsapakṣādapi
vyāvṛttatvena paṭapṛthaktvasyāsādhāraṇatvācca| kiṃ
paṭapṛthaktvaśabdena paṭānyonyābhāvaviṣayāṇāṃ
siddhasādhanāt| guṇaviśeṣavivakṣāyāṃ ca tavāśrayāsiddheḥ|
siddhau vā
dharmigrāhakapramāṇā(?)bādhātpṛthakpratītisuddhasya
dharmitvamiti cet---na,
ghaṭaniṣṭharūpādīnāmapīndriyasannikṛṣṭānāṃ
pṛthakpratītiviṣayāṇāṃ dharmitve bādhāt|
ghaṭatanniṣṭharūpādivyatiriktasya ghaṭātpaṭaḥ pṛthafiti
pratītiviṣayasya dharmitvamiti cet---na, bhavanmate
paṭānyonyābhāvasyaiva ghaṭatanniṣṭharūpādivyatiriktasya
paṭādghaṭaḥ pṛthagiti pratītiviṣayatvādasmanmate
.api paṭānyonyābhāvasyendriyasannikṛṣṭasya paṭādghaṭaḥ
pṛthagiti pratītiviṣayatvāt| tasya ca dharmitve siddhasādhanāt|
ghaṭatanniṣṭharūpādi paṭānyonyābhāvavyatiriktasya
paṭādghaṭaḥ pṛthagiti pratītiviṣayasya ca dharmitve bādhāt,
tavāśrayāsiddheśca|
pṛthakpadavācyasya dharmitvamiti cet---na, pṛthakpadena
pṛthaktvaviśiṣṭadravyavācinā
pṛthaktvavaiśiṣṭyadravyayorapyabhidhānāt| tayośca
dharmitvabādhātpṛthaktvaśabdavācyasya dharmitvamiti cet---na,
bhavanmate
anyonyābhāvasyaiva pṛthaktvaśabdavācyatvāt| asmanmate
ca guṇaviśeṣavadanyonyābhāvasyāpi pṛthaktvapadavācyatvāt|
pṛthaktvapadasyānekārthatvasvīkārāt| tasya ca dharmitve
siddhasādhanāt| anyonyābhāvavyatiriktasya pṛthakpadavācyasya
dharmitvamiti cet---na, tasyānyonyābhāvatvasādhane bādhāditi
paṭānyonyābhāve na paṭapṛthaktvamabhāvatvāt,
sampratipannavaditi pratiṣedhācca|

paṭānyonyābhāvaḥ paṭapṛthaktvam, paṭānyonyābhāvatvāt, na
yadevaṃ na tadevam, yathā ghaṭa ityādinā vyatirekiṇā
satpratipakṣatvamiti cet---na, asya viśeṣaviruddhatvāt|
hetuviśeṣasyābhāvatvasya sādhyābhāvavyāptatvena
sādhyaviruddhatvāt| viśeṣyāsiddhavat
viruddhasyāpi dūṣaṇatvāt|
viśeṣyaviruddhasyādūṣaṇatve ca viruddhāntarasyāpyadūṣaṇatvaprasaṅgo nyāyasya tulyatvāt|
ghaṭaḥ paṭapṛthaktvātiriktānyonyābhāvapratiyogi meyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi siddhe paṭapṛthaktvabhinne paṭānyonyābhāve paṭānyonyābhāvasya gatatvena viruddhatvācca|
paṭapṛthaktvaśabdena paṭānyonyābhāvavivakṣāyāṃ siddhasādhanāt|
guṇaviśeṣavivakṣāyāṃ ca bādhātpṛthakpratītisiddhapṛthaktvaśabdavācyavivakṣāyāṃ ca pūrvoktadoṣāt|
etena pṛthaktvamanyonyābhāvaḥ pṛthaktvāt, anyonyābhāvaḥ pṛthaktvamanyonyābhāvatvāditi vyatirekiṇau nirastāviti|

paṭapṛthaktvaṃ na paṭānyonyābhāva ityādi bhavadīyānumāne
.api pṛthaktvaśabdenānyonyābhāvavivakṣāyāṃ guṇaviśeṣavivakṣāyāṃ cāśrayāsiddhyādiriti cet---na, asmābhirguṇaviśeṣasya
vakṣyamāṇanyāyena sādhanādiḥ paṭaḥ paṭapṛthaktvavyatiriktapaṭaniṣṭhatvarahitabhāvāśrayaḥ, dravyatvāt, ghaṭavadityādinā paṭapṛthaktvasya bhāvatvasiddheśca|

na ca tṛtīyaḥ| ayaṃ ghaṭaḥ
pṛthaktvātiriktaghaṭaniṣṭhatvarahitaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ vā
pṛthaktvātirikta etanniṣṭhatvarahitaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ vā
paṭapṛthaktvātiriktaitanniṣṭhatvarahitaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ vā
dravyatvāt guṇatvāt samavāyikāraṇatvāt ca paṭavadityādinā
paṭapṛthaktvādeḥ guṇatvasiddheranumānācca
pṛthaktvaguṇasiddheḥ| tathā ca pṛthivī
rūparasagandhasparśasaṃkhyāparimāṇasaṃyogavibhāgaparatvāparatvagurutvadravatvasaṃskāravyatiriktaguṇādhikaraṇam,
dravyatvāt, ātmavat| dravyatvarahitapṛthivīpakṣīkaraṇe ca
dravyatvātiriktaviśeṣaṇaṃ nopādeyameva dravyatvena
siddhasādhanārthāntarayorasambhavāt| na cātrātmatvamupādhiḥ,
ākāśe sādhyavyāpakatvabhaṅgaprasaṅgāt| tatra
rūpādivyatiriktasya snehasya sattvāt| nāpyapṛthivītvamupādhiḥ,
pakṣetaratvāt| na cāsya kālādau vyabhicāraḥ, teṣāṃ
caivāsiddhapṛthaktvasambhavena pakṣatulyatvāt| na cātra
pratipakṣasambhavaḥ dravyatvādinā sopādhitvāt| udakaṃ
rūparasagandhasparśasaṃkhyāparimāṇasaṃyogavibhāgaparatvāparatvagurutvadravatvasnehasaṃskārātiriktaguṇādhikaraṇamātmavat|
tejo
rūpasparśasaṃkhyāparimāṇasaṃyogavibhāgaparatvāparatvagurutvadravatvasaṃskārātiriktaguṇādhikaraṇam,
guṇatvāt, ātmavat| rasarahitatejaḥpakṣīkaraṇe tu
dravatvātiriktaviśeṣaṇaṃ nopādeyameva| vāyuḥ
sparśasaṃkhyāparimāṇasaṃyogavibhāgaparatvāparatvasaṃskārātiriktaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ
guṇatvāt, ātmavat| na cātra gandhenārthāntaratā, vāyo
rūpatvādinā (?) gandhābhāvasiddheḥ, tannirāsārthaṃ
gandhavyatiriktatvasya viśeṣaṇasya copādānāt| ākāśaṃ
śabdasaṃkhyāparimāṇasaṃyogavibhāgātiriktaguṇādhikaraṇam,
drava[vya]tvāt, ātmavat| dikkālau
saṃkhyāparimāṇasaṃyogavibhāgātiriktaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ
dravyatvāt, ātmavat| ātmā
buddhisukhaduḥkhe[cchā]dveṣaprayatnadharmādharmabhāvanāsaṃkhyāparimāṇasaṃyogavibhāgātiriktaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ
dravyatvāt, udakavat| na cātra sparśavattvamupādhiḥ, gagane
sādhyavyāpakatvabhaṅgāt| adravyānāṃ
guṇināmapi
mūrtatvaṃ tata eva nāpi kāryatvam| tata eva paramāṇūṣu
sādhyavyāpakatvabhaṅgācca| manaḥ
saṃkhyāparimāṇasaṃyogavibhāgaparatvāparatvasaṃskāra
dhikaraṇam, dravyatvāt, vat| na cātra
viśeṣaṇaguṇavattvamupādhiḥ, pakṣetaratvāt| viśeṣapadasya
vyāvartyaviśeṣābhāvāt| adravyāṇāṃ guṇatvenaiva
vyāvṛttatvāt, manasaśca pakṣatulyatvāt| dikkālayośca
pakṣatulya[tvāt ]sādhakānumāne
dviṣṭhatvaviśeṣaṇopādānena tebhya eva tatsiddheḥ| ākāśaṃ
śabdasaṃkhyātiriktādviṣṭhaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ dravyatvāt,
ghaṭavat, dikkālasaṃkhyātiriktādviṣṭhaguṇādhikaraṇatvādeḥ
sattvāt|

nanvastu pṛthaktvam, dvipṛthaktvaṃ tu na sidhyati,
pramāṇābhāvāt| dvau pṛthagiti pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇamiti
cet---na, tasya dvitvasahitaikatvapṛthkatvābhyāmevopapatteḥ|
anyathā dviparatvadviśuklatvadvimadhutatvāderapyāpatteḥ| dvau
parau dvau śuklau dvau madhurāvityādeḥ pratyakṣasya
durvāratvāt| na ca dviparatvāṅgīkāraḥ tatraivaiṣa
paratvāparatvādijātisamāveśāpatteḥ ekasyeṣatparatvāditarasya
parataraparatvāditi vācyam|
īśatparatvaparataratvayorekaikatanniṣṭhaparatvāśritayordviparatvaniṣṭhatvāṅgīkārāt|
tatraiveṣatparatvāparatvādijātisamāveśāpatteḥ|
ekasyeṣatparatvāditarasya parataraparatvāditi vācyam,
īṣatparatvaparataratvayorekaikatanniṣṭhaparatvāśritayoḥ
dviparatvayordviparatvaniṣṭhatvāṅgīkārāt, jātisaṃkarasya
cādūṣaṇatvāt|

na ca mā bhūddvipṛthaktvādikamiti vācyam|
ekapṛthaktvasyānekadravyasamānadharmatvānupapatteḥ|
sūtrakāreṇa
ca pṛthaktvasyānekadravyānekadravyadharmatvapratipādananāditi|
tadayuktam, anumānādanekapṛthaktvādisiddheḥ| tathā cāyaṃ
ghaṭaḥ
saṃyogatvavibhāgatvasaṃkhyātvarahitānetanniṣṭhaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ
dravyatvāt, paṭavat|
ayaṃ ghaṭo 'anetanniṣṭhapṛthaktvādhikaraṇaṃ dravyatvāt, kālavaditi|
na cātra kālatvopādhiḥ|
kālaḥ kālaniṣṭho dravyatvāt, paṭavaditi siddhaḥ|
kālapṛthaktvavatyakāle sādhyavyāpakatvabhaṅgāt|
na cātra kālatvaupādhiḥ, pakṣetaratvāditi|
saṃkhyātvaṃ kālamātraniṣṭhakālākālaniṣṭhaguṇatvāvāntarajātyanyat,
meyatvāt, paṭavadityekānekapṛthaktvasiddheśca| na caivaṃ
dvipṛthaktvādyasiddhiḥ| uktasādhyeṣveva
bahuvṛttitvarahitaviśeṣaṇopādānena tatsiddheḥ| ayaṃ ghaṭa
etanniṣṭhasaṃyogatvavibhāgatvasaṃkhyātvarahitadvimātraniṣṭhaguṇādhikaraṇam,
dravyatvāt, ghaṭavadityādinā
tripṛthaktvādhikapṛthaktvasiddheḥ|

na caivaṃ pṛthaktvādyasiddhiḥ, uktasādhyeṣveva
bahuvṛttitvarahitaviśeṣaṇopādānena tatsiddheḥ| ayaṃ ghaṭa
etanniṣṭhasaṃkhyātvarahitatrimātravṛttiguṇādhikaraṇaṃ,
dravyatvāt, paṭavadityādinā pṛthaktvāsiddheḥ| na ca
dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatvam| etadghaṭaniṣṭhatvarahitasya
stambhakuḍyaparavṛttitvādeḥ paṭe .api sattvāt| na ca
dviparatvādi pratibandī, tatra mānābhāvāt| dvau parāviti
pratyakṣasya
dvitvasamānādhikaraṇyaikaparatvābhyāmanyathāsiddhetvāt| ayaṃ
ghaṭo .anetanniṣṭhaparatvādhikaraṇam, paratvādhikaraṇatvāt,

paṭavadityādeḥ
bhūṣaṇādīnāmaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvātsvarūpāsiddhatvācca|
paratvāsiddhāvanetanniṣṭhaparatvādhikaraṇatvāderasiddheḥ|
vaiśeṣikāṇāṃ
ca vyāpyatvāsiddhatvāt| paṭe
.anetanniṣṭhaparatvābhāvādīnāṃ
[paratvādhikaraṇatvasyānetanniṣṭhaparatvādhikaraṇena
vyāptyabhāvāt, bhāve vā paṭe .anetanniṣṭhaparatvasya
durvāratvāt| tatsādhane cāpasiddhāntāt, bādhācca|
vyāptyabhāvamabhyupagacchatā satsādhakapramāṇābhyupagamāt|
ghaṭe .anetanniṣṭhaparatvasādhane
.apyapasiddhāntabādhayostadavasthatvāt|
vṛttiparatvāvyāvṛttatvātsattāvat ityādeḥ
bhūṣaṇadīnāmāśrayāsiddhatvāt|
 vyāptyabhāvāt| anekavṛttiparatvābhāvavadasattve
paratvāvyāvṛttatvasyānetanniṣṭhavṛttitvena vyāpterabhāvāt|
bhāve vānekaparatvasya durvāratvāt| tatsādhane bā
ntācceti|

na ca pṛthaktve kāraṇānupapattirbādhikā
ghaṭādiniṣṭhaikapṛthaktve ghaṭādīnāṃ samavāyikāraṇatvāt|
ghaṭapaṭaikapṛthaktvagocarabuddheśca nimittakāraṇatvāt|
ghaṭapaṭastambhatripṛthaktvādiṣu ca ghaṭapaṭastambha
pṛthaktvagocarabuddhīnāṃ samavāyyasamavāyinimittakāraṇatvāt,
īśvarādṛṣṭādīnāṃ ca sarvatra nimittakāraṇatvāt| na cātra
pramāṇābhāvaḥ, saṃkhyāsamānanyāyasiddhatvāditi cet---na,
saṃyoge pramāṇābhāvāt| saṃyuktāviti pratyakṣa tvāt| na
cedamaviraladeśotpādanānyathāsiddham,
saṃyogātiriktasyāviraladeśotpādasya nirvaktumaśakyatvāt|

na ca vibhāgābhāva evaṃ saṃyuktapratyayaviṣaya iti yuktam|
ghaṭapaṭasaṃyogau ghaṭapaṭavibhāgābhāvaḥ saṃyogatvāt,
saṃyogavat| vibhāgābhāvo na saṃyogaḥ, abhāvatvāt,
ghaṭābhāvavadityādinā pratiṣedhāt| rūparasādau satyapi
vibhāgābhāve saṃyuktapratyayānudayācca| vibhāgadhvaṃsaḥ
saṃyuktapratyayālambanamiti cet---na, satyapi
prācīnavibhāgapradhvaṃsae .api bhāgāntaravati
saṃyuktapratyayānudayādyāvadupapannavibhāgadhvaṃsāḥ
saṃyuktapratyayālambanamiti cet---na,
yāvadutpannaghaṭapaṭavibhāgānubhavarahitānāmapi ghaṭapaṭādau
samyuktāviti pratyayodayāt| asatyapi vibhāgadhvaṃse
utpannamātrapaṭa ityādau saṃyogapratyayācca| tatredaṃ
hetvabhāvena saṃyogātpūrvaṃ vibhāgānupapatteḥ| anutpannasya
dhvaṃsāsambhavāt|
bheryākāśabherīdaṇḍasaṃyogayorasamavāyikāraṇanimittakāraṇayorasambhavādbherīśabdāderanudayaprasaṅgācceti|

nanu vibhāge kiṃ pramāṇam| vibhaktāviti pratyakṣamiti cet---na,
tasya saṃyogābhāvālambanatvāt| na caivaṃ tantupratītāvapi
vibhaktapratyayaprasaṅgaḥ, tatra saṃyogābhāvasyāpratīteḥ|
pratītau vā vibhaktapratyayasyāpyanaṅgīkārāt|
saṃyogadhvaṃsasya paṭavibhāgapratyayānālambanatvāt| na caivaṃ
ghaṭavināśāt ghaṭapaṭasaṃyogavināśe ghaṭapaṭau
vibhaktāviti pratyayaprasaṅgaḥ| tadālambanasya ghaṭasyābhāvāt|
anyathā ghaṭapaṭau dhvaṃsasaṃyogāviti pratyayaprasaṅgāt|
ghaṭapaṭapratītāvapi saṃyogadhvaṃsaḥ pratīyata iti
cet---tarhi vibhāge .api pratīyata eveti santoṣṭavyam,
vibhāgasyobhayaniṣṭhatvāt| ghaṭābhāvo .apratītiriti
cet---na, saṃyogadhvaṃsasyāpi ubhayaniṣṭhatvāt| dhvaṃsasya
pratiyogisamānadeśatvātpratiyoginaśca
saṃyogasyobhayaniṣṭhatvāditi| vibhaktāviti pratyayaścāsya
siddheśca vibha śabdaprayogāttatsiddheriti cet---na, tasya
saṃyogadhvaṃsapratīterevopapatteḥ| evaṃ sati
pṛthaktvapratītirapi vidhīyate, pṛthagiti
vyavahārasyānyonyābhāvapratīterevopapatteriti cet---tataḥ kiṃ
pṛthaktvavila numānāttatsiddheḥ| vibhāge .apyanumānaṃ
pramāṇamiti cet---na, tasyānirūpaṇāt|
vaṃśadalapāṭanānantaraṃ jāyamānaḥ śabdo
.asamavāyikāraṇajanyaḥ, bhāvakāryatvāt, kāryaguṇatvāt,
śabda vaṃśadalākāśasaṃyogenārthāntaragrastatvāt| ayaṃ
śabdo na saṃyogāsamavāyikāraṇakaḥ, saṃyogajanyatvāt,
śabdajaśabdavaditi cet---śrūyamāṇaśabdasya dharmitve
sādhanāt| tasya dharmitvamiti cet---na,
saṃśadalavibhāgābhidhāne asmānpratyāśrayā (?) na
bherīdaṇḍasaṃyogajādiśabdasyāpi dharmitve bādhāt|
śabdasaṃyogajanyaśabdasyāpi dharmitve āśrayāsiddheḥ|
śabdasaṃyogājanyaśabdasyāsmābhiḥ ṇāt|
vaṃśadalapāṭanajanyaśabdasya dharmitvamiti cet----na,
vaṃśadalapāṭanaśabdena vaṃśadalavibhāgābhidhāne
asmānpratyāśrayāsiddhatvāt|
vaṃśadalavibhāgaśabdānaṅgīkārāt| vaṃśadalakarmābhidhāne
cobhayairapyāśrayāsiddheḥ janyaśabdānaṅgīkārāt|
vaṃśadalasaṃyogadhvaṃsābhidhāne ca vāśrayāsiddheḥ|
vibhāgajaṃ śabdamicchatā tvayā
vaṃśadalasaṃyogadhvaṃsasya śabdahetutvānaṅgīkārāt|
vaṃśadalapāṭanānantaraṃ śrūyamāṇaśabdasantānādyaśabdasya
dharmitvamiti cet---na, saṃyogaśabdasantānāntyaśabdasyāpi
vaṃśadalapāṭanānantaraṃ śravaṇasambhavāt|
tatsantānādyaśabdasya ca dharmitve bādhāt| saṃyogājanyatvaṃ
vaṃśadalapāṭanānantaraṃ
śrūyamāṇaśabdasantānādyaśabdasya ca dharmitve
asmānpratyāśrayāsiddheḥ
saṃyogajanyatvasyāsmānpratyapakṣadharmatvācca| tasyāsmābhiḥ
vaṃśadalākāśasaṃyogajanyatvasvīkārāt|
nimittasaṃyogājanyatvaṃ heturiti cet---na, tasyāpyasiddheḥ|
prayatnavadātmavaṃśadalasaṃyogādṛṣṭavadātmavaṃśadalasaṃyogāderapi
śabdakāraṇatvasvīkārāt| mūrtamātraniṣṭhasaṃyogājanyatvaṃ
heturiti cet---tasyāpyasiddheḥ|
vaṃśadalaśabdasaṃjātanimittakāraṇapavanasaṃyogasyāpyādyasantānanimittapavanānabhyupagame
śabdasantānānuparamaprasaṅgāt| tadabhyupagame ca vegakṣayāt|
pavanaviratau śabdāntarānārambhe śabdasantānoparamasambhavāditi
śabdajanyaśabdatvābhāvena sopādhitvācca|

etenāyaṃ śabdo na vaṃśadalākāśasaṃyogajanyaḥ śabdatvāt,
anyaśabdavadityādayaḥ prayogā nirastāḥ|

vaṃśadalābhighātajanyavaṃśadalākāśasaṃyogajanyaśabdavyabhicārāccāyaṃ
vaṃśadalākāśasaṃyogo naitacchabdajanakaḥ saṃyogatvāt,
anyasaṃyogavaditi cet---na, etacchabdaśabdena
śrūyamāṇaśabdavivakṣāyā dhanāt|
śabdājanyaśabdavivakṣāyāmapi
siddhasādhanādbherīdaṇḍasaṃyogajādīnāmapyetadvaṃśadalākāśasaṃyogājanyatvāt|
śabdasaṃyogājanyaśabdavivakṣāyāṃ
cāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt| śabda janyaśabdāprasiddhau
tajjanakatvābhāvasyāprasiddheḥ vaṃśadalapāṭanānantaraṃ
śrūyamāṇaśabdasantānādyaśabdavivakṣāyāṃ ca
siddhasādhanāt| vaṃśadalapāṭanānantaraṃ
śrūyamāṇabherīdaṇḍasaṃyogaśabda śabda
vaṃśadalākāśasaṃyogasyākāraṇatvāt|
vaṃśadalapāṭanānantaraṃ

śrūyamāṇaśabdasantānādyaśabdasaṃyogājanyaśabdavivakṣāyāṃ
cāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt| evaṃvidhaśabdā
tvasyāpyasiddheḥ| tasmānnāsti vibhāga iti|

tadayuktam| vibhāgasādhakānumānasattvāt| tathā hi
dravya[va]tvarahitā pṛthivī,
rūparasagandhasparśasaṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvaparatvāparatvagurutvasaṃskārasaṃyogatvarahitaguṇādhikaraṇam,
pṛthivītvāt, dravyatvācca, dravyatvādhārapṛthivīvat| na cātra
snehaśabdabuddhyādibhirarthāntaratā pṛthivīniṣṭhatvenaivāsya
snehaśabdabuddhyādivyatirekasiddheḥ| na
cānekarūpatvādirahitaguṇasiddhāvapi vibhāgāsiddhiḥ|
rūpatvāditrayoviṃśatirahitaguṇatvasyaiva vibhāgalakṣaṇatvāt|
nāpyanena snehaśabdatvādirahitatvādisiddheḥ vibhāgāsiddhiḥ|
anena snehatvādirahitāsiddhāvapi yatra snehādirahitatvāttatra
siddheḥ tasyaiva vibhāgaśabdārthatvāt| snehatvādirahitasya ca
tallakṣaṇatvāt| tasya ca mānāntarādapi
siddhāviṣṭābhāvātsnehatvādibādhakarahitātprakṛtānumānādeva
rūpatvāditrayoviṃśatijātirahitaguṇāsiddheḥ| na cātra
dravyatvamupādhiḥ, ātmani sādhyāvyāpakatvāt|
nāpyātmatvamupādhiḥ, gagane sādhyāvyāpakatvāt| nāpi
sarvagatatvamupādhiḥ, udake sādhyāvyāpakatvāt|
nāpyapṛthivītvamupādhiḥ, dravyatvavatyāṃ pṛthivyāṃ
sādhyāvyāpakatvāt| nāpi
dravyatvapṛthivīvyatiriktatvamupādhiḥ, vyāvartyavivakṣābhāvena
pakṣetaratvāt|

na cāsya satpratipakṣatvam,
apekṣetaropādhivirahāṇāmupādhisādhyāvyāpakatvasthale
vyabhicārātpakṣetaropādhivirahasya cāsādhāraṇatvāt|
pratipakṣāntarāṇāṃ vā dravyatvena sopādhitvāditi
[sāṃ]siddhikadravatvarahitamudakaṃ
rūparasagandhasparśasaṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvaparatvāparatvagurutvasnehasaṃskārasaṃyogatvarahitaguṇādhikaraṇamudakatvāt,
sampratipannavat, dravyatvāt, ghaṭavat| na ca
sāṃsiddhikadravatvarahitodakasiddhikarāṇāṃ
pratyakṣasiddhatvāt| na

cātrānudakatvamupādhiḥ, sāṃsiddhikadravatvavati
sādhyāvyākatvāt| upādhyantarāṇāṃ ca pūrvoktanyāyena
nirākaraṇāt| tejo
rūpasparśasaṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvaparatvāparatvadravatvasaṃskārasaṃyogatvarahitaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ
dravatvāt, ātmavat| vāyuḥ
sparśasaṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvaparatvāparatvasaṃskārasaṃyogatvarahitaguṇādhikaraṇam,
dravyatvāt, ātmavat| ākāśaṃ
śabdasaṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogatvarahitaguṇādhikaraṇam,
dravyatvāt, ātmavat|

na caivamādīnāṃ dikkālayoḥ vyabhicāraḥ,
tayorapyudakarūpopapannavibhāgavattayā samūhyamānayoḥ
pakṣatulyatvāditi| dikāl[au]
saṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogatvarahitaguṇādhikaraṇam,
dravyatvāt, ātmavat| ātmā
buddhisukhaduḥkhecchā(dveṣa)prayatnadharmādharmabhāvanāsaṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogatvarahitaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ

dravyatvāt, ghaṭavat| manaḥ
paratvāparatvavegasaṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogatvarahitaguṇādhikaraṇam,
dravyatvāt, ghaṭavat| ayaṃ ghaṭa
etanniṣṭhasaṃyogatvarahitakarmajanyadviṣṭhaguṇādhikaraṇam,
dravyatvāt, ghaṭavat|
ākāśamākāśaniṣṭhasaṃyogatvarahitakarmajanyaguṇādhikaraṇam,
dravyatvāt, ghaṭavat|

na cātropādhisambhavaḥ, pūrvoktādeva nyāyāt| idaṃ
karmaitajjanyasaṃyogatvarahitadviṣṭhaguṇajanakaṃ karmatvāt,
anyakarmavat| na cāsya dravyakarmaṇi vyabhicāraḥ|
pagamāt| saṃyogatvarahitakarmajanyadviṣṭhaguṇānyaḥ etadanyaḥ
saṃyogatvarahitaitatkarmajanyadviṣṭhaguṇānyo vā saṃyogatvāt|

na cātropādhipratipakṣasambhavaḥ, kevalā | yadvā kālo
rūpasparśagandhasnehadravatvagurutvaparatvāparatvaśabdabuddhisukhaduḥkhecchādveṣaprayatnadharmādharmasaṃskārasaṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvakālaniṣṭhasaṃyogatvarahitaguṇādhikaraṇam,
dravyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi prayoktavyam|

na cātrārthāntaram| gandho viṃśatijātirahitaguṇasya
ghaṭādāvaprasiddhatvādaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam| dṛṣṭāntasya
ca sādhyavikalatvamiti vācyam|
rūpādidvāviṃśatikālaniṣṭhasaṃyogatvarahitatvaviśiṣṭaguṇasya
ghaṭādiniṣṭhasaṃyogatvābhāvenaiva siddheḥ
kālaniṣṭhatvabhāvavyāhatatvāt|

na ca rūpatvāditrayoviṃśatijātirahitaguṇasya
ghaṭādāvaprasiddhatvādaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam, dṛṣṭāntasya
ca sādhyavikalatvamiti vācyam|
rūpatvādidvāviṃśatikālaniṣṭhasaṃyogatvarahitatvaviśiṣṭaguṇasya
ghaṭādiniṣṭhasaṃyogasyaiva ghaṭādau prasiddhatvāditi|

na caivaṃ dravatvarahitā pṛthivī
rūparasagandhasparśasaṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvaparatvāparatvagurutvasaṃskāratvasaṃyogatvavibhāgatvarahitaguṇādhikaraṇam,
dravyatvāt, ātmavadityādinā
rūpādivibhāgāntavyatiriktaguṇasiddhiprasaṅgaḥ,
anukūlakarmavirahāt| tadasiddhau vibhāgāderapyaprasiddhiriti na
vācyam, vyāghātasyodbhaṭatvāt| rūpatvādirahitasya
vibhāgatvarahitatvānupapatteḥ| vibhāgatvarahitasya ca
rūpatvādirahitatvānupapatteḥ| rūpatvādirahitaguṇatvasya
vibhāgalakṣaṇatvādvibhāgatvajātivyavasthāpakatvācceti|

nanvevaṃ tarhi vibhāgo .api na sidhyet| tatsādhakānāṃ
pṛthaktvenānyathāsiddhatvāt| rūpatvādirahitaguṇatvasya
pṛthaktvalakṣaṇatvopapatteḥ,
pṛthaktvajātivyavasthāpakatvācceti| tanna, rūpatvādirahitasya
guṇatvasya pṛthaktvalakṣaṇatve vibhāgasya pṛthaktva
evāntarbhāvāt| bāhyaikendriyagrāhyatvarahitaguṇatvasya
saṃkhyālakṣaṇatve parimāṇādīnāmapi
saṃkhyāyāmantarbhāvāt|
sparśavadindriyagrāhyatvarahitaguṇatvasya ca śabdalakṣaṇatve
buddhyādīnāmapi śabde .antarbhāvāt| rūpatvarahitaguṇatvasya
rasalakṣaṇatve ca gandhādīnāmapi rasa evāntarbhāvāt|
guṇatvasya ca rūpalakṣaṇatve rasādīnāmapi rūpa evāntarbhāvāditi|

tarhi guṇānāṃ caturviṃśatitvānupapattiriti cet---na, evaṃvidhapṛthaktvādilakṣaṇapakṣe guṇānāśrayo viṃśatitvādinā caturvimśatitvānabhyupagamāt|
vibhāgādivyāvṛttapṛthaktvādilakṣaṇapakṣe caturviṃśatitvasya
durvāratvāditi|| 24||

nanu karmāpyanekeṣāṃ samavāyikāraṇānāṃ kāryasāmānyaṃ
paṭakarmaṇaḥ paṭāvayavatadavayavādisamavetatvāt| anyathā
calati teṣāmuttaradeśāsaṃyogānutpatteḥ tatrāpi
karmakalpanāyāmanantakalpanāpatterityāśaṅkāparihārārthaṃ
sūtram---

asamavāyātkāryaṃ karma na vidyate|| 1-1-25||

asamavāyādityatrānekeṣviti śeṣaḥ| na tāvatpaṭakarmaṇo
.anekāśritatve pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇam, ananubhavāt|
nāpyanumānam, paṭakarma paṭavyatiriktasamavetam, karmatvāt,
ghaṭakarmavadityādīnāṃ pratyakṣabādhitatvāt| paṭaścalatīti
pratyakṣeṇa paṭakarmaṇaḥ paṭamātraniṣṭhatvapratīteḥ|
anyathā paṭarūpaṃ paṭavyatiriktasamavetam, rūpatvāt,
ghaṭarūpavadityāderapi prasaṅgāt| nāpi
paṭāvayavāderuttaradeśasaṃyogānupapattiḥ
paṭāvayavādikarmaṇaivopapateḥ| na cānantākarmakalpanāpatteḥ,
pratyakṣasiddhatvāt| anyathāsiddhottaradeśasaṃyogabalena
kalpanāyāṃ cādoṣatvāt| karma nānekasamavāyikāraṇajanyam,
anekāsamavetatvāditi sādhyāviśiṣṭateti cet---na,
sādhyāviśiṣṭatvaṃ kiṃ sādhyābhinnatvam tadrahitatve
.api jātyādau vidyamānatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
anekāśritatvābhāvasya pratyakṣasiddhatvāt| samavāyatve
satyanekāśritasya pratyakṣasiddhatvāt| samavāyatve
satyanekāśritatvasyānekasamavetatvaśabdārthatvāt| na
cāsminsūtre
anekasamavetatvābhāvenānekasamavāyikāraṇajanyatvā[nu]bhāvo
.anumīyate| kiṃ
tvanekasamavāyikāraṇajanyatvamanekasamavetatvagarbham| tacca
niṣpramāṇakaṃ pratyakṣabādhitaṃ ceti pratipādyate| tena
sādhyāviśiṣṭatvadoṣānavakāśa

iti|

ekadravyamiti karmalakṣaṇasūtreṇedaṃ
gatārthamiti cet---na, tasyaiva
sūtrasiddhaikadravyatvādiviśiṣṭalakṣaṇaparatvāt| atra
caikadravyatvasamarthanādgatārthatvena sūtrabhaṅge
.apyavirodhāditi|| 25||

saṃyogānāṃ dravyam|| 1-1-26||

saṃyogau ca saṃyogāśca saṃyogāḥ| teṣāṃ dravyaṃ kāryaṃ
sāmāyam| tathā ca kiṃciddravyaṃ dvayoḥ saṃyogayoḥ kāryam,
yathā tritantukaṃ kiṃcidbahūnāṃ yathā paṭa iti|
dvitantukādikaṃ punaranekenaiva saṃyogena janyata iti na
saṃyogayoḥ nāpi saṃyogānāṃ samānaṃ kāryamiti|

ātreyācāryāstu
punarevamāhuḥ| tantubhyāṃ dvitantukamārabhyate|
dvitantukatantva[nta]rābhyāṃ ca tritantukamiti
tritantu[kame]kenaiva saṃyogena janyate| tathā prasāriteṣu
tantuṣvantarālapraviṣṭena tantunā prathamaṃ khaṇḍapaṭa
ārabhyate| tadanantaraṃ tena tantvantareṇa
khaṇḍapaṭāntaramityanena krameṇa paṭasyotpādātpaṭo
.apyekenaiva saṃyogenārabhyata iti na
bahūnāṃ saṃyogānāṃ dravyamiti sūtraṃ
yathāśrutamanupapannameveti
paṭāvayavatadavayavaniṣṭhākhaṇḍapaṭārambhakasaṃyogānāṃ
kāraṇakāraṇādīnāmapi pūrvabhāvitvamātreṇa
kāraṇatvopacārādupacārapravṛttyā vyākhyeyamiti|

tadanupapannam| tryaṇukasya dvyaṇukaniṣṭhānekasaṃyogajanyatvāt|
dvābhyāṃ tryaṇukābhyāmekadravyamārabhyate| na
dvyaṇukāntareṇa ca tryaṇukamiti| tryaṇukamapyekenaiva saṃyogena
janyata iti cet---na, dvābhyāmārabdhe tryaṇuke
mahattvābhāvaprasaṅgāt|
kāraṇabahutvamahattvapracayānāmasambhavāt| paṭe ca
khaṇḍapaṭānāṃ samavāyikāraṇatvābhāvāt| tathā ca
khaṇḍapaṭo na paṭasamavāyikāraṇam, atantutvāt, ghaṭavaditi|
na cāsya pratyakṣabādhaḥ, khaṇḍapaṭārabdhaḥ paṭa
ityananubhavāt| nāpi paṭārambhakakhaṇḍapaṭābhyāṃ
vyabhicāraḥ| tayorapi pakṣatulyatvāt| na ca
khaṇḍapaṭābhyāmārabdhaḥ paṭa iti pratyakṣādeva
khaṇḍapaṭayoḥ paṭārambhakatvamiti vācyam,
tathāvidhānubhavābhāvāt| bhāve .api vā tasyāpramāṇatvāt|
paṭasya ca tantubhya evopapatteḥ|

na cātra khaṇḍapaṭābhāva upādhiḥ, vyāvartyavivakṣābhāvena
pakṣetaratvāt| tasyāpi khaṇḍapaṭasya paṭārambhakatvābhāvāt|
nāpi khaṇḍapaṭatvānyataratvābhāva upādhiḥ, pakṣe
khaṇḍapaṭānyatamatvasya tantukhaṇḍapaṭavyatiriktatvābhāvāt|

tadabhāvasya ca tantukhaṇḍapaṭavyatiriktatvarūpatvāt|
khaṇḍapaṭavyatiriktasya ca pakṣamātravyāvṛttasya
vipakṣādavyāvṛttatvāt| vipakṣādavyāvṛttavadvipakṣā
[vi]śeṣaṇavato .api pakṣetaratvāt| anyathā
dhūmādagnyanumānavi vahadaparvatānyataratvābhāvena
sopādhitvāt|

na cāsya tantupaṭānyataratvādinā satpratipakṣatvaṃ tantutvena
sopādhitvāt| ayaṃ khaṇḍapaṭo naitatpaṭasamavāyikāraṇam,
khaṇḍapaṭatvāt| anyakhaṇḍapaṭaḥ samavāyikāraṇakaḥ
paṭatvādanyapaṭavadityanumānācca| tasmāttantava eva
saṃyogasahitāḥ paṭamārabhanta iti saṃyogānāṃ paṭakāryaṃ
sāmānyamiti| yatra ca yugapatsaṃyuktāstantavastatra tritantukaṃ
catvāraḥ pañca vā catustantukādikaṃ dravyamārabhante,
ubhayakarmaṇīva

saṃyogam| tatrāpi dvayoḥ saṃyogayoḥ
saṃyogasaṃyogānāṃ vā saṃyogānāṃ dravyārambhakatvaṃ
viśeṣāgrahaṇāt| anyathā saṃyogasyāpyubhayakarmajatvabhaṅgāt|
yugapatsaṃyoga na jāyata iti cet---na, karmaṇorapi tulyatvāt|
tatra yugapaccalitāviti pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇamiti cet---na,
yugapattantavaḥ saṃyuktā iti pratyakṣasya tulyatvāt|
bādhābhāvasyobhayasamānatvāt|

tasmātsaṃyogau saṃyogāśca dravyamārabhanta ityupapannaṃ
sūtram
saṃyogānāṃ dravyam [1-1-26] iti| ādyāditantavaḥ
khaṇḍapaṭānārabhya paṭamārabhante anārabhyaiva vā| nādyaḥ,
mūrtānāṃ samānadeśatvaprasaṅgāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
khaṇḍapaṭapratyakṣakāraṇatvācca| samānajātīyasaṃyogasya
dravyārambhakatvaniyamācceti cet---na, mūrtānāṃ
samānamūrtadeśatvasya
kvaciddravyaprasiddhasyāpādayitumaśakyatvāt| āpādakanirukteśca
āpādyaprasiddhau tena sahāpādakasya prāptigrahaṇasambhavena
mūlaśaithilyācca asmābhiḥ khaṇḍapaṭādīnāṃ mūrtānāṃ
samānadeśasya iṣṭatvācca| tarhi ghaṭapaṭādīnāmapi
samānadeśatvaprasaṅga iti cet---na, khaṇḍa[paṭa]paṭādīnāṃ
samānadeśatvasya ghaṭapaṭādīnāṃ
samānadeśatvenāvyāptatvāt| khaṇḍapaṭādisamānadeśatvasya
bhavato .aprasiddhatvācca| asmābhiśca ghaṭapaṭādīnāṃ
samānadeśasyeṣṭatvāt| etasminneva bhūtale ghaṭapaṭādīnāṃ
samāveśāt| ghaṭāvacchinne bhūtale paṭādipradhvaṃsa iti
cet---na, iṣṭatvādeva ghaṭavadbhinnabhūtalātpaṭādimato
bhūtalasya bhedābhāvāt| jagati bhūtalasyaikatvādyāvati bhūtale
ghaṭastāvatyeva paṭādidhvaṃsa iti cet---na, iṣṭatvādeva|
ghaṭāvacchinnaṃ yāvadbhūtalaṃ tāvatpaṭādimaditi sambhavati
bhūtalasyābhāvatvādeva| yasminbhūtalāvayave ghaṭaḥ tatraiva
paṭādiprasaṅga iti cet---na, iṣṭatvādeva| yasminbhūtalāvayave
gṛhādau paṭastatraiva paṭādisamāveśādghaṭamātrasamāveśe
.api tatparimāṇavati paṭādiprasaṅga iti cet---na, tatra
ghaṭādimati paṭādīnāmasamāveśe .api bhūtalādau ghaṭa
paṭādīnāṃ samāveśena mūrtatvasamānadeśatvayoḥ
virodhabhaṅgasya tadavasthatvāt|
ghaṭamātrasamāveśocitaparimāṇavati
paṭāvayavatadavayavādīnāṃ samāveśācca|

mūrtānāṃ saṃyogaṃ pratyekāśritatve .apyekasamavetatvaṃ
viruddhamiti cet---na, ekasamavetatvaṃ kimekena samavāyaḥ,
samavāyapakṣe satyekāśritatvaṃ vā| nādyaḥ,
anekeṣāmapyavayavānāmekāvayavinā samavāyāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
samavāyavatāṃ ghaṭapaṭādīnāmekasminbhūtale āśritatvāt|
ghaṭādīnāṃ bhūsamavāyo nāstīti cet---na, samavāyaikatvena
tasyāpi sattvāt| vṛkṣe śākhānāṃ paṭe tantūnāṃ gavi
viṣāṇayoḥ samavetatvāt| vṛkṣādau śākhādayo na samavetā iti
cet---na, tatsamavāyatve sati tadāśritatvasya tatsamavetatvasya
sattvāt| vṛkṣādayaḥ śākhādiṣvāśritāḥ, na tu śākhādayo
vṛkṣādiṣviti cet---na, vṛkṣe śākhāḥ paṭe tantavo gavi
viṣāṇe iti pratyakṣasya sattvāt| śākhāsu vṛkṣo vṛkṣe ca
śākhā iti parasparāśraya iti cet---na,
utpattijñaptipratibandhakatvābhāve
parasparāśrayasyādūṣaṇatvāt|

astu vā tantūnāṃ khaṇḍapaṭārambhakatvam| tathāpi na
mūrtānāṃ samānadeśatvaprasaṅgaḥ
premā(?)bhighātasamutpannakarmajavibhāgādārambhakasaṃyogavināśātpūrvatra
nivṛttau punarutpannakarmajanyatantusaṃyogau paṭārambhāt| na
caivaṃvidhakalpanāyāṃ mānābhāvaḥ| mūrtānāṃ
samānadeśatvānupapattereva tatra pramāṇābhāvāt| astu vā
pūrvaṃ khaṇḍapaṭānutpattereva paṭārambhakatvam| anyathā
bhavanmate .api paṭārambhakakhaṇḍapaṭānyatantusaṃyogasya
dravyāntarārambhakatvaprasaṅgāt| tena mūrtānāṃ
samānadeśatvaprasaṅgaḥ| na cātra pramāṇābhāvaḥ, mūrtānāṃ
samānadeśatvānupapattereva tatra pramāṇatvāt|
antyatantuvyatiriktaitapaṭajanakatantava etadavyavahitapūrvakṣaṇe
na kāryadravyavantaḥ, etatpaṭasamavāyikāraṇatvāt,
antyatantuvaditi anumānācceti|

na caivaṃ khaṇḍapaṭapratyakṣavirodhaḥ, tasya bhāvastattvāt|
nāpi saṃyogasya dravyānārambhe .anapekṣakāraṇatvam|
antyatantusaṃyogavyatiriktadravyārambhakasaṃyogānāṃ
sāpekṣatvāt|

tathā bhavatīti sāpekṣebhyo .anapekṣebhyaśceti sūtreṇa|
dravyārambhe vā saṃyogasya
sāpekṣatvānapekṣatvavyutpādanātpūrvaṃ
khaṇḍapaṭānutpattāvantyatantvapekṣaṇe ca paṭasya vināśānna
kiṃcidupalabhyeteti cet---na, avasthitatantusaṃyogaiḥ
paṭāntarārambhāt|

anye tu yadi tantavaḥ khaṇḍapaṭānārabhya paṭamārabhante
tadādyatantvapakarṣaṇāttadārabdhakāryaparamparāyāḥ
paṭayuktāyā vinaṣṭatvānna kiṃcidupalabhyeta [iti]
ārabhyārambhakavāde dūṣaṇamāhuḥ| tadayuktam|
ādyatantvārabdhakāryaparamparāyā vināśe .api
dvitīyāditantvārabdhakāryaparamparāyā
avināśādavasthitasaṃyogebhyaḥ paṭāntarodayācca|

anye tu kusūlā[dā]vārabhyārambhakabhāvasya
pratyakṣasiddhatvātpaṭādāvapi tathaivāṅgīkartavyamityāhuḥ|
tadapi na yuktam| kusūlādāvārabhyārambhakabhāvasya
pratyakṣeṇānupahāsātpūrvoktanyāyavirodhācceti|
tasmātparasparasaṃyuktāstantava eva paṭamārabhanta iti siddhaṃ
saṃyogānāṃ dravyakāryasāmānyamiti|

nanvanupapannamidam| tantuvyatiriktānāṃ paṭādīnāmavayavinām
[a]bhāvāditi saugatāḥ|
tadayuktam| paṭādyavayavināṃ paṭo .ayamiti
[pratyakṣasiddhatvāt|] na cātra tantvādaya eva ālambanamiti
yuktam| parasparāyukteṣu tantvādiṣu paṭa iti
pratyakṣa[prasaṅgāt|] parasparāsaṃyuktāstantavaḥ paṭa
ityādipratyakṣālambanamiti cet---na,
tantuvāyavemādivyāpārābhāvena [yathākathaṃcit]saṃyuktādiṣu
tantvādiṣu paṭo .ayamityādi pratyayaprasaṅgāt|
tantuvāyavemādivyāpārarahitāḥ parasparasaṃyuktāstantvādayaḥ
paṭa ityādipratyayālambanamiti cet---na,
tantuvāyavemādivyāpārābhāve .api paṭa
ityādipratyayodayātsaṃsthānaviśeṣavantaḥ tantava (eva paṭa)
ityādipratyayālambanamiti cet---na,
saṃsthānaviśeṣaśabdenāvayavivivakṣāyāmavayavisiddheḥ
tantvādisaṃyogavivakṣāyāṃ ca pūrvoktadoṣāt|
tathābhūtasaṃyogasahitāstantvādayaḥ paṭādīnārabhante,
tathābhūtasaṃyogasahitāstantvādayaḥ paṭa
ityādipratyayālambanamiti cet---na, paṭādijanakatvātiriktasya
nirvaktumaśakyatvāt| paṭādijanakatvasya ca paṭo .ayamiti
pratyayālambanatve paṭādyavayavisiddheḥ|
anupalakṣitarekhoparekhādyavayavasaṃsthānaviśeṣanistalasuvarṇagolako
.ayamityavayavipratyayācca| tantvādīnāmapyavayavaviśeṣāṇāṃ
saugatairanaṅgīkārācca|

tantvādiśabdena paramāṇusamūho vivakṣita iti cet---na,
paramāṇuśabdena dvyaṇukopādānābhidhāne teṣāmasiddhau taiḥ
paṭādipratyayānyathāsiddhervaktumaśakyatvāt| dvyaṇukādeva
tatsiddhau dvyaṇukasyaivāvayavinaḥ siddheḥ dvyaṇukāsiddhau ca
tadavayavino .apyasiddheḥ| trasareṇordvyaṇukasiddhau ca
trasareṇāvavayavābhidhāne ca tadasiddhau taiḥ
paṭādipratyayādyasiddhervaktumaśakyatvāt| trasareṇoreva
tatsiddhau tasyaivāvayavināṃ siddheḥ
dvyaṇukatadavayavayoścāsmadādyatīndriyatvenāsmadādyatīndriyaghaṭādipratyayaviṣayatvānupapatteśca
asmadādyatīndriyagrahaṇayogyatayotpannāḥ
paramāṇavo .asmadādyaindriyakapaṭādipratyayaviṣayā
bhaviṣyantīti cet---na,
utpattimatā[ma]smadādīndriyagrahaṇayogyānāṃ
paramāṇūnāmasiddhatvena taiḥ paṭādipratyayānyathāsiddheḥ
kartumaśakyatvāt|

trasareṇvabhidhāne ca teṣāmevāvayavināṃ siddhesta eva
niravayavāḥ paramāṇava iti cet---na, nirākariṣyamāṇatvāt|
samūhasya samūhivyatiriktasyānaṅgīkārāt| aṅgīkāre vā
tasyāvayavinaḥ siddhe[ddeḥ|] senāvanādau kaḥ samūhaśabdārtha
iti cet| āropito .avayavyeva| sa evasmākamapi samūhaśabdārtha iti
cet---na, avayavinaḥ kvacidapyanabhyupagame
tadāropasyaivānupapatterasaṃkhyātairnirākaraṇāt|
ekadeśāvacchinnaṃ samūha iti cet---na, avayavyanabhyupagame ekasya
deśasyāsambhavāt| kevalaparamāṇūnāṃ ca paramāṇuṣu paṭa
ityādipratyayaprasaṅgāt| parasparasaṃyuktānāṃ
paṭādipratyayālamabanamiti cet---paramāṇusaṃyogasyārthasiddhau
tena paṭādipratyayānyathāsiddheraśakyatvāt|
dvyaṇukāsamavāyikāraṇatayā ca tatsiddhau dvyaṇukasyāvayavinaḥ
siddheḥ bhavadbhiḥ saṃyogivyatiriktasya
saṃyogasyānabhyupagamācca| viśiṣṭa utpādaḥ sa iti cet---na,
tasya nirvaktumaśakyatvāt| paramāṇūnāmutpādāsāmbhavācca|

ete paramāṇavo naitatpaṭapratyayālambanam, paramāṇutvāt,
anyaparamāṇuvat| ayaṃ paṭa iti pratyayo naitatparamāṇuviṣayaḥ,
asmanniṣṭhāparokṣapratyayatvāt,
asmanniṣṭhāparokṣapratyayāntaravat| ete paramāṇavo na paṭaḥ,
paramāṇutvāt, anyaparamāṇuvat| ayaṃ naitatparamāṇusamūhaḥ,
paṭatvāt, anyapaṭavadityādinā pratiṣedhācca|
paṭādisiddherāśrayāsiddhiriti cet---na,
paṭasyobhayavādisiddhatvāt| tasya paramāṇutvavyatiriktatvayoreva
vivādātsthūlatvaikatvābhyāṃ ca paṭasya
sūkṣmānekaparamāṇuvyatiriktatvasiddheḥ| sthūlatvaikatve
paṭasyāsiddhe iti cet---na, sthūlaḥ paṭa ekaḥ paṭa
ityādipratyakṣasiddhatvāt| na cedaṃ[dama]pramāṇam,
bādhakābhāvāt| na sthūlo naikaḥ paṭa
ityādipratyakṣābhāvāt| anumānāsambhavācca| yadanekavṛtti
tadanekam, yathā bhinnabhājanagatānyāmraphalāni|

anekavṛttitvāvayavitve bādhakamiti cet---na,
jātisamavāyābhāveṣu vyabhicārāt| te vipakṣatulyā iti
cet---na, ayaṃ paramāṇurekaniṣṭhādhikaraṇam, meyatvāt,
anyavadityādinā siddhe .asya dharmitve
dharmigrāhakapramāṇābhāvāt| anyadharmitve
tatraivānekavṛttitvasya vyabhicārāt,
avayavasiddhāvāśrayāsiddheḥ| siddhau vā
dharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhācca| dharmigrāhakapramāṇena
cāvayavina ekatvenaiva siddheḥ| anekavṛttitvasya tavāsiddheśca|
avayavinamabhyupagacchatā
tasyānekavṛttitvasyābhyupagantumaśakyatvāt|
yadviruddhadharmādhyastaṃ tadanekaṃ yathā dahanatuhinau|
viruddhadharmādhyastaṃ cāvayavītyekatve bādhakamiti cet---na,
pūrvoktadoṣāt| viruddhadharmādhyāsasyāsiddhatvācca|

kampākampau viruddhadharmāviti cet---na,
kampākampayoryugapadekatrābhāvāt| kṣaṇabhaṅgavādinaśca
krameṇāpyekatra kampākampayorasambhavāt| sthiravādināṃ ca
krameṇa
kampākampayoraviruddhatvāt| ekasminnavayave calati
anyasmiṃścāvayave niścala iti kampākampau yugapatprasiddhāviti
cet---na, avayave calatyavayavino .avaśyaṃ calanābhyupagamāt
hastacalane śarīracalanānupalambhāt, [??]
hastacalanahetormandaprayatnādeḥ śarīracalanajanakatvācca|
rāgārāgau viruddhadharmāviti cet---na, rāgārāgau
kiṃ raktabhāvatadabhāvau vivakṣitau rāgadravyasaṃyogatadabhāvau
vā| nādyaḥ, raktarūpatadabhāvayorekatrāvayavinyasiddhatvāt| na
dvitīyaḥ, saṃyogasya vyāpyavṛttitve
rāgadravyasaṃyogatadabhāvayoryugapadekatrāsiddhatvāt| na,
saṃyogādayo vyāpyavṛttayaḥ, guṇatvāt, rūpavadityādinā
saṃyogādeḥ vyāpyavṛttitvasiddheḥ|

kathaṃ tarhi ardharakte paṭe rakto .araktaśceti vyavahāra iti
cet---avayavavartinā rāgadravyasaṃyogābhāveneti
grahaṇādrāgadravyasaṃyogasya vyāpya[vṛttitvāt]
vayavāvaraṇe .api raktaḥ paṭa iti pratyayaprasaṅga iti cet---na,
āvṛttatvena rāgadravyānupalambhe
tanniṣṭhasaṃyogasyāpyapratīteḥ| saṃyogapratītau
saṃyogidvayapratītikāraṇatvāt| anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ
tathaiva kāryakāraṇabhāvāvadhāraṇāt| avyāvṛttitve ca
rāgadravyasaṃyogatadabhāvayorekatra pramitatvena
viruddhadharmasyāsiddheḥ| tathā
cāyametadrāgasaṃyogātyantā meyatvāt, anyavaditi| na
caitadrāgasaṃyogagrāhakapratyakṣabādhakam, etadrāgasaṃyogasya
pūrvānumānasādhye
tadrāgadravyasaṃyogātyantābhāvavirahatvābhāvāt|
abhāvābhāvasya bhāvavyatiriktatvāt|
pūrvoktasādhyābhāvāgrāhakatve .api
tadviruddhaitadrāgadravyasaṃyogāgrāhakatvāt| tadyedaṃ bādhakaṃ
vahniruṣṇo dravyatvādityatraivauṣṇyagrāhakaṃ pratyakṣamiti
cet---na, tadrāgadravyasaṃyogasya
tadatyantābhāvaviruddhatvasyāsiddheḥ, vahniruṣṇa
ityādāvauṣṇyagrāhakapratyakṣasya bādhakatvāt| tarhi
vahniruṣṇa ityādikamanumānabādhitaṃ syāditi cet---nāyamuṣṇo
vahnirityādipratyakṣeṇa bādhāt| tarhi prakṛtamapyanumānaṃ
nāyametadrāgadravyasaṃyogātyantābhāvāditi
pratyakṣabādhitamastviti cet---na, paṭe
rāgadravyasaṃyogatadatyantābhāvāvabhyupagacchatā
evaṃvidhapratyakṣādinānaṅgīkārāt| pratyuta paṭo rakto
.araktaścetyanubhavābhyupagamādetadrāgadravyasaṃyogena
tadatyantābhāvānumānamiti cet---na, aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt|

saṃyogāvyāpyavṛttitvapakṣe etadrāgadravyasaṃyogātyantābhāvasya sarvatra vidyamānatvena
tadatyantābhāvasya sarvatra vidyamānatvena tadatyantābhāsya
kvacidapyaprasiddheḥ|
etadrāgadravyasaṃyogātyantābhāvenāpratiyogī| samānādhikaraṇo
.atyantābhāvavattvāt, rūpātyantābhāvavaditi cet---na, bādhāt|

etadrāgadravyasaṃyogātyantābhāvasyāpyatyantābhāvasya
pratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatvāt dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatvācca|
rūpātyantābhāvapratiyogikaṃ jñānādisamānādhikaraṇatvāt| na
ca pratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatvābhāvātsādhyata iti na,
svaśabdenaitadrāgadravyasaṃyogasyātyantābhāvābhidhāne
tatpratiyogyetadrāgadravyasaṃyogasyātyantābhāvābhidhānaitatpratiyogyetadrāgadravyasaṃyogasamānādhikaraṇarūpātyantābhāvaniṣṭhavyabhicārāt|

rūpātyantābhāvābhidhāne ca pūrvoktadoṣāt| anyasya ca
svaśabdārthasyānirūpaṇāt| sandigdhasyāpi
ghaṭātyantābhāvādau vyabhicārāt| ekadaiva bhūtale
ghaṭatadatyantābhāvayoḥ svīkārāt| ayamiha bhūtale ghaṭo
nāstīti bhūtalaniṣṭhaghaṭasyaiva bhūtale
.atyantābhāvānubhavāt|
etadrāgadravyasaṃyogasamānādhikaraṇatvābhāve
caitadabhāvatvavirahasyaivopādhitvāt|

na cāsya pakṣetaratā,
etadanyonyābhāvādervipakṣatvādvyāvṛttatvāt|
etadrāgadravyasaṃyogābhāvatvena satpratipakṣatvācca|
etadrāgadravyasaṃyogatvādetadrāgadravyasaṃyogābhāvavāniti
vyāhatam|
svapratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatvarahitābhāvatvasyātyantābhāvalakṣaṇavāniti
cet---na, pratiyoginirapekṣaṃ
svapratiyoginiṣṭhābhāvatvasyātyantābhāvalakṣaṇatvāt|
tathāvidhasādhane ca vyāghātābhāvāt| tathāpyabhāvasya
pratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatve vyāhatam|
pratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatvarahitaviśeṣasyaiva nābhāvatvāditi
cet---na,
pratiyoginirapekṣalakṣaṇarahitatvasyābhāvalakṣaṇatvāt|
anyathānyonyābhāvāderapyabhāvatvālābhāt|
āvṛtatvānāvṛtatve viruddhadharmādhyāsa iti cet---na,
ardhāvaraṇe .apyavayavino vṛtatvāt, anāvṛtatvābhāvāt| etena
grahaṇāgrahaṇe viruddhadharmādhyāsa ityapi nirastam|
grahaṇāgrahaṇayorātmadharmatvenāvayavidharmatva[a]sambhavācca|

yadyavayavicalanāvaraṇādāvapyavayavino na calanāvaraṇādi,
tadāyutasiddhiprasaṅga iti cet---na, ayutasiddhiḥ kiṃ pṛthaktvaṃ
vibhāgo vā, taṃ calatyavayavāyuniṣṭha(?)sambandho vā
pṛthagāśrayasiddhatvaṃ vā pṛthaggatimattvaṃ vā
tadanāśritatvaṃ vā| nādyaḥ, iṣṭatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
mūlaśaithilyāt| avayavacalane .apyacalanatve
.avayavarūpāderavayavavibhāgābhāvāt| na tṛtīyaḥ, iṣṭatvāt|
āpādyāpādakayorbhedābhāvācca| na caturthaḥ,
pūrvavanmūlaśaithilyāt| na pañcamaḥ, iṣṭatvādeva|
vāgbhaṭādīnāṃ
tantvāśrayāśuvyatiriktaṃ tvādyāśritatvāt| na ṣaṣṭhaḥ,
iṣṭāpādanāt| tantupaṭayoḥ parasparaṃ pṛthagbhūtatvaṃ
gatimattvāt| pūrvavanmūlaśaithilyācca| na saptamaḥ,
mūlaśaithilyādeva| avayavacalane
.apyacalanāvayavarūpāderavayavāśritatvāt| yadyavayavacalane
.apyavayavī na calati, tarhi tasya pūrvadeśavibhāgo na
syātkāraṇābhāvāditi cet---na,
avayavakarmajanyāvaya[va]pūrvadeśavibhāgādeva tadupapatteḥ|

avayavyavayaveṣu kārtsnyena vartate, ekadeśena vā| nādyaḥ,
dvitīyāvayavādāvavayavino virahaprasaṅgāt, prathamāvayavino
virahaprasaṅgāt| prathamāvayava eva sarvasyāvayavinaḥ
parisamāptatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ, avayaveṣu pratyekamavayavino
.avayavāntarānaṅgīkārāt| aṅgīkāre vā
sarvatāvivṛttyarthamavayavāntarānusaraṇādanavasthānāditi
cet---na, avayavino .avayaveṣu kārtsnyena vṛttisvīkārāt|

na caivamavayavino .a(vaya)vāntareṣu anupalambhaprasaṅgaḥ|
tatrāpi kṛtsnasyāvayavino vidyamānatvāt| yadekatra kārtsnyena
vartate tadanyatra [na] vartate yathā rūpamiti cet---na,
kārtsnyagrahaṇavaiyarthyāt| ekadeśenānekasthūṇāsu
vartamānavaṃśādivyāvṛttyarthaṃ kārtsnyagrahaṇamiti cet---na,
pratisthūṇaṃ kṛtsnasyaiva vaṃśasya vartamānatvāt|
tathāsti[sati] sthūṇāntare vaṃśo na syāditi cet---na, tatrāpi
kṛtsnasyaiva vartamānatvāt| yadekatra kārtsnyena vartate na
tadanyatra vartata ityetadevātra bādhakamiti cet---na, atrāpi
kṛtsnagrahaṇasya prayojanābhāvāt| saṃyogādinivṛttyarthaṃ
kṛtsnagrahaṇamiti cet---na, saṃyogāderapi pratya[kṣā]śrayaṃ
kṛtsnasyaiva vṛtteḥ| svarūpātiriktakārtsnasya
nirvaktumaśakyatvāt| ekadeśena vartata ityasyāpi
nirvaktumaśakyatvācca|

vaṃśa[ḥ] sthūṇāsvekadeśena vartata iti kiṃ yatra vaṃśo
vartate tatra tadavayave .api vartata iti vivakṣitam. kiṃ vā
tadavayave na vartata iti| nādyaḥ, avayavyanaṅgīkāro[re]
vaṃśatadavayavādīnāmasambhavāt| na dvitīyaḥ, tata eva
vaṃśaḥ sthūṇāsu vartata iti pratyakṣabādhācca|
vaṃśatadavayavādiśabdena paramāṇavo vivakṣita[a] iti cet---na,
teṣāmekadeśābhāvenaikadeśena vṛttyasambhavāt| yadekatra
vartate tadanyatra na vartata iti etāvadevāstu bādhakamiti
cet---na, avayavī avayavāntare na vartate,
etadavayavavṛttitvādetanmātraniṣṭhavadityāderavayavasya
prasiddhāvāśrayāsiddhatvāt| siddhau vā
dharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhāt| ayametanniṣṭhavān, meyatvāt,
anyavaditi siddhe anekavṛttitvasya dharmitve
dharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhāt| etena yatsattanniravayavam, yathā
rūpam, asyāvayavini
sāvayavitvāparaparyāyāvayavitvābhāvasya
niravayavasyāprasiddhatvāt| yatsattanna kāryadravyāśrayaḥ,
yathā rūpam| santaśca paramāṇava ityavayavīti bādhakamiti
cet---na, kāryadravyāśrayāsiddhau
tadāśrayaniṣedhasyāprasiddhatvāt| prasiddhau vā tatraiva
sattvahetorvyabhicārāt|

paramāṇavo na dravyāśrayā iti sādhyata iti
cet---pūrvoktadoṣādapasiddhāntācca na dravyāśrayasya
kvacidaprasiddhāvaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāsiddhau vā tatraiva
hetorvyabhicārāt| paramāṇvasiddhāvāśrayāsiddheḥ|
dvyaṇukāśrayatvena tatsiddhau ca dharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhāt,
paramāṇavo na dravyārambhakā iti sādhyata iti cet---na,
pūrvoktadoṣādapasiddhāntācca| tvayā
pūrvaparamāṇūnāmuttarottaraparamāṇujanakatvasvīkārātkāryadravyānārambhakatvasādhane
.apyapasiddhāntāt| uttarottaraparamāṇūnāmapi bhavanmate
kāryatvāt| yadindriyaṃ tanna dravyagrāhakam, yathā
ghrāṇarasane, indriye ca cakṣuḥsparśane ityavayavini bādhakamiti
cet---na, dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatvāt| ghrāṇarasanayorapi
gandharasādhikaraṇaṃ[ṇa] candanaguṇādigrāhakatvāt| mano
dṛṣṭānta iti cet---na,
tasyāpyātmagrāhakatvena sādhyavikalatvāt|
śrotraṃ dṛṣṭānta iti cet---na, dṛṣṭāntasya
sādhyavikalatvāt| śrotraṃ prayatnavadadhiṣṭhitam, dravyatvāt,
ghaṭavadityātmagocarānumitijanakatvena śrotrasyāpi
dravyagrāhakatvāt| dharmiṇo .api hetuvadanumitijanakatvāt
śrotraṃ dṛṣṭānta iti cet---dravyasākṣātkārajanakatvaṃ
dravyagrāhakatvam| tacca śrotre nāstyeveti cet---na,
dikkālavadākāśasyāpi sattvakāryajanakatvapakṣe śrotrasyāpi
ghaṭādidravyasākṣātkārajanakatvena dravyagrāhakatvāt|
dikkālayoḥ sarvendriyagrāhakatvāt| dikkālayorapratyakṣatve .api
prakṛtasādhyasādhane bādhāt| cakṣuḥsparśanayorasmanmate
ghaṭādyavayavigrāhakatvāt| tvanmate
ca paramāṇusamūhagrāhakatvāt| rūparasagandhasparśavyatiriktāḥ
paramāṇavaḥ saugatairanaṅgīkriyanta
iti cet---na, dravyagrāhakatvasya kvacidapramitau
tadabhāvasādhanasyāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvātkvacidindriye pramitau
tatraiva hetorvyabhicārāt| anyatra tatpramitau
kimavayavagrāhakatvena dravyagrāhakatvasiddhiḥ,
ākāśādidravyagrāhakatvena vā| nādyaḥ, avayavisiddheḥ| na
dvitīyaḥ avayavivirahe
taddṛṣṭāntabalapravṛttāvākāśādyanumānasambhavāt|
kāryatvaṃ na dravyavṛttikāryavṛttitvāt| kāryarūpavaditi
cet---na, dravyavṛttitvasya
kvacidasiddhāvaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt| siddhau
vāvayavivṛttitvena tatsiddhiḥ| ākāśavṛttitvena vā (?nākaḥ),
avayavisiddheḥ| na dvitīyaḥ, avayavyasiddhau na
dṛṣṭāntabalapravṛttānumānāsambhavo nākāśāderaprasiddheḥ|
