tasmādbādhakābhāvādghaṭo .ayamiti pratyakṣamavayavini
pramāṇamiti siddham|| 26||

rūpāṇāṃ rūpam|| 1-1-27||

rūpe ca rūpāṇi ca [rūpāṇi|]
teṣāmavayavagatānāmavayavigataṃ rūpaṃ samānaṃ kāryam|
upalakṣaṇaṃ cedaṃ rasādīnām| tasmādavayavagatānāṃ
rūparasagandhasparśagataikatvaikapṛthaktvamahattvadīrghatvagurutvadravatvasnehānāmavayavigatā
rūparasagandhasparśatvaikatvaikapṛthaktvamahattvadīrgha[tva]gurutvadravatvasnehāḥ
samānaṃ kāryamiti sūtrārthaḥ|

idamapi sūtraṃ mandaprayojanam, guṇāśca guṇāntaramārabhante
[1-1-9] ityanenaiva gatārthatvāditi|| 27||

gurutvaprayatnasaṃyogānāmutkṣepaṇam|| 1-1-28||

yadā

devadattaḥ prayatnena karādinā gurutvavantaṃ
pāṣāṇādikamupakṣipati, tadotkṣepaṇe pāṣāṇādigataṃ
gurutvaṃ kāraṇam| gurutvarahitānāṃ
pavanādīnāmutkṣepaṇāsambhavāt| tathā devadattaprayatnaḥ,
tadabhāve utkṣepaṇāsambhavāt| tathā
gurutvādhārādipāṣāṇādidevadattahastasaṃyogaḥ, tadabhāve
.apy utkṣepaṇānudayāt|
tasmādgurutvaprayatnasaṃyogānāmādyamutkṣepaṇādikamutkṣipya
tadotkṣepaṇe samānaṃ kāryam| dvitīyādikamutkṣepaṇaṃ tu
prathamotkṣepaṇajanyatvenāpi janyate iti
gurutvaprayatnasaṃyogavegānāṃ sāmānyaṃ kāryamiti|| 28||

saṃyogavibhāgāḥ karmaṇām|| 1-1-29||

kāryaṃ sāmānyamityanuvartate| idaṃ tu sūtraṃ yathāśrute
anupapannameva| bahūnāṃ karmaṇāṃ
saṃyogavibhāgajanakatvābhāvāt| anyatarakarmaṇā vā
saṃyogavibhāgau janyete| yathā śyenakarmaṇā sthāṇostena
saṃyogavibhāgau|| ubhayakarmabhyāṃ vā saṃyogavibhāgau janyete|
yathā mallakarmabhyāṃ mallasaṃyogavibhāgau| yathā ca
meṣakarmabhyāṃ meṣasaṃyogavibhāgau bhavata ityatra pramāṇaṃ
nāsti| pratyakṣānumānāgamāsambhavāt| pramāṇāntarasya
cānaṅgīkārāt| yatra yugapadeva bahavaḥ parasparaṃ saṃyujyante
viyujyante ca tatra bahūnāṃ karmaṇāṃ saṃyogavibhāga[a]ḥ
samānaṃ kāryamiti pratyakṣamiti cet---na,
tatrābahuniṣṭhasaṃyogavibhāgasambhavāt|
saṃyogavibhāgayordvimātraniṣṭhatvācca|
dvimātraniṣṭhasaṃyogavibhāgānāṃ cobhayakarmajanyatvena
bahukarmajanyatvāsambhavāt| bahūnāṃ saṃyogajanakānāṃ
vibhāgajanakatvāsambhavāt| vibhāgajanakānāṃ
saṃyogajanakatvāsambhavācca| tasmādubhayakarmajaḥ saṃyogaḥ
karmaṇāṃ samānaṃ kāryam| ubhayakarmajaśca vibhāgaḥ karmaṇoḥ
samānaṃ kāryam|| 29||

nanu kaścana saṃyogo vibhāgo vā bahūnāṃ karmaṇāṃ samānaṃ
kāryamiti| na tu saṃyogavibhāga[a]ḥ karmaṇāṃ kāryaṃ
sāmānyamiti vācyam| dvitīyādikarmaṇāṃ
prathamādikarmajanyatvāditi śaṅkāparihārārthaṃ sūtram---

kāraṇasāmānye dravyakarmaṇāṃ karmā kāraṇamuktamiti||

kāraṇasāmānyaṃ pratipādyate yasminprakaraṇe
tatkāraṇasāmānyam| tatra karma [na] karma
janayatītyuktam---guṇavaidharmyānna karmaṇām [1-1-21] iti
sūtreṇeti| idamapi sūtramanārambhaṇīyameva---karma
karmasādhyaṃ na vidyate [1-1-10] guṇavaidharmyānn karmaṇām
[1-1-21] ityetābhyāṃ gatārthatvāt| sa evārthaḥ
śaṅkāparihārārthaḥ smaryata iti cet---na, tābhyāmeva
śaṅkāparihārasiddheḥ| anyasakalasūtrārthasmāraṇārthaṃ
sūtrārthapraṇayanaprasaṅgāt|
śaṅkāntaraparihārārthamevamastviti cet---na,
evaṃvidhaśaṅkānāṃ sarvatra sulabhatvāt| punaḥ
punaruktārthasmāraṇe sūtravyāghātācceti|| 30||

iti
śrīharakiṅkaraparamapaṇḍitanyāyācāryabhaṭṭavādīndrakṛtau
kaṇādasūtravārtike prathamādhyāye dvitīyāhnikam||

tṛtīyamāhnikam|

 ityādinā kāryasāmānyaprakaraṇaparyantena
paramāṇvādīnāṃ dvyaṇukādau kāraṇatvaṃ pratipāditam|
tada[nupapa]nnam, paramāṇvādīnāṃ dvyaṇukā[dyā]rambhakatve
yugapadeva sarvadvyaṇukādyudayaprasaṅgāt| samarthasya
kṣepāyogāt| asamarthake vā sarvadā dvyaṇukānudayaprasaṅgāt|
arthakriyāvirahāt| arthakriyākāritvaṃ sattvalakṣaṇaṃ
syādityāśaṅkāparihārārthaṃ sūtradvayam---

kāraṇābhāvātkāryābhāvaḥ|| 1-2-1||

na tu kāryābhāvātkāraṇābhāva iti|| 1-2-2||

paramāṇvādayo dvyaṇukādīnārabhamāṇā api na
yugapadevārabhantem apekṣaṇīyasya saṃyogādeḥ
kāraṇasyābhāvāt| na caivamapekṣaṇīyātsaṃyogādereva
dvyaṇukādyutpatteḥ paramāṇvādīnāṃ [na] kāraṇatvaṃ
dharmigrāhakapramāṇena dvyaṇukādikāraṇatvenaiva
paramāṇvādisiddheḥ| janayantu paramāṇvādayo yugapadeva
sarvakāryāṇi na caivaṃ dvitīyakṣaṇe
arthakriyāvirahātparamāṇvādīnāmasattvaprāptau
kṣaṇabhaṅgasiddhiḥ| arthakriyākāritvasya
sattvaśabdārthānaṅgīkārāt| sattājāteḥ prameyatvasya vā
sattvaśabdārthatvāt| tadidamuktam [na tu]
kāryābhāvātkāraṇābhāvaḥ 1-2-2] iti| yadā pūrvamuktaṃ
kāryāvirodhi dravyaṃ kāraṇāvirodhi ca [1-1-11] ubhayathā
guṇaḥ [1-1-12] iti kathaṃ kāryakāraṇāvirodhīnāṃ
dravyaguṇādīnā. vināśa ityapekṣāyāmuktam
kāraṇābhāvātkāryābhāvaḥ [1-2-1] iti|

nanu kāryavināśa eva dravyādivināśahetuḥ kiṃ na syādityata
uktam---na tu kāryābhāvātkāraṇābhāvaḥ [1-2-2] iti| yadvā
prathamāhnike
dravyaguṇakarmāṇi āpādyante| dvitīyāhnike
kriyāsāmānyaviśeṣāḥ pratipādyante sāmānyaviśeṣābhāvād
iti sūtreṇa
tayoreva buddhiḥ tattvāt| tatra sāmānyaviśeṣau na staḥ,
kāraṇābhāvāt| kāryābhāvācca| na hi kāraṇamantareṇa
kiṃcidātmānaṃ labhate, kāraṇādhīnatvātsarvavastūnām| na ca
kāryamantareṇa kasyacitsattvaṃ sambhavati, kāryajanakatvasyaiva
sattvalakṣaṇatvāt|
kāraṇakāryayorabhāvena
sāmānyaviśeṣayoranumānāsambhavācca|
liṅgāntarasyāpyabhāvāt| pratyakṣasya ca
sāmānyādyasādhakatvāt|
gaurgaurityādisa[vi]kalpakasyāpramāṇatvātpramāṇatvāt
(?) sāmānyagocaranirvikalpakasya ca paraṃ
pratyakṣasiddheriti saugatāḥ| tannirākaraṇārthaṃ
sūtradvayam---kāraṇābhāvātkāryābhāvaḥ [1-2-1]
na tu kāryābhāvātkāraṇābhāvaḥ [1-2-2] iti|
kāraṇaṃ hi vyāvartamānaṃ kāryaṃ vyāvartayati|
na ca sāmānyaviśeṣau kāryau, tatkathaṃ
kāraṇānivṛttau sāmānyaviśeṣanivṛttisiddhiriti|
kiṃ ca yatkāraṇarahitaṃ tannāsti yathā
gaganakamalam| kāraṇarahitau ca
sāmānyaviśeṣāviti vivakṣitam| kiṃ vā
yatkāraṇarahitaṃ tanna jāyate iti yathā gaganaṃ
kāraṇarahitau ca sāmānyaviśeṣāviti| nādyaḥ,
sāmānyaviśeṣasiddhau
dharmigrāhakapramāṇābādhādasiddhāvāśrayāsiddheḥ|
nāstīti ca kimanyonyābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ sādhyate
pradhvaṃsapratiyogitvaṃ vā,
arthakriyākāritvābhāvo vā, janakatvābhāvo vā,
prameyatvābhāvo vā, abhāvatvaṃ vā|

nādyaḥ, siddhasādhanāt|
sāmānyaviśeṣayorapyanyonyābhāvapratiyogitvāt|
ghaṭaḥ sāmānyaviśeṣau na bhavatīti svīkārāt|
na dvitīyaḥ, arthāntaragrastatvāt|
sāmānyaviśeṣayoḥ pradhvaṃsasiddhāvapi
svarūpānavalābhā[pā]t, gaganādau vyabhicārācca|
na tṛtīyaḥ, siddhasādhanādeva ghaṭe gotve
viśeṣau na sta iti svīkārāt| na caturthaḥ,
arthakriyāviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyāt|
janakatvāparaparyāyakāritvābhāvasiddhyā
caritārthatvāt| na pañcamaḥ,
arthāntarātsāmānyaviśaṣayorajanakatvena
svarūpānapalāpāt| na ṣaṣṭhaḥ,
aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt| prameyatvasya
kevalānvayitvāt|
sāmānyaviśeṣāpramitāvāśrayāsiddheśca,
pramitau ca vyāghātāt| nāpi saptamaḥ,
abhāvābhāvasya bhāvatvapakṣe siddhasādhanāt|
sāmānyaviśeṣayorapi
svātyantābhāvādyabhāvatvāt| vyatiriktatvapakṣe
cābhāvatvaṃ kiṃ
dravyādiṣaṭ[ḍ]lakṣaṇarahitaṃ[tatvaṃ]
vivakṣitam, niṣedhatvaṃ vā| nādyaḥ,
sāmānyaviśeṣayoḥ
sāmānyaviśeṣalakṣaṇarahitatvasādhane
bādhavyāghātayordurvāratvāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
sāmānyaviśeṣayoḥ bhāvatvenaiva siddhe
dharmigrāhakapramāṇavirodhāt|

kiṃ ca sāmānyaśabdena
kimanyavyāvṛttirvivakṣitā, tadvyatiriktatvaṃ vā|
nādyaḥ, bhāvāśrayāsiddheḥ| viśeṣaśabdena ca
kimekaikaniṣṭho vivakṣitaḥ, guṇakarmavyatirikta
ekaikaniṣṭho vā, guṇakarmābhāvavyatirikta
ekaikaniṣṭho vā| nādyaḥ, rūpādīnāmapi
dharmitve bādhāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
prāgabhāvādīnāmapi dharmitve siddhasādhanāt| na
tṛtīyaḥ, bādhavyāghātāpatteriti|

na dvitīyaḥ, siddhasādhanāt|
sāmānyaviśeṣayorajanyatvasvīkārāt| nāpi
kāryavyāvṛttau kāraṇaṃ vyāvartate|
dhūmavyāvṛttāvapi vahnerapyavyāvṛtteḥ| kiṃ ca
yatkāryarahitaṃ nāsti, yathā gaganakamalamiti
vivakṣitam, kiṃ vā yatkāryarahitaṃ
tadastīti vyāghātaḥ, kāryajanakasyaiva
sattvaśabdārthatvāditi| nādyaḥ, pūrvoktadoṣāt,
antyamuktau vyabhicārāt| dṛṣṭāntasya
sādhyavikalatvācca| gaganakamalasyāpi
sattvātsaṃyogādijanakatvācca| taṭākavartina eva
kamalasya gaganasaṃsṛṣṭayā pratīyamānasya
gaganakamalaśabdārthatvāt|
asatkhyāternirākariṣyamāṇatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
kāryajanakatvasya sattvaśabdārthatvānaṅgīkārāt|
sattājāterajātirūpasya vā sattvasya vā
prameyatvasya vā sattvaśabdārthatvāt| tatra ca
vyāghātābhāvāt|

etacca sūtradvayaṃ
sāmānyaviśeṣaviṣayasambādhako
bhāvopalakṣaṇārtham| tenānyo na
viśeṣabādhakādipūrvavannirākāryāṇi|
tadyathā---gotvādisāmānyaṃ kiṃ sarvagatam,
piṇḍasarvagataṃ vā| ādye stambhādāvapi
gotvādāvapi gotvādyupalabdhiprasaṅgaḥ| dvitīye
sarvatrotpannaśābaleyādīnāṃ gotvasya
sambandhābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ| pūrvaṃ
tatrāvidyamānasya tatrotpannena gopiṇḍena
sambandhābhāvāt| gopiṇḍāntarādāgatya
tatrotpannena gopiṇḍena sambadhyate iti cet---na,
jāterniṣkriyatvāt| yatra yatra gopi te tatra
gotvamasti na sarvatreti cet---na, sarvatra
gopiṇḍādarthasambhavāditi|
tasmātsarvasarvagatapiṇḍasarvagatatvayorasambhavānnāsti
sāmānyamiti tadayuktaṃ sarvagatatvam| yadi
sarvamūrtaiḥ saṃyogaḥ tanneṣyata eva
nirguṇatvena saṃyogāsambhavāt| sarvāśritatvaṃ
vā| tadā sarvagopiṇḍāśritatvaṃ
gotvajātyabhyupagamaḥ| na tu piṇḍāśritamiti
stambhādau tadabhāvasya pratyakṣasiddhatvāt|
sādhakapramāṇābhāvācca| pūrvaṃ tatrāpi
śritamiti cet---na, pūrvaṃ tatrāvidyamānānāmapi
rūpādīnāmiva|
tatrotpannapiṇḍāśritatvasambhavāt|
paścādanutpannasya pūrvaṃ tatrāvidyamānasya
kathaṃ tatrotpannapiṇḍāśritatvamiti na tau
sahotpannau tatrāśritadravyavadupapatteḥ, tatsaha
paścāccānutpannasya tadā vidyamānasya ca kathaṃ
tatrotpannapiṇḍāśritatvamiti cet---kathamiti| kiṃ
tatrāśritatve pramāṇapraśnaḥ kāraṇapraśno vā|
nādyaḥ, pakṣasyaiva pramāṇatvāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
tatrāśritatvasya janyatvena kāraṇe .anapekṣatvāt|

etena tatrāvidyamānasya kathaṃ
tatrotpannapiṇḍasambandha iti nirastam| samavāyasya
pratyakṣānumānādyanyatarapramāṇasiddhatvāt|
ajanyatvena kāraṇānapekṣatvācceti|
yatsambhavatvāccānutpannaṃ tatrāvidyamānaṃ tanna
tatrotpannapiṇḍāśritam, iti vyāptisiddhiriti
cet---na, kiṃ gotvaṃ sāsnādimatpiṇḍāśritaṃ
tena saha paścāccānutpannatve sati tadutpatteḥ
pūrvaṃ tatrāvidyamānatvādityanumānaṃ
vivakṣitam, kiṃ vā yadi gotvaṃ tena
sambhavatvāccānutpannatve sati tadutpatteḥ pūrvaṃ
tatrāvidyamānādeḥ karmaviparyayaḥ syāt|
vidyamānatvaparyavasāyitve pratyakṣabādhāt|
paścātsahotpattiparyavasāyitve
jāteranāditvagrāhakapramāṇabādhāt| etadvyakteḥ
pūrvaṃ gotvaṃ kutrāśritamiti
cet---prācīnagovyaktyantara iti brūmaḥ| pūrvaṃ
kvāśritamiti cet---na, tatpūrvotpannagopiṇḍāntara
ityanādiḥ panthāḥ| pralaye sarvatra gopiṇḍocchede
kutrāśritamiti cet---ekabrahmāṇḍapralaye
brahmāṇḍāntaravartigopiṇḍeṣviti brūmaḥ|
sarvabrahmāṇḍapralaye kutrāśritamiti cet---na
kvaciditi| grahaṇaṃ [evaṃ] tarhi tadānīṃ
nityamanekasamavetamiti
lakṣaṇābhāvājjātitvānupapattiriti cet---na,
tadānīṃ nityatve satyanekasamavetatvasya
jātilakṣaṇatvānaṅgīkārāt| nityatve
satyanekasamavetatvasya ca
pūrvamapyanekasamavetatvenopapatteriti mā bhūt||

sāmānyaviśeṣayorbādhakaṃ kimityapekṣāyāṃ
sūtram---

sāmānyaṃ viśeṣa iti buddhyape[kṣami]ti|| 1-2-3||

buddhiṃ pramāṇatayāpekṣata iti buddhyapekṣamiti|
sāmānyaṃ viśeṣa ityevamapi
sambuddhyapekṣamityarthaḥ| tathā cāyaṃ
gaurityādikānuvṛttākārā sākṣātkāriṇī
buddhiḥ sāmānye pramāṇam| ayaṃ
paramāṇurguṇakarmavyatiriktaitanmātraniṣṭhabhāvavāniti
sākṣātkāriṇī buddhiḥ parameśvarādiniṣṭhā
viśeṣe pramāṇamiti| sākṣātkāriṇī buddhiḥ
pramitirna tu pramāṇamiti cet---na, pramiterevātra
pramāṇatvena vivakṣitatvāt| sāmānyaṃ viśeṣa
iti vyavahāramātram| na tvevaṃ
buddhirastītyatrāpīdamevottaraṃ sāmānyaṃ
viśeṣa iti tu buddhyapekṣamti| sāmānyaṃ viśeṣa
iti vyavaharaṇaṃ buddhyapekṣaṃ
buddhijanyamanvayavyatirekābhyāṃ
vyavahārabuddhijanyatvāvadhāraṇāt| anyathā
kumbhādivyavahārātkumbhādijñānamapi na
sidhyediti|

nanu sāmānyaviśeṣaviṣayā nirvikālpikā
buddhirasiddhā savikalpikā te
pramāṇamityatrāpīdamevottaraṃ sāmānyaṃ viśeṣa
iti buddhyapekṣamiti [1-2-3] asti
tāvatsāmānyādigocaraṃ savikalpakaṃ gaurityādi
tacca nirvikalpakamapekṣate, tajjanyatvāt| na hi
viśeṣaṇādigocaraṃ nirvikalpakamantareṇa
viśiṣṭaviṣayaṃ savikalpakamudeti
viśeṣaṇādijñānajanyatvādviśiṣṭajñānasya|
tasmātsāmānyaviśeṣayoḥ pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇamiti
sūtrārthaḥ| yadā sāmānyaṃ viśeṣa iti sūtreṇa
sāmānyaviśeṣasadbhāvo[ve] .anumānaṃ
vyutpādyate, tathā ca sāmānyaṃ viśeṣa iti
jñānaṃ liṅgagocarabuddhyapekṣamiti
sūtrārthamāhuḥ| na tāvatsāmānye .ayaṃ gauriti
pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇam| parovartivyaktigocaratvāt|
tarhīdaṃ gauriti jñānaṃ vyaktyantare na syāditi
cet---na, satyamidaṃ vyaktyantare na jāyate eva|
tatra gauriti jñānasyānyatvāt| vyaktyantare
jāyamānaṃ gauriti jñānaṃ gotve pramāṇamiti
cet---na, tasya vyaktyantarālambanatvopapatteḥ|
gaurgaurityanuvṛttaṃ jñānaṃ gotve pramāṇamiti
cet---na, jñānavyaktīnāṃ
parasparavyāvṛttatvenānuvṛttatvābhāvāt|
anubhūtasya ca gojñānatvasya pramāṇābhāvāt| na
cānuvṛtte gojñānatve pramāṇamiti
anuvṛttapratyayasya pūrvoktanyāyena niṣedhāt| astu
vā gaurgauriti anuvṛttapratyayāt| anyathāpi na
gotvasiddhiḥ|
gotvavyavahārasthāpakasāsnātvādetadupapatteḥ|
na ca vyavasthāpakamupādāya jātinirākaraṇe
sarvajātyucchedāpattiḥ, iṣṭatvāt| astu vā
gaurgaurityanuvṛttapratyayasyāgovyāvṛttiranuvṛttālambanatvamiti|
nāpyanumānam, gotvādivyāptaliṅgānavagamāt|
sāsnādimattvaṃ liṅgamiti cet---na, tasya
gotva(a)siddhau tadvyāptatvānavagamāt| etena
govyavahāro liṅgamiti nirastam| govyavahāro
vyavahartavyajñānanibandhana iti sādhyata iti
cet---na, taddharmagotvādisiddherarthānna
govyaktijñānena siddhasādhanāt|
govyaktivyatiriktajñānanibandhana iti sādhyata iti
cet---na, govyaktijñānasyāpi
govyaktivyatiriktatadgatarūpādigocaratvena
siddhasādhanasya tadavasthatvāt|
govyaktitaddharmavyatiriktajñānanibandhana iti
sādhyata iti cet---na,
taddharmagotvādisiddherarthāntaratvādgovyaktijñānasya
govyaktitaddharmavyatiriktendriyasannikṛṣṭagotvavyaktyavayavabhūtalādiviṣayatvena
siddhasādhanasya tadavasthatvācceti|

tadayuktam| ayaṃ gaurityādisākṣātkārasya
gotvādijātisādhakatvāt| tathā hi yathāyaṃ śukla
ityādisākṣātkāraḥ purovartiviśeṣaṇatvena
śuklādīnviṣayīkaroti, tathāyaṃ
gaurityādisākṣātkāraḥ purovartiviśeṣaṇatvena
gotvādīnapi| vyaktimātrālambanatve ayamityeva
syāt| na tvayaṃ gauriti| anyathā
vyaktiviśeṣaṇatvena śuklatvādirapi na sidhyet|
purovartivyatiriktaśiklādirapi neṣyata eva|
aśuklādivyāvṛttirevāyaṃ śukla ityādau
viśeṣaṇamiti cet---na, śuklādi
numānayo tathā viśeṣaṇatvena
gotvasiddheragovyāvṛttirevātra viśeṣaṇamiti
cet---na, vyāvṛtteranirukteḥ| anyonyābhāvo
vyāvṛttiriti cet---abhāvasya anirukteḥ|
dravyādiṣallakṣaṇarahite
bhāvarūpajātyabhāve tallakṣaṇasyāsambhave
tadrahitatvasyāsambhavitvāt| abhāvarūpajāti
vṛttau sutarāṃ sambhavāt| bhāvarūpajātisvīkāre
ca jātisiddheḥ| [bhāvaniṣedho .abhā]va iti
cet---na, niṣedhatvasyānirukteḥ| pratiyogino
.agorajñāne .api tannirūpakasya gotvasyābādhitvena
pratibhāsācca| anyathā gopiṇḍāderapi
niṣedhatvāpatteḥ| paramāṇvatirikto gopiṇḍo
iti cet---na, paramāṇvāderapi niṣedhatvāpatteḥ| kiṃ ca
kimekaikāgovyāvṛttiḥ
gotvaṃ sarvāgovyāvṛttirvā| nādyaḥ,
ekaikāgovyāvṛtterago.antare .api vidyamānatve .api
tatrāpi gauriti pratyayaprasaṅgāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
sarvavyaktyanyasyāgocarāprasiddhatvena
tadvyāvṛtterapyaprasiddhatvāt| na tṛtīyaḥ,
yatkiṃcidgovyaktyanyatvena śābaleyāderapyagotvena
tadvyāvṛttirahitatvena gotvābhāvaprasaṅgāt| na
caturthaḥ,
sarvagopratītimantareṇāgopratītyanupapattāvagovyāvṛtterapi
pratyetumaśakyatvāt, gotvānaṅgīkāre ca
gopiṇḍasya nirvaktumaśakyatvāt| anaṅgīkāre ca
kiṃ gotvajātiḥ, agovyāvṛttirvā| nādyaḥ,
jātisiddheḥ| na dvitīyaḥ, parasparāśrayāt|
agovyāvṛttiviśiṣṭagosiddhiriti| na ca
sāsnādikameva gaurityatra viśeṣaṇam,
asāsnādāvapi gauriti pratyayāt| sāsnādimattvāt|
gavaye gaurityapratyayācca| sāsnādimattvaṃ ca
kimetatsāsnāvattvaṃ
sāsnādijātimattvaṃ vā| nādyaḥ,
ekasāsnāvirahiṇi gopiṇḍāntare gauriti
pratyayānudayaprasaṅgāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
sāsnāvattvajātereva siddheḥ| astu
sāsnāvattvamiyaṃ sāsneti
pratyayasyānanyathāsiddhatvāt| gotvaṃ tu na
sidhyati| gauriti pratyayasya ca
sāsnāvattvenānyathāsiddheriti cet---na,
saugatasyāpasiddhantatvāt| tena
sāsnāvattvajāterapyanaṅgīkārāt| anyaṃ prati
lūnasnāne .api gauriti pratyayadarśanena
sāsnāvatyapi gavaye gauriti pratyayādarśanena ca
sāsnāvattadatiriktagotvasiddheḥ|
vakṣyamāṇarītyā
sāsnāvanmātraniṣṭhajātyanumānāt|
nāpyavayavasaṃsthānaviśeṣa eva gaurityatra
viśeṣaṇam| gavayādivyāvṛttasya
sakalagopiṇḍānugatasya
gotvātiriktasyāpyavayavasaṃsthānaviśeṣasyānirukteḥ|
avayavasaṃsthānaviśeṣasyāvayavasaṃyogaviśeṣatvenāvayavaniṣṭhasya
piṇḍaviśeṣaṇatvāsambhavācca|
avayavasaṃsthānaviśeṣasya
caitadgopiṇḍaniṣṭhasya gopiṇḍāntare .asambhavena
tatra gauriti pratyayābhāvaprasaṅgāt| anugatasya
cāvayavasaṃsthānaviśeṣatvasya jāteḥ
saugatairanaṅgīkārāt| tadvyāvṛtteśca
pūrvavanniṣedhāt| gaurgaurityanuvṛttapratyayācca
gotvasiddheḥ| anyathā vyāvṛttavastuni ca
pratyayasyānuvṛttatvāsambhavaḥ|
anuvṛttavastuviṣayatvasyānuvṛttatvasya sambhavāt|
na cānuvṛttavastupratyayo .asiddhaḥ| ayaṃ gaurayaṃ
gaurityādyanuvṛttavastuvyavahāreṇa tatsiddheḥ|
anyathā vyāvṛttavastuvyavahāreṇa
vyāvṛttavastupratyayasiddhau vyāvṛttasyāpi
vastunā vilayāt|

na cānuvṛttavastusiddhāvātmāśrayaḥ,
anuvṛttavastupratyayasya
jātiviśeṣaṇatvānaṅgīkārāt|
anuvṛttavastupratyayādvānuvṛttavastusiddhirityatrātmāśrayatvāsambhavācca|
agovyāvṛttigaurgaurityanuvṛttipratyayālambanamiti
cet---na, gotvābhāvenopagṛhītagopiṇḍāpratītau
tadvyāvṛttyupagṛhītau gopiṇḍāpratītau
govyāvṛtteḥ pratyetumaśakyatvāditi, anumānācca
jātisiddheḥ| tathā cāyaṃ gopiṇḍa
etanniṣṭhābhāvatvarahitaitanniṣṭho
nityatvarahitānetanniṣṭhatvāt, meyatvāt,
ghaṭavaditi| na cātra samavāye
siddhasādhanamarthāntaraṃ vā, tasyānāśritatvāt|
āśritatve vā sambandharahitatvena
samavāyarhitatvena vā sādṛśyaviśeṣaṇāt| na
cātropādhipratirodhasambhavaḥ, kevalānvayitvāditi|
yadvāyaṃ gauḥ
samavāyikāraṇarahitānetanniṣṭhanityabhāvavān,
meyatvāt, dravyatvācca, gaga[na]vat| na cāsya
jātyādau vyabhicāraḥ, sarvatrāṅgīkārāt| na
cānayornityatvamupādhiḥ, pakṣetaratvāt|
gaganādyanityarahitoktasādhyavadanyat, meyatvāt, iti
siddhe prakṛtasādhyavatyanitye
sādhyavyāpakatvabhaṅgācca,
upādheradūṣanatvācca| yadvā idānīmutpanno gauḥ
samavāyatvarahitaciravinaṣṭagoniṣṭhābhāvavān,
bhāvāśrayatvādityādi|

nanu
bhāvatvadravyādiṣallakṣaṇānyameyatvaṃtattvajātisiddheḥ
pūrvamasiddhamityaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇateti
cet---atra[a]bhāvo .ayaṃ bhāvo .ayamiti
pratītisiddhabhāvatvavato bhāvaśabdārthatvāt| na
cātra ghaṭādau vyabhicāraḥ, tatrāpi
dravatvādeḥ sattvāt| sidhyatu jātimātram,
gotvāsiddhiḥ kathamiti cet---ayaṃ gaurayaṃ gauriti
pratyakṣādeveti gṛhāṇa|

viṣṇumiśrāstu
punarayaṃ
sāsnāvānetanniṣṭhābhāvatvarahitaitanniṣṭhasāsnāvānvyāvṛttatvarahitaitanniṣṭhāsāsnāvadvattitvarahita
etanniṣṭhānityatvarahitavān, meyatvāt,
ghaṭavaditi gotvasādhanamāhuḥ| tanna,
sāsnāvadgovayavasādhāraṇajātisiddhasya
caritārthatvena gotvāsādhakatvāt| ayaṃ
gavayasāsnāvilakṣaṇasāsnāvānetanniṣṭhābhāvatvarahitaitanniṣṭhagavayasāsnāvilakṣaṇasāsnāvadvyāvṛttarahitaitanniṣṭhagavayasāsnāvilakṣaṇasāsnāvattvānākrāntavṛttitvarahitaitanniṣṭhānityatvarahitavān,
bhāvatvāt, ghaṭavaditi gotvasiddhiriti cet---na,
asya lūnasāsnāgopiṇḍavyāvṛttajātisādhakatvena
gotvāsādhakatvāt|
gavayasāsnāvilakṣaṇasāsnāvanmātraniṣṭhaṃ
lūnasāsnāvadgopiṇḍaṃ vyāvṛttaṃ sāmānyaṃ
gotvam, taccānena sidhyati cet---lūnasāsnāgopiṇḍe
.api gotvasya bādhitānubhavasiddhatvāt| tatra
gotvānubhava eva nāstīti cet---na, gauriti
vyavahāreṇa tatsiddheḥ|
gosādṛśyāllāṅgulādimayādgauṇo .ayaṃ
govyavahāro
.agnisādṛśyātpaiṅgalyādivāgnivyavahāro
māṇavaka iti cet---na, mukhye sādhakābhāvāt|
bhāve vāstu gotvasiddhirasmādevānumānāditi| evaṃ
mahiṣītvāśvatvaghaṭatvādijātayaḥ sattvānneyā
iti|

nanu sidhyatu jātiḥ, viśeṣāstu na sidhyanti
pramāṇābhāvāt| nityadravyāṇi
vyāvartakadharmavanti, dravyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi
cet---na, dravyatvādinā siddhasādhanāt| na
dravyavyāvartakadharmavanti iti
[pṛthi]vītvādinā

siddhasādhanāt| na pṛthivyādiparamāṇavaḥ
pṛthivyādivyāvartakadharmavanta iti sādhyata
iti pākajarūpādiḥ
siddhasādhanātkāryapṛthivyāṃ teṣāmabhāvāt|
apārthivaparamāṇu vartakadharmavāniti
sādhyata iti cet---na,
śuklapārthivaparamāṇupakṣīkaraṇe śukla
rtakatvāt śuklapārthivaparamāṇuḥ
śiklapārthivaparamāṇu sādhyata iti cet---na,
śuklataraśuklatamarūparahitaśuklapārthivaparamāṇupakṣīkaraṇe
śuklatvarahitaśuklarūpeṇa siddhasādhanāt|
śuklataraśuklatamatvarahitaśuklarūpavānpārthivaparamāṇuḥ
śuklataraśuklatamatva vartakadharmavāniti
sādhyata iti cet---na,
kālāntarotpannaitannikṛṣṭakṛṣṇarūpādibhiḥ
siddhasādhanāt| ekasminneva paramāṇau kālabhedena
pākajakṛṣṇarūpādisvīkārāt| idānīṃ
śuklataraśuklatamatvarahitatvaśuklarūpapārthivaparamāṇuridānīṃ
śuklataraśuklatamatvarahitaśuklarūpavānpārthivaparamāṇuvyāvartakadharmavāniti
sādhyata iti cet---na, rasagandhasparśādiviśeṣaiḥ
siddhasādhanāt| ayaṃ
pārthivaparamāṇuretatsamānaguṇapārthivaparamāṇuvyāvartakadharmavāniti
sādhya iti cet---na, guṇānāṃ samānatvaṃ kiṃ
sajātīyasattvaṃ vijātīyatvābhāvo vā| nādyaḥ,
etanniṣṭhaśuklarūpādibhiḥ siddhasādhanāt|
teṣāmapi
śuklarūpādijātīyakṛṣṇarūpādimatpārthivaparamāṇuvyāvartakatvāt|
na dvitīyaḥ, evaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇoḥ
utkṣepaṇavato

dharmitve
evaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇoranutkṣepaṇādimataśca
vyāvartakadharmasādhana utkṣepaṇādinaiva
siddhasādhanāt|
utkṣepaṇādimattvenānvitaivaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇuranutkṣepaṇādimattvenāniścitaivaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇorvyāvartakadharmavattvasādhane
cotkṣepaṇādinārthāntaraprasaṅgāt|
utkṣepaṇavato
evaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇoranutkṣepaṇavata
evaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇorvyāvartakadharmavattvasādhane
ca yadi dvyaṇukavato dharmitvaṃ dvyaṇukarahitācca
vyāvartakadharmavattvaṃ sādhyate tadā dvyaṇukena
siddhasādhanam|

atha
dvyaṇukavattvenāniścitasyaivaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇordharmitvaṃ
dvyaṇukavattvenāniścitaivaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇośca
vyāvartakadharmavattvaṃ sādhyate| tadā
dvyaṇukenārthāntaram, dvyaṇukavata
evaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇordharmitvaṃ
dvyaṇukavataivaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇośca
vyāvartakadharmavattvaṃ sādhyate| tadāpi
yadvikṣepaṇava[d]dvyaṇukavata
evaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇordharmitvamanutkṣepaṇādivadevaṃvidhadvyaṇukavataḥ
pārthivaparamāṇośca vyāvartakadharma(vat)tvaṃ
sādhyate, tadotkṣepaṇavatā dvyaṇukena
siddhasādhanam,
evaṃvidhatvenāniścitasyaivaṃvidhatvenāniścitādvyāvartakadharmavattvasādhane
cotkṣepaṇavatā dvyaṇukenārthāntaram|
athotkṣepaṇavaddvyaṇukaivaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇordharmitvamutkṣepaṇavaddvyaṇukavataścaivaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇorvyāvartakadharmavattvaṃ
sādhyate, tadāpi yathārabdhatryaṇukadvyaṇukavata
evaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇordharmitvamanārabdhatryaṇukadvyaṇukavataścaivaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇorvyāvartakadharmavattvaṃ
sādhyate, tadārabdhatryaṇukadvyaṇukena
siddhasādhanam| aniścitaivaṃvidhasya
pārthivaparamāṇoraniścitaivaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇuvyāvartakadharmavattvasādhane
ca
vidhapārthivaparamāṇoretaddvyaṇukavataivaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇorvyāvartakadharmavattvaṃ
sādhyate| tarhi
atītānāgataivaṃvidhapārthivaparamāṇvantaraitanniṣṭhadvyaṇukena
siddhasādhanam| athāyaṃ
paramāṇuretanniṣṭhajātirahita
tvānadhikaraṇedānīntanaitanniṣṭhaguṇakarmāvṛttijātimattvarahitedānīntanaguṇakarmāśrayedānīntanaitatpratidvyaṇukādhikaraṇedānīntanavyāvartakābhāvāśraya
iti sādhyate, tadapi na, evaṃvidhavyāvartakatvaṃ
kimevaṃvidhavyāvartakatvamevaṃvidhavyāvṛttyutpādakatvaṃ
vā evaṃvidhavyāvṛttijñāpakatvaṃ vā
evaṃvidhavyāvṛttisākṣātkārajanakatvaṃ vā|

nādyaḥ etatparamāṇuniṣṭharūpādibhiḥ
siddhasādhanāt|

dvitīye .aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt|
vyāvṛtterajanyatvena tadutpādakasyāprasiddheḥ|

na tṛtīyaḥ prameyatvādibhiḥ siddhasādhanāt|
ayaṃ
paramāṇurevaṃvidhavyāvṛttimānprameyatvādityādibhiḥ
evaṃvidhavyāvṛtteranumānāt|
etatparamāṇuniṣṭharūpādibhiśca siddhasādhanāt|
ayaṃ paramāṇuritarebhyo bhidyate etasmādrūpādeḥ|
na yadevaṃ na tadevam, yathā ghaṭa ityanumānācca|

na caturthaḥ, etanniṣṭharūpādibhiḥ siddhasādhanāt| teṣāmapi
yoginiṣṭhasvaviśiṣṭaparamāṇusākṣātkārajanakatvāt,
viśiṣṭasākṣātkāre viśeṣaṇātyantābhāvavato vyāvṛtteḥ
sphuraṇāt| etatparamāṇurūpādayo
naivaṃvidhaparamāṇuvyāvartakāḥ evaṃvidharūpāditvāt,
evaṃvidhaparamāṇvantararūpādivaditi cet---na, dṛṣṭāntasya
sādhyavikalatvāt|
evaṃvidhaparamāṇurūpāderapyevaṃvidhaparamāṇvantaravyāvartakatvasvīkārāt|
etatparamāṇurūpādayo naivaṃvidharūpādi tato vyāvartakāḥ,
evaṃvidharūpāditvāt, evaṃvidharūpādivaditi cet---na,
evaṃvidharūpādimata etadvyāvartakasya
aprasiddhyaitadabhāvasyāprasiddhatvenāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt|
viśeṣaṇādavyāpyasiddheḥ| rūpādīnāṃ bhavatā
vyāvartakatvāniṣedhāt|

ayaṃ
paramāṇuretanniṣṭhadravyaguṇakarmatvarahitaivaṃvidhaparamāṇuvyāvartakadharmavāniti
sādhyata iti cet---na,
etanmātraniṣṭhaitadguṇakarmaprāgabhāvapradhvaṃsādibhiḥ
siddhasādhanārthāntarayordurvāratvāt|

ayaṃ
paramāṇuretanniṣṭhadravyaguṇakarmatvarahitaivaṃvidhaparamāṇuvyāvartakabhāvavāniti
sādhyate iti cet---na, etatparamāṇuniṣṭharūpādisamavāyena
siddhasādhanārthāntarayoranyatarasya durvāratvāt|
samavāyasyaikatvena sarvatra sattvāt|
naivaṃvidhaparamāṇuvyāvartakatvamiti cet---na,
etatparamāṇurūpādisamavāyasyaivaṃvidhaparamāṇvantaravyāvartakatvasiddhyāsya
caritārthatvenārthāntarasya tadavasthatvāt|
evaṃvidhaparamāṇnantarāvyāvṛttatve .api
samavāyasyaivaṃvidhaparamāṇvantaravyāvṛttisākṣātkārajanakatvāsambhavācca|
samavāyavyatiriktatvena sādhyaṃ viśeṣaṇīyamiti cet---na,
samavāyavyatiriktatvena tadrūpādimattvenārthāntaraprasaṅgāt|
etadrūpādyāśrayatvamapyabhāva eveti bhāvagrahaṇena nirastamiti
cet---na, bhāvatvaṃ kiṃ vidhitvaṃ
dravyādiṣallakṣaṇāyatamatvaṃ vā| ādya etadrūpādimattvasyāpi
bhāvatvameveti durvāramarthāntaratvam,
pratiyogijñānamantareṇāpi etadrūpādimānityetadrūpādimattvaya
vidhitvena pratyayāt| dvitīye punaraprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam,
viśeṣaṇāsiddhau tallakṣaṇasyāpyaprasiddhatvena
dravyādiṣallakṣaṇānyatamatvasyāpyasiddheḥ|

mīmāṃsakādīnprati śaktijñānādibhirarthāntaraprasaṅgācca
vyāvartakagrahaṇayorvaiyarthyāt| etena samānajātiguṇakāryāḥ
paramāṇavo muktātmānatva[śca] mitho vyāvartakadharmasamavāyino
dravyatvāt| ghaṭavadityādayaḥ paryogā nirastāḥ|

vyāghātācca| ekaikaniṣṭhānāṃ
parasparavyāvartakatvāsambhavāt| parasparaṃ vyāvartakānāṃ
caikaikaparamāṇvādiniṣṭhatvānupapatteḥ| mithaḥśabdasya ca
parasparābhidhāyakatvāt| mithaḥśabdārthasya ca
paramāṇughaṭapaṭādyanugatasya nirvaktumaśakyatvāt|
vyāvṛttasya vyāvartakatvānupapatteḥ
pakṣīkṛtaparamāṇurūpādibhiḥ siddhasādhanāt| teṣāmapi mitho
ghaṭaparamāṇuvyāvartakatvāt| ghaṭādiniṣṭharūpādibhiḥ
siddhasādhanācca|

paramāṇūnāmapi ghaṭādiniṣṭharūpādisamavāyāśrayatvena
tatsamavāyitvāt| samavāyasyaikatvādvyāvartakadharmāśrayatvaṃ
sādhyate| na ca ghaṭādirūpādayaḥ paramāṇvāśrayā iti na
siddhasādhanamiti cet---na,
parasparavyāvartakābhāvasiddhyā[dhyā]sya
caritārthatvenārthāntaraprasaṅgāt|

yadapyanumānam---mano mano.antaravyāvṛttaṃ niḥsāmānyaṃ
samavāyidravyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi, tadapi na yuktam|
mano.antaravyāvṛttamanastvāvāntarajātisiddhyā[dhyā]rthāntaratvāt|
mano.antaravyāvṛttapadena
pakṣīkṛtamanovyatiriktasakalamanovyāvṛttatvaṃ vivakṣitamiti

cet---na, tathā sati pakṣīkṛtamanovyatiriktamanaḥsu dravyatvasya
vyabhicārāt| pakṣīkṛtamanovyatiriktatvaṃ
hetorviśeṣaṇamādeyamiti cet---na, amanastvena sopādhitvāt|

na cāsya pakṣetaratā| mano.antaravyāvṛttapadena
pakṣīkṛtamanovyatiriktasakalamanuvyāpyādvipakṣādapi
vyāvṛttatvāt| manastvena pratipakṣatvācca| mano
.anekamanovṛttitvarahita[m] niḥsāmānyasamavāyidravyatvāt,
ghaṭavadityapi na yuktam| ghaṭatvena siddhasādhanāt|
tasyāpi mano.amanovṛttirahitatvādanekamanovṛttirahitatvācca
sāmānyarahitatvācca pakṣīkṛtamanaḥsamavāyitvācca
samavāyasyaikatvānmano.amanovṛttitva sāmānyāśraya iti
sādhyata iti cet---na,
manoniṣṭhakarmavegaparatvāparatvaprāgabhāvasiddhasādhanāt|
 na dattottaratvācca| tasya bhāvatvasiddhyāpyasya
caritārthatvenārthāntarasya tadavasthatvācceti|

yadapyanumānaṃ vimataṃ tiriktasamavāyidravyatvāt,
ghaṭavaditi tva dvyaṇukairaeva siddhasādhanāt|
ākāśakāladigātmanāṃ ca dharmitve
karmavyatiriktaviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyāt| teṣu karmābhāvena
vyāvartyābhāvāt| manasaśca dharmitve
dvyaṇukatvasamavāyikāraṇasiddhyā asya
caritārthatvenārthāntaragrastatvāt| manaso .api
guṇakarmasāmānyātiriktaghaṭasamavāyitvena siddhasādhanācca|

samavāyasyaikatvādguṇakarmasāmānyātiriktaghaṭaniṣṭhasamavāyāśrayatvāt|
guṇakarmasāmānyātiriktabhāvāśrayatvaṃ sādhyata iti cet---na,
arthāntarasya tadavasthatvāt| dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatvācca|
ghaṭe guṇakarmasāmānyātiriktabhāvābhāvādghaṭāvayavānāṃ
ghaṭānāśritatvāt| udakādayo ghaṭāśritāḥ santīti ghaṭo
dṛṣṭānta iti cet---tarhi gaganādiṣvapi paramāṇvādayaḥ
santīti siddhasādhanaṃ durvāram|

gaganādisaṃyuktāḥ paramāṇvādayo na tadāśritā iti cet---na,
gaganaṃ dravyāśrayo dravyatvāditi dravyāśrayatvasiddheḥ|
mūrtānāṃ gaganāśritatvāsiddhyāpyasya
caritārthatvenārthāntarasya durvāratvācca|
dravyaguṇakarmasāmānyātiriktāśraya iti sādhyata iti cet---na,
samavāyena siddhasādhanāt| samavāyo .anāśrita iti cet---na,
tasya samavāyaniṣṭhatayaiva siddheḥ
nityadravyābhāvasambandhatvādināśritatvānumānācca
samavāyasyāśritatvasiddhyāpyasya caritārthatvenārthāntarasya
durvāratvācca| dravyaguṇakarmasāmānyasamavāyātiriktāśrayaḥ
sādhyata iti cet---na, abhāvena siddhasādhanāt|
dravyaguṇakarmasāmānyasamavāyātiriktabhāvāśrayasyāśraya iti
sādhyata iti cet---na, bhāṭṭaṃ
prati śaktijñātatābhyāṃ prābhākaraṃ
prati

śaktisaṃkhyāsādṛśyakramaiḥ siddhasādhanāt|
tadvyatiriktaviśeṣaṇopādāne
cāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāttadasiddhau
tadvyatiriktatvasyāpyaprasiddheḥ, bhāṭṭādīn
pratyasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvācca| teṣāmapi
dravyaguṇakarmasāmānyasamavāyaśaktyādivyatiriktabhāvaprasiddheḥ|
bhāṭṭānāṃ
samavāyāsiddhau tadvyatiriktatvāsiddheśca| ākāśaṃ
dravyaguṇajātivyatiriktabhāvāśraya iti sādhyate| tathā ca
nāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatvaṃ

vā| ghaṭe karmaṇa eva tathāvidhasya prasiddhatvāditi cet---na,
anutpannakarmavinaṣṭaghaṭādau
vyabhicārāt| anutpannakarmavyatiriktatvena heturiśeṣaṇīya iti
cet---na, karmatvena sopādhitvāt| na cāsya pakṣetaratvam|
anutpannakarmavinaṣṭaghaṭādervipakṣādapi vyāvṛtteḥ
pūrvoktasiddhasādhanādestadavasthatvācca|

yadapyanumānam----paramamahattvaṃ
guṇatvavyatiriktāvṛttibhāvasamānādhikaraṇam, mahattvāt,
ghaṭamahattvavaditi tadapyayuktam| śaktijñātatādibhiḥ
bhātṭādīn
prati siddhasādhanāt|
avyāvṛttākāśaniṣṭhadravyatvāvāntarajātisiddhyāpi
caritārthatvenārthāntaragrastatvā[t] sāmānyavat|
sāmānyātiriktasamavāyisamavāyitvāt|
dāyāṃśasamavāyāṃśavyāvṛttatvasiddhyāpyasya
caritārthatvenārthāntaragrastatvācca| nityaparimāṇatvābhāvena
sopādhitvācca|

na cāsya pakṣetaratā, paramāṇuparimāṇādvipakṣādapi
vyāvṛtteḥ| upādhiviraheṇa satpratipakṣatvācceti
pūrvoktasādhyaśatabhāvaviśeṣaṇasya doṣagrastatvācceti|

yadapyanumānam---ayaṃ
paramāṇuretanniṣṭhasāmānyavatsāmānyātiriktasamavāyī,
samavāyitvāt, dravyatvācca, ghaṭavaditi, tadapi na| gaganādinā
siddhasādhanāt tasyaitanniṣṭhasāmānyavatsāmānyātiriktatvāt|
asya ca paramāṇostanniṣṭhasamavāyāśrayatvāt,
samavāyasyaikatvāt,
etanniṣṭhasāmānyavatsāmānyātiriktāśrayatvaṃ sādhyata iti
cet---na, gaganādinaiva siddhasādhanāt| prābhākaraiḥ
sāmānyarahitatvasarvamūrtāśrayatvasvīkārāt|
sarvamūrtasamānadeśatvāditi praśastapādavacanāt|
gaganādayo dravyāśritāḥ, prameyatvādityādipramāṇatvācca|
ākāśakāladigātmavyatiriktatvena sādhyaṃ viśeṣyata iti
cet---na, etanniṣṭhābhāvena siddhasādhanāt| bhāvatvamapi
sādhyaviśeṣaṇamiti cet---na, prābhākaraṃ
prati rūpādinā siddhasādhanāt| tanmate gaganarūpādīnāṃ
niḥsāmānyatvāt| [etat]
etatparamāṇuniṣṭhasāmānyatvavadasiddhau
tadvyatiriktatvasādhyaprasiddhatvenāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvācca
bhāvaviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyācca|
prabhākareṇābhāvāṅgīkārāttaṃ
prati bhāvaviśeṣaṇaṃ nopādīyate iti cet---na, bhāvābhāvena
siddhasādhanādyāpatteḥ|
etatparamāṇuniṣṭhasāmānyaprasiddheretatparamāṇuniṣṭhasāmānyavadvyatiriktatvasya
prābhākaraṃ
pratyasiddhatvācca| bhāṭṭaṃ
pratyasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvācca| tena
dharmadharmiṇorabhedamabhyupagacchatā kasyacidapi
paramāṇuniṣṭhatvānaṅgīkāre tadvirahasyāpyanaṅgīkārāt|
sāmānyarūpādīnāmapyabhedamabhyupagacchantaṃ prati
tadvattasyāpyaprasiddhatvāt| vyarthaviśeṣaṇatvācca|
samyagdharmadharmiṇorabhedamicchatā etanniṣṭhasāmānyavato
vyāvartyasyānaṅgīkārāditi|

yadya[da]pyanumānaṃ
sattāsāmānyasamavāyātiriktābhāvavyāvṛttājātitvāt,
gotvavaditi, tadapi na, pūrvoktabhāvaniruktiprayuktadoṣāt

savyāvṛttitvena sopādhitvācca|

na ca prameyatvādivipakṣāṇāṃ
vyāvṛttatvenaivāvyāvṛttatvādviśeṣaṇasya
vyāvartyavipakṣābhāvādasya pakṣetaratvamiti vācyam|
bhāvatvādeḥ vipakṣasya vyāvartyasya sattvāt|
kiṃ ca satteti

kiṃ vyāvṛttatvarahitā jātirvivakṣitā
dravyaguṇakarmamātravartinī vā| nādyaḥ, vyāghātāt| na
dvitīyaḥ, vyāvṛttatvarahitajātau vyabhicārāt| vyāvṛttā
jātitvāditi cet---bhāvatve vyabhicārāt|

bhāvatvaṃ na jātiriti cet---na, gotvatulyanyāyena tasya
jātitvasiddheḥ| ghaṭo
ghaṭavṛttibhāvavyāvṛttatvarahitajātimān, bhāvatvāt,
ghaṭādivaditi bhāvamātraniṣṭhajātisiddheśca
dravyaguṇakarmamātrajātitvāditi cet---na, dṛṣṭāntasya
sādhanavikalatvāpatteḥ| gotvasya
dravyaguṇakarmavṛttitvābhāvāddravyaguṇakarmātiriktāvṛttijātitvavaditi
cet---na, vṛttatvena sopādhitvāt| na cāsya pakṣetaratvamm,
bhāvatvādeḥ vipakṣādvyāvṛttatvāt| arthāvyāvṛttatvena
satpratipakṣatvācceti
dravyaguṇakarmasāmānyasamavāyātiriktabhāvāśrayaḥ
tasmātsādhakābhāvādbādhakasadbhāvācca
na viśeṣasiddhiriti| tadayuktam| kālaḥ
saṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogatvavibhāgatvarahitasamavāyāśrayaḥ,
dravyatvāt, ghaṭavadityādeḥ sattvāt| na cātra
samavāyābhāvābhyāṃ siddhasādhanamarthāntaraṃ vā
samavāyādhārapadena tayorvyāvṛtteḥ| nāpi
kāryatvādikamupādhiḥ| paramāṇvādau sādhyāvyāpakatvāditi|

idamasmānnirādarśi viśeṣāṇāṃ samarthanam|

pūrvavaiśeṣikācāryamaryādātikramaṃ vinā||

yadā tu samavāyābhāvayorapi
samavāyādhāratvamityasmanmatasvīkārastadā
pūrvānumānānāṃ samavāyābhyāṃ
siddhasādhanārthāntaragrastatvāt| anyathā prayujyate kālaḥ
saṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgatvarahitajātitvasamavāyitvarahitajātitvasamavāyitvarahitabhāvāśrayaḥ,
dravyatvāt, ghaṭavat| na ca ghaṭādinā
siddhasādhanamarthāntaraṃ vā, ghaṭādeḥ
kālāśrayatvaniṣedhāt|

aniṣedhe vā dravyatvarahitatvena sādhyaviśeṣaṇāt| nāpi
saṃkhyāśrayatvaparimāṇāśrayatvādinā
siddhasādhanamarthāntaraṃ vā, tasyābhāvatvena
bhāvatvaviśeṣaṇena nirastatvādbhāvāśrayatvarahitatvena
sādhyāviśeṣaṇāt| na ca saṃkhyāśrayatveṣvāśrayatvābhāvaṃ
pṛthaktveṣu pṛthaktvavatparatveṣu
paratvavadaparatveṣvaparatvavatsaṃkhyādyāśrayeṣvāśrayatvasyānubhavasiddhatvāt|

nāpi śaktijñātatādibhiḥ siddhasādhanam, teṣāmapi siddhatvāt|
bhāṭṭādīnāṃ
tāni siddhānīti cet---na, vinā pramāṇaṃ teṣāmapyasiddheḥ|
arthāpattyādibhiḥ tāni teṣāṃ siddhānīti cet---na,
anyathopapattyādibhisteṣāṃ dṛṣṭatvāt| nāpi
śaktyādibhirarthāntaramapi [tā, vi]kalpāsahatvāt| tathā hi kiṃ
viśeṣalakṣaṇarahitapramāṇāntarasiddhaśaktyādinedaṃ
caritārthamityarthāntaram| kiṃ vā
viśeṣalakṣaṇarahitapramāṇāntaraprasiddhaśaktyādinedaṃ
caritārthatvaṃ kiṃ vā yadanena sidhyati tasya
śaktyādisaṃjñetyarthāntaratvam| nādyaḥ,
śaktyādigocarapramāṇaniṣedhāt|
na dvitīyaḥ, viśeṣalakṣaṇarahitasyāsmādasiddheḥ| na
tṛtīyaḥ, avivādāt| puruṣecchayā yasya kasyāpi padasya yatra
kvacana saṃketasambhavāt| bhāṭṭādīn
prati śaktyādinā siddhasādhanārthāntaranirāsārthatvaṃ vā
sādhye pratyakṣaviśeṣaṇopādānāt| bhāṭṭādibhiḥ
śakterapratyakṣatvābhyupagamāt|
kālaniṣṭhasaṃkhāsādṛśyakramāṇāmapi
prābhākareṇāpratyakṣatvābhyupagamāt|
bhāṭṭenāpi
jñānaphalasaṃvedanamātmaniṣṭhamabhyupagamyatayā
jñātatānaṅgīkārāttayoḥ
siddhasādhanārthāntarayorvaktumaśakyatvāt| jñātatāvādināpi
bhāṭṭena
kālādīndriyatvavādinā
tanniṣṭhajñātatāyāḥ
pratyakṣatvānabhyupagamātkālapratyakṣavādināpi
tanniṣṭhajñātatā bhāṭṭaṃ
prati siddhasādhanārthāntaraparihārārthaṃ sādhye
jñānājanyaviśeṣaṇopādānāt
jñānājanyaviśiṣṭoktasādhyasya
ghaṭādāvabhāvāddravyatvavyabhicārātsādhyavikalaśca| ghaṭo
jñāna[ta] iti cet---na, nityatvena
hetuviśeṣaṇātparamāṇvādeśca
dṛṣṭāntatvenopādānātkālaniṣṭhajñānajanyatvarahitatvena vā
sādhyaviśeṣaṇāt| evaṃvidhasādhyasya vā ghaṭādāvapi
sattvāt| bhāṭṭaṃ prati
siddhasādhanānivārakatvācca manaso vā dharmitvenopādānāt|
tasya ca bhāṭṭamate
.api apratyakṣatvena tanniṣṭhajñātatāyā apratyakṣāyā
apratyakṣaviśeṣaṇatvena nirāsāt| Jñātatālakṣaṇarahitatvena
vā sādhyaviśeṣaṇāt| na ca jñātatāsiddhau
tallakṣaṇasyāsiddhatvena
tadrahitasyāpyaprasiddheraprasiddhaviśeṣaṇateti vācyam| jñāto
.ayamityanubhavabalena jñātatāyā ubhayavādisiddhatvāt|

tasmājjñānaviṣayatvatadatiriktayoreva vivādātsaṃkhyāyāśca
saṃkhyātvarahitatvena nirāsāt, sādṛśyasya ca jātitve
jātitvarahitatvena nirākaraṇāt, bhūyo.avayavasāmānyagotve ca
tasya samavāyalakṣaṇarahitatvena nirākaraṇāt, kāle sambhavācca
tadarthāntaratve ca bhāvaviśeṣaṇatvena nivartanāt, bhāvatve vā
tasya sarvatraikatve sādṛśyavyatiriktatvena sādhyaviśeṣaṇāt,
bhinnatve ca sādṛśyalakṣaṇarahitatvena sādhyaviśeṣaṇāt,
kramasya ca deśakṛtasyāsarvagataniyamasya sarvagate kāle
.asambhavāt, kālakṛtasya kāryaniyatasyākārye .akāle
.asambhavāt| sambhave vā tannivṛttyarthaṃ
kramalakṣaṇarahitatvena sādhyaviśeṣaṇātsādṛśyakramayoḥ
padārthāntarayoḥ siddhau tallakṣaṇasyāprasiddhatvena
taddvitvarahitasyāsiddhaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇateti cet---na, tayoḥ
padārthāntaratāsiddhāvapi sadṛśo .ayaṃ
kramavānayamityanubhavasiddhatvena tallakṣaṇasiddhau
tadrahitatvasyāpi siddheḥ| evameva
svamatakalpitapadārthāntarāvaṣṭambhapravṛttatīrthikāntaravyutpāditasiddhasādhanārthāntarayornirāsāt|

yadvā kālaḥ
kālaniṣṭhaikatvaikapṛthaktva[tattva]atiriktānekaniṣṭharahitakālaikagocarajñānajanyatvarahitāśrayatvarahitapratyakṣābhāvāśrayaḥ,
dravyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi| na cātra mīmāṃsakaṃ
prati śaktyāditvasiddhasādhanamarthāntaraṃ vā| ekatvasaṃkhyayā
siddhasādhanāderekatvavyatiriktatvena nirāsāt,
anekasaṃkhyāsādṛśyakramānekaniṣṭhajñātatādibhiḥ

siddhasādhanārthāntarayośca kālaikagocarajñānājanyatvena
nirāsāt| śakyāsiddhasādhanayoḥ pratyakṣatvena
nirāsānmīmāṃsakānāṃ śakterapratyakṣatvaṃ
viśeṣaṇamayuktam|

vaiśeṣikamate sarvapadārthānāṃ
pratyakṣatvenāpratyakṣavyāvartya[a]bhāvena viphalatvāditi
cet---na, mīmāṃsakaṃ prati siddhasādhanatānivṛttyaiva
phalavattvāt| nāpi
kālagatikatvāśrayakālagataikapṛthaktvāśrayatvakālagatamahattvāśrayairasiddhasādhanamarthāntaratvaṃ
vā teṣāmabhāvatvena bhāvaviśeṣaṇena
nirāsātkālagataikatvātyantābhāvātyantābhāvasyaiva
kālagataikatvāśrayatvāt|
kālagataikapṛthaktvātyantābhāvātyantābhāvasya ca
kālagatamahattvātyantābhāvātyantābhāvasya ca
kālagatamahattvāśrayatvāt|
kālagatikatvaikapṛthaktvamahattvāśrayatvātirikta
samavāyitvarahitabhāvāśrayaḥ dravyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi| na cātra
karmaṇā siddhasādhanamarthāntaraṃ vā, tasya
mūrtamātrāśrayasya kāle .asambhavāt| nāpi
śaktyādibhirarthāntaraṃ teṣāmasiddhau
kṛterarthāntarasyāpyasiddhatvāt| siddhau vā teṣāṃ
dravyādāvantarbhāvena dravyatvarahitādinā nirāsāditi|

yadvā kālaḥ kālaniṣṭhārthatvarahitābhāvāśrayaḥ
dravyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi| yadvā kālaḥ
kālaniṣṭhārthatvarahitānekaniṣṭhatvarahitābhāvāśrayaḥ
dravyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi| na cātrārthatvāsiddhau
tadrahitatvāsiddheraprasiddhaviśeṣaṇateti vācyam|
dravyaguṇakarmamātraniṣṭhajāterubhayavādisiddhatvāt| tasya
evārthasiddhaśabdapravṛttinimittatvāditi| na cāmīṣāṃ
dravyatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitadigākāśa

vyatiriktābhāvatvādirupādhiḥ
pakṣetaratvāt, sādhyāvyāpakatvācca| bhavanmate
śaktijñātatādīnāṃ guṇādiṣvapi sambhavāt|
upādheradūṣaṇatvācceti| nāpi
dravyatvāvāntarajātimattvarahitadigākāśarahitadigākāśavyatiriktatvena
satpratipakṣatvam| adravyatvena sopādhitvāditi| evaṃ
digādisarvanityadravyapakṣīkaraṇe
tattanniṣṭhaguṇatvadravyatvajātitvadravyapakṣīkaraṇe
tattanniṣṭhaguṇatvadrava[vya]tvajātitvasamavāyitvarahitābhāvāśrayatādikaṃ
dravyatvameva sādhanīyamiti|

na ca samavāyasyaikatvena samavāyatvajāte[ra][riti] siddhau
tadrahitasyāpyasiddheraprasiddhaviśeṣaṇateti vācyam|
samavāyalakṣaṇasyaiva samavāyatvasyobhayavādisiddhatvāt|
tasmādanvayavyatirekiṇā nityadravyeṣu viśeṣasiddhiriti
kevalānvayinā ca| tathā hi kālo
guṇatvajātitvadravyatvasamavāyatvarahitakālaniṣṭhābhāvatvarahitāśrayo
meyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi|
kāla ekatvaikapṛthaktvamahattvātiriktānekaniṣṭhatvarahitakālaniṣṭhābhāvatvarahitāśrayo meyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi|
na ca rūpādiṣvanekaniṣṭhatvarahitaivaṃdharmābhāvavānkevalānvayitvānupapattiḥ|
tattadrūpādyanyatvātyantābhāvādeḥ sambhavāt|
kālasaṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgatvarahitajātitvasamavāyatvadravyatvarahitakālaniṣṭhābhāvatvarahitāśrayo
meyatvāt, ghaṭavat| kālaḥ
saṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgatvarahitajātitvasamavāyatvadravyatvarahitakālaniṣṭhābhāvarahitāśrayo

meyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi|

na ca
saṃkhyādipañcaguṇamātrāśrayāyadiśyevaṃ[?]vidhasādhyābhāvānna
pakṣatvāsambhavaḥ| kālānyonyābhāvādimādāya
sapakṣatvopapatteḥ| viśeṣamādāya pakṣatulyopapatteśca|

na cātra śaktyādibhiḥ siddhasādhanam, arthāntaraṃ vā,
pūrvoktādeva nyāyāt| na cātropādhipratipakṣasambhavaḥ,
kevalānvayitvāditi| na cāyaṃ ghaṭaḥ etadanyādi
tvaniṣṭhatvarahitanityaniṣṭhaguṇakramatvarahitakiṃcinniṣṭhādviṣṭhabhāvātyantābhāvavān,
meyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi|

yadvā
kālaniṣṭhāmahattvamakālaniṣṭhaikatvaikapṛthaktvānyakālaikaniṣṭhabhāvavān,
meyatvāt, ghaṭavadityādikevalavyatirekiṇā ca| tathā hi kālaḥ
kāryatvarahitaguṇatvarahitasamavāyatvarahitabhāvavān,
nityadravyatvāt, na yadevaṃ na tadevaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ,
anityadravyatvāttasmāttatheti| na cātra paramāṇubhiḥ
siddhasādhanamarthāntaraṃ vā, teṣāṃ
nityadravyatvenānāśritatvāt| yadvā kālo
mūrtatvarahitaguṇatvakarmatvajātitvasamavāyatvarahitabhāvabhāvavān,
vibhutvāt, na yadevaṃ na tadevaṃ yathā ghaṭo vibhuścāyaṃ
tasmāttatheti|

na caivaṃvidhabhāvaprasiddhe .aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇateti vācyam|
gaganādereva tathāvidhasya prasiddhatvāt| na
caivaṃvidhabhāvatvasyāpyaprasiddheraprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvamityapi
sādhanīyaḥ| tasya sādhyapakṣasaṃsargarūpasya
sarvānumāneṣvaprasiddhasyaiva siddheḥ| na caivaṃ
vidyamāne[na]sapakṣatvena kevalavyatirekibhaṅgaḥ|
evaṃvidhabhāvasiddhāvapi tasyānāśritatvena tadvataḥ
sapakṣasyāsambhavāt| sādhanāt evaṃvidhamātrasādhane ca
bādhābhāvāt| tasyāśritasyāpi sambhavāt| na ca kāla
evaṃvidhabhāvavān, meyatvāditi satpratipakṣatvam|
evaṃvidhabhāvātyantābhāvasādhane gaganātyantābhāvena
siddhasādhanarthāntarayoranyataratvāpatteḥ|
evaṃvidhasarvabhāvātyantābhāvaprasādhane ca
sarvaśabdenānyāśritaivaṃvidhasarvabhāvavivakṣāyāṃ
siddhasādhanādestadavasthāt|
āśritānāśritataivaṃvidhasarvabhāvavivakṣayā
prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt|

āśritaivaṃvidhabhāvāsiddhau tadabhāvasyāprasiddhatvāt|
evaṃvidhasarvabhāvapratītau tadabhāvasyāvagantumaśakyatvācca|
evaṃvidhabhāvatvātyantābhāvasādhane
.apyaprasiddhaviśeṣa[ṇa]tvāt| viśeṣasiddheḥ
pūrvamevaṃvidhabhāvatvasyāprasiddhatvena
tadatyantābhāvasyāprasiddhatvāt| ayaṃ ghaṭo

bhūtalanirguṇādaya(?) ityādau ca
ghaṭavattvaguṇavattvādiniṣedhau
sarvaghaṭasarvaguṇaniṣedhopapatteḥ pratibandha[a]navakāśāt|
ekasminnapi ghaṭe guṇe ca
ghaṭavattvaguṇavattvāderatyantābhāvāsambhavāt| na ca
bhāvatvaṃ sādhyaṃ evaṃvidhabhāvatvāśrayo vraktirvā|
nādyaḥ, kālādau naivaṃvidhabhāvatvāsiddhāvaviśeṣāsiddheḥ|
na dvitīyaḥ, yāvatpramitatvākāśādyanyatamāśritavyaktisādhane
bādhāt| tadatiriktavyaktisādhane cāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāditi|
tadayuktam|
āśritatvānāśritatvaviśeṣaṇānavacchinnaivaṃvidhabhāvatvāśrayavyaktisādhanāt|
tasyāścāprasiddhatvāt| tathāpi daivagatyā kimasāvāśritā
utā[nā]śritā| ubhayathāpi pūrvadoṣa[apattiḥ|]
disādhakapramāṇāt| pramāṇāntarād[ddhi] nādyaḥ,

gaganādisādhakapramāṇena
gaganādivyatiriktatathāvidhavyaktyasiddheḥ| na dvitīyaḥ,
tasyābhāvāditi| grāhakapratyakṣeṇa
tadviśeṣaṇatvapratyāsattyāḥ sarvatadvyaktigrāhakeṇa
tadagrahaṇāt| tathāvidhabhāvatvaṃ
nityadravyavyatiriktavṛttitvātiriktaniṣṭhatvarahitavat, meyatvāt,
ghaṭavadityādinānumānena ca tatsiddheḥ| viśeṣaṇasādhanenaiva
viśeṣa[a]siddheḥ, mūlānumānavaiyarthyamiti cet---na,
tasyaivaṃvidhenāśritānityadravyaivaṃvidhānāmāśritābhāvāntarasiddhyā
vā caritārthasya viśeṣasādhakānmūlānumānasya ca
tathāvidhanityadravyāśritasādhakatvena viśeṣasādhakatvāt|

astu vā viśeṣaṇasādhakādeva viśeṣasiddhiḥ| tathāpi siddhaṃ
naḥ samīhitam||

kiṃcitsāmānyaviśeṣaḥ, kiṃcidveśeṣa evetyuktam| tatkiṃ
sāmānyamevetyapekṣāyāṃ sūtram---

bhāva iti|| 1-2-4||
iha sa[bhū]sattāyāmityasya dhātorbhāvotpannaṃ yattasya rūpaṃ
tena bhāvo bhavanaṃ satteti yāvat| yathā
dravyaguṇakarmasvanuvṛttitvātsattā sāmānyaṃ tathā
sāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyebhyo vyāvṛttatvādviśeṣo .api|
tasmātsāmānyaviśeṣaḥ sattā, na tu sāmānyameveti| tanna,
sattāyāḥ sāmānyebhyo vyāvṛttatvā[da]naṅgīkārāt|
dharmagrāhakapratyakṣānumānābhyāṃ sāmānyādivyāvṛttatvarahitāyāśca sattāyāśca siddheḥ|
vyāvṛttau vā sāmānyādibhyaḥ sattā, tathāpi nāsau
sāmānyaviśeṣaḥ| na dravyaguṇakarmānyatāvyāvṛttatvasya
jātimadvyāvṛttatvasya vā viśeṣatvasya sattāyā asambhavāt|
vyāvṛttatvamātrasya cātra viśeṣatvenāvivakṣitatvāt|
tasmātsāmānyameva sattā| na mūrtitve sati
dravyaguṇakarmānyatamavyāvṛttatvarahitatvasyānuvṛttatve sati
jātijātimadvyāvṛttatvarahitatvasya vā
kevalasāmānyalakṣaṇatvāditi|

evaṃ sati prameyatvādikamapi kevalasāmānyaṃ syāt| bādhaḥ
[bhāvaḥ?]| astu vā satyapi sāmānyaviśeṣa evānuvṛttatve sati
vyāvṛttatvasya sāmānyaviśeṣalakṣaṇatvāt| kathaṃ tarhi
sāmānyameveti pravādaḥ| na kathaṃcitsūtrakāreṇa sattā
sāmānyamevetyanabhidhānāt| bhāva iti sūtreṇa sattā
sāmānyamevetyanabhidhīyate iti cet---na, abhāva iti
sattābhidhāyino .arthasya kevalasāmānyarūpatānabhidhānāt| kiṃ
sāmānyamevetyākāṃkṣāyāmavatarato bhāva iti sūtrasya bhāvaḥ
sāmānyameveti tātparyamiti cet| kiṃ sāmānyaviśeṣa
ityākāṃkṣāyāmeva sūtrāvatārāt| kiṃ
sāmānyamevetyākāṃkṣāyā uttaramiti cet---na,
kiṃcitsāmānyamevetyākāṃkṣāyā evābhāvāt| bhāve vā
prameyatvādikamevetyuttaraṃ sūtrakārasyābhipretamiti
varṇanīyamiti|| 4||

yadā tu sattā sāmānyameva tadā kaḥ sāmānyaviśeṣa
ityākāṃkṣāyāṃ sūtram---

dravyatvaṃ guṇatvaṃ karmatvaṃ ca sāmānyāni viśeṣāśca||

iti| nanvanupapannametat, nityatve satyanekasamavetatvasya
sāmānyalakṣaṇatvāt|
anekaniṣṭhatvarahitasattānāśrayābhāvatvasya ca
viśeṣalakṣaṇatvāt, tayoścaikatrāsamāveśā[t|]
samāveśo[śe] vātivyāpakatvaprasaṅgāditi| tadayuktam|
dravyatvādau sāmānyalakṣaṇatve .api
viśeṣalakṣaṇābhāvādviśeṣapadaprayogasya ca
viśeṣasādṛśyavyāvṛttyarthatvaniścayatvena gauṇatvāt|

yadvā sāmānrapratiyogī nimittaṃ viśeṣapadaprayoge ca
vyāvṛttatvaṃ tadubhayaṃ dravyatvābhāva iti dravyādīnāṃ
sāmānyaviśeṣatvaṃ mukhyameveti| evaṃ tarhi
kāryadravyasaṃyogavibhāgānekasaṃkhyānekapṛthaktvānyapi
sāmānyādīni svarūpānu śeṣāḥ syuḥ, vyāvṛttatvāditi
cet---satyametat| ata eva sūtrakāreṇa kāryasāmānyaprakaraṇe
kāraṇadravyādīnāṃ sāmānyarūpatā vyudapādi| cakāro
.anuktasattārahitasamavāyavatpṛthivītvādirūpatvādyutkṣepaṇatvādisaṃgrahārthaḥ|
sattārahitasamavāya[sya?] evāsminnāhnike
pratipādyatvāditi|| 5||

yadi sattārahitasamavāyavatsarvaṃ sāmānyaviśeṣāpattiḥ,
[ viśeṣaḥ] tarhi antyaviśeṣā api sāmānyaviśeṣāḥ
syurityāśaṃkāparihārārthaṃ sūtram---

anyatrāntyebhyo viśeṣebhyaḥ|| 1-2-6||

iti| anyatreti
parivarta[rja]nenāntyaviśeṣavyatiriktasattārahitasamavāyavatsarvaṃ
sāmānyaviśeṣaḥ pratipādyata iti| kiṃ punarantyatvaṃ
viśeṣāṇām| ante bhavatvam| utpattivināśayorante vartata
ityantaśabdena nityadravyāṇyucyante| teṣu bhavantītyantyaśabdena
viśeṣā ucyante iti ātreyācāryāḥ|

yadvā anyeṣāṃ viśeṣāṇāmante .avasāne bhavantītyantyā
ucyante| tathā hi sattāpekṣayā dravyatvaṃ viśeṣaḥ|
tadapekṣayā pṛthivītvādi| tadapekṣayaikadravyaniṣṭho
viśeṣaḥ| na cāntyaviśeṣātiriktavyāvartakamasti,
tatrānuvṛttatvānupapatteḥ| tasmātsa eva viśeṣaṇāmante
bhavatītyantya ucyate iti|

yadvā ante sarvamuktikāle bhavantītyantyā

ucyante| tarhi[na hi] tadānīmātmākāśasya
cāntyaviśeṣātiriktavyāvartakamasti,
sāmānyaguṇānāmavyāvartakatvāt| viśeṣaguṇānāṃ ca
sarveṣāmasattvāditi|

yadvāntaśabdena kāla ucyate| tatra bhavatītyantya ucyate| na
hi kālamātravṛttirantyo vyāvartakaḥ sambhavati|
saṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgānāmavyāvartakatvāt|
anyathā
tadanusandhānakālādāvātmasaṃdehaviparyayānudayaprasaṅgāditi||

sattādravyatvaguṇatvakarmatvādau pramāṇavyutpādanārthaṃ
sūtram---

sa[di]ti yato dravyaguṇakarmasu sā satteti|| 1-2-7||

asti dravyamasti guṇo .asti karmeti pratyayaprayogau yato
viśeṣaṇādutpadyete sā sattā| upalakṣaṇaṃ caitat| tena
dravyamiti yataḥ pṛthivyādiṣu navasu taddravyatvam| guṇa iti yato
rūpādiṣu caturviṃśatiṣu tadguṇatvam| karmeti yata
utkṣepaṇādiṣu tatkarmatvam| evaṃ sarvāsu jātiṣu draṣṭavyam|
evaṃ yāvadāhnikasamāpti sarvo .api sattāgocaro .api
dravyatvādiṣvapi draṣṭavya iti|

nanvetadapyanupapannam| saditi
pratītiprayogayorvyaktisvarūpasamavāyaprameyatvādibhyo
vyutpatteranyathā sāmānyādau saditi prayogābhāvaprasaṅgāt|
tatra bhavadabhimatasattāvirahāt| sattāropāttatrāstīti cet---na,
prameyatvenābādhitasatyavyavahāropapattau tat
niṣpramāṇatvāt| na ca sattāyāmanumānaṃ pramāṇam|
[dravyaguṇa]karmāṇyekasāmānyavanti sāmānyavaditi cet---na,
dravyeṣu dravyatvena guṇeṣu guṇatvena karmasu karmatvena
siddhasādhanāt| dravyaguṇakarmasvekamabhinnaṃ sāmānyaṃ
sādhyate| na caivaṃ dravyatvaguṇatvakarmatvāni teṣāṃ
bhinnatvāditi cet---na,
dravyaguṇakarmaniṣṭhaikasāmānyavattvasādhane
prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt|
sattāsiddheḥ pūrvaṃ
dravyaguṇakarmaniṣṭhaikasāmānyāprasiddheḥ dravyaguṇakarmasu
sāmānyaṃ siddham| pakṣadharmatābalādekameva sidhyatīti
cet---na, pakṣadharmatāyāḥ
sādhyapakṣasaṃsaktātiriktāsādhakatvāt| dravyaguṇakarmasu
sāmānyaṃ sidhyatkalpanālāghavādekameva sidhyati|

kṣityādiṣvīśvaravaditi cet---na,
kalpanālāghavasyānumānasahakāritvānaṅgīkārāt| anyathā
pārtiva[tvā]di dvyaṇukatryaṇukādi
samavāyikāraṇāsamavāyikāraṇajanyāni
kāryatvādghaṭavadityādinā kalpanālāghavasahitena
pṛthivyādidvyaṇukeṣu
samavāyikāraṇāsamavāyikāraṇaikatvasiddhiprasaṅgāt|
ākāśādayaḥ saṃkhyāparimāṇapṛthaktvasaṃyogavibhāgavanto
dravyatvādityādinākāśakālādiṣu
saṃkhyāparimāṇādyekatvasiddhiprasaṅgācceti|

ākāśaṃ dravyatvātiriktajātimat, jātimattvāt dravyatvācca,
gotvavadityapi na|
sattāsādhakadravyatvāvāntaravibhutvādisiddhyāsya caritārthatve
vārthāntaragrastatvāt| dravyatvāvāntararahitatvena sādhyaṃ
viśeṣyata iti cet---na, godṛṣṭāntasya
sādhyavikalā[tvā]patteḥ| rūpādayo dṛṣṭāntā gavādayaśca
pakṣatulyā iti cet---na, teṣāṃ dravyatve
sādhanavikalatvātsādhanāntarapakṣe .api pramāṃ prati
sādhyavikalatvācca| tena kāryadravyavyatirikte
jātyanaṅgīkārāttimirāṇāṃ jñānatvasvīkārāt
jñānadṛṣṭānta iti cet---na, śālikamiśrādīnprati
sādhyavikalatvasya tadavasthāt| tairjñānatvānaṅgīkārāt|
rūpādayo jātimantaḥ sāmānyaviśeṣasamavāyātirekibhāvatvāt,
govaditi cet---na, dravyatvena sopādhitvāt|

prābhākareṇa
dravyatvaṃ nāśrīyata iti cet---na, tena guṇavattvādereva
dravyatvenāṅgīkārāt| vaiśeṣikādibhiśca
dravyatvajāteraupādhikatvena vaktuṃ śakyatvācca| kāryatvena
sopādhitvācca| na cāsya sādhanavyāpakatā|
sāmānyāvyatirekabhāvatvasādhanavato
nityarūpādervyāvṛttatvāt| na cānayoḥ pakṣetaratvam,
jātimattvarahitātsāmānyādervipakṣādapi vyāvṛttatvāt|
dravyatvaṃ jātivyāvṛttamadravyāvṛttitvāt, paṭavadityapi
jātimattvasiddheḥ| dravyatvasya sādhyāvyāpakateti cet---na,
jātimadvyāvṛttatve dravyavyāvṛttatvasyopādhitvāt|

na cāsya pakṣetaratā|
jātimadvyāvṛttatvarahitātprameyatvādervyāvṛttatvāt|
prameyatvādīnāmavyāvṛttatvenaiva vyāvṛttatvāt|
dravyatvaviśeṣaṇasya vyāvartyatvapakṣābhāvātpakṣetaratvamiti
cet---na, dravyatvaviśeṣaṇavyāvartyasya bhāvalakṣaṇādeḥ
sattvāttasyābhāvādvyāvṛttatvena
vyāvṛttatvaviśeṣaṇenāvyāpyavṛtterdravyāvyāvṛttatvena
satpratipakṣatvācceti| karma
goniṣṭhajātimatkāryabhāvatvādgovadityapi na,
sattāsādhakadravyatvādinā sopādhitvāt|

nāpi kāryamātraniṣṭhajātyārthāntara ttā
anityaguṇasamavetakarmaṇa dravyaniṣṭhajātimatī
anityasamavetatvāt govadityapi na yuktam|
anityasamavetamātraniṣṭhajātyārthāntaratvācca| dravyatvādinā
sopādhitvācceti| dvyaṇukādiṣu prābhākareṇa
svīkārāt| dravyatvasya sāmānyavyāpyatvabhaṅgādanupādhitvamiti
cet---na, sādhyavyāpakatvasya upādhilakṣaṇānantargatatvāt|
ghaṭo ghaṭaniṣṭhajātimatsamavāyikāraṇam,
samavāyikāraṇatvāt ghaṭāvayavatvavaditya
kāryābhāvamātraniṣṭhajātyā arthāntaratvāt| tasyā api
paṭatatsamavetakāryasamavetatvātsparśavattvena sopādhitvācca| na
cāsya sādhanavato .api gaganādervyāvṛttatvāditi|

[dravyavyaktisvarūpasamavāyaprameyatvādibhyo vyatiriktāyāḥ
sattāyā na pramāṇamiti cet] tadayuktam| ayaṃ ghaṭaḥ sanniti
pratyakṣāt ghaṭaniṣṭhasattāsiddheḥ|
na ceyametadghaṭasvarūpameva etanniṣṭhatvādrūpavat,
etadghaṭavirahiṇi ghaṭāntare .api sattvācca|

na ceyaṃ dravyaṃ guṇaḥ karma veti guṇavṛttitvāt| na ceyaṃ
viśeṣaḥ kāryavṛttitvāt,
yogīśvaretarapratyakṣatvavedyatvācca| na ceyaṃ samavāyaḥ na
ceyamabhāvaḥ, rūpādāviva sattāyā niṣedhatvābhāvasya
pratyakṣatvāt| tataḥ pariśeṣāt jātireva satteti|

anumānācca| ghaṭo ghaṭaniṣṭhavyāvṛttatvarahitajātimān,
jātimattvāt, ghaṭavat, ayaṃ ghaṭo
etanniṣṭhavyāvṛttatvarahitajātimān, jātimattvāt, ghaṭavaditi
vyāvṛttatvarahitajātisiddheḥ|
na cātra dravyatvaṃ kāryatvaṃ copādhiḥ, kevalānvayitvāt,
rūpādayo jātimanto meyatvāt, ghaṭavaditi rūpādau
sādhyavyāpakatvabhaṅgācca| na cātrāpyupādhisambhavaḥ|
kevalānvayitvādeva| na ca satpratipakṣatvam|
kevalānvayisādhyābhāvasyāprasiddhatvāt| yadā tu
dravyaguṇakarmānuvṛttyā[ttā] jātiḥ satteti pakṣaḥ, tadā
ghaṭo
ghaṭaniṣṭhadravyaguṇakarmavyāvṛttatvarahitajātyādivyāvṛttajātimat[mān]|
ayametanniṣṭhaguṇakarmavyāvṛttatvarahitajātyādivyāvṛttajātimān
jātimattvāt| ghaṭo
ghaṭaniṣṭhajātimadvyāvṛttatvarahitajātyādivyāvṛttajātimān,
jātimattvāt|
ayametanniṣṭhajātimadvyāvṛttatvarahitajātyādivyāvṛttajātimān
jātimattvāt ghaṭavaditi prayoktavyam| na cātra dravyatvakāryatve
upādhiḥ| rūpatvādijātimati rūpādau sādhyavyāpakatvāt|
rūpaṃ rūpaniṣṭhajātitvarahitāśrayo meyatvāditi rūpādau
jātimattvasiddheriti|

nanvastu sattā, dravyatvaṃ tu na sidhyati, mānābhāvāt| na
tāvadatra pratyakṣaṃ mānam, idaṃ dravyamityanubhavābhāvāt|
svātantryānubhava eva dravyatvānubhava iti cet---na,
svātantryānubhavo tadyanāśritatvānubhavaḥ sad[tadā]
ghaṭādāvasiddhaḥ| siddhau vā tasya bhrāntitvāt|
ghaṭādīnāmāśritatvāt| abhrāntitve vā anāśritatvenaiva tasya
caritārthasya dravyatvāt| tathāśritatvānālambano .anubhavaḥ
tadapi tasyāśritatvāsādhakatvāt vyavasthāpakatvāt
jñānādīnāṃ sāmānyaṃ jñānaṃ vyavasthāpakaṃ ca lakṣaṇam|
guṇavattvajñānānantaraṃ dravyatvaṃ pratyakṣeṇānubhūyate eva|
golakṣaṇasāsnāvattvajñānānantaramityagotvamiti cet---na,
guṇavattvānusandhānānantaramapi
dravyatvādanubhavadravatvadravyamitītyanugatavyavahārasya ca
guṇavattvānusandhāne ca upapatteḥ|

na caivaṃ sāsnāvattvānusandhānādeva gotvavyavahāropapatteḥ
go iti vācyam| lūnasāsne .api śāvaleyādau
gotvānubhavavyavahārayoranubhavasiddhatvāt|
saṃyogavibhāgādisamavāyikāraṇasyaitadvyaktiniṣṭhatve
vyaktyantare tadabhāvaprasaṅgāt| katipayavyaktiniṣṭhatve
tattaddharmigrāhakatayā dravyatvasiddhiriti cet---na, guṇatvena
tadupasaṃgrahopapatteḥ| upasaṃgrāhakāntareṇāpi dravyatvasyaiva
samavāyikāraṇatvasya katipayavyaktiniṣṭhatvopapatteḥ|
dravyatvavadupasaṃgrāhakāntarāpekṣāyāmanavasthānāt|

api ca kimanena dravyatve pratyakṣamupanyastamanumānaṃ vā|
tadupapādakastarko vā| nādyaḥ, uktottaratvāt| na dvitīyaḥ,
prayogānirūpaṇāt| na tṛtīyaḥ,
āpādyāpādaka[a]nirūpaṇātsaṃyogādijātīye pṛthivyādīnāṃ
samavāyikāraṇātirekārthamavadhāraṇīyam| na ca
tadekaikapṛthivyādivyaktīnāṃ tadabhāve .api
saṃyogādijātitvasya sambhavena vyatirekābhāvāt| nāpi
samuccitānāṃ pṛthivyādivyaktīnāṃ
samuccitapṛthivyādivyaktyantare kasyacidapi saṃyogajātīyasya
sambhavenānvayāsambhavāt| tasmātsaṃyogādijātīye
pṛthivyādīnāmanvayavyatirekasiddhaye dravyatvamaṅgīkartavyamiti
cet---na, guṇatvenaiva tadutpatteḥ|
saṃyogaviśeṣa eva dravyaviśeṣasyaiva samavāyikāraṇatvācca|
tasya ca jātiviśeṣamantareṇaivānvayavyatirekasambhavāt| anayāpi
bhaṅgyā dravyatve pratyakṣānumānatarkāvyutpādanācca
nānumānamanirūpaṇāt|

ākāśamaguṇavṛttijātimat, jātimattvāt, ghaṭavaditi cet---na,
vibhutvagaganatvādijātisiddhau arthānta[rā]nusparśavat
vṛttitvena sādhyaviśeṣaṇe
cendriyatvādijātisiddhāvarthāntarātsparśavattvena
sopādhitvācca| guṇavadvyāvṛttatvācca|
guṇavadvyāvṛttirahitaguṇāvṛttijātisādhane ca
siddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt|
guṇavṛttiguṇavadvyāvṛttitvarahitaguṇāvṛttijātisādhane ca
dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatvāt| karma dṛṣṭānta iti cet---na,
tasyāpi sādhyasādhanavikalatvāt| prābhākareṇa
karmaṇo .aṅgīkārāt|

idaṃ karmeti pratītiprayogayoḥ
saṃyogavibhāgāsamavāyikāraṇatvenopapatteḥ| kāryatvena
sopādhitvācca| sa kathaṃ
guṇavṛttikarmavṛttiguṇāvṛttijātisiddhāvarthāntarācceti|
karmāvṛttitvenāpi sādhyaviśeṣaṇe dṛṣṭāntasya
sādhyavikalatvāt| guṇakarmāvṛttijātiniṣṭhāvarthāntarācca|
guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitanirguṇāvṛttijātimattvaṃ
sādhyaṃ[dhya] ta iti cet---na,
asparśavatkāryapakvā[vattvā]bhyāṃ sopādhitvābhyāṃ
tadviraheṇa satpratipakṣatvācceti|
etenākāśaṃ sattāvyatiriktajātimat, sattāntarajātitvāt, guṇavattvādghaṭavadityādiprayuktamiti cet---evaṃ guṇatvādijātayo nirasanīyā iti|

tadayuktam| dravyatvādivyavasthāpakaguṇavattvādimati gaganādau
tadvṛttitadvyāvṛttatvarahitatadrahitāvṛttidravyatvādijātīnāmanumānātsiddheḥ|

tathā hyākāśaṃ
guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitaguṇakāraṇavṛttijātimat, jātimattvāt,
karmavaditi| na cātra sparśavattvamupādhiḥ| karmatvādimati
karmādau sādhyāvyāpakatvāt| nāpi rūpatvādimati rūpādau
sādhyavyāpakatvāt| asmanmate
ca kevalānvayitvena nirūpitatvāt|
jātāvapi jātitvasvīkārā| na cātropādhiviraheṇa
satpratipakṣatvamupādhiḥ sādhyavyāpakabhaṅgasthale
vyabhicārāt| kevalānvayitvācca|

na ca
guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitanirguṇāvṛttijātmānityetāvatātmāvṛttijāteradravyatvasiddheḥ|
prathamaviśeṣaṇāntargataguṇavadvṛttitvadvitīyaviśeṣaṇāntargataguṇavadguṇatvayorvaiyarthyamiti
vācyam| viśeṣaṇaprasiddhatvāt
guṇavadvyāvṛttitvarahitanirguṇatvādeva
guṇavadvṛttiguṇavadvyāvṛttarahitatvajāterguṇavṛttitvarahitatvādvā|
nādyaḥ,
guṇavadvṛttitvarahitajāternirguṇāvṛttitvāsambhavāt|
na dvitīyaḥ,
guṇavyāvṛttatvarahitanirguṇāvṛttijāterdravyatvātiriktāyā
asambhavāt| tasmādapi
dvitīyaviśeṣaṇāntargataguṇavattvamapi
sādhyaprasiddhyarthamupādeyameva na
caiva[ma]sambhavāt| me pi sādhyāprasiddhiḥ
guṇavadvṛttiguṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitaguṇavannirguṇāvṛttikarmatvādimati
sādhyaprasiddheḥ| guṇavadvyāvṛttatve .api
karmatvāvṛttitvarahitanirguṇāvṛttijātyanyanyā
(?) meyatvādghaṭavaditi tatsiddhiriti cet---na,
dravyatvasiddheḥ
pūrvamāśrayāsiddherevaṃvidhasādhyasiddherasambhavāt|
dravyatvasiddhyuttarakālīnaivaṃvidhasādhyavidhe[siddhe]śca
dravyatvānumityapratibandhakatvenādūṣaṇatvāt| na ca
guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitatvanirguṇāvṛttijātisiddhāvapi
dravyatvāsiddherarthāntaratā|
guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitanirguṇavṛttijātitvasya
dravyalakṣaṇatvena tatsiddhereva
dravyatvasiddhatvāt|

nanu nedaṃ dravyatvalakṣaṇam| dravyatvaṃ
guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitanirguṇāvṛttijātyanyatvaṃ
meyatvāt ghaṭavaditi
siddhāyāmatiriktajātāvatadvyāvartakatvāditi| na
dravyatvāsiddhau
dvitīyānumānasyāśrayāsiddha
guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitanirguṇāvṛttijātisiddhistu
durvārā| tena pūrvānumānasiddhā
guṇatvavyāvṛttatvarahitā nirguṇāvṛttijātyantā
meyatvād ghaṭavaditi siddhāyāṃ dvitīyajātāvapi
guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitanirguṇāvṛttijātirdravyatvam|
tarhi dvitīyānumānasiddhāyāṃ
jātau prakṛtadravyatvalakṣaṇasyātivyāptatvam|

atha dvitīyānumānasiddhajātidravyatvaṃ tarhi
prathamānumānasiddhāyāṃ jātau
prakṛtadravyatvalakṣaṇasyātivyāpakatvamiti nedaṃ
dravyatvalakṣaṇam| tenaitatsiddhāvapi na
dravyatvasiddhirit, tanna,
prathamadvitīyānumānasiddhajātyoruktadravyatvalakṣaṇatvāt|
dravyatvaṃ ca padavācyatvāt
dravyapravṛttinimittatvāt yadetaddravyagatatve
.apyarthagatatvābhāvenātivyāpakamanyathaikapṛthivyādivṛttitvena
pṛthivyādilakṣaṇānāmativyāpakatvāpatteḥ|
tasmādetallakṣaṇavatsiddhāvapi
yaddravyatvasiddhiriti
gatatvābhāvenātivyāpakatvamanyathaikapṛthivyanyasādhakasyaitanniṣṭhadravyāntaratvābādhakatvadoṣā[pā]yānna
tanniṣṭhadravyatvasādhakatvāvirodhitvāt|

anyathaitatparvataniṣṭhavahnisādhakasyaitatparvataniṣṭhavahnyantarāsādhakatvena
dṛṣṭatvaprasaṅgāt| dvitīyatṛtīyayostu sādhanasyaivottaram| na
cātra tulyavṛttitvaṃ doṣaḥ, tasya doṣatve pramāṇābhāvāt| na
ca dvitīyadravyatvaṃ na jātiḥ| na
caitatparvataniṣṭhavahnyantarāsambhavaḥ| etatparvataniṣṭho
vahniretatparvataniṣṭhavahnyanyo meyatvāt ghaṭavaditi siddhiḥ|
tatkiṃ dravyamanekamanekatvāt| kimanekasaṃsyā
ekatvābhāvāt| dravyatvānyonyābhāvo vā| prathame ne jātau
saṃkhyānaṅgīkārāt| dvitīyatṛtīyayostu bādha ityevottaram| na
cātra tulyavyaktitvaṃ doṣaḥ| tasya doṣatve pramāṇābhāvāt| na
ca dvitīyaṃ dravyatvaṃ na jātiḥ|
prathamavṛttidravyatvavyatiriktatve sati tattulyavyaktitvāt
guṇavattvavaditi vācyam| dharma pramāṇabādhāt| na
cānatiriktajātidvaye mānābhāvaḥ| anumānasyoktatvāt| na
coktenaiva dvayena dvitīyagotvāpattiḥ| astai bādhake
tasyāpīṣṭatvāt| sati tu tata evānāpatteḥ
guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitanirguṇāvṛttijātyanyatvarahitā,
tatatvāt (?)| na yadevaṃ na tadevaṃ, yathā ghaṭa iti|
dvitīyadravyasādhakasya satpratipakṣatvācca| na
cāsyāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam|
guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitanirguṇāvṛttijātyantatvam|
anyonyābhāvavyatiriktābhāvapratiyogi prameyatvāt ghaṭavaditi
viśeṣaṇasiddheriyaṃ
guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitanirguṇāvṛttijāti
(guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitanirguṇāvṛttijāti)
guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitanirguṇāvṛttijātyanyaniṣṭhatvarahitāśrayaḥ
meyatvāt ghaṭavaditi kevalānvayinā satpratipakṣatvācca|
evaṃvidhayetyevaṃvidhajātyanyaniṣṭhatvarahitaye[e]vaṃvidhajātiniṣṭhatvarahito
vā syāt evaṃvidhajātyanyaniṣṭhatvarahito vā| prathamaḥ pakṣo
vyāghātabādhābhyāṃ niruddhaḥ| dvitīyastu pakṣe[kṣaḥ]
tarhyeva syādyadi pakṣasyaivaṃvidhajātyanyatvaṃ na bhavet|
anyathā pakṣaniṣṭhasyaivaṃvidhajātyanyāniṣṭhatvena
tadrahitātvaṃ vyāghātāditi pakṣe
evaṃvidhajātyanyatvāsiddhiriti tato .arthāntarateti| etena
dvitīyagotvādisādhakāni nirastāni|

nanu mā bhūdguṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitanirguṇāvṛttijātitvasya
dravyalakṣaṇasyāvyāptirativyāvṛttitvāt, svarūpāsiddhistu
bhaviṣyati tatsādhakābhāvāt| tasyāprasiddhasya
prakṛtahetorasmādasiddheḥ| prakṛtahetuvyāpakasya tato .anyatvāt|
tadvyāpakatvācca| na cāsya pakṣadharmatābalātsiddhiḥ|
hetupakṣadharmatvasya
sādhyapakṣadharmatvātiriktāsādhakatvātkarmādau jātimattvasya
tadvyāpyatvabhaṅgāccāparyavasānāttatsiddhiriti nāvyāptiḥ|
pakṣadharmatātiriktasyāparyavasānasyānumānāṅgatve
pramāṇābhāvāditi|

na ca
prakṛtānumānasiddhaguṇavadvṛttiguṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitaguṇaṃ
guṇavadvṛttitvāt guṇavadvyāvṛttatvābhāvasiddhiḥ|
guṇavannirguṇavṛttitvarahitaguṇavadvṛttitvācca
nirguṇāvṛttitvasiddheḥ| jātitvasiddhiśca prakṛtānumānādeveti
dravyatvalakṣaṇasiddhiriti vācyam|
prathamānumānāddravyatvalakṣaṇasiddhau
tasmāddravyatvāsādhakatvavyavasthitiriti|

tadayuktam|
guṇavadguṇavṛttiguṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitaguṇavannirguṇāvṛttijātimattvasya
dravyatvalakṣaṇāt| tasya ca prakṛtānumānasiddhatvāt| na ca
vyarthaviśeṣaṇatvam, asiddhiparihārārthatvāt
tadasiddhestvayaivoktatvāt| asiddhiranumānasya dūṣaṇam, na
lakṣaṇasyeti cet---na, lakṣaṇasyāpi kevalavyatirekyanumānatvāt|
na cātra vyarthaviśeṣaṇatvamapi,
guṇavadvṛttiguṇavadvyāvṛttitvarahitasya
ghaṭādinivṛttyarthatvāt| guṇavannirguṇāvṛttitvasya
sattvānivṛttyarthatvāt| jātitvasya
guṇavattvādinivṛttyarthatvāt|
guṇavadvṛttiguṇavyāvṛttatvarahitetyatra guṇavadvṛttitvaṃ
vyartham| guṇavannirguṇavṛttitvarahitetyatrāpi guṇavadvṛttitvaṃ
vyarthamiti cet---na, tasya hetuviśeṣaṇatvābhāvāt,
guṇavadvṛttiguṇavadvyāvṛttatvābhāvāt,
bhāvaviśeṣaṇatvācca| guṇavadvyāvṛttatvābhāve ca
guṇavadvṛttitvasyāviśeṣaṇatvāt| tasya
hetuviśeṣaṇatvenānupādānāt| pratiyogino
.apyabhāvaviśeṣaṇatvāsambhavācca|
guṇavadvṛttiguṇavadvyāvṛttitvayoḥ hetuviśeṣaṇībhāvāt|
viśeṣādeva hetuviśeṣaṇatāhetuviśeṣaṇā
viśeṣaṇavaiyarthyaṃ doṣa iti cet---na, guṇavattvasya
hetuviśeṣaṇābhāvaviśeṣaṇavadvyāvṛttatvaviśeṣaṇaṃ
guṇavadvyāvṛttitvaṃ vyarthamiti cet---na, guṇavadvyāvṛttatvasya
hetuviśeṣaṇaguṇaguṇavadvṛttiguṇavadvyāvṛttatvāt
guṇavadvṛttigrahaṇamātrāt
guṇavadvyāvṛttatvagrahaṇamātratvāsambhava iti|

tasmānna kiṃcidvyarthamiti
guṇavyāvṛttatvarahitatvanirguṇāvṛttitvayoraparyavasānādeva
siddheśca| na cāparyavasānasyānumānāṅgatvam, dhūmatvena
parvataniṣṭhavadvahniviśeṣaṇāsiddhiprasaṅgāt| na ca
pakṣadharmatābalātsiddhiḥ hetupakṣadharmatvasya
sādhyapakṣadharmatvātiriktāsādhakatvasya tvayaivoktatvāt|
anyathā pakṣadharmatābalādeva
guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitatvanirguṇāvṛttitvajātitvānāṃ
dravyatvayorapi siddhiprasaṅgāditi| mā bhūdvā prathamānumānena
dravatvalakṣaṇadravyaśabdavācyatvāditi siddhiḥ|
dravyatvasiddhistu durvāraiva|

evaṃ tadanumānasiddhajātāveva
dravyatvalakṣaṇadravyatvaśabdavācyatvāderanumānāt| tathā iyaṃ
jātiḥ guṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitā
guṇavadvṛttiguṇavadvyāvṛttitvarahite sati
guṇavadvṛttitvāsattvācca| iyaṃ jātirguṇavadvṛttitvarahitā,
guṇavannirguṇāvṛttitve sati guṇavadvṛttitvāt, govaditi|
jātitvasya ca dharmigrāhakādeva siddheḥ| na ca
guṇavyāvṛttatvarahitatvāderekaikasyālakṣaṇatvādviśiṣṭasya
kenāpyasiddherlakṣaṇāsiddhiriti vācyam|
bhinnapramāṇavyāvṛttatvarahitatvanirguṇāvṛttitvājātitvānāṃ
dravyatvalakṣaṇatvāt| viśiṣṭasyāpi pratyakṣatvācceti|

astu dravyatvam, guṇatvaṃ tu neṣyate| rūparasādau guṇo .ayaṃ
guṇo .ayamiti pratyakṣābhāvāt|
guṇalakṣaṇādivyavasthāpakānusandhānānantarabhāvinatvā[ścā]nugatapratyayasya
tenaivānyathāsiddhatvāt| upadeśāpekṣeṇa cākṣa[cakṣu]rādinā
guṇatvādisiddhirityapi na yuktam| upadeśaḥ kiṃ
guṇalakṣaṇavatto guṇaśabdavācya iti kiṃ vā guṇalakṣaṇavanto
guṇavanta iti kiṃ vā guṇalakṣaṇavanto
guṇalakṣaṇavadvṛttiguṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitaguṇalakṣaṇatadrahitāvṛttijātimanta
iti|
nādyaḥ, evaṃvidhopadeśāntaramapi guṇaśabdavācyatvātiriktāpratīteḥ guṇalakṣaṇavanto guṇaśabdavācyā iti upadeśāpekṣeṇa cakṣuṣā guṇatvābhāvasiddhau vaktumaśakyatvācca|
na dvitīyaḥ, guṇatvādisiddhau guṇatvaśabdasya nirarthakatvāt|
anyathā guṇalakṣaṇavanto guṇatvābhāvavanta ityupadeśasāpekṣe cakṣurādīnāṃ guṇatvābhāvasiddhi deśāntaramapi cakṣurādinā guṇatvāpratīteḥ|
anyathā guṇalakṣaṇavanto naivaṃvidhatvādimanta ityupadeśāsāpekṣeṇa cakṣuṣā tathāvidhajātyabhāvasiddheratiprasaṅgāt|
ta eva guṇatvasiddheśca cakṣurādivaidharmyam|
atha nāptavācyaṃ tarhi na tatsahakṛtādapi cakṣurādeḥ guṇatvādisiddhiriti anumānāsambhavācca|
rūpaṃ rūpatvatadavāntarasattātiriktajātimat, jātimattvāt, govaditi cet---na, guṇakarmamātravṛttijātisiddhāvarthāntarāt
guṇakarmavṛttitvena sādhyaṃ viśeṣyata iti cet---na,
sattātiriktavadviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyāpātāt
guṇadravyamātravṛttijātisiddhāvarthāntarācca|
guṇadravyāvṛttitvenāpi sādhyaṃ viśeṣyat iti
cet---na, rūparasamātravṛttisiddhāvarthāntaratvāt|
rūparasamātravṛttitvarahitatvenāpi sādhyaṃ viśeṣyata iti
cet---na, rūparasagandhamātravṛttijātisiddhāvarthāntarāt|
rūparasagandhamātrāvṛttitvenāpi sādhyaṃ viśeṣyata iti
cet---na,
rūparasagandhasparśamātravṛttijātisiddhāvarthāntarāt| evaṃ
rūpāditrayoviṃśatiguṇaparyantaṃ
rūparasagandhasparśamātrāvṛttimā[? ityatrā]dināpi
sādhyaviśeṣaṇe .api
rūpaśabdamātravṛttirūparasaśabdamātrādivṛttijātisiddhāvarthāntarasya
tadavasthatvāt, rūpaśabdamātrāvṛttitvādinā sādhyaviśeṣaṇe
.api
rūpasāmānyamātravṛttirūpaviśeṣamātravṛttirūpasamavāyamātravṛttirūpābhāvamātravṛttijātisiddhāvarthāntarasya
tadavasthatvāt| rūpasāmānyavṛttitvādināpi sādhyaviśeṣaṇe
sattānyabhāvasiddhāvarthāntarāt| bādhādevaṃvidhajātyasiddhau
nārthāntarateti cet---na, tadabhāvāt| anyathā
guṇatvasyāpyasiddhiprasaṅgāt| etena rūpaṃ
rūpamātraniṣṭhatvarahitasattāvāntarajātimat jātimattvād,
govaditi nirastam|

raso guṇalakṣaṇarahitāvṛttirūpavṛttijātimān,
guṇalakṣaṇavattvāt, rūpavaditi cet---na,
rūparasamātravṛttijātisiddhāvarthāntarāt|
rūparasamātravṛttitvādinā sādhyaviśeṣaṇe .api
pūrvoktanyāyenārthāntarasya tadavasthatvāt| rūpatvena
sopādhitvācceti| ākāśaṃ rūparasaniṣṭhajātyantarabhāvavat,
guṇalakṣaṇarahitatvāt, sāmānyavaditi cet---na,
rūparasamātraniṣṭhajātyantarābhāvasiddhāvarthāntarāt|
rūparasamātraniṣṭhatvarahitatvena sādhyaviśeṣaṇatve
rūparasagandhamātraniṣṭhajātyantābhāvādinā
arthāntarāt|
guṇalakṣaṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitajātyantābhāvasādhane ca
guṇakarmamātraniṣṭhajātyantarābhāvasiddhāvarthāntarāt|

guṇakarmamātravṛttitvenāpi sādhyaviśeṣaṇe ca
guṇamūrtamātraniṣṭhajātyantābhāvasiddhāvarthāntarāt
guṇakarmamūrtamātrāvṛttitvenāpi sādhyaviśeṣaṇe
guṇakarmamūrtamātraniṣṭhajātyantābhāvādinārthāntarāt|
guṇakarmamūrtamātrāvṛttitvenāpi sādhyaviśeṣaṇe
sattātyantābhāvenārthāntarāt| ākāśaṃ saditi
pratyakṣabādhāt| nākāśe sattātyantābhāvasiddhiriti cet---na,
ākāśasya parokṣatvenākāśaṃ saditi pratyayābhāvāt| bhāve
vā sāmānyaṃ saditi bhrāntatvenāpyupapatteḥ|
jātimattvābhāvena sopādhitvāt| na cāsya pakṣetaratā,
rūparasādeḥ vipakṣādapi vyāvṛtteḥ| upādhiviraheṇa
satpratipakṣatvācceti| sattā
guṇalakṣaṇavadvyāvṛttatvarahitajātyanyā meyatvāt,
ghaṭavadityapi na yuktam|
guṇakarmamātrajātyādisiddhāvarthāntarāt|
sattānyonyaguṇavṛttitvarahitatvasiddhāvapyaguṇavṛttitvarahitatvāsiddherarthāntaratvāt|
sattānyāguṇavṛttitvarahitatvasyāguṇavṛttitvarahitatvānyatvāttadvyāpyatvācca|
tasya tena vināpi sattāyāḥ sattvāditi| evamanumānānyapi
guṇatvasādhakāni nirākaraṇīyāni| tasmānnāsti guṇatvamiti|

tadayuktam| rūpaṃ guṇalakṣaṇavṛttiguṇalakṣaṇavat
guṇalakṣaṇarahitāvṛttijātimat, jātimattvāt, govaditi
guṇatvasiddheḥ| na cātrāpi arthāntaratāva[anava?]kāśaḥ|
pūrvameva nirāsāt|
evaṃ karmā vapi
karmādilakṣaṇavadvṛttivyāvṛttatvarahitakarmādilakṣaṇavat
tadrahitavṛttijātimattvasādhanena sarvajātisiddhiḥ draṣṭavyeti|

nanu samavāyikāraṇeṣu guṇakarmasu saṃyogavibhāgakāraṇeṣu
dravyaguṇakarmasu acalanātmakajātimat sadravyaguṇeṣu
kāryānāśrayajātimatsvaguṇeṣu karmasu mūrteṣu
viśeṣaguṇavadasparśavatsvarūpavatsurasavatsu viśeṣaguṇeṣu
sāmānyaguṇeṣu ca pūrvanyāyena jātiḥ prasiddheti na| asati
bādhe tasyā apīṣṭatvāt| sati bādhe tata eva tadasiddheriti|

nanu tathāpi na brāhmaṇatvasiddhiḥ
kṣatriyādivyāvṛttatvadevadattādiniṣṭhabrāhmaṇatvasya
pratyakṣeṇānanubhavāt| ākārata evāyaṃ mayā brāhmaṇa iti
jñāta iti brāhmaṇatvajñānamanuvyavasitamiti cet---na,
tasyāpyasiddheḥ| kṣatriyādivyāvṛtta[a]kārasyāpyasiddhāyā
ākārata ityasyāpyaprasiddheḥ| brāhmaṇalakṣaṇāvagamasahitena
cakṣurādinā tadanubhava iti cet---na, tadabhāvāt| ata eva
pūrvoktanyāyenāpi tadasiddheḥ| brāhmaṇamā[tāpitṛjanyatvaṃ
tal]lakṣaṇamiti cet---na, brāhmaṇamātāpitṛjanyatvasya
tatsaṃyogavibhāgānāmativyāpakatvāt|
brāhmaṇamātāpitṛjanyaśarīratvaṃ tasya lakṣaṇamiti cet---na,
vikṛtasūkarādiṣvativyāpakatvāt|
brāhmaṇamātāpitṛjanyamanuṣyatvaṃ tallakṣaṇamiti cet---na,
sargādijātabrāhmaṇeṣvavyāpakatvāt| kalaśasambhūtāgastyādau
kṣatriyasambhūtaviśvāmitrādāvavyāpakatvācca| teṣāṃ
tathābhūtatvabrāhmaṇatvayoḥ purāṇasiddhatvācca| pra
rabhedavādivaiśeṣikādimate
dvitīyādidivasotpannabrāhmaṇeṣvavyāpakatvācca|
ekaikāvayavāvagamavinaṣṭapūrvabrāhmaṇānantarotpannabrāhmaṇeṣvavyāpakatvācca|
brāhmaṇatvasiddhau brāhmaṇamātāpitṛjanyatvasiddhiḥ| tatsiddhau
ca brāhmaṇatvasiddhiritītaretarāśrayācca|

etena brāhmaṇaśabdavācyamātāpitṛjanyamanuṣyatvaṃ
brāhmaṇalakṣaṇamiti nirastam|
adṛṣṭaviśeṣajanyatallakṣaṇamiti cet---na, tasyāprasiddheḥ|
brāhmaṇaśarīreṇa kāryeṇa tadanumānamiti cet---na, siddhe
brāhmaṇatve adṛṣṭaviśeṣajanyatvasiddhiḥ, tada[tat?]siddhau ca
brāhmaṇatvasya siddheritītaretarāśrayatvāditi| brāhmaṇa[a]aḥ
abrāhmaṇamanuṣyāvṛttijātimantaḥ, brāhmaṇamanuṣyātiriktatve
sati anyāvayavatvāditi brāhmaṇatvasiddhiriti vādivāgīśvaraḥ|

tadapi na, brāhmaṇatve brāhmaṇatvasyāsiddhatve
.anyabrāhmaṇamanuṣyāvṛttijāterapyasiddhatvāt|
brāhmaṇamātāpitṛjanyatvaṃ brāhmaṇatvama[tvā]numānāṅgamiti
cet---na, tathā sati
brāhmaṇamātāpitṛjanyamātṛ[tra]aniṣṭhajātisiddhāvarthāntarāt|
brāhmaṇatvasya
sargādibhavakalaśodbhavādisādhāraṇatvādbrāhmaṇatvāvāntarajātisiddhāvarthāntarācceti|
tadayuktam, brāhmaṇo .ayamiti pratyakṣādeva tatsiddheḥ|

na caivaṃvidhapratyakṣāsiddhiḥ| brāhmaṇo .ayamiti
vyavahārātsiddheḥ| na ca tasyāpi asiddhiḥ|
sarvalokānubhavasiddhatvāt| anyathā gotvāderapi vilayāt| na ca
brāhmaṇalakṣaṇenāsyānyathāsiddhiḥ| tadabhāvasya
tvayaivopapāditatvāditi| evaṃ kṣatriyādayaḥ samarthanīyā iti|

pṛthivītve pramāṇam|
ghaṭo
gandhavadvṛttigandhavadvyāvṛttatvarahitagandhavannirguṇavṛttitvarahitajātimān,
jātimattvāt| ghaṭo
ghaṭavadvṛttigandhavadvyāvṛttitvarahitagandhavannirgandhavṛttitvarahitajātimān,
jātimattvāt| ayaṃ ghaṭa
etadvṛttigandhavadvyāvṛttatvagandhavannirgandhavṛttitvarahitajātimān,
jātimattvāt| ayaṃ ghaṭa
etadvṛttigandhavyāvṛttatvarahitagandhavannirgandhavṛttitvarahitajātimān,
jātimattvāt, karmavadityādi|

udakatve .api tatpramāṇam|
saricchota[?]vṛttiśītavyāvṛttatvarahitagandhavannirgandhavṛttitvarahitaśītāvṛttitvarahitajātimān,
jātimattvāt|
saridvṛttiśotavyāvṛttatvarahitaśītāśītavṛttijātijātimatī
jātimattvāt| iyaṃ
saridetadvṛttiśītavyāvṛttitvarahitaśītāśītavṛttijātimatī
jātimattvāt, govadityādi|

tejastve .api pramāṇam| dīpaḥ
uṣṇapratyuṣṇavyāvṛttatvarahitoṣṇānuṣṇāvṛttijātimān,
jātimattvāt| dīpaḥ
dīpavṛttyuṣṇavyāvṛttatvarahitoṣṇānuṣṇajātimān,
jātimattvāt| ayaṃ dīpa
etadvṛttyuṣṇavyāvṛttatvarahitoṣṇāvṛttijātimān,
jātimattvāt, go[tva]vadityādi|

vāyutve pramāṇam| vāyuḥ
nīrūpasparśavadvṛttivannīrūpasparśavadvyāvṛttatvānadhikaraṇanīrūpasparśarahitāvṛttijātimān,
jātimattvāt, ghaṭādivadityādi|

ātmatve pramāṇam| ātmā
buddhimadvṛttibuddhimadvyāvṛttatvarahitabuddhimadabuddhimadvṛttijātimān,
jātimattvāt| sukhī
sukhivṛttibuddhimadvyāvṛttatvarahitabuddhimadabuddhimadvṛttijātimān,
jātimattvāt| ayamātmā
etadvṛttibuddhimadvyāvṛttatvarahitanīrūpasparśavannīrūpasparśarahitāvṛttijātimān,
jātimattvāt, ghaṭavadityādi|

manastve pramāṇam| mano
.asparśāṇuvṛttisparśāṇuvyāvṛttatvarahitasparśāṇvasparśāṇurahitāvṛttijātimān,
jātimattvāt|
mano
vṛttyavṛttisparśāṇuvyāvṛttatvarahitāsparśāṇusparśāṇurahitāvṛttijātimān,
jātimattvāt, ghaṭavadityādi|

rūpatve pramāṇam rūpaṃ
cakṣuṣṭvarahitabāhyendriyāgrāhyacakṣurgrāhyaguṇavṛttitvavyāvṛttatvarahitacakṣuṣṭvarahitabāhyendriyāgrāhyacakṣurgrāhyaguṇatvavṛttitadvyāvṛttatvarahitacakṣuṣṭvarahitatadbāhyendriyāgrāhyacakṣurgrāhyaguṇacakṣuṣṭvarahitabāhyendriyāgrāhyacakṣurgrāhyaguṇatvāvṛttirahitavṛttijātimat,
jātimattvāt| nīlaṃ
nīlavṛtticakṣuṣṭvarahitabāhyendriyāgrāhyacakṣurgrāhyaguṇavyāvṛttatvarahitacakṣuṣṭvarahitabāhyendriyāgrāhyacakṣurgrāhyaguṇatadanyavṛttitvarahitajātimat,
jātimattvāt| idaṃ
rūpametadvṛtticakṣuṣṭvarahitabāhyendriyāgrāhyacakṣurgrāhyaguṇavyāvṛttatvarahitacakṣuṣṭvarahitabāhyendriyāgrāhyacakṣurgrāhyarahitāvṛttijātimat,
jātimattvāt, ghaṭavadityādi|

evaṃ rasatvādinirapekṣarasādilakṣaṇavastu rasādiguṇeṣu
utkṣepaṇādikarmasu ca
tallakṣaṇavyāvṛttatvarahitatallakṣaṇavadrahitāvṛttijātimattvānumānena
rasatvādijātayaḥ samarthanīyāḥ|

jātinirapekṣalakṣaṇarahitaśuklādiṣu śuklo .ayaṃ śuklo
.ayamityādyanugatapratyakṣādeva śuklatvādijātayaḥ
samarthanīyāḥ|

jātinirapekṣalakṣaṇarahiteṣu anugatapratyayavismareṣu
[?vairahiteṣu?] ca vegabhāvanāsthitasthāpakaniṣṭhaṃ kathaṃ
saṃskārasiddhiriti cet---na, anumānādeva
vegāvegavṛttivegavyāvṛttatvarahitavegavṛttibhāvanāvyāvṛttatvarahitavegavṛttisthitisthāpakavyāvṛttatvarahitavegavṛttivegatvabhāvanātvasthitisthāpakatvarahitajātimattvāt,
ghaṭavadityādeḥ|

dharmādharmayoradṛṣṭatvenaikaguṇatve guṇacaturviṃśatitvānupapattiriti cet---na, dharmādharmatva[a]bhedavivakṣayā guṇacaturviṃśatitvopapatteḥ|
guṇatvāvāntarajātyapekṣayā guṇacaturviṃśatitvānupapattiriti cet--na, dharmādharmayorapi guṇatvāvāntarajātimattvātsākṣādguṇatvāvāntarajātyapekṣayā caturviṃśatitvaṃ vivakṣitam|
adṛṣṭatvāṅgīkāre ca na dharmādharmayoḥ sākṣādguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvamiti cet---
na, viśeṣaguṇatvasāmānyaguṇatvayoḥ sākṣādguṇatvāvāntarayoḥ sambhavenādṛṣṭatvena rūpatvāderapi sākṣādguṇatvāvāntarajātimattvānupapatteḥ|
sākṣādguṇatvāvāntarajātyapekṣayā guṇacaturviṃśatitvaṃ
sūtrakārairvivakṣitam|

astvatra mānā[ntaram

dravyaguṇakarmabhyo .arthāntaraṃ sa]ttā|| 1-2-8

iti tadanupapannam, vikalpāsahatvāt| tathā hi kimidaṃ
pratijñāparaṃ hetuparaṃ vā| prathame .api kiṃ sattā
dravyavyatiriktā sattā guṇavyatiriktā sattā karmavyatiriketi
pratijñātrayam| kiṃ vā sattā
vyabhicārādguṇavṛttitvakarmavṛttitvayośca
dṛṣṭāntābhāvagrastatvādguṇakarmatvādeśca pakṣatulyatvena
dṛṣṭāntānupapatteḥ|

nāpi dvitīyaḥ, sādhyātideśāt|

ātreyācāryastu
sattāyā anekasamavetatvādinā nityatvamāśaṅkya sattā nityeti
pratijñāmanusandhāya dravyaguṇakarmabhyo .arthāntaratvaṃ
heturanena sūtreṇa
pratipādyata ityāha| tadapyayuktam,
dṛṣṭāntābhāvāt, viśeṣasamavāyābhāvānāmapi
nityatvāsiddheḥ| ata eva tatsiddhau dṛṣṭāntābhāvasya
tadavasthatvāt, pradhvaṃse vyabhicārācca| anityatve
dravyaguṇakarmānyatamatvaṃ jātimattvaṃ copādhibhirabhinīyata
iti cet---na, pradhvaṃse sādhyavyāpatvabhaṅgāt|
tasmādnupapannaṃ sūtramiti|
tanna, asya pratijñāsūtratvāt|
tathā ca sattā dravyaguṇakarmavyatirikteti pratijñā| na cātra
hetvabhāvaḥ, guṇavṛttitvakarmavṛttitvayorvakṣyamāṇatvāt| na
guṇo na karmeti pratijñayoreva tayorhetutvamiti cet---na, atrāpi
tasya hetutvasambhavāt| na ca dṛṣṭāntābhāvaḥ,
guṇakarmavṛttyabhāvanāṃ dṛṣṭāntopapatteḥ|
viśeṣasamavāyayorapi dṛṣṭāntatvāsambhavāt|
dravyaguṇakarmavyatiriktatvenaiva tayordharmigrāhakapramāṇasiddhatvāditi|

ekadravya[vṛttitvāt] sattā na dravyam|| 1-2-9

ekadravyāśritatvāt, rūpavaditi| tadayuktam| sattāyā
ghaṭapaṭādyanekadravyāśritatvenaikadravyāśritatvāsiddheḥ|
ekadravyendriyasannikarṣeṇopalabhyamānatvamekadravyāśritatvamiti
ātreyācāryāḥ|
tanna, paṭādau vyabhicārāt| bhūyo.avayavendriyasannikarṣaiḥ
paṭādaya upalabhyanta iti cet---na, sattāyāmapi
bhūyo.avayavasannikarṣairupalabhyamānatvāt|
ekadravyasannikarṣeṇāpi sattopalabhyate| na tu ghaṭādaya iti
cet---na,
bhūyo.avayavasannikarṣanirapekṣadravyasannikarṣajanyasattvopalabhasya
niṣpramāṇatvāt paṭasattādīnāṃ tulyopalambhasāmagryekatvāt|
ātmā sannityatraikadravyasannikarṣeṇa sattva upalabhyate iti
cet---na, ātmano .apyatraikadravyasannikarṣeṣopalabhyamānatvena
vyabhicārasya tadavasthatvāt| ekaśabdasannikarṣeṇopalabhyate
sattā| tena ekāśrayasannikarṣeṇopalabhyamānatvamadravyatve
heturiti cet---na, ātmani vyabhicārāt tasyāpi
sattāśrayatvādekatvācca| ātmā svasannikarṣeṣaiva
sukhādisannikarṣeṇāpyupalabhyate,
sukhādisahitasyaivopalambhāditi cet---na,
sukhaduḥkhādiviśiṣṭasyāpyātmana upalambhāt, dravyagrahaṇe
vaiyarthyācceti| tasmādanupapannaṃ sūtram|

tanna, sūtrasyarthāntarasambhavāt|
tathā hi dvividhaṃ dravyam
ekadravyamanekadravyaṃ ca|
tatraikadravyaṃ dravyatvāvāntarajātirahitamākāśādi,
ekaikatvāt| anekadravyaṃ dravyatvāvāntarajātiyuktaṃ
pṛthivyādi, tadvyaktīnāmanekatvāt|
tenaikadravyavattvādityākāśāśritatvāt, kālāśritatvāt,
digāśritatvāt, dravyatvāvāntarajātirahitāśritatvāt,
ākāśādiguṇavaditi
sūtrārthaḥ|| 9||

na cātrānekasamavetatvābhāva upādhiḥ| saṃyoga | na ca
guṇatvarahitatve satyanekasamavetatvādbhāva upādhiḥ
vyāvartyavipakṣābhāvena pakṣetaratvāt|
kāryadravyāṇāmanekasamavetatvābhāvenaiva vyāvṛttatvāt| na ca
sattā dravyaṃ guṇatvarahitatve saty paṭavaditi
satpratipakṣatvam| ākāśāśritatvādi na sopādhitvāditi|
kiṃ[tu]

guṇakarmasu bhāvānna karma na guṇaśca|| 1-2-10

[ca]kārāddravyaṃ kimiti| tena sattā na dravyam, sattā na guṇaḥ,
sattā na karma, guṇavṛttitvāt, karmavṛttitvāt,
tadgatābhāvavat|

na ca guṇatvādau vyabhicāraḥ, pakṣatulyatvāt| na ca guṇatve
dravyatvarahitānekasamavetatvābhāva upādhiḥ,
vyāvartyavipakṣarahitaviśeṣaṇatvenāsya pakṣetaratvāt|
anekasamavetaguṇānāmanekasamavetatvabhāvenaiva vyāvṛttatvāt|

na ca sattā guṇaḥ, dravyatvarahitānekasamavetatvāt,
sambhoga(?)vaditi satpratipakṣatvam| guṇavṛttitvābhāvena
sopādhitvāditi| na cākramatve
guṇatvarahitaghaṭasamavetābhāva
upādhiḥ| pūrvavatpakṣetaratvāt| na ca sattā
karmaguṇatvarahitapaṭatvasamavetatvāt| ghaṭakarmavaditi
satpratipakṣatvam| karmavṛttitvābhāvena sopādhitvāt|
aśrāvaṇatvena sopādhitvācca|
karmatvābhāvagrāhakapratyakṣabādhācceti|

sāmānyaviśeṣābhāvena ceti|| 1-2-11

sāmānyaviśeṣā dravyeṣu pṛthivītvādayaḥ karmasu
cotkṣepaṇatvādayaḥ| teṣāṃ bhāvena
dravyatvaguṇatvakarmatvābhāvaḥ sattāyāmanumīyate|
dravyatvaniṣedhahetau ca dikkālākāśavyatiriktatvaṃ
viśeṣaṇamupādeyam|

prayogāstu sattā na dravyam, dikkālākāśavyatiriktatve sati
pṛthivītvāt tejastvavāyutvātmatvarahitatvāt, rūpavat| sattā na
guṇo rūpatvarasatvādicaturviṃśatisāmānyarahitatvāt,
ghaṭavaditi| sattā na karma,
utkṣepaṇa[tvāvakṣepaṇatva]tvākuñcanatvaprasāraṇatvagamanatvarahitatvāt,
ghaṭavaditi|

yadvā sāmānyaśabdena jātirviśeṣaśabdena jātiviśeṣaḥ
sattā| tadabhāvena sattāyā dravyatvaguṇa[tva]karmatvaniṣedho
.anumīyata iti sūtrārthaḥ|

tathā ca sattā na dravyam, na guṇaḥ, na karma,
jātirahitatvāccābhāvavaditi| etacca pūrvācāryābhiprāyeṇa,
asmābhiḥ sattāyāmabhāve vā sattāṅgīkārāt hetorasiddhatvāt
na dṛṣṭāntasya ca sādhyavikalatvāditi|

anye tu sāmānyaviśeṣo dravyatvaguṇatvakarmatvādi[ḥ]|
tadrahitatvātsattā na dravyam, na guṇaḥ, na karmeti
sūtrārthaṃ
varṇayanti| tadayuktam| sattā na dravyam,
dravyatvarahitatvādīnāmasiddhatvāditi| sūtre ācāryāstu
sāmānyaviśeṣau aparasāmānyam, tadabhāvena sattā
dravyaguṇakarmaṇo .arthāntaramiti sūtrārthaṃ
varṇayanti| tadapi
na, sāmānyābhāvasya vyabhicārābhāvena
vaiyarthyātsāmānyaviśeṣarahitatvasya paraṃ
pratyasiddhatvācceti|| 11||

idānīṃ dravyatvaguṇatvakarmatvādīnāṃ dravyaguṇakarmbhyo
.arthāntaratvapratipādanārthaṃ ṣaṭsūtrī---

ekadravyavattvena dravyatvamuktam|| 1-2-12

sāmānyaviśeṣābhāvena [ca]|| 1-2-13

guṇeṣu bhāvādguṇatvamuktam|| 1-2-14

sāmānyaviśeṣābhāvena|| 1-2-15

karmasu bhāvātkarmatvamuktam|| 1-2-16

sāmānyaviśeṣābhāvena ceti|| 1-2-17

dravyatvamuktaṃ [guṇatvamuktaṃ] karmatvamuktamityatra
dravyaguṇakarmabhyo .arthāntaramiti śeṣaḥ| śeṣaṃ pūrvavat||

nedamanupapannam| udāharaṇabhedamātreṇa ca ca
sūtrabhedapraṇayane
pṛthivītvādijātiṣu pratyekaṃ
sūtrapraṇayanaprasaṅgāt|
ekatra nyāyavyutpādane .anyatrāpi
jāyata iti sarvasūtrapraṇayanamiti
cet---na, dravyatvādiṣvapi
tulyatvāditi|

liṅgāviśeṣādviśeṣaliṅgābhāvāccaiko bhāva iti|| 1-2-18

kiṃ punaranena pratipādyate| sarvadeśeṣu sarvakāleṣu
vartamānāsu ca vyaktiṣu sattaikatvamiti brūmaḥ| tathāpi
athaitadghaṭagatā sattā svasmānna bhidyate
etadghaṭasattāgrāhakapramāṇena svato bhedānavagāhanāt|
bhedasādhakapramāṇāntarābhāvācca|
iyametadanyatvarahitetyabhedagrāhakapramāṇasattvācca| tathā
paṭagatasattāpi na ghaṭasattāto bhidyate|
paṭagatasattāgrāhakapramāṇoktaṃ syāt,
ghaṭagatatatsattābhedānavagāhanātpramāṇāntareṇāpi
paṭagate[ta]sattāyā ghaṭagatasattābhinnatvānavadhāraṇācca|
paṭagatasattā paṭagatasattānyatvarahiteti pratyakṣeṇa
nirṇayācca| ghaṭasattānyatvābhāvasya pratyakṣapratiyogitvena
pratyakṣatvāt|
tadidamuktam---salliṅgāviśeṣādviśeṣaliṅgābhāvāccaiko
bhāvaḥ [1-2-18] iti| saditi bhāvapradhāno nirdeśaḥ, liṅgamiti
pramāṇam `heturapadeśo liṅgaṃ nimittaṃ kāraṇaṃ
pramāṇamityanarthāntaram' ityagre sūtraṇāt|
pramāṇaṃ cātra
pratyakṣameva viśeṣābhadaḥ| sa ca vyāvṛttiḥ,
vyāvṛttijñāpako vā sattāgrāhakapramāṇenāpi viśeṣāt
bhedāgrahaṇāt| abhedagrahaṇācca bhedakapramāṇābhāvāccaikā
satteti sūtrārthaḥ|

yadā tu sattā na pratyakṣā kiṃ tvanumānagamyā tadā ghaṭo
ghaṭaniṣṭhavyāvṛttatvarahitajātimān, meyatvāt,
ghaṭavadityādinā sattānumīyate| tadā dharmigrāhakānumānena
sattaikatvasiddheḥ bhedagrāhakapramāṇānāṃ ca
dharmigrāhakapramāṇabādhenāpramāṇatvādekaikasatteti
tadidamuktam---salliṅgāviśeṣādviśeṣaliṅgābhāvāccaiko
